%Completed on :
%Typed by V. SARATHA

 //śrīḥ//

mālatīmādhavam/

prathamo'ṅkaḥ/

bhāvapradīpikā/

ajandavyā kalayāvataṃsitakacaṃ saundaryasārāspadaṃ
kāruṇyāmṛtapūrapūritalasallīlākaṭākṣāṅkuram/
udyatkoṭidineśamaṇḍalarucinyakkāribhābhāsuraṃ
vande pāśasṛṇīkṣucāpasumanobāṇāpahaṃ tanmahaḥ//

yadgaṇḍamaṇḍalagalanmadavāridhārāḥ karṇadvayavyajanavātavidhūtiśīrṇāḥ/
bhaktāntarāyanivahā iva sāryamāṇā bhānti dvipendravadanaṃ tamupāśrayāmaḥ//

ekāmramūlanilayakaribhūdharanāyakau/
kāñcīpurīśvarau vande kāmitārthaprasiddhaye//

padavākyapramāṇajño bhavabhūtirmahākaviḥ/
mālatīmādhavaṃ nāma yadrūpakamarīracat//

kriyate vivṛtistasya bhāradvājena dhīmatā/
yajvanaḥ parvateśasya sūnunā tripurāriṇā//

santi yadyapi caitasya vyākhyāḥ prācyāstathāpi tāḥ/
avaktavyoktivaktavyatyāgābhyāṃ nādṛtā budhaiḥ//

ato mitena vacasā laghvī bhāvapradīpikā/
mālatīmādhavasyeyaṃ racyate rasikapriyā//

adṛṣṭapūrvavyākhyānāmastu vaitanmanoharam/
adṛṣṭadivyakāntānāṃ mānavya iva kāminām//

prāgvyākhyāsaṃnidhāne vā bhavatvasyopayogitā/
bhānorasaṃnidhāne kiṃ na grāhyā karadīpikā//

purātananibandheṣu yeṣāṃ dhīrnāvagāhate/
teṣāmasmādṛśāṃ dupayoŸŸŸyiti//

sarvathā sādaraṃ santaḥ pratigṛhṇantvamatsarāḥ/
ko vā praṇayini prahve kuryādevāvadhīraṇām//

athavā naḥ kimetena dainyena yadi matkṛtau/
viśeṣaṃ cedviśeṣajñaḥ paśyedgṛhṇātu tāṃ tadā//

sāhityādhvaparikṣuṇṇadhiyāmasmādṛśāṃ śramam/
tādṛśā eva jānanti viduranye kathaṃ khalāḥ//

atha tatrabhavānbhavabhūtiḥ kīrtyādisatkāvyanirmāṇaprayojanaprāptaye prekṣyaprabandhabhedanāṭyapradhānaṃ prakaraṇaṃ sāṅgaṃ mālatīmādhavaṃ nāma rūpakamātatāna/ tatra prakaraṇalakṣaṇaṃ bharate - ``tatra kavirājabudhdyā vastuśarīraṃ ca nāyakaṃ caiva/ svayamutpādya viracayettajjñeyaṃ prakaraṇaṃ nāma//'' iti/ daśarūpako'pi - ``atha prakaraṇe vṛttamutpādyaṃ lokasaṃśrayam/ amātyavipravaṇijāmekaṃ kuryāttu nāyakam// dhīrapraśāntaṃ sopāyaṃ dharmakāmārthatatparam/ śeṣaṃ nāṭakavatsaṃdhipraveśakarasādikam//'' iti/ tatra nāṭyopakrame vighnopaśāntaye kuśīlavaiḥ pūrvaraṅgaḥ kriyate/ taduktam - ``prathamaṃ pūrvaraṅgaśca tataḥ prastāvaneti ca/ ārambhe sarvanāṭyānāmetatsāmānyamiṣyate// yannāṭyavastunaḥ pūrvaṃ raṅgavighnopaśāntaye/ kuśīlavāḥ prakurvanti pūrvaraṅgaḥ sa kīrtitaḥ//'' iti/ tatra cānuṣṭhitabharatopadiṣṭapūrvaraṅgāṅgakalāpena sūtradhāreṇa pāṭhyāṅgabhūtā nāndī prayoktavyā/ taduktam - ``yadyapyaṅgāni bhūyāṃsi pūrvaraṅgasya nāṭake/ teṣāmavaśyaṃ kartavyā nāndī nandīśvarapriyā//'' iti/ nāndīlakṣaṇaṃ tu - ``āśīrnamaskriyārūpaḥ ślokaḥ kāvyamukhoditaḥ/ nāndīti kathyate tasyāṃ padādiniyamo'pi vā// māṅgalyā śaṅkhacakrābjakokakairavaśaṃsinī/ nāndī padairdvādaśabhiraṣṭābhirvāpyalaṃkṛtā//'' iti/ ``api vā'' iti padāniyamapakṣopakṣepaḥ/ tathāha vidyānāthaḥ - ``kaiścinnāndyāṃ padaniyamo nābhyupagataḥ'' iti/ tatra kaviścikīrṣitasya prakaraṇasyāvighnaparisamāptaye viśiṣṭeṣṭadevatānamaskāralakṣaṇaṃ maṅgalamācaranpūrvaraṅgāṅgabhūtāmaṣṭapadātmikāmāśīralaṃkṛtāṃ nāndīṃ sūtradhāraprayojyāṃ tāvadāha - cūḍeti/ eṣā ca ``saṃdigdhamugdhendavaḥ'' iti candranāmāṅkitā nāndī/ taduktam - ``candranāmāṅkitā kāryā rasānāṃ sa yato nidhiḥ/ prīte candramasi sphītā rasaśrīriti vālukiḥ//'' iti/ ayamarthaḥ - bhūteśasya jaṭāḥ pāntviti saṃbandhaḥ/ tā viśinaṣṭi - cūḍeti/ cūḍā śikhā/ ``śikhā cūḍā keśapāśaḥ'' ityamaraḥ/ tayā ca svāśrayabhūtaṃ śiro lakṣyate/ kapālamālikāyāḥ śirobhūṣaṇatvāt/ tasyāpīḍastatra tiryaṅnaddhā srak/ ``āpīḍaśekharottasāvataṃsāḥ śirasi srajaḥ'' iti halāyudhaḥ/ āpīḍaśabdenaiva śiraḥsraguktāvapi cūḍāśabdastatsaṃnidhānapratītyarthaḥ/ karṇāvataṃsavat/ yathāhuḥ - ``karṇāvataṃsādipadaiḥ karṇādidhvaninirmitaḥ/ saṃnidhānapratītyartham'' iti/ tatra kapālāḥ/ tadgrathitatvādāpīḍasya/ teṣu saṃkulaṃ vyāptaṃ galatatpūraṇādadhaḥsravacca mandākinyāḥśaṃbhuśirogaṅgāyā vāridhārā yāsu tāḥ/ taḍittulyenālikanetratejasā sājātyādviśeṣeṇa miśrībhūtā dīptiryāsāṃ tāḥ/ komalayā ketakagarbhadroṇyā saha saṃdigdhaḥ samānākṛtivarṇatvātkiṃ ketakaśikheyamuta śaśirekheti saṃdehagocaro mugdho manohara induryāsu tāḥ/ jaṭīkṛtya baddhāsu jūṭīkṛtāsu viprakīrṇāsu/ ekasyāścandrakalāyāstirodhānaprasaṅgena ketakaśikhāsaṃśayajanakatvāyogāt/ bhujaṅgā eva vallayo latāstābhireva valayairvalayīkṛtaiḥ/ latāsadṛśairiti yāvat/ nirmitayā srajā naddhā jūṭā yāsāṃ tā iti/ atra rūpakasaṃdehānuviddhā svabhāvoktiralaṃkāraḥ/ tallakṣaṇāni tu vistarabhayānna likhyante/ atreśvarasya jaṭājūṭālaṃkṛtasya praśāntākārapratīterdhīraśāntaḥ kathānāyakaḥ sūcitaḥ/ jaṭā iti kartṛbahutvena nāyakanāyikābahutvam, bhūteśasyaityaupasarjanyena jaṭā iti svātantryeṇa nāyakasya svaprayojananirvāhe'nyapāratantryam, kāmandakyāditapasvinījanasyaiva tatkāryanirvahaṇanirantarodyogitvaṃ sūcitamiti manākkāvyārthasamunmeṣa ūhyaḥ//1//

 pracitādeva maṅgalāttādṛgantarāyanirāsaṃ matvāha - sānandamiti/ vināyakasya saṃbandhīni mukhavidhūnanāni pāntu/ vināyakasya vighnarājatvāttanmukhavidhūnanāni pratyūhanivartakānītyāśayaḥ/ teṣu nimittamāha - śūlapāṇestāṇḍave nandikaravādyamānamurajadhvaninā ghanagarjitasadṛśenāhūtātskandavāhanamayūrāttrāso bhayaṃ tasmādbhogasaṃkocabhāji sūkṣmarandhrapraveśasidhdyarthaṃ saṃkucitaśarīre phaṇipatāvupavītīkṛte/ nāsāgrarandhraṃ puṣkaravivaraṃ sānandaṃ yathā bhavati tathā/ tadaiva saṃnihitavivaralābhāt/ viśati sati/ kīdṛśyaḥ/ kapolatalotthitamadhukaramālāmukharitadiśaḥ/ cītkāro'nukaraṇaśabdastatsahitāḥ/ kāvyaliṅgamalaṃkāraḥ// etābhyāṃ ślokābhyāṃ jaladharasamayaprārambhaḥ sūcyate/ tasya nirvahaṇasaṃdhau vakṣyamāṇatvāt/ mukhasaṃdhisūcitasyaiva nirvoḍhavyatvāt/ tatra kapāletyanena balākāvaliḥ, jalakṣaraṇena vṛṣṭiḥ, vidyudityanena ca vidyut, ketakaśikhetyanena ketakodgamaḥ, bhujagavalayabaddhajaṭāpaṭalatvena jaladodayaḥ, murajadhvānena garjitaśabdaḥ, kaumārabarhīti mayūraharṣaśca sūcyata iti kecidvyācakṣate/ tadetacchabdaśaktyā pratīyate'rthaśaktyā veti ta eva jānanti/ natvete śabdāḥ prakṛtārthaniyantritā api śaktyantarabalādanvitamarthāntaraṃ pratyāyayantīti kasyacidvyutpannasya pratītiḥ/ artho'pi prakṛto naitādṛśasyārthāntarasya pratītau samartho'nubhūyate sahṛdayaiḥ/ kathāṃśānāmeva nirvahaṇe nirūpyamāṇānāṃ mukhasaṃdhau sūcananirbandhaḥ/ na tu tatra vakṣyamāṇasya sarvartuvarṇanāderapi/ tato naiṣo'rthaḥ kavervivakṣagocaro'pi/ tasmānnāyaṃ dhvanimārgavidāṃ panthāḥ//2//

 ``dantaśreṇiṣu'' ityādi ślokadvayaṃ prakṣiptamiti pūrvairavyākṛtamapi kvacitpustakeṣu dṛṣṭatvādasmābhirvyākhyāyate - danteti/ cūḍāsravantyūrmayaḥ śironadītaraṅgā vaḥ pāntu/ brahmakapālakaṃdaraparispandolbaṇā vidhiśiraḥkaroṭipariskhalanaprabhūtāḥ/ dantapaṅktiṣu saṃgalantyaḥ kṣarantyaḥ kalakalasahitena vyāvartanena paribhramaṇena vyākulāḥ/ nāsādisuṣirapradeśeṣu budbudaśabdavatyaḥ/ gaṇḍasthalābhighātādviśīrṇā atyunnatapradeśādadho viṣamasthale patantyaḥ nirbharavāridhārā yāsāṃ tāḥ/ svabhāvoktiralaṃkāraḥ//3//

 pakṣmeti/ tṛtīyameva tārtīyīkam/ ``tṛtīyādīkak svārthe vaktavyaḥ'' itīkak/ madanaploṣaṇaṃ smaradāhi locanaṃ vo'vatu/ kīdṛk/ yatpakṣmapaṅktipiṅgalatāyāḥ sarvo'pi taḍitsaṃgho leśaḥ/ kālo mṛtyureva yajvā/ īṣadvighaṭitamukule kiṃcidunmīlite/ yasminnāhavanīyasthāne jagaddhutavān/ jvālāsaṃtāpitaśiraścandrakalāsudhādhārānipātena prataptāyasi jalabindusecaneneva śātkāriprāntam/ udāttālaṃkāraḥ//4//

 atha prastāvanāṃ vivakṣustadaṅgayoḥ prarocanāmukhayoḥ prathamaṃ prarocanāṃ prastauti - nāndyanta iti/ kṛtapūrvaraṅge prathamapraviṣṭe sūtradhāre nāndīṃ paṭhitvāvaśiṣṭaṃ ca pratyāhāramārjanālepanādipūrvaraṅgāṅgakalāpaṃ niṣpādya vinirgate tatsadṛśo'nyaḥ sūtradhāraḥ praviśya prastāvayatītyarthaḥ/ taduktaṃ daśarūpake - ``pūrvaraṅgaṃ vidhāyādau sūtradhāre vinirgate/ praviśya tadvadaparaḥ kāvyamākhyāpayennaṭaḥ//'' iti/ prarocanāmukhalakṣaṇaṃ tu - ``nivedanaṃ prayojyasya nirdeśo deśakālayoḥ/ kāvyārthasūcakaiḥ śabdaiḥ sabhāyāścittarañjanam// kavikāvyanaṭādīnāṃ praśaṃsā ca prarocanā//'' ādiśabdena sabhāvastunāyakānāṃ grahaṇam/ ``sūtradhāro naṭīṃ brūte māriṣaṃ vā vidūṣakam/ svakāryaprastutākṣepi citroktyā yattadāmukham//'' iti alamiti/ aŸŸŸ dīśloka ŸŸkaṃ pūrvasūtriṇaṃ prati vilāsamasahamānasyoktiḥ/ prabhātakālaṃ nirdiśati - uditeti/ bhūyasā maṇḍalena bhāgenoditaḥ/ uditakalpa iti bhāvaḥ/ upatiṣṭhe stotreṇārādhayāmi/ ``pathyārādhanayośca'' ityātmanepadam/ tathā karoti - kalyāṇānāmiti/ viśvamūrte sarvātmaka/ ``sūrya ātmā jagatastasthuṣaśca'' iti śruteḥ/ tvaṃ kalyāṇānāṃ maṅgalarūpāṇāṃ tejasāṃ sthānamasi/ mayi dhuryāṃ sakalasaukhyasaṃpādanasamarthāṃ lakṣmīṃ dhehi prasannobhava/ yadyaditi vīpsayā prakārakārtsnyamabhipraiti/ yena kenacidrūpeṇa sthitaṃ sarvaprakāraṃ yatpāpamasti namrasya me tathābhūtaṃ sarvaprakāraṃ pāpaṃ pratijahi nivartaya/ atra yadyaditi vīpsayā yādṛśo'rtha uktastasyaiva prakṛtaparāmarśinā tacchabdenopādānānna dvitīyastacchabdo'pekṣita ityāhuḥ/ tena pūrvavākye yacchabdadvayadarśanāttaduttaravākye tacchabdadvayaṃ draṣṭavyamityapavyākhyānam/ bhagavan, bahuprakāraṃ maṅgalaṃ bhūyase maṅgalāya vitara/ maṅgalānubandhīni maṅgalāni prayacchetyarthaḥ/ iyaṃ kāvyārthasūcakena ślokena saṃskṛtaprāyavāgvyavahārarūpā bhāratī vṛttiḥ/ prarocanāṅgaṃ kāvyārthasūcanaṃ ca/ kathānāyakatanmitrādīnāmaniṣṭaparihāreṣṭaprāptipratīteḥ/ tathāpi yadyadityaghoraghaṇṭaśārdūlanandanādikṛtopadravanirdeśaḥ/ bhadraṃ bhadramiti mālatīmadayantikāprāptirūpaṃ maṅgaladvayaṃ sūcitam/ bhūyase maṅgalāyeti kapālakuṇḍalāgṛhītamālatīvipannistāraḥ/ ārakṣagṛhītamādhavamakarandaprāṇarakṣādayaḥ sūcyante//5//

 nepathya iti/ nepathyo nāma raṅgasthalasya paścādyavanikāntarito varṇagrahaṇādiyogyakuśīlavakuṭumbāvasthānadeśaḥ/ ``kuśīlavakuṭumbasya sthalī nepathya iṣyate'' iti vacanāt/ māriṣo naṭaḥ// naṭaḥ sūtradhāreṇa māriṣa iti vācyaḥ/ ``sūtrī naṭena bhāveti tenāsau māriṣeti ca'' ityuktatvāt/ suvihitāni sādhu saṃpāditāni/ kālapriyanāthasya mahākālāspadasya śaṃbhoḥ/ nānādeśanivāsītyanena sarvakalāveditvaṃ pariṣado darśayati/ vidagdhapariṣatsaṃnidhau hi prayogakauśalaprakaṭanasyotsāhaḥ/ bharatāstacchāstrādhyetāro naṭāḥ/ kiṃ tarhi taiḥ prayojyamityāha - ādiṣṭo'smīti/ nirdiṣṭaguṇaprabandhamiti/ rañjanīyavidvatpari ŸŸŸ guṇāstacchālinaṃ prabandhamityarthaḥ//

āryā iti sarvavidyāsthānavedinaḥ/ miśrā iti pūjyanirdeśaḥ/ bhūmidevā iti tatpradhānatvāt, sabhāyā guṇadoṣavivecanasya ca tadadhīnatvāt, devatātmakatvena teṣāmārādhanīyatvācca/ anena sabhāpraśaṃsā kṛtā// ete yānudāharanti ta ete guṇāḥ ka iti naṭasya praśnaḥ/ tāneva guṇānāha - bhūmneti/ bhūmnā prācuryeṇa rasānāmaṅgāṅgibhāvena niviṣṭānāṃ śṛṅgārādīnāṃ prayogā abhinayā gahanāḥ sthūladarśibhirbhāvayitumaśakyāḥ/ rasānāṃ hi vyañjanena viṣayāṇāmagūḍhatve guṇībhāvaprasaṅgāt/ gūḍhatayaiva kāminīkucakalaśavaditi/ bhāvanāccarvaṇādau sūkṣmamataya eva sahṛdayāḥ samarthāḥ/ na tu padapadārthajñānakṛtārthāḥ paṇḍitaṃmanyāḥ/ yathāha dhvanikṛt - ``śabdārthaśāsanajñānamātreṇaiva na vedyate/ vedyate sa hi kāvyārthatattvajñaireva kevalam//'' iti/ anena rasaikapravaṇatvaṃ prabandhasya jīvitabhūto guṇo darśitaḥ/ tathā sauhārdhena nirupādhikasnehena hṛdyāni manoharāṇi viceṣṭitāni nāyakatanmitrādīnāṃ vyāpārāḥ/ anena dhīrapraśāntanāyakopetatvaṃ nāma guṇo darśitaḥ/ tathā auddhatyaṃ śṛṅgārarasapradhānasyāpi nāyakasya tadvirodhibībhatsādbhutaraudrarasālambanatvam/ tathā āyojitamānukūlyena saṃghaṭitaṃ kāmasūtraṃ nāyikānāyakayoranyonyānurāgabījaṃ yena tādṛśam/ kāmo'nurāgaḥ/ sa eva vartiṣyamāṇasarvakāryabījabhūtatvātsūtramiva sūtram/ ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - mahāmāṃsavikrayāghoraghaṇṭādau yadbībhatsaraudraśṛṅgāravirodhirasopādānaṃ tadapi pradhānamālatīlābhalakṣaṇaprayojanānuguṇyena śṛṅgārāṅgabhāvāpattyā virodhaparihāreṇaucityameva saṃpādayatīti/ taduktam - ``vivakṣite rase labdhapratiṣṭhe tu virodhinām/ bhāvānāmaṅgabhāvaṃ vā prāptānāmuktiracchalā//'' iti/ atra khalvavadhānavatā kavinā bhavitavyam/ yadvirodhirasopādāne'pyanaucityaleśo'pi nāspadaṃ labhate/ yadāha - ``anaucityādṛte nānyadrasabhaṅgasya kāraṇam/ prasiddhaucityabandhastu rasasyopaniṣatparā//'' iti/ anenaucityaśālitvaṃ nāma guṇo darśitaḥ/ kathāścitrāḥ/ vastu punarāścaryajanakamiti vṛttavaicitryaṃ guṇaḥ/ vāci vidagdhatā uktau cāturyam/ nirdoṣaguṇālaṃkārayogo'nena darśitaḥ/ īdṛśaguṇagariṣṭhaprabandhaprayogārthamādiṣṭo'smīti bhāvaḥ//6//

 kasminniti/ ŸŸdṛśaḥ prabandhaḥ prāyeṇa lakṣyata ityāśayaḥ// smṛtamiti/ asti kaścidīdṛśaḥ prabandho mayedānīṃ diṣṭyā smṛta iti bhāvaḥ/ taittirīyāḥ tittiriproktaṃ chandastaittirīyam/ tadadhīyata iti taittirīyāḥ/ pañcāgnayo gārhapatyādipañcāgniparicaryāparāḥ/ āhitāgnaya iti yāvat/ somapīthinaḥ somapānameṣāmastīti te/ tathā jyotiṣṭomādikratvanuṣṭhātāra ityarthaḥ/ dhṛtavratāstaponiyamaniṣṭhāḥ/ udumbarākhyaṃ kulanāma yeṣāṃ te/ tathā brahmavādino'dhyātmaniṣṭhāḥ/ ta iti/ śrotriyāḥ sāṅgavedādhyayanatadarthajñānatadanuṣṭhānaniṣṭhā viśiṣṭakulaprasūtāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ/ ``śrotriyaṃśchando'dhīte'' iti nipātanātsādhuḥ/ tattvasya pāramārthikasya brahmasvarūpasya paramapuruṣārthasya viśeṣataḥ asaṃbhāvanāviparītabhāvanānirāsapūrvakaṃ niścayo nirvicikitsaṃ jñānaṃ tasmai prayojanāya/ bhūriśrutaṃ śāśvatam/ ``brāhmaṇena niṣṭādharmaḥ ṣaḍaṅgo vedo'dhyeyo jñeyaḥ'' iti nityavacchrutibodhitatvena nityaṃ śrutaṃ śāstraśravaṇamādriyante, na tu prativādigarvanirvāpaṇena khyātilābhārtham/ yāgādyanuṣṭhānārthaṃ taṭākādinirmāṇārthaṃ cārthānādriyante, na tu bhogecchayā/ saṃtatisaṃpādanāya dārān, na tu manmathaṃ kṛtārthayitum/ tapaḥsaṃvardhanārthamāyurapekṣante, na tu jīvanalobhena maraṇabhayena veti/ parisaṃkhyālaṃkāraḥ//7//

 āmuṣyāyaṇasya amuṣyakulaprasūtasya putrasya/ naḍāditvātphak/ ``āmuṣyāyaṇāmuṣyaputrikāmuṣyakuliketi cālugvaktavyaḥ'' iti ṣaṣṭhyā aluk/ śrīkaṇṭhapadaṃ lāñchanaṃ yasya saḥ/ bhavabhūtiriti vyavahāre tasyaiva nāmāntaram/ bharateṣu nisargasauhṛdena nāṭyavedapāragatvenābhinayārthaṃ nāyakādinirmāṇanipuṇatvena ca naṭāstasmingurau yathā bhaktiṃ kurvantīti teṣu tadvātsalyādvartamānaḥ svakṛtimevaṃguṇabhūyasīṃ bhūmnetyatra nirdiṣṭaguṇaśālinīṃ sabhāsu prayogeṇa prakhyāpayantvityasmākamarpitavān/ yatra kṛtau khalu prasi Ÿ vāco ŸŸrvacobhaṅgiḥ/ ye nāmetyādi/ atra pramukhe vinayapradarśanaṃ kavitvagavagddhatyaprakaṭanaṃ ca/ nāmeti prasiddhau/ madapekṣayāpi prasiddhā ye kecinmahātmāno no'smākamavajñāṃ prathayanti kiyadetadityavajānanti/ te hi vivekasalilasaṃkṣālitasaṃsāravāsanāpaṅkakalaṅke manasi pratyagātmavastveva paribhāvayantastṛṇamiva jagadaśeṣamavalokayantaḥ kimapi rahasyamupaniṣadekagamyaṃ vastu jānanta iti yuktaiva teṣāmasmāsvavidyāparatantreṣvavajñā/ ata eva tānuddiśya nāyaṃ yatnaḥ/ kiṃ tarhyatītānāmanena prayojanābhāvādvartamānānāmapyuttamādhamānāmatrāpravṛtteḥ sadṛśasya caivaṃvidhagranthanirmāṇadakṣasyaitadanupādānāt/ yo matsadṛśa utpatsyate taṃ pratīti yattadoranupādānepi sāmarthyāttadubhayalābhaḥ/ tādṛśasyotpattiḥ saṃbhāvyata ityata āha - kāla iti/ niravadhiranantaḥ/ vipulā vistīrṇā/ ataḥ kasmiṃściddeśe kasmiṃścitkāle matsadṛśo'pi kvacidetadupādāyī janiṣyata iti saṃbhāvyata ityarthaḥ/ athavā nāmeti kilārthe/ ye kecidyādṛśāstādṛśāno'vajñāṃ kila prathayanti svamahimnā jagatsarvamabhibhavatāmasmākamapi/ uḍubhiriva bhānoḥ kṣudraiḥ kācidavajñāvārteti citramiti bhāvaḥ/ te kiṃ jānanti/ na kimapītyarthaḥ/ ajñatvānna tadavajñayāsmākaṃ kācitkṣatiriti bhāvaḥ/ apiśabdo bhinnakramaḥ/ eṣa yatno'pi tānprati na/ teṣāmalakṣyatvāt/ kaṃ prati tarhi/ atītaiḥ prayojanābhāvādvartamānasya ca matsadṛśasyābhāvādyaḥ kaścinmādṛśa utpatsyate taṃ prati/ kathamidānīmetāvati prapañce'pyavidyamānastvādṛśa itaḥ paramutpatsyata iti/ saṃbhāvanetyata āha - anavadhau kāle'pyaparicchinne deśe yathāhameka etāvataḥ kālasyotpannastadvadeva ko jānīte kadā vā kutra vā tādṛśaḥ kaścidutpadyetāpītyarthaḥ/ hetvalaṃkāraḥ//8//

 guṇairityādi/ satāmasmadgurūṇāṃ guṇaiḥ prakhyāpyamānaiḥ/ tacchiṣyasya mama ko guṇo na prakaṭīkṛtaḥ tādṛśairgurubhiḥ śikṣitatvādeva svasminsarve saṃbhāvyanta ityarthaḥ/ tameva guruṃ nidarśayati - yathārtheti//9//

 yadvedeti/ upaniṣadāṃ vedaśirasāṃ sāṃkhyasya kāpilasya yogasya pātañjalasya ca mokṣaśāstrasya/ nahīti tatparijñānaṃ na kāvyanirmāṇanaipuṇyopayogamityarthaḥ/ kiṃ tarhi nāṭaŸŸnopayuktamatha āha-vacasāṃ yatprauḍhitvaṃ vivakṣitārthasamarpaṇaprāvīṇyam/ ``vivakṣitārthanirvāhaḥ kāvye prauḍhiriti smṛtā'' iti vacanāt/ ``pade vākyārtharacanā vākyārthe ca padābhidhā/ prauḍhirvyāsasamāsau ca sābhiprāyatvamasya ca//'' ityevaṃrūpaṃ vaicitryaṃ vā/ udāratā prabandhasaundaryaṃ ślāghyārthatvaṃ vā/ arthato gauravamanekārthapratipādanasāmarthyaṃ gāmbhīryarūpaṃ tadetaccedasti/ tadeva pāṇḍityasya vyutpattervaidagdhyasya kavitāśakteśca gamakaṃ jñāpakam/ prabandhaguṇāndṛṣṭvā svayameva vidvāṃso mama vaidagdhyaṃ jñāsyanti/ natvetanmayā svakaṇṭhena vaktavyamityarthaḥ//10//

 etāvatā kavikāvyapraśaṃsā kṛteti prarocanāṃ nirvṛtyāmukhaṃ prastauti - tāvadityādi/ vīthyaṅgānāmāmukhe vikalpitatvānnātra teṣāmupādānamiti mantavyam/ tāvatsākalyena bhūmikāstattadabhinayakāryaṃ prati janocitavarṇaveṣabhāṣābhinayādiparigrahānsvavargāntargatāḥ pāṭhitāḥ śikṣitāḥ/ saugatajaratpravrājikāyā bauddhavṛddhasaṃnyāsinyāḥ prathamāmāditaḥ praveśanīyām// tataḥ kim/ brūhīti śeṣaḥ/ tata iti/ āvābhyāṃ tāvatkāmandakyā avalokitāyāśca bhūmikādvayaṃ prathamameva gṛhītam/ pradhānanāyakabhūmikāparigrahastu kathaṃ kena vā kartavya ityarthaḥ/ uttaramāha - kalahaṃseti/ kalahaṃso mādhavasya ceṭaḥ, makarando mittram, tatpraveśāvasara evaitanmādhavabhūmikāparigrahaṇaṃ bhaviṣyatīti nedānīṃ taccintanīyam/ tena sarvaṃ suṣṭhu vihitam, na kiṃcidapi kartavyamavaśiṣṭamityarthaḥ/ tadanena prathamaṃ kāmandakyavalokitāpraveśaḥ sūcitaḥ/ kecittu yāvantyatra prakaraṇe pātrāṇi teṣāṃ sarveṣāmapi prathamataḥ praveśaḥ sūcita iti vyācakṣate/ tadasaṃgatam/ tatpraveśāvasara eva praveśasya sūcanīyatvāt/ na tu kṛtsnaprakaraṇapātrapraveśaḥ prathamataḥ sūcanīya iti prakaraṇalakṣaṇatve na kvacidapi granthe dṛśyate/ tattatpātrapraveśasamaye tattatsūcanaṃ sphuṭamevāgre dṛśyata ityalam// teneti/ sāmājikanirdiṣṭaguṇayogitvena vargairadhītatvena cetyarthaḥ samāje ŸŸŸ[samave]tānsāmājikān/ ``paśūnāṃ samajo'nyeṣāṃ samājo'tha sadharmiṇām'' ityamaraḥ// bāḍhamiti/ tathā kurma ityarthaḥ/ eṣa iti/ sūtradhārarūpaparityāgena kāmandakībhūto'smi// ahamapyavalokitā saṃvṛttetyetadāmukhāṅgaṃ prayogātiśayaḥ/ taduktam - ``eṣoya'mityupakṣepātsūtradhāraprayogataḥ/ pātrapraveśo yatrāyaṃ prayogātiśayo mataḥ//'' iti/ anyasya pātrasya praveśa eṣo'yamityupakṣepaḥ/ sūtradhārasyaiva samanantarapātratva eṣo'hamityupakṣepa iti nātīva bhedaḥ// iti prastāvanā// parivṛtya nirgamanaṃ nāṭayitvā/ kāṣāyaśāṭīviracitaveṣāvupaviṣṭau kāmandakyavalokitāveṣadhāriṇau praviśataḥ/ ityevaṃ prastāvanāṃ parisamāpya sūcanīyakathāṃśasūcanārthaṃ miśraviṣkambhamārabhate - kāmandakītyādi/ vadatīti śeṣaḥ/ viṣkambhalakṣaṇaṃ tu - ``vṛttavartiṣyamāṇānāṃ kathāṃśānāṃ nidarśakaḥ/ saṃkṣepārthastu viṣkambho madhyapātraprayojitaḥ//'' iti/ 

śuddhaḥ saṃkīrṇo vā dvividho viṣkambhako'pi vaktavyaḥ/ madhyamapātraiḥ ``śuddhaḥ saṃkīrṇo nīcamadhyamakṛt//'' iti/ śeṣaṃ nāṭakavadityatideśātprakaraṇe'pi viṣkambhakaniveśaḥ/ tathā cāhuḥ - ``prakaraṇanāṭakaviṣaye praveśako nāma vijñeyaḥ/ viṣkambhakastu kāryo nāṭakayoge prakaraṇe ca//'' iti// avalokiteti/ ājñāpayatu bhagavatī// apināmeti/ kalyāṇinoḥ kalyāṇaṃ maṅgalamanyonyānurūpaṃ vayorūpabhāgyasaubhāgyādikamanayorastīti tau tathoktau/ anena cānyonyānuguṇayogānmālatyeva mādhavasya yogyā vadhūḥ, sa eva tasyāḥ samucito varaḥ, iti sūcitam/ bhūrīti ślāghyavaṃśaprasūtatvena kulatatsaṃbandhārhatvaṃ dhvanitam/ mālatīmādhavayoritaretarayogadvandvanirdeśena sāhityapratīteḥ/ taddheturanyonyānurāgo'stīti dyotyate/ abhimataṃ tayorbandhūnāmanyeṣāṃ ca sahṛdayānāmabhilaṣitamityarthaḥ/ tadanena mālatīmādhavayoranyonyānurāgo'tra prakaraṇe bījabhūtaḥ sūcito bhavati/ yathā - ``stokoddiṣṭaḥ kāryaheturbījaṃ vistāryanekadhā'' iti/ prathamaṃ svalpamevoddiṣṭaḥ paścādanekadhā vistārī yaḥ kāryasya prayojanasya hetuḥ so'rtho bījamityucyate/ atra ca prakara ŸŸŸtīprāŸrmādhavasya pradhānaṃ kāryam/ taddhetuśca tayoranurāgo bījam/ bījaṃ nāma prāthamikī arthaprakṛtiḥ/ kāryaṃ pañcamī/ prā[pā]ṇigrahaṇamaṅgalamapi nāma syāt/ kiṃ nu khalu bhavet/ anena mālatīmādhavasamāgamarūpaphalārthamautsukyapratīterārambho nāma prathamāvasthā darśitā/ yathā - ``autsukyamātramārambhaḥ phalalābhāya bhūyase'' iti/ bījārambhasamavyayāccāyaṃ mukhasaṃdhiḥ/ taduktam - ``bījabindupatākākhyaprakarīkāryalakṣaṇāḥ/ ārambhayatnaprāptyāśā niyatāptiphalāgamāḥ// arthaprakṛtayaḥ pañca pañcāvasthāsamanvitāḥ/ yathāsaṃkhyena jāyante mukhādyāḥ pañca saṃdhayaḥ//'' iti/ tatrārthahetumadbhedo yatra bījasya tanmukham/ tasya ca mukhasaṃdherupakṣepādīnyaṅgāni dvādaśa bījārambhānuguṇyena prayoktavyāni/ tāni ca yathāvasaramagrata eva nirūpayiṣyanta ityalam// saharṣamiti/ vicintyamānamanorathasiddhisūcakanimittalābhāt/ tadeva darśayati - vivṛṇvateti/ āntarajñenevāsminnarthe sva[kha]lviyaṃ saṃdigdhe kimetatprayojanaṃ bhaviṣyati na veti, tadasyāḥ saṃśayamapanayāmītyevamasmadabhiprāyavedineva kalyāṇaṃ vivṛṇvatā bhāvimaṅgalaṃ sūcayatā sphuratā vāmakena savyena/ atha ca pratikūlenāpi satā cakṣuṣā dākṣiṇyaṃ cintyamānakāryasiddhiśaṃsitvenāprātikūlyam/ atha ca savyetaratvamaṅgīkriyate/ strīṇāṃ vāmākṣispandaḥ śubhaśaṃsīti naimittikāḥ/ virodhābhāso'laṃkāraḥ//11//

 avalokitā - mahānkhalveṣa cintārūpo bhagavatyāścittāvakṣepo manaḥkhedaḥ/ āścaryamāścaryam/ yadidānīṃ cīrai rathyāpatitapaṭaccarakhaṇḍaiḥ kṛto yaścīvaraḥ kanthā tanmātraprāvaraṇāṃ piṇḍasya bhaikṣakavalasya prāṇadhāraṇocitasya pāta udarakuhare nikṣepastanmātraprāṇavṛttiḥ/ pātre pāṇau mukhe vā yairyadannaṃ vinipātyate tadevauṣadhavannigīryate, na tu rasāsvādapūrvakaṃ bhujyata ityarthaḥ/ bhagavatīṃ māhātmyavatīmātmavatīmīdṛśeṣvāyāseṣu duḥkhajanakeṣu rāgijanocitadūtyādikṛtyeṣvamātyabhūrivasurniyojayatīti tasya tāvatkāryagrastatvādanyena cāsya duṣkaratvādanucite'pi vyāpāre bhagavatīṃ niyojayatu nāma/ tasmiṃścāyāsa utkhaṇḍita ucchinnaḥ saṃsāra evāvagraho niḥśreyasāmṛtavarṣapratibandhaḥ/ mokṣāntarāya iti yāvat/ sa yasya ātmā yuṣmābhirapi nikṣipyata iti yadetatsarvamāścaryamiti yojanā// kāmandakī/ vatse iti/ naivaṃ vaktavyamityarthaḥ// yanmāmiti/ sa pūjyo bhavānbhūrivasurmāṃ vidheyaviṣaye kṛtyaviṣaye niyuṅkta iti yattatsnehasya premṇaḥ phalam/ asau visrambhayogyaṃ premaiva praṇaya ityucyate/ nirvyājaṃ saujanyameva praṇaya iti kecit/ tasya sāraḥ sthirāṃśaḥ/ snehātirekādanucite'pi karmaṇi niyogo'smākaṃ ca tadaṅgīkaraṇaṃ yuktameveti bhāvaḥ/ prāṇairathavā tebhyo'pi ślāghyaistapobhirvā suhṛdabhilaṣitakṛtyaṃ ghaṭeta yadi sidhyet/ tattarhi niḥśreyasādapyadhikaṃ kṛtyamasmatprāṇaistapobhiśca kṛtaṃ niṣpāditaṃ syādityāvṛttyā yojanīyam/ mitrakāryaniṣpādanaṃ mokṣādapi garīya iti bhāvaḥ/ amātyabhūrivasuḥ svasutāṃ mālatīṃ svabālamitrasya devarātasyātmajāya mādhavāya praditsurnarapaternandananāmne narmasacivāya mālatīṃ yācamānādbhayena prakāśaṃ dātumaśaknuvangūḍhavivāhanirvartanavyāpāre kāmandakīṃ niyuktavāniti kathā upari sphuṭībhaviṣyati//12//

 kimiti bhūrivasormādhavāyaiva mālatī dātavyeti nirbandhaḥ/ bhagavatyā vā prāṇavyayenāpyetatkāryaṃ saṃpādyamiti kimityabhiniveśa ityata āha - kiṃ na vetsīti/ ābālyānmadantevāsinī tvamapyetatsarvaṃ jānāsyeveti bhāvaḥ/ na iti/ bhūrivasudevarātayormama cetyarthaḥ/ nānādigantavāsena bahudeśavāsena sāhacaryaṃ sahacarabhāvaḥ/ ekatrādhyayananibandhanā maitrī yadaivābhūttadaivāsmaditi pañcamībahuvacanam/ asmatta eva matkāritaiva yayoriyaṃ pratijñā pravṛttā/ ketyata āha - āvābhyāmiti/ ataśca pūrvapratijñātvādbhūrivasormatpuraḥsaratvānmama cātra nirbandho yukta iti bhāvaḥ/ matprathamaśiṣyā saudāminyapyatrārthe sākṣiṇītyata āha - saudāminīsamakṣamiti/ tena tasyā apyetatprayojananirvāhakāntaḥpātitvapratīteḥ ante tayā kariṣyamāṇo mālatyupadravaparihāraḥ kathāṃśaḥ sūcito bhavati/ nāpi māṃ bhūrivasurdūtye niyuṅkte/ kiṃ tvahameva matkāṅkṣitapratijñānirvāhārthaṃ pravarte/ na me dūtyalāghavamāśaṅkanīyamityapi sūcyate/ mama ca saudāminyāścāsmatsaudāminīsamakṣamiti vyaŸŸānamanupapannam/ sa ca devadattaśca tāvitivat ``tyadādīni sarvairnityam'' ityekaśeṣaprasaṅgāt/ dviparyantādīnāmeva tyadāditvamiti tu tyadādyatvamātranirāsārtham/ tadidānīmiti/ tatpratijñātam/ mantriṇetipadaṃ bahuprayojanoddeśena putramatra preṣitavāniti tasya buddhikauśalaṃ prakaṭayitum/ ānvīkṣikī nyāyaśāstram/ nītiśāstrasyāpi nyāyamūlakatvāttadantargatiḥ/ suvihitaṃ sādhu saṃpāditam// suvidhāne yuktimāha - apatyeti/ pūrvameva saṃbandhasya pratijñātatvātsvayameva bhūrivasuḥ sutāṃ mādhavāya dāsyatīti yadi tamatra devarāto na preṣayettadā smārakābhāvātkālaviprakarṣācca bhūrivasuḥ pratijñāṃ vismaredapi/ tadanena putrapreṣaṇena suhṛdaḥ pratijñā smṛtiviṣayaṃ prāpitā/ yadi vā kadācidadṛṣṭavaśātsmarannapi varaguṇaparijñānādatilokalāvaṇyaśālinīṃ sutāṃ dātuṃ saṃśayīta, tadapi nirākṛtam/ yataḥ prarocanārthaṃ saṃbandhecchotpādanārthaṃ yūnoranyonyānurāgajananārthaṃ cānitarasādhāraṇaguṇastanūjaḥ prakaṭīkṛtaśceti yuktidvayātpratijñātaṃ sutapreṣaṇena suvihitamityarthaḥ//13//

 avalokitā - kimiti mālatīmamātyo mādhavasyātmanā na pratipādayati/ yena cauryavivāhe bhagavatīṃ tvarayati/ mādhavasyeti caturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī// svayamapradāne nimittamāha - kāmandakī/ tāmiti/ narapaternarmasuhṛtkrīḍāsacivonandanastāṃ mālatīṃ nṛpamukhena yācate/ nandanāya prayaccheti rājñā yācayatītyarthaḥ/ rājaguṇena yācate/ na tu ślāghyavaraguṇeneti bhāvaḥ/ tasyā narapatiprārthanāyāḥ sākṣānmukhato na prayacchāmītyeva niṣedho rājñaḥ kopāya syāt/ ayaṃ tu cauryavivāharūpa upāyaḥ śivaḥ śobhanaḥ/ abhimatasya saṃbandhanṛpadveṣalakṣaṇeṣṭāniṣṭaprāptiparihārahetutvāt//14//

 avalokitā - āścaryamāścaryam/ na khalvamātyo mādhavasya nāmāpi jānātīti mādhave nirapekṣatā lakṣyate/ kathaṃ tasmai sutāṃ praditsustaṃ mādhavamapyajānāna iva tasminnaispṛhyamācaratītyarthaḥ// kāmandakī - saṃvaraṇaṃ tat, rājabhayenākāragopanaṃ tat/ ajñānanaṭanamityarthaḥ/ ito'pyajñānaṃ naṭanīyamityāha - viśeṣatastviti/ bālyānantarabhāvitvādbhūyasā taddharmānuvṛtteśca/ bālyamiha prathamayauvanamucyate/ tena yuktāyuktavicāravidhure, ākāragopanānabhijñe, madanājñaikavaśaṃvade, gurujanamullaṅghyāpi manorathaṃ saṃpādayitumudyuñjāne, prathamayauvane vartamānatvādvivṛtabhāvayoḥ prakaṭībhūtānyonyānurāgakuṇṭhībhāvaparihārārthaṃ viśeṣataḥ svamatestadanyonyānurāgaviṣayāyā nihnavaḥ kāryaḥ pracchādanaṃ kartavyam/ mālatyapi mādhavānuraktāṃ māṃ tāto na jānātīti yathā manyate, mādhavo'pi svasutānuraktaṃ māmamātyo na vettīti yathā pratyeti, tathā tadajñānaṃ nāṭayitavyam/ tathā satyakuṇṭhitotsāhayostayoḥ svata evābhimatasaṃghaṭanaṃ sidhyet/ anyathā gurujanena viditamasmaccāpalamiti lajjayā tadudyogabhaṅgādabhilaṣitaḥ kṛtakapariṇayo na niṣpadyeteti bhāvaḥ/ atra nāyakayoranyonyānurāgasya bījasyopanyāsādupakṣepo nāma mukhasaṃdheraṅgamuktaṃ bhavati/ yathā - ``bījanyāsa upakṣepaḥ'' iti//15//

 anurāgeti - amātyo mādhavasya nāmāpi na jānātītyetatsaṃvaraṇamātramiti kiṃ vaktavyam/ yataḥ vatsayorubhayorapi kāmandakīṃ prati priyasuhṛdapatyatvādvatsayoriti nirdeśaḥ/ anurāgapravādaḥ sārvalaukikaḥ sarvalokaviditaḥ/ ``lokasarvalokābhyām-'' iti ṭhak/ anuśatikāditvādubhayapadavṛddhiḥ/ pāmarā api jānanti kimutāmātya iti bhāvaḥ/ anena bījasya bahulīkaraṇātparikaro nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktaṃ bhavati/ yathā - ``tadbāhulyaṃ parikaraḥ'' iti/ evaṃ ca satyasmākaṃ śreyo bhavati/ kutaḥ/ hi yasmādrājanandanau pratāyag vañcayituṃ śakyau syātām/ yadi kṛtakapariṇaye vṛtte rājā nandano vā kupyet kathamanayā varākyā bhikṣukyāsmadvirodha ācarita iti, tadā na mamaŸā doṣaḥ tāvanyonyānurāgavaśena svata eva saṃmilitau/ sa cānurāgaḥ sarvalokaŸŸddha ityasmākaṃ ŸŸnaṃ sulabhamityarthaḥ//16//

 evameva ca vidagdhāḥ svayamudāsīnavadvartamānāstadvārtāmapyajānānā iva paraiḥ pratīyamānā buddhikauśalenaiva kāryāṇi nirvahanto dṛśyanta ityāha - bahiriti/ vidvānkāryavedyeko'nekāptavarganikṣiptakāryabharo'pyanyairekavatpratīyamānaḥ san bahiḥ svakīyavāstavavyāpārapracchādanena lokaṃ vañcayitumeva kevalamārabdhe svābhilaṣitarahasyakāryādbahirbhūte'nyasminmanaḥpūrvake'pi vyāpāre sarvasyākārasya veṣabhāṣāvyavaharaṇāderanubhāvasya prāvaṇyena/ bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ/ tadekaparatvena ramaṇīyaṃ lokasya cittarañjanaṃ yathā bhavati tathā vyavaharannayamaharniśametadekaparo vartate/ na hyasāvānyakāryavārtāmapi jānātīti yathā randhrānveṣiṇāmapi pratītistathā vyavaharannityarthaḥ/ pareṣāṃ śatrūṇāmabhyūhasya rahasyotprekṣaṇasya samucitāni sthānāni chidrāṇi tanutarāṇyapyatisūkṣmāṇyapi sthagayati pidhatte evaṃ sakalasūkṣmadarśinamapi janaṃ kapaṭairvañcanavyāpārairatisaṃdhāya viśvāsajananena vañcayitvā svayaṃ taṭastha udāsīna iva pratibhāsamāna eva svānarthānghaṭayati saṃpādayati/ maunaṃ ca bhajate/ abhimatakāryaviṣaye vāṅmātramapi bahirna vadatītyarthaḥ/ vidagdhajanasvabhāvoktiḥ//17//

 idānīmavalokitā svānuṣṭhitakāryāṃśamāha - avalokitā/ mayeti/ sa ca mayāpi yuṣmākaṃ vacanāttena tenopanyāsena bahuvidhānāśaṅkanīyavyājoktiprayogeṇa bhūrivasumandirasyāsannatararājamārgeṇa mādhavaḥ saṃcāryate/ yathā mālatīnayanagocaro'sau bhavati tathā kṛtamityarthaḥ/ āsannatvena sarvāvayavasauṣṭhavasya samyagvibhāvyatā sūcitā/ bahuśaḥ saṃcaraṇe'pyanāśaṅkanīyatvaṃ rājamārgapadena sūcitam// tvatprayuktopāyasya phalamasmābhiradhigatamityāha - kāmandakī/ kathitamiti/ dhātryāḥ sutā dhātreyī/ ``strībhyo ḍhak''/ tena tasyāḥ saha stanyapānasahapāṃsukrīḍādinātivisrambhasthānatvātsarvamapi tanmanogataṃ vettīti sūcitam/ kimiti kathitamata āha - bhūya iti/ svamandirāsannapuravīthyāṃ bahuśo gatāgatāni kurvāṇaṃ mādhavaṃ krīḍāsaudhaśikharagopānasīsaṃnihitatuṅgagavākṣamārgasthitā mālatī pratyakṣaṃ nūtanaṃ dāhānantarotpannaṃ kāmaṃ ratiriva nūtanotpannā satītvena tasminneva svabhartari baddhānurāgā dṛṣṭvā sābhilāṣaṃ punaḥ punarvilokya gāḍhotkaṇṭhā tattadanubhāvavyajyamānapravṛddharaṇaraṇikālulitalulitairmadanasaṃtāpenātyantakaluṣitaiḥ/ kāmāvasthānimittakamanīyavarṇāntaraśālibhiraṅgakairiti yāvat/ tāmyati mlāyatīti kathitamityanvayaḥ/ atrotkaṇṭhāsvarūpaṃ tadanubhāvāśca bhāvaprakāśikāyām - ``sarvendriyasukhāsvādo yatrāstīti manastriyaḥ
(
ṛ) tatprāptīcchāṃ sasaṃkalpāmutkaṇṭhāṃ kavayo viduḥ// antaḥ saṃbhogasaṃkalpastattadāśāvalokanam/ aṅgamlānirmanoraktirmanorathavicintanam// adhijānubhujālambikapolatalamāsanam/ prasannamukharāgaśca svedoṣmā gadgadā ca vāk// utkaṇṭhānubhavā bhāvāḥ kathyante bhāvakovidaiḥ//'' iti// atra mālatyā mādhavaviṣayo'bhilāṣaḥ prathamāvastheti mantavyam/ yathā - ``abhilāṣamanaḥsaṅgau saṃkalpo guṇanutiḥ kriyādveṣaḥ/ tāpo mānatyāgonmādau mūrcchā mṛtirdaśāvasthāḥ//'' atra ca ``abhilāṣaspṛhā ramye dṛṣṭe cālambane smṛte/ autsukyasvedaromāñcaharṣavismayasādhvasaiḥ// cāpalastambhalajjādyairanubhāvaiśca bhāvyate//'' iti//18//

 avalokitā - bāḍham/ yallavaṅgikayā kathitaṃ taddṛḍhamityarthaḥ/ dṛḍhatve yuktyantaramāha - tatastayā mālatyodvegavinodanaṃ mādhavapraticchandakaṃ tatsadṛśī pratimābhilikhitaṃ yatpraticchandakaṃ lavaṅgikayā mandārikāhaste'dya nikṣiptaṃ tāvat/ krameṇa mādhavahastaṃ prāpsyatīti tāvacchabdārthaḥ// kāmandakī/ vicintyeti/ mandārikāhaste citraphalakanidhānasya tātparyaṃ vicārya tadvivṛṇotīti śeṣaḥ/ suvihitamityādi/ anucaraśceṭaḥ/ vihāradāsīṃ bauddhālayaparicārakāṃ tāṃ mandārikām/ tīrthenāvataraṇamārgeṇa/ upoddhātāya prakṛtakāryasiddhihetubhūtāyārthāya/ sa cārtho mayīyamanurakteti mādhavasya pratyayaḥ/ satyaiva tasmiṃstasya kṛtakārye pravṛttisiddherityabhiprāyaḥ/ idaṃ tātparyaṃ mandārikāhaste citraphalakanidhānasyetyarthaḥ// avalokitā/ svānuṣṭhitaṃ kāryāntaramāha - atra mādhavo'pi kautūhalamutpādya mayā pravṛttamadanamahotsavaṃ madanodyānaṃ prabhāte'nupreṣitaḥ/ kimartham/ tatra kila mālatī gamiṣyati/ tato'nyonyadarśanaṃ bhaviṣyatīti hetoḥ// kāmandakī/ sādhvityādi/ matpriye madabhilaṣitasaṃpādane'bhiyogo'tyantābhiniveśastena// avalokitā - bhagavati/ sedānīṃ saudāminī samāsāditāścaryamantrasiddhiprabhāvā śrīparvate kāpālikavrataṃ dhārayati// kāmandakī/ kuta iti/ kasmādupalabdhetyarthaḥ// avalokitā - astyatra nagaryāṃ mahāśmaśānapradeśe karālā nāma cāmuṇḍā// kāmandakī/ astīti/ asti/ prasiddhaiva sā śrūyata ityarthaḥ/ vividhānāṃ manuṣyapaśuprabhṛtīnāṃ jīvānāmupahāre priyā/ anena pañcamāṅke mālatyupahārapradarśanaṃ sūcitam/ sāhasikānāṃ sāhasaśīlānām// avalokitā - tasminkhalu śrīparvatādāgatasyeto nātidūrasmaśānavāsinaḥ sādhakasya mantrasādhanodyatasya muṇḍadhāriṇo naraśiraḥkapāladhāriṇo'ghoraghaṇṭanāmadheyasyāntevāsinī mahāprabhāvā kapālakuṇḍalā nāmānusaṃdhyamāgacchati/ tata iyaṃ pravṛttirvārtā/ mayā śruteti śeṣaḥ// kāmandakī/ sarvamiti/ saṃbhāvyata iti/ anenottaratra saudāminīsādhyānyadbhutāni sūcitāni// avalokitā - alaṃ tāvadetenālaṃ tāvadanupayuktenāghoraghaṇṭavṛttāntakathanenetyarthaḥ/ bhagavati, so'pi pārśvacaro mādhavasya bālamitraṃ makarando nandanasya bhaginīṃ madayantikāṃ yadi samudvahati tadapi mādhavasya dvitīyaṃ priyaṃ bhavati/ anenopanāyakasya makarandasya garbhasaṃdhau patākāvṛttāntaḥ sūcito bhavati// kāmandakī/ niyukteti/ tadapi mayā pūrvamevānusaṃhitamityarthaḥ/ avalokitā - suvihitaṃ bhagavatyā// kāmandakī/ taditi/ mādhavapravṛttimupalabhya kiṃ madanodyāne tayoranyonyadarśanamabhūnna veti vārtāṃ jñātvetyarthaḥ/ mālatīmeva paśyāvaḥ/ kulakumārījanānucite hyasmadupadiṣṭe svayamātmapradānasāhase yathāsau nāśaṅkate tathā naivātiparicayena vaśīkartavyeti bhāvaḥ// kāmandakī/ vicintyeti/ kīdṛśaṃ dūtyamācaraṇīyamiti vimṛśya/ niścinotīti śeṣaḥ/ atyudārātigambhīrā prakṛtiryasyāḥ sā/ tathā gāmbhīryaṃ ca - ``yasya prabhāvādākārā harṣakrodhabhayādiṣu/ bhāveṣu nopalakṣyante tadgāmbhīryaṃ prakīrtitam//'' ityuktarūpam/ tena mālatyatigambhīraprakṛtitvāccaramāvasthāyāmapi svābhiprāyaṃ svayaṃ na nivedayatīti hetornipuṇaṃ yathā syāttathā nisṛṣṭārthā yā dūtī tasyāḥ kalpo yā vyāpārapaddhatistantrayitavyaḥ pradhānīkartavyaḥ/ prādhānyenāśrayaṇīya ityarthaḥ/ nisṛṣṭo nikṣiptaḥ ``tvameva vetsi sarvaṃ kṛtyam'' iti samarpito'rthaḥ kāryabhāro yasyāmiti vyutpattyā svatantrakāryanirvāhikā dūtī kathyate/ taduktam - rūḍhānurāgayoryūnorabhilāṣaṃ manogatam/ jñātvā tābhyāmanādiṣṭā tadapekṣitasiddhaye// yā pravarteta sā proktā nisṛṣṭārtheti kovidaiḥ/ nisṛṣṭaḥ kāryabhāro'syāmiti vyutpattisaṃbhavāt//'' iti/ avācyakauṭilyo yatkṛtāḥ saṃdhyādayaḥ pramāṇaṃ sa nisṛṣṭārtho dūtaḥ/ yathā bhagavānpāṇḍavānāmiti// idānīṃ svavyāpārasāphalyamāśāste - sarvatheti/ śarajjyotsneti/ kalyāṇī manoharākārānuguṇaśīlasaṃpannā sujātaṃ sujanmānaṃ kāntaṃ svaprāṇanāyakaṃ śāradacandrikākumudamiva samānurāgasaṃghaṭane dṛṣṭāntaḥ/ nandayatu niratiśayānandasamṛddhaṃ karotu/ tena sa ca yuvā kṛtapuṇyaścaritārtho bhavatu/ ubhayoḥ samānurāgatvena sa ca tāṃ nandayatu/ sā ca kṛtakṛtyā bhavatvityayamartho'rthāllabhyate/ taṃ cakāro dyotayati/ evaṃ ca sati garīyāngurutaro'nyonyasya parasparasya praguṇā anuguṇā ye guṇāsteṣāṃ nirmāṇe nipuṇo vidhāturvyāpāraḥ phalatu tatsaṃghaṭanalakṣaṇenaiva phalena phalavānbhavatu/ manojñaḥ sarvajanaślāghyo bhavatu/ tādṛganyonyānurūpanirmāṇasya tayoḥ saṃghaṭanameva phalam/ anyathā vyarthameva syāt/ yadāha kālidāsaḥ - ``paraspareṇa spṛhaṇīyaśobham'' ityādiślokena/ atropamopastutyamanyonyamalaṃkāraḥ/ atra parasparānurāgasya bījasyānurūpeṇa stutervilobhanaṃ nāmāṅgam/ yathā - ``tadguṇākhyā vilobhanam''iti//19//

 miśraviṣkambho'yam/ nīcamadhyamapātraprayojitatvāditi// ``mādhavānucaraḥ kalahaṃso nāma'' iti vākye sūcitamupadarśayaŸŸmārabhate - tata ityādi/ aṅkalakṣaṇaṃ yathā - ``dvedhā vastuvibhāgaḥ syātprakāśyaḥ sūcya ityapi/ prakāśyo madhurodāttarasabhāvanirantaraḥ// aṅkaiḥ prakāśayedenamaṅkalakṣma pravakṣyate/ tatpratyakṣanetṛcarito bījabindupuraskṛtaḥ// prasaktārthopasaṃhārī patākāsthānakānvitaḥ/ aṅko nānāprakārārthasaṃvidhānarasāśrayaḥ// ekāhe vaikarātre vā caritaṃ tatra varṇayet/ prayuktaḥ pañcaṣaiḥ pātraisteṣāmante ca nirgamaḥ//'' iti/ gṛhītaṃ citraphalakamevopakaraṇamupāyanatvena mādhavasaṃdarśanasādhanaṃ yena saḥ/ kvedānīṃ tulitamakaradhvajāvaleparūpavibhramākṣiptamālatīhṛdayamāhātmyaṃ śithilīkṛtamanmathagarvo yo rūpavibhramaḥ saundaryasaṃpadvilāsastenākṣiptaṃ nirastaṃ mālatīhṛdayasya māhātmyaṃ gāmbhīryaṃ yena tam/ mālatīlikhitamādhavapraticchandakadarśanena mālatī manmathena mādhavaṃ lakṣyīkṛtya sudūramākṛṣṭā calitadhairyā jātetyanena jñāteti mantavyam/ nāthaṃ mādhavaṃ paśyāmi/ svāmino mādhavasyātmani mālatyanurāgaṃ saṃbhāvayatastatpratyayahetutvenātyantopādeyacitraphalakānayanānmahatsvāmikāryamanuṣṭhitamiti kvedānīmityautsukyagarbhā sasaṃbhramoktiḥ/ parikramyetyādi/ pariśrānto'smi/ yāvadihodyāne vane muhūrtaṃ viśramya makarandasahacaraṃ nāthaṃ mādhavaṃ prekṣiṣye/ ``makarandanandanam'' iti pāṭhe prāṇamitratayā tasyānandajanakamityarthaḥ/ kliṣṭatvātsutamiti vyākhyānamupekṣitam/ anenaiva makarandapraveśaḥ sūcitaḥ// tadāha - tata ityādi// madanodyānaṃ kāmadevāyatanopalakṣitamudyānamityanena śṛṅgāroddīpanavibhāvatvamuktam/ diṣṭyeti tṛtīyāntapratirūpakamavyayaṃ bhāgyavāci/ abhivartate/ abhimukhamāgacchatītyarthaḥ/ anyamanaskatayā na māṃ paśyatīti bhāvaḥ/ nirūpya nirvarṇya/ vadatīti śeṣaḥ/ asya tviti ślokopaskāro gamanamityādinānvīyate/ gamanamiti/ gamanamalasaṃ lakṣyābhāvādanicchāpūrvakameva kathaṃcidavalambitam/ anena vipralambhasyālasyākhyo vyabhicārī sūcitaḥ/ yathā - ``kriyā vidveṣa[kriyāvidveṣa] ālasyam'' iti/ tathā dṛṣṭiḥ śūnyā cintāpahṛtena manasyadhiṣṭhitā/ tata eva svaviṣayaparicchedarūpaphalaśūnyatvena śūnyā ca/ śūnyā yathā - ``tārāsamapuṭā snigdhā niṣkampā śūnyadarśanā/ bāhyārthagrāhiṇī śyāmā śūnyadṛṣṭistu cintanāt//'' iti/ anena cintākhyo vyabhicārī vyajyate/ ``prayatnapūrvikārtheṣu smṛtiścinteti kathyate'' iti/ tathā śarīramasauṣṭhavaṃ balahīnaṃ svavyāpārāsamarthakaracaraṇādikamityarthaḥ/ anena glānyākhyo vyabhicārī vyajyate/ yathā - ``vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmāṇi glānirgamayatīti yat'' iti/ śvasitamadhikaṃ pūrvāpekṣayā prabalam/ anena śramaviṣādamohādayo vyajyante niḥśvāsasyānubhāvasyānekabhāvasādhāraṇatvāt/ evaṃ cānusmṛtirūpā tṛtīyāvasthā darśitā/ yathā - ``muhurmuhurniḥśvasiti manorathavicintanaiḥ/ pradveṣādanyakāryāṇāmanusmṛtirudāhṛtā//'' iti/ kiṃ nvetatsyāt/ eṣāṃ cintālasyādīnāṃ nimittaṃ kiṃ nu bhavediti makarandasya vitarkaḥ/ vimṛśya tannimittaṃ nirdhārayati - kimanyaditi/ athavā kimatra vitarkeṇa madanavikāravyatiriktaṃ kimeteṣāṃ nimittaṃ saṃbhāvyate/ na kiṃcidapītyarthaḥ/ tadeva nimittamāha - bhramatīti/ bhuvane kaṃdarpājñābhramati/ apratihatā vijṛmbhate/ yauvanaṃ ca prathamaṃ dvitīyaṃ vā/ itarayoratrāyogyatvāt/ vikāri vikārajananaśīlam/ yauvanaṃ yathā - ``rativyāyāmasahano mattebhasyeva mattatā/ vidyate yuvabhāvo yastadyauvanamudāhṛtam//'' iti/ ``yauvanaṃ tu caturdhāsyāt'' ityādinā bhāvaprakāśe yauvanaviśeṣalakṣaṇaṃ prapañcitam/ tata eva jñātavyam/ lalitāḥ sundarā nayanasukhakarā madhurā manoharāśca te te bhāvā indūdyānādaya uddīpanavibhāvāḥ svabhāvadhīrasyāpi dhīratāṃ dhairyaṃ kṣipantyapasārayanti/ dhairyaṃ yathā - ``cāpalenānupahatā sarvārtheṣvavikatthanā/ svābhāvikī cittavṛttirdhairyamityabhidhīyate//'' iti/ nūnamadya madanodyāne madanotsavadarśanāyāgato'sau kasyāṃcidāsaktacitto madanāvasthayaivābhibhūto vartata ityarthaḥ/ sasaṃdeho niścayāntaḥ samuccayālaṃkāraḥ//20//

 diṣṭetyādinā mādhavapraveśaḥ sūcitaḥ/ tadāha - tata ityādinā/ yathānirdiṣṭaḥ gamanamityādinā nirūpitāvasthaḥ// tāmityādi/ me cetaḥ kartṛ/ induvanmanojñamukhīṃ tāṃ pūrvakṣaṇe nayanātithīkṛtāṃ suciraṃ bahukāla[laṃ] vibhāvya ajakāgryeṇa viṣayāntaraparityāgena bhāvanāviṣayaṃ kṛtvā/ dhyātvetyarthaḥ/ kathaṃkathamapi kenakenāpi prakāreṇa kadācidapyananubhūtacareṇa/ īdṛgasāviti nirūpayitumaśakyenāvasthābhedenetyarthaḥ/ vyapavartate vaikṛtādanyathaiva vartate/ vikāramupayātītyarthaḥ/ kiṃ kṛtvetyapekṣāyāṃ tāmevāpathavṛttimāha - akāṇḍe'navasare/ prathamadarśanasamaya eva/ lajjām/ kathamāryavigarhite paropahāsanīye karmaṇi pravṛtto'hamiti manaḥsaṃkocanaṃ lajjā/ yathā - ``manaḥsaṃkocanaṃ lajjā hyanaucityapravartanāt'' iti/ tāṃ vijitya nirasya vinayaṃ vinītatāṃ vinivārya/ dhairyaṃ dhīratāmunmathya mathitvā mantharo mandībhūto vivekaḥ kṛtyākṛtyajñānaṃ yathā syāttatheti// atra lajjātyāgo vivekamāndyena unmādaśceti saptamāṣṭamāvasthādvayamādāveva rāgotkarṣapradarśanārthamuktamiti draṣṭavyam/ mantharavivekamiti cetoviśeṣaṇaṃ vā//21//

 āścaryam/ yadvismayeti/ yanmadīyaṃ manastasyāḥ saṃnidhau vismayenāmānuṣalokocitāpūrvalāvaṇyadarśanajanitena cittavikāsenādbhutarasasthāyinā rasatāmāpannena stimitaṃ niṣpandamastamitānyabhāvaṃ vigalitavedyāntaramamṛtaplavanādiva sudhārṇavanimajjanenevānandena niratiśayasukhāvirbhāvena mandaṃ tadāsvādaikaparatayā niruddhavṛttyantaramabhūt/ tadevedānīṃ tadasaṃnidhāvaṅgāracumbitaṃ pradīptendhanaśakalasaṃspṛṣṭamiva/ dagdhamiti vaktavye manogatapreyasīdāhabhiyā cumbitamiti komaloktiḥ/ vyathamānaṃ saṃtapyamānamāste/ tatsaṃnidhānāsaṃnidhānayoḥ kṣaṇamātrabhedenātīvamanovasthāvailakṣaṇyamidamāścaryamityarthaḥ//22//

 makarando'nyamanaskatayātmānamapaśyantamapi svayameva prajñāpayannākārayati - makarandaḥ/ sakhe mādhaveti/ ita itaḥ/ āgamyatāmiti śeṣaḥ/ lalāṭetyādi/ lalāṭaṃtapo nabhomadhyavartītyarthaḥ/ ``asūryalalāṭayordṛśitapoḥ'' iti khac/ ``khityanavyayasya'' iti mumāgamaḥ// kalahaṃsaḥ - kathaṃ makarandasahacara idameva bālodyānamalaṃkaroti mādhavaḥ/ taddarśayāmyasya madanavedanāsvidyamānamālatīlocanasukhāvaham/ citrasya darśanamātraphalakatvāt/ ātmanaḥ praticchandakam/ athavā viśrāmasaukhyaṃ tāvadanubhavatu/ paścāddarśayiṣyāmīti śeṣaḥ// makarandaḥ - taditi/ vikasitakusumakiñjalkānāṃ kaṣāyaśītalo ya āmodo dūravisṛmaro gandhaḥ/ kaṣāyatvaśiśiratvayo rasadharmatve'pi tādṛśarasenaikārthasamavāyādāmodadharmatvopacāraḥ/ tena surabhīkṛtodyānasya kāñcanapādapasya cāmpeyatarośchāyāyāmupaviśāvaḥ// sakaletyādi/ anyādṛśaṃ vilakṣaṇāvastham/ apiḥ praśne// vihasyeti/ svamanogatajñānādavanatānane mādhave satyaṃ madanavikāra eva madunnīto heturetadvailakṣaṇasyeti svamatisaṃvādādvihasya svānukūlyapradarśanena lajjāṃ śithilayitumāheti śeṣaḥ/ kimiti/ mugdhaṃ sundaram/ tvadantaḥkaraṇanirviśeṣe mayi kimāśaṅkayā lajjayā vā/ tāvadvisrabdhaṃ kathayetyarthaḥ/ kiṃ ca na khalu tvadupajña evāyaṃ smarābhiṣaṅgaḥ surāsuranarasādhāraṇatvāt/ tenānanyakṛtapūrvātyantānucitavannaitadapahnavo nyāyya ityāha - anyeṣviti/ yo viśvasya dhātari jagatsraṣṭari surajyeṣṭhe svaduhitṛkāmuke/ parameśvare smaraśāsane'pi vāmadehārdhaniveśitapreyasīke tamasāvṛteṣvajñānopahatavivekeṣvanyeṣu jantuṣu/ prāṇimātra ityarthaḥ/ samo'vaiṣamyeṇākuṇṭhitavyāpāraḥ/ sa tādṛśo'yaṃ tvanmanogataścittajanmā prasiddhavibhavaḥ prathitaprabhāvaḥ khalu brahmādīnapi svavaśīkaroti/ kā kathānyeṣviti bhāvaḥ/ tato lajjayā tava manogatasya vikārahetorapahnutirapahnavaḥ/ citte niyamanamiti yāvat/ mā bhūt/ lajjāṃ visṛjya kathayetyarthaḥ//23//

 mādhavaḥ - vayasya, kiṃ na kathayāmi/ sarvavisrambhāspadasya tavāpyapahnavaḥ kimityāśayaḥ/ yadāhuḥ - ``samānurāgavyasano rahasyākhyaikabhājanam/ vipatpratikriyādakṣo vayasyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ//'' iti/ avalokitayā janitaṃ kautukaṃ ``adya khalu mahānutsavo madanodyāne nāgarikāṇām, tattvayāpi taddarśanārthaṃ gantavyam'' ityevamutpādito gamanotsāho yasya so'haṃ kāmadevāyatanamutsavasthānaṃ gato'bhūvam/ tatra cetastataḥ parikramya ramyavastuvilokanalobhātpariśramātparikramaṇasaṃjātādullasito visarpanyo madhuro manoharo madirāmodasamāno yaḥ parimalaḥ/ kesarakusumasyeva jātiprayuktaḥ/ tenākṛṣṭaṃ yatsakalānāṃ milatāmalīnāṃ paṭalaṃ tena saṃkulā vyāptākulitā ca yā mukulaśreṇiḥ saiva manoharamābharaṇaṃ yasya saḥ/ tathā ca ramaṇīye'ṅgaṇe kāmadevāyatanapurobhāge bhavati tathā/ tena samyagutsavāvalokanayogyadeśatvamuktam/ bālabakulasya/ bālatvādupaviśya karāpacayakusumasyetyarthaḥ/ ālavālaparisare pratidinajalasekaśītale sthitaḥ/ nirantaranipatitāni sāndranipatitāni/ upaveśanasthala eva yāvadapekṣaṃ labhyāni vikasitāni tasya kusumāni yadṛcchayā svecchayādāya vidagdhā manojñā yā racanā grathanā tayā manoharāṃ srajamabhinirmātumārabdhavān/ anantaramiti/ saṃcāriṇī saṃcaraṇaśīlā jagadvijayavaijayantikā tribhuvanavijayalāñchanabhūtā puraḥsarī patākā/ tāmevānu manmatho jagajjigīṣayā nirgataḥ/ tasya cāhamādiśaravyatāmupagato'smītyāśayaḥ/ ujjvalaṃ prakāśamānaṃ vidagdhamagrāmyaṃ mugdhaṃ sundaraṃ bālocitanepathyaṃ prasādhanaṃ tasya yā viracanā tayā vibhāvitaḥ kumārībhāvaḥ kanyātvaṃ yasyāḥ/ prathamayauvane vartamānatvāt/ vayasā punarna jñāyate kumārīyamityāśayaḥ/ mahānubhāvaprakṛtiratigambhīrasvabhāvā/ atyudāro'tidakṣiṇaḥ/ ``udāro dātṛmahatordakṣiṇe cābhidhīyate'' iti darśanāt/ parijano yasyāḥ sā/ tata eva yatrāhaṃ sthitastatraiveti saptamyarthe tasiḥ/ sārvavibhaktikatvāt/ atha tatprathamato locanapathagocaraṃ tasyāḥ svarūpamidānīmapi pratyakṣamiva vibhāvyamānaṃ nirvarṇayati - seti/ ramaṇīyasya bhāvo rāmaṇīyakam/ ``yopadhādgurūpottamāt'' iti vuñ/ ābhirūpyamiti yāvat/ tasya nidhirnikṣepaḥ śaṅkhapadmādivat/ tasyādhiṣṭātṛdevatā vā/ saundaryasya sarvāvayavasauṣṭhavasya niketanaṃ mandiraṃ vā/ sakhe, tasyā nirmāṇe kāraṇamupādānamindvādi/ na tu bhautikam/ madanaśca vedhāḥ/ na tu sādhāraṇo vedābhyāsajaḍaḥ/ yadāha - ``asyāḥ sargavidhau prajāpatirabhūccandro nu kāntiprabhaḥ'' ityādi/ anena nāyikāguṇavarṇanena tadviṣayo mahānurāgo'pi śreyāniti bījaguṇapraśaṃsanādvilobhanaṃ nāma mukhasaṃdheraṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``tadguṇākhyā vilobhanam'' iti/ guṇasvarūpanirūpaṇādguṇastutirūpā caturthyavasthā mādhavasya pradarśitā/ yathā - ``rūpaudāryaguṇairlīlāceṣṭāhasitavīkṣitaiḥ/ darśanālāpamādhuryairnāstyatastatsamā vadhūḥ//'' iti/ ``yatredṛśī śivā vāṇī bhavetsaiva guṇastutiḥ'' iti/ vitarkaśca vyabhicārī vyajyate/ yathā - ``ūho vitarka ityuktaḥ parārtheṣu yathāmati'' iti/ saṃśayāpahnavayoḥ saṃsṛṣṭiḥ//24//

 atheti/ praṇayinībhiḥ premaviṣayābhiranucarībhiḥ parisaravartinībhiḥ/ kusumasaṃcayasyāvacaya eva līlā tasyāmabhilāṣavatībhirabhyarthamānā svayameva gantukāmāpi śālīnatayā kuṇṭhitecchā satī tadapanodanāya manovinodinībhiḥ prārthyamānā/ tameva yatrāhaṃ sthitastasyāśca kasmiṃścidapi lokottare/ mahābhāgadheyādaśvamedhādyapi tatkarmakalāpaṃ yadutpādane varākaprāyaṃ tādṛśasucaritaviśeṣājjanma yasya tasmin/ anyādṛśasya tadabhilāṣapātratālakṣaṇatribhuvanasāmrājyasiṃhāsanādhirohaṇasaubhāgyānarhatvāt/ bahudivasopacīyamānamaticirakālātpravardhamānaṃ manmathājjātā yā vyathā tajjanyaṃ vikāramupalakṣitavānasmi/ unnayane liṅgānyāha - yataḥ/ parimṛditeti/ parimṛditāpacayena mlāyamānā mṛṇālī visatanturiva/ ``jāterastrīviṣayādayopadhāt'' iti ṅīp[ṣ]/ mlānaṃ glānimupagatam/ kārśyaṃ nāma madanadaśā sūcitā/ kriyāsu snānapānādikāsu parivāraprārthanābhiḥ parijanasya cāṭuvākyapādapraṇāmādibhiranunayaiḥ kathamapi kṛcchreṇa pravṛttiḥ/ na tu svārasyena/ kriyādveṣarūpāratirnāma manmathāvasthā darśitā/ vyādhivaśādapyetatsaṃbhavatītyanyadasādhāraṇaṃ liṅgamāha - kalayatīti/ abhinaveti/ sadyaḥkṛtadviradaradanacchedakamanīyaḥ kapolaśca niṣkalaṅkasya himāṃśorlakṣmīṃ śobhāṃ kalayati ca/ pāṇḍimno nimittāntarātsaṃbhave'pi niṣkalaṅkendubimbasādṛśyamanyatra na saṃbhavatīti vipralambhaikānubhāvatvam/ atra yadyapi śarīraglānyādayo'nubhāvāḥ kevalamupanibaddhāstathāpyetadanubhāvaniyatā nirvedaglānijaḍatāviṣādautsukyacintāmohāvegaprabhṛtayo vyabhicāriṇo mahābhāgadheyasya janmanīti nirdiṣṭālambanavibhāvo madanodyānādayaścoddīpanavibhāvāḥ pratīyanta iti vibhāvānubhāvavyabhicāripoṣitaratyākhyasthāyībhāvaparipūrṇo'bhilāṣavipralambhaśṛṅgāraraso dhvanyate//25//

 sā mametyādi/ amṛtamayatūlikeva darśanenaiva cakṣuṣorniratiśayaṃ nirākṛto'tiśayaḥ prakarṣo'nyeṣāmānandānāṃ yena tamānandamutpādayantī/ mama punarantaḥkaraṇamayaskāntamaṇiśalākā lohakarṣakamaṇiśalākā svasaṃnidhānamātreṇa lohadhātumayo vikāramivopasaṃhṛtavatī svasamīpaṃ pratyākṛṣṭavatī/ kiṃ bahuneti/ tadānīmutpannena, ānantyādanubhavaikapramāṇatvācca viśeṣato vaktumaśakyena, kathaṃcinnirūpyamāṇena manovikāraviśeṣeṇa kiṃ prayojanam/ idaṃ tāvanniṣpannamityāha - saṃtāpeti/ etanmadīyaṃ ceto'napekṣito na vimṛṣṭo heturāsaktau nimittaṃ bāhyopādhirūpaṃ yena tādṛśaṃ sadāsaktam/ na cedaṃ śubhodarkam/ yenedānīṃ kathaṃcidviṣahyeta/ kiṃtvanarthaikaparyavasāyītyāha - saṃtāpasaṃtatiḥ saṃtanyamānaḥ saṃjvara eva mahadutkṛṣṭaṃ vyasanaṃ maraṇakāraṇaṃ duḥkhaṃ tadarthameva/ tatprāptirūpasya tāpanirvāpaṇauṣadhasyātidurlabhatvāt/ itaḥparaṃ maraṇameva śaraṇamiti bhāvaḥ/ tarhyevaṃ jānatā tvayā kimiti mano na nivāryata ityāśaṅkya purātanakarmavāsanāvaśaṃvadānāmasmākaṃ na kutrāpi svātantryamityāha - prāya iti/ bhagavatī māhātmyavatī/ īśvarasyāpi tatsāpekṣatvāt/ uktaṃ hi - ``jīvādṛṣṭasāpekṣo hi bhagavāñjaganti nirmimīte'' iti/ sarvaṃkaṣā sarvakāryanimittatayā sarvārthavyāpinī/ na hi tayā vinā kiṃcinniḥśvāsādikamapi kāryaṃ bhavituṃ prabhavati/ tādṛśī bhavitavyatā prāktanādṛṣṭaparipaktireva jantoḥ prāṇimātrasya śubhamaśubhaṃ ca vidadhāti/ yadvadiṣṭasaṃpādane na vayaṃ svatantrāstadvadaniṣṭapratīkāre'pi/ karmavaśādyadupanata[taṃ] tattadavaśyamanubhoktavyamityarthaḥ/ arthāntaranyāso'laṃkāraḥ//26//


2praˇˇ makarandaḥ - snehaśceti/ nimittasavyapekṣo bāhyopādhisāpekṣaḥ/ snehatvamaupādhikatvaṃ cetyetaddharmadvayamekatra dharmiṇi viruddhamityarthaḥ/ yattu bhāvaprakāśe snehadvaividhyamuktam, yathā - ``manaso yaddayārdratvaṃ viṣayeṣu ca tattvataḥ/ bhayaśaṅkāvasānātmā sa eṣa sneha ucyate// dvidhā dravaḥ syānmanasaḥ sparśanāddarśanādapi/ jatuvadvahnisaṃsparśāddarśanāccandrakāntavat// ārdratā śiśiratvaṃ yatsarvāvasthāsu mānasam/ yayoḥ parasparasyāste tadapi sneha īritaḥ// dvidhā bhavetsa ca snehaḥ kṛtrimākṛtrimātmakaḥ/ sopādhiḥ kṛtrimaḥ sneho nirupādhirakṛtrimaḥ// upādhau vinivṛtte tu tajjanyo'pi nivartate/ snehasvabhāvajo yāvaddravyabhāvī bhaviṣyati//'' ityādinā, tatsopādhike snehe snehatvopacārātsnehaśabdaprayoga iti mantavyam/ svābhāvikasnehasya bāhyopādhinairapekṣyamevopapādayati/ paśyetyādi/ dṛṣṭāntamukhenemamarthamavadhārayetyarthaḥ/ vyatiṣajatīti/ padārthānbhāvānacetanāṃścetanāṃśca/ āntaro gūḍhaḥ kāryaikasamadhigamyaḥ ko'pīdataṃyā nirūpayitumaśakyo heturvyatiṣajati parasparaṃ saṃghaṭayati/ punaḥ prītayaḥ snehā bahirupādhīnbāhyāni nimittāni na saṃśrayante/ dṛṣṭāntamāha - pataṅgasya raverudaye puṇḍarīkaṃ vikasati/ himaraśmau candre codgata udite candrakāntamaṇirdravati/ na cātra paridṛśyate bāhyopādhiḥ kaścidṛte svabhāvasauhārdāt/ tadvatkasyacitkutracidavalokanamātreṇāntaḥkaraṇaṃ vikasati dravībhavatīti svābhāvikasnehasya mahimāyamityarthaḥ/ dṛṣṭāntālaṃkāraḥ//27//

 tatastata iti/ anantaravṛttāntaṃ brūhīti śeṣaḥ// mādhavaḥ - tataśceti/ tadanantaraṃ tatra tasminnevāvasare/ sabhrūvilāseti/ tasyāścatureṇa vidagdhena sakhījanena māmavalokya/ atha madavalokanānantaraṃ smitasudhāmadhurā darahasitāmṛtasāhacaryamanoharāḥ kaṭākṣā ākūtadyotakā dṛṣṭiviśeṣā anyonyaṃ muktāḥ prahitāḥ/ māṃ dṛṣṭvā smitapūrvakaṃ parasparāvalokanaṃ kṛtavatya ityarthaḥ/ atha māmavalokyetyavalokanakriyāṃ viśinaṣṭi - sapratyabhijñamiveti/ pratyabhijñā nāma pūrvānubhūtasya purovartyaikyāvagāhinī dhīḥ/ sā ca parabuddhatvānna mayā pratyakṣīkṛtā, kiṃtūtprekṣiteti ivaśabdena dyotayati/ kathaṃ tvayaitadutprekṣitamityāśaṅkyabījamāha - so'yamitīva nāmeti/ nāma prasiddhau/ yo mālatījīvitavallabho mādhavo nāma prasiddhaḥ sakhījanakathāsu sa evāyamitīva tattedantāṃśāvacchedaḥ pratyabhijñotprekṣābījam/ tasyāpi tadidaṃśabdābhilāparūpasya tadānīmabhāvāt/ jñānākārasya apratyakṣatvāt so'pyutprekṣita evetīvaśabdena sūcayati/ tadapi kathamunnītamityāśaṅkya sphuṭaṃ talliṅgamāha - sabhrūvilāsamiti/ bhruvorvilāso vikṣepaḥ/ yo'smābhirbhartṛdārikācittacoro mādhavo nāma goṣṭhīṣu prasajyate sa evāyamityevaṃrūpārthadyotikā ceṣṭā bhrūlatonnamanacāturī tatsahitaṃ yathā bhavati tathā pūrvānubhūtatvapurovartitvalakṣaṇatattedantābhivyañjikā bhrūceṣṭā padārthābhinayarūpatvācceṣṭāyāḥ/ tena tayā tattedantāvacchedanenāsādhāraṇadharmeṇa madviṣayā tāsāṃ pratyabhijñābhyūhitetyarthaḥ/ bhāvikamalaṃkāraḥ//28//

 makarandaḥ - svagatam/ kathamiti/ nāmeti saṃbhāvanāyām/ ananubhūtapūrve kathaṃ tāsāṃ pratyabhijñāsaṃbhava iti vitarkaḥ/ idaṃ cātmagataṃ veditavyam/ anyathā mādhavenottarasya vaktavyatāpatteḥ/ mādhavaḥ - atheti/ anantaraṃ tasyāḥ purata eva matsamīpamāgatāstāḥ paricārikā māṃ dṛṣṭvā pratinivṛttya paścādāgacchantyai tasyai/ aṅgulya eva dalāni/ aṅgulīnāṃ dalatvarūpaṇaṃ karasya kamalatvaṃ dyotayati/ teṣāṃ vilāso'grāmyalalitakriyā tena māṃ kathitavatyaḥ/ kathaṃ pratinivṛttyetyapekṣāyāṃ trīṇi kriyāviśeṣaṇāni/ tatra salīlaṃ līlayā sahitaṃ yathā bhavati tathā/ līlā nāmāpāṅgavyāpāreṇābhīṣṭārthānukaraṇam/ yathā - ``manomadhuravāgaṅgaceṣṭitaiḥ prīticoditaiḥ/ priyānukaraṇaṃ līlā sā syātstrīpuṃsayorapi//'' uttālā unnatāḥ karakamalayostālikā ūrdhvaprasāritāṅgulīkakarataladvayaparasparāsphālanaṃ tena taralā valayāvalī kaṅkaṇaśreṇiryathā syāttathā/ uttrastā udvignā mattāśca ye kalahaṃsāsteṣāṃ vibhramo viśiṣṭabhramaṇaṃ manojñā gatistadvadabhirāmaṃ caraṇasaṃcaraṇaṃ tena raṇaraṇāyamānaṃ manojñaṃ yanmañjīraṃ nūpuraṃ tasya raṇitena śiñjitenānuviddhaḥ saṃbhinno yaḥ kāñcīdāmaniviṣṭakṣudraghaṇṭikāraṇaraṇatkārastena mukharaṃ yathā syāttatheti/ kimityākhyātavatya ityapekṣāyāṃ bhartṛdārike diṣṭyā bhāgyena vardhāmahe/ yasmādatraiva pradeśe ``yā latānviṣyate saiva lagnā saṃprati pādayoḥ'' iti rītyā purata eva ko'pyayam/ asāviti sākṣānnirdeṣṭumanucitatvāttatpracchādanāya sāmānyoktiḥ/ kasyā apītyatrāpi tavetyanaucityādgūḍhoktiḥ/ vallabho dayitastiṣṭatīti/ kecittu strīliṅgamapyayuktaṃ manyamānāḥ ``kasyāpi'' iti paṭhitvā janasyeti vyācakṣate// makarandaḥ/ hanteti harṣe/ mahato'tiprarūḍhasya pūrvānurāgasyodbhedaḥ prakāśaḥ/ pratibhātīti śeṣaḥ/ udbhedo nāma mukhasaṃdhyaṅgam/ yathā - ``udbhedo gūḍhabhedanam'' iti// kalahaṃsaḥ/ anayoḥ sarasaramaṇīyānubandhinyavicchinnā strīkathā/ vartata iti śeṣaḥ// makarandaḥ - tatastata iti// mādhavaḥ - atrāntara iti/ asminnavasara āyatākṣyāstasyā mānmathamācāryakam/ manmatha eva hi kumārījanasyātyantāparicitatādātmiko dīyamānavividhavilāsaśikṣāyāmācāryaḥ/ tasyācāryakamācāryabhāvaḥ/ ``yopadhādgurūpottamādvuñ''/ vividhaśṛṅgāraceṣṭāśikṣāprāvīṇyamāvirāsītprādurabhūt/ kīdṛśam/ vāgvibhavātivṛttavaicitryamuktiprakārasarvasvollaṅghivicitrabhāvam/ ata eva kimapi viśeṣato nirdeṣṭumaśakyam/ ullasito vibhramo yatra/ vibhramaḥ śṛṅgāraceṣṭāviśeṣaḥ/ yathā - ``vāgaṅgasattvābhinayabhūṣāsthānaviparyayaḥ/ tvarayā kalpito'bhīṣṭadarśane yaḥ sa vibhramaḥ//'' iti/ tanmanasaiva kevalamanusaṃdhīyamānaṃ bhūrisāttvikavikāraṃ pracuratarastambhādisāttvikabhāvam/ sāttvikabhāvādiryaduktam - ``tadbhāvabhāvanaṃ yena bhavettadanukūlataḥ/ tatsattvaṃ tena nirvṛttāḥ sāttvikā ityudīritāḥ//'' yathā - ``stambhaḥ svedo'tha romāñcaḥ svarabhedaśca vepathuḥ/ vaivarṇyamaśrupralaya ityaṣṭau sāttvikā matāḥ//'' iti/ apāstadhairyamanyonyadarśanādubhayorapyapākṛtacittasthairyam/ vijayi jayaśīlam/ kutrāpyakuṇṭhitaprasaramiti/ atra paripūrṇaśṛṅgārānubhavanibandhanāttaducitamohāvegādivyabhicāripratīterālambanoddīpanavibhāvasaṃnidhānapūrṇaḥ śṛṅgārarasadhvaniḥ/ parikaraścālaṃkāraḥ//29//

 ataśca cirakālapravṛttasya garīyasaḥ pūrvānurāgasya śabdavikalpasmaraṇasaṃtānopacitasya jīvitasarvasvāyamānasya tasyaiva svālambanavibhāvasya sāṃmukhyādanyonyakaṭākṣamiśrībhāvādutpūrasya sudhākarakarasparśādivakṣīrasāgarasyāpūritāntaratayā paripūrṇakumbhoccalananyāyena muhurbahiruccalato niratiśayaparipoṣātmikāṃ rasatāmāpannasya taraṅgacchaṭā iva prathamānubhāvabhūtāḥ śṛṅgāradṛṣṭayo madālambanāḥ prādurāsannityāha - tataśca/ stimiteti/ ahamālokitānāṃ śṛṅgāradṛṣṭiviśeṣāṇāṃ vividhaṃ pātramabhūvam/ ``prasādalalitā kāntā śālinī manmathāśayā/ vicaladbhūkaṭākṣā ca śṛṅgāre dṛṣṭiriṣyate//'' iti/ tadviśeṣaṃ darśayituṃ viśinaṣṭi - stimitānāmunmīlanānantarameva purovartini mayi nipatya darśanalobhena tatraiva viṣaye niścalānām/ stimitaṃ nāma śṛṅgāradṛṣṭivikāraḥ/ yathā - ``svagocarānna cālyeta yattatstimitamucyate'' iti/ tato vikasitānāṃ viṣayagatāśeṣaviṣayagrāhiṇām/ vikasitadṛṣṭivikāro yathā - ``vikāsitaṃ yadviṣaye viśeṣamavagāhate'' iti/ ato viṣayāsvādajanmānandabhāvanayollasantī kiṃciducchvasantī caturākhyabhrūkarmasahitā bhrūlatā yeṣām/ caturaṃ nāma bhrūkarma/ yathā - ``caturaṃ kiṃciducchvāsānmadhurā racanā bhruvoḥ'' iti/ prasannākhyaścānena dṛgvikāra uktaḥ/ yathā - ``prasannaṃ tadbhavetsabhrūvilāsaṃ sasmitaṃ ca yat'' iti/ ato masṛṇānāmanurāgapeśalānām/ masṛṇadṛgvikāro yathā - ``masṛṇaṃ tattu vijñeyamanurāgakaṣāyitam'' iti/ tataḥ sukhapāravaśyānmukulitānāmanunmīlitānām/ mukulitā dṛṣṭiranenoktā/ mukulitākhyaśca dṛgvikāro yathā - ``sphuritāśliṣṭapakṣmāgrā mukulordhvapuṭocchritā/ sukhonmīlitatārā ca mukulā dṛṣṭiriṣyate// dṛṣṭirmukulitā svapnasukhanidrāsu vartate//'' iti anena vikūṇitākhyadṛgvikāra uktaḥ/ vikūṇitaṃ yathā - ``bhāgatrayasya saṃkocovikāsastvaparasya ca/ yasyā dṛṣṭervilakṣeṇa tadvikūṇitamucyate//'' iti/ tataḥ punardarśanaspṛhayā prānte'pāṅgadeśe vistārabhājāṃ visṛtvarāṇām/ samyagāśliṣṭaviṣayāṇāmiti yāvat/ apāṅgadṛṣṭirhi tatraiva nipatantī samyagviṣayaṃ paricchinatti/ anena vistārī saspṛhaśceti dṛgvikāradvayamuktam/ yathā - ``yenāśliṣṭo hi viṣayastadvistārīti kathyate/ bhūyobhūyaḥ spṛhā yatra dṛṣṭestatsaspṛhaṃ bhavet//'' iti/ tataḥ pratinayanasya tadavalokanāya prahitasya mannetrasya nipāta ābhimukhyena yugapadupasthāne lajjayā kiṃcidākuñcitānāmīṣatsaṃkucitānām/ saṃkucitaṃ nāma dṛgvikāro yathā - ``apāṅgabhāgasaṃkoco yatra tatkuñcitaṃ bhavet'' iti/ bahuvacanena dhārāvāhinyāyena pravṛttipratīterlolākhyo dṛṣṭivikāra uktaḥ/ yathā - ``dhārāvāhikasaṃcāro yasya tallolamucyate'' iti/ vividhapadenānyānapi madhurasābhilāṣasotkaṇṭhitasotsukasamanmathapraṇayipremagarbhavikocalalitakomalamukhāñśṛṅgāradṛṣṭivikāraviśeṣānsaṃgṛhṇāti/ tallakṣaṇāni vistarabhayānna likhitāni/ parikara evālaṃkāraḥ//30//

 idānīṃ tādṛgavalokanapātramabhūvam/ tasmātpunastādātmikīmavasthāmāha - tataśca/ alaseti/ pakṣmale atisāndrapakṣmamāle akṣiṇī yasyāstasyāḥ kaṭākṣaiḥ ``apāṅge tāravikṣepaḥ kaṭākṣa iti kathyate'' ityuktalakṣaṇairdṛṣṭiviśeṣaiḥ pūrvoktairmehṛdayamaśaraṇaṃ sattadā mama pāravaśyenākiṃcitkaratvādanyena ca rakṣaṇasyāśakyatvāttadabhāvācca rakṣitāramalabhamānaṃ sat/ apahṛtaṃ balādācchidya gṛhītam/ kaṭākṣāṇāṃ manoharatvāttadā manaḥśūnya ivābhavam/ pramuṣitamatitvāttatastairapahṛtya tadapaviddhamatitīkṣṇatvātteṣāṃ nārācairiva vikṣatam/ niśitaśalākāgrakṛtarandhramiva vyathāmanvabhūdityarthaḥ/ tataśca saṃtaptamadananārācakalpatvātteṣāṃ tadapavedhena dravībhūya tatraiva dhārājalanyāyena vilīnatvāttaiḥ pītamiva/ yathā pītaṃ salilādikaṃ pāturantarvilīyate, evaṃ kaṭākṣeṣu manmano vilīnamityarthaḥ/ tataśca tairmadīyahṛdayālavāle līnasyāpi manasaḥ punaraṅkurodayaśaṅkayā tadunmūlitamutkhātamūlaṃ kṛtam/ apunarudbhavayogyaṃ kṛtamityarthaḥ/ manaḥpāravaśyātiśayoktiḥ/ kavinā prauḍhyutkarṣādevaṃ nirūpitam/ kecittu - vidagdho yathā kaścinnālikeratarostatphalamapahṛtyāyaḥśūlenāpavidhya sarandhraṃ kṛtvā tadantaḥsalitaṃ[laṃ] nipīya tadantaḥsāramunmūlya bhakṣayati, evaṃ kaṭākṣā api mādhavādeva tarormanaḥphalamapahṛtya gāmbhīryadhairyādinā dṛḍhatvātprathamamapavidhya sakṣataṃ kṛtvā tadantargataṃ lajjāvinayavivekādikaṃ kṣatavivaranirgataṃ nipīya tāvatāpyatṛptatvāccaitanyalakṣaṇaṃ mūlamunmūlyāgrasannityevaṃ nālikeraphalasamādhirukteti vyācakṣate/ kīdṛśaiḥ kaṭākṣairityata āha - alaseti/ alasāḥ prathamapātitāḥ/ purataḥ prasāritāḥ purovartini mayyeva nipātitāḥ/ paścādvibhāvya vrīḍayeva pratinivṛttāḥ/ alasādayaḥ śṛṅgāradṛṣṭivikārāḥ/ tatrālasaṃ yathā - ``alasaṃ tadabhīṣṭārthādvrīḍādyairyannivartate'' iti/ tato valitāḥ punardarśanotkaṇṭhayā tiryagudañcitāḥ/ valitaṃ yathā - ``valitaṃ tannivṛttasya bhūyastryaśrāvalokanam''/ ``tryaśraṃ tiryagudañcitam'' iti tryaśralakṣaṇam/ tato mugdhāḥ savyājabhāvagarbhasvabhāvāḥ/ mugdhaṃ yathā - ``svabhāvālokita[taṃ] mugdhaṃ bhāvagarbhamapi cchalāt'' iti/ tataḥ snigdhāḥ snehaparyāyaratibhāvānuviddhāḥ snigdhā dṛṣṭiryathā - ``snigdhaṃ yadratibhāvena snehaprāyeṇa saṃyutam'' iti/ tato niṣpandā viṣayādanyatra kvāpi na calantaḥ/ niṣpandaṃ yathā - ``niṣpandaṃ tadyadanyatra dṛṣṭānna spandate kvacit'' iti/ tato mandā viṣayāntaragamanānudyuktāḥ/ mantharadṛṣṭiranenoktā/ yathā - ``mantharā mandasaṃcārā'' iti/ adhikaṃ vikasannāścaryakārivastudarśanalakṣaṇāduddīpanādvistāraṃ rasabhāvonmukhatvamupanayan/ antaḥkaraṇagato vismayo'dbhutarasasthāyī/ tena smerāḥ sphuratpakṣmapaṅktibhirutphullatārā yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ/ smeraṃ yathā - ``prasphuratpakṣmatāraṃ yattatsmeramiti kathyate'' iti/ iyameva vismayarasavyañjikā vismitākhyā dṛṣṭiḥ/ yathā - ``vismayotphullatārā ca hṛṣṭobhayapuṭāñcitā/ samā vikasitā dṛṣṭirvismitā vismaye smṛtā//'' iti/ atra kaṭākṣairuktarūpairanubhāvaistatkāraṇasya kāṣṭhāṃ prāptasya mādhavaviṣayasya ratibhāvasya tatsamucitavyabhicāriṇāṃ pratīteḥ mādhavasyāpyapahṛtamityādinā mālatyanurāgagocaragāḍhānurāgatanniyatavyabhicāritattaducitānubhāvapratīteḥ, yugapadanyonyālambanaśṛṅgārarasadhvaniḥ/ ata eva bījasya parasparānurāgasya pariniṣpatteḥ parinyāso nāma mukhasaṃdhyaṅgamuktaṃ bhavati
la yathā - ``tanniṣpattiḥ parinyāsaḥ'' iti//31//

 ahaṃ tviti/ sarvairākārairavayavasaṃsthānadṛgvilāsāṅgavalanādibhirmanoharāyāstasyāḥ saṃbhāvitaḥ pūrvoktamadgocaradṛṣṭiviśeṣaiḥ saṃbhāvyamānaḥ sneha eva raso rasanīyatvādyatra tena saṃnidhinā matsamīpāvasthānena vidheyīkṛto vaśīkṛto'pi/ anyatroparatendriyavyāpāro'pi/ ātmanaḥ pāriplavatvaṃ dhairyabhraṃśaṃ parebhyo nihnotuṃ [kāmo yasya saḥ]/ pūrvopakrāntasya kesaradāmnaḥ śeṣaṃ kathaṃcidanyamanaskatayātinibandhena grathitavāneva/ tato militavetretyādi vākyārthaḥ spaṣṭaḥ/ madanodyānaṃ tu cirādapakrāntapūrṇacandramaṇḍalamivāmbaramandhatamasāvṛtamiva kimapyavalokanayogyaṃ nābhūditi bhāvaḥ/ gamanasamaye ca tayā kṛtamātmanaḥ prāṇadhāraṇaṃ pratyāśānibandhanamāha - yāntyeti/ yāntyā gacchantyā gamanasamaye'pi maddarśanautsukyena valitakaṃdharaṃ parivartitagrīvam/ ata evāvṛttavṛntaśatapattranibhaṃ kusumabandhanādapavṛttavikacapuṇḍarīkadṛśamānanaṃ vahantyā tayā pakṣmalākṣyā kṣipteṣvapi bahuṣu kaṭākṣeṣu nīrandhrataruśākhāniruddhatayoparuddheṣu kaścidekaḥ kaṭākṣo daivānmama hṛdaye gāḍhaṃ dṛḍhaṃ nikhāto vidārya hṛdayamantaḥpraveśita iva/ gāḍhamityanena punaruddharaṇāśakyatvaṃ sūcayati/ dṛḍhasaṃlagnatayedānīmapi tathaiva vartamāno'ntaḥśalyamiva vyathayatīti bhāvaḥ/ manmatho dhanvī tāmeva dhanurlatāmādhāya tatkaṭākṣameva nārācaṃ manmanasi lakṣye kadācidapyanuddharaṇīyaṃ nikhātavāniti samādhiḥ/ kīdṛśaḥ kaṭākṣaḥ/ amṛtena viṣeṇa ca digdhaḥ/ patanasamaye niratiśayānandajanakatvādidānīmapi jīvanahetutvāccāmṛtena digdhaḥ/ durviṣahaviprayogavedanāhetutvādviṣeṇa digdha iti draṣṭavyam/ anena vidhānaṃ nāmāṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``vidhānaṃ sukhaduḥkhakṛt'' iti/ nikhāta iveti tīkṣṇatayā kaṭākṣasya nārācatvasaṃbhāvanāt/ utprekṣālaṃkāraḥ//32//

 tata iti/ paricchedeti/ tatkaṭākṣanikhātamārabhya ko'pi vikāro vaikṛtaḥ/ antaramantaḥkaraṇaṃ jaḍayati pratipattiśūnyaṃ karoti/ tatraiva tāpaṃ ca kurute saṃtāpayati/ jāḍyasaṃjvarayormadanāvasthayoriyamuktiḥ/ tatra jāḍyaṃ yathā - ``jāḍyamapratipattiḥ syātsarvakāryeṣu sarvadā/ iṣṭāniṣṭaṃ na jānāti sukhaduḥkhaṃ na vetti ca// praśne na kiṃcitprabrūte na śṛṇoti na paśyati/ hāheti bhāṣaṇākāṇḍahuṃkāraśithilāṅgatā// kārśyavaivarṇyaniśvāsastambhasparśānabhijñatā/ ete jāḍyabhavā bhāvā mīnaketanamānitāḥ//'' iti/ saṃjvaro yathā - ``aviṣahyo manastāpaḥ saṃjvaraḥ parikīrtitaḥ/ yenāṅgāranadīmadhye viluṭhanniva bhāvyate//'' iti/ kiṃ ca jāḍyamatiśītaladravyasaṃśleṣātstabdhatvam/ tena jaḍayati/ atiśaityātstabdhayati/ tāpaṃ ca tanuta ityukte virodhapratītiḥ/ śaityasvabhāvasya tāpahetutāvirodhāt/ parihārastu valitagrīvāvalokanasya snehasūcakatvāt/ gāḍhānurāgāmṛtānuliptatayedānīmanusaṃdhīyamāno'pi/ amṛtanimagnamiva kaṭākṣaḥ karoti/ punastatprāptinairāśyapratipādanena vicintyamāno viṣamiva vyathayatīti/ tena virodhābhāso'laṃkāraḥ/ kīdṛśo vikāra ityata āha - paricchedeti/ iyānetāvānityavadhāraṇātmakaṃ jñānaṃ paricchedaḥ/ tadatītastadaviṣayaḥ/ tathā sakalavacanānāmabhidhālakṣaṇādisarvaprakāraśabdavyāpārāṇāmaviṣayo'gocaraḥ/ anena viśeṣaṇadvayena vāṅmanasayoragamyatvamuktam/ na cāsau pūrvānubhūtena kenacidapi vikāreṇa tādṛgayamityupamātuṃ śakya ityāha - punariti/ janmāntare kimanubhūtamiti na jñāyate/ prāgjanmavṛttasyājātismarairasmaraṇāt/ asmiṃstu janmani punarnetaḥ pūrvamīdṛśo vikāro'nubhavapathamavatīrṇo yenāyamupamīyeteti bhāvaḥ/ tathā vivekasyājñānavirodhinaḥ śāstraśravaṇādijanmano vijñānasya prakarṣeṇa dhvaṃsādatyantasamucchedādupacitaḥ pravṛddho yo mahāmoho dṛḍho bhramo'vidyā tena gahano viṣama ityarthaḥ/ anena conmādo nāma madanāvasthā sūcitā/ yathā - ``unmādo virahottho ya atasmiṃstadgrahāgrahaḥ/ sarvāvasthāsu sarvatra sarvathā sarvadā manaḥ// tadgataṃ tatkathāhlādi pradveṣṭīṣṭānapītarān/ dīrghaṃ muhurniśvasati tiṣṭhatyanimiṣekṣaṇaḥ// vihārakāle rudati krandati dhyāyati kṣaṇam/ glāyati spandate svasminhasati stauti muhyati// itthamunmādajā bhāvāḥ kathitā nāṭyakovidaiḥ//'' iti/ etaireva viśeṣaṇaiḥ ko'pīti vyākhyātam//33//

 idānīmasya vikārasya kāryāṇyanubhavāścāparicchedyā ityāha - paricchedavyaktiriti/ puraḥsthepīndriyasaṃnikṛṣṭe grahaṇayogye viṣaye paricchedena ghaṭo'yaṃ paṭo'yamityavadhāraṇātmakena samyaganubhavena kṛtā vyaktirabhivyaktistatra sphuraṇaṃ tajjanyaṃ prākaṭyaṃ vā na bhavati/ anena manaso vikāropahatatvāccakṣurādyanavasthānena bāhyārthapramāyāmavyāpāra uktaḥ/ smaraṇe'pi na samyagvyāpriyata ityāha - bhavatīti/ anabhyaste sakṛdāpātato gṛhīte kadācidanubhavo na saṃskāramādadhyāt/ āhito vā saṃskāro dārḍhyābhāvātkadācinna smṛtiṃ janayedapi/ punargṛhīte vastuni dṛḍhataraṃ saṃskārādhānādyathānubhavaṃ smṛtyā bhāvyam/ iha punarabhyaste'pi viṣaye na yathānubhavaṃ smṛtirutpadyate vidyamāneṣvapi sadṛśavastudarśanādiṣu saṃskārodbodhakāraṇeṣu/ pratyuta doṣavaśādatathābhāvenātathātvena virasaṃ viparyastaṃ smaraṇaṃ bhavati/ kvacidapi pūrvānubhūte smaraṇaṃ notpadyate/ api tu smṛtiviparyaya evetyarthaḥ/ himasarasi tuṣāravāpyāṃ vā candramasi vāmṛtamayaśarīre saṃtāpacchedo na bhavati/ kenāpi śiśiradravyeṇānirvāpaṇātsaṃtāpo na nivartata ityarthaḥ/ tena tvayā na kasmiṃścidapi śaityopacāre prayatitavyamiti bhāvaḥ/ tathāpi mano niṣṭhayā nirvahaṇena śūnyam/ kaṃcidarthamanusaṃdhātumupakramya madhya eva tasyātyantavismaraṇātprakrāntanirvahaṇāsamarthamityarthaḥ/ tatra hetumāha - bhramati ceti/ kvacidapyarthe na kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ/ ``jaḍayati ca tāpaṃ ca tanute'' ityuktayoreva jāḍyasaṃtāpayoretaddvivacanamiti mantavyam/ virodhābhāsa evālaṃkāraḥ atiśayoktirvā//34//

 kalahaṃsaḥ - dṛḍhaṃ khalveṣa kayāpyadyāpahṛtaḥ/ api nāma mālatyeva sā bhavet/ dṛḍhaṃ yathā vanitāntaravārtāmapi na sahate tathā/ kayāpyadya yātrāsamaye nayanapathapatitayā/ yātrā hi yūnoḥ prathamāvalokane prāthamikopāyaḥ/ yathā - ``yātrā puraskandanamutsaho vā svapno'tha citraṃ ratisaṃkathā vā/ bhavantyupāyāḥ prathamāvalokane yūnoryato vṛddhimupaiti rāgaḥ//'' iti/ apahṛtaḥ svāyattacittaḥ kṛtaḥ/ sā etaccittākarṣiṇī/ apināmeti saṃbhāvanāyām/ mālatyeva saṃbhāvitā/ tasyā evaitadabhilāṣagocaratāyogyalāvaṇyaśālitvāditi bhāvaḥ// makarandaḥ - svagatamiti/ aśrāvyārthaprayoge svagatamiti prayujyate/ taduktam - ``aśrāvyaśrāvyabhedena dvidhā vastu prakīrtitam/ sarvatra niyatasyeti śrāvyaṃ ca dvividhaṃ smṛtam// aśrāvyaṃ svagataṃ tatra prayojyaṃ svagataṃ tviti/ sarvaśrāvyaṃ prakāśaṃ syātprakāśamiti tadvadet// śrāvyaṃ tu niyatasyaiva nāṭyadharmamapekṣya ca/ dvidhā vibhajyate tatra janāntamapavāritam/ tripatākākareṇānyānapavāryāntarā kathām/ anyonyāmantraṇaṃ yatsyāttajjanāntikamucyate// rahasyaṃ kathyate'nyatra parāvṛtyāpavāritam/ vastunirvāhakatvena nāṭyadharmaprasaṅgataḥ//'' iti/ abhiṣaṅgo'bhiniviśaḥ/ apūrvo'yamekapade cittāsaṅgaprakarṣa ityadbhutāveśātparibhāvanaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktaṃ bhavati/ ``paribhāvo'dbhutāveśaḥ'' iti/ tatkimiti/ itthamidānīmasyonmādadaśā vartate/ tena samanantaradaśopanipātātpūrvameva kimenamasmādabhiṣaṅgānnivārayāmīti svātmanyeva vitarkaḥ/ evaṃ vitarkya nivāraṇamaśakyamiti nirdhārayati - athavā/ mā mūmuhaditi/ ananyajanmāsvacittaikayoniḥ kāmo bhavantaṃ mā mūmuhanna mohaṃ prāpayatu/ āśiṣi luṅ/ tava matirmalīmaso malinastrivargapratyanīkatvānnindito yo vikāro manmathābhiṣaṅgastena ghanā sāndratarā channā mā bhūdityādyupadeśajātamihaivamavasthe'sminnirarthakamakāryakāritvānniṣphalam/ nanvityātmana evāvadhāraṇe/ nairarthakye hetumāha - yasminmādhave kāmaśca jṛmbhitāḥ saṃjātajṛmbhāḥ pratyagravyaktanidrā vijṛmbhamāṇaguṇā virahijanaprāṇahāriṇo vyāpārā yasya tathābhūto vartate/ yauvanaṃ ca pravṛddhavivekapramoṣaṇādidharmakaṃ vartate/ tasmānnedānīmasya kiṃcidapyupadeṣṭavyamasti/ pāṭaccaraluṇṭhitasarvasvaṃ pathikaṃ prati ``corabhūyiṣṭho'yaṃ mārgaḥ, tannānena pathā gantavyam'' ityupadeśavadvṛthaiva balavanmanmathākṛṣṭamānasaṃ prati madanavaśago mā bhūrityupadeśo'tītakālatvādvyartha eva/ yadāha - ``yo hi brūyādbhuktavantaṃ mā bhukthā iti kiṃ tena kṛtaṃ syāt'' iti//35//

 evamātmanyeva niṣedhāśakyatāṃ nirdhārya tadabhilaṣitasaṃpādanenaiva tadupadravaṃ parijihīrṣaurānukūlyamavalambya pṛcchati - prakāśamiti/ vayasya, sā tāvattvaduparyanurakteti tvaduktena tacceṣṭāviśeṣeṇa jñātam/ kasminkule jātā saṃbandhayogyā vā kiṃnāmadheyā vetyetaditaḥ paraṃ jñātavyam/ tatsādhanāya tvayā yatkiṃcidviditamiti praśnaḥ// mādhavaḥ - śrūyatāmityādi/ vilambya kusumāpacayavyājena vilambaṃ kṛtvā/ kusumāpacayakrameṇa tānyupacinomi/ tāni tānītyevaṃrūpeṇa nedīyasībhūyaḥ[ya] saṃnihitā bhūtvā kusumāpīḍavyājena praṇamya śirasi sragbandhanavyājena lalāṭataṭaghaṭitakarakamalasaṃpuṭā namaskṛtya/ namaskāreṇa svabhartṛdārikāyāstvameva bharteti tathā sūcitam/ māmevaṃ vakṣyamāṇamuktavatī/ sā tāvatpūrvamevāvalokitādimukhānmādhavasya sarvakalākauśalaṃ jñātavatī satī nipuṇaṃ vaktumupakramate - mahābhāgetyādi/ mahānbhāgo bhāgadheyaṃ yasya/ ``nāmarūpabhāgebhyo dheyaḥ'' iti svārthe dheyapratyayavidhānādbhāga eṣa bhāgadheyam/ ākṛtiviśeṣeṇaivopalakṣyamāṇamahābhāgyātiśayetyarthaḥ/ eṣa saṃniveśaḥ kusumasaṃniveśanaprakāraḥ sragviracanāvaidagdhyaṃ suśliṣṭaguṇatayā susaṃghaṭitagranthimatsūtratvena ramaṇīyo manoharaḥ/ kutūhalinī cāsminvidagdhātiśaye kusumadāmaviracanāviśeṣe cāsmākaṃ bhartṛdārikā/ bhūrivasoramātyasya rājatulyatvāttatsutāpi bhartṛdāriketyucyate bahumānasūcanāya/ tasyāmadhikaraṇabhūtāyāmabhinavo'nyatrādṛṣṭapūrvo vicitraścamatkārakārī kusumeṣu viṣaye vyāpāro mālyagrathanārūpo'sti/ svayamasyāṃ kusumaracanāyāmatiśayenāsādhāraṇīṃ prasiddhimupagatā svavaidagdhyasadṛśaṃ bhavato mālyagrathanakauśalamupalabhyedānīṃ kautukākrāntamānasā vartata ityarthaḥ/ tattasmāttvadīyasya vaidagdhyasya kusumadāmaracanāprāvīṇyasya kṛtārthatāvidagdhayā tayopalālanāccaritārthatā bhavatu/ yathāha - ``ā paritoṣādviduṣāṃ na sādhu manye prayogavijñānam'' iti/ asyā api srajo nirmāṇasya ramaṇīyatābhijñajanamanorañjanenaiva phalena phalatu apacitakusumānāṃ svasvaphalājanakatve'pyetadeva phalamastvityarthaḥ/ tadeṣa te saraso'mlānaḥ, tathā sārasyena cāturyeṇānvitaḥ, tathā tvayānurāgeṇa samarpitaḥ, kusumavyāpāro bhartṛdārikāyāḥ kaṇṭhāvalambanena grīvālaṃkāratayā dhāraṇena mahārghatāṃ mahāmūlyatāmāsādayatu prāpnotu/ yathā kiṃciduttamaṃ ratnamuttamakanakabhūṣaṇasthāpitaṃ mahāmūlyaṃ bhavati, tathedamapi bakuladāmaratnabhūtaṃ bhartṛdārikākaṇṭhābharaṇatvena mahāmūlyaṃ bhavatviti prakṛto'rthaḥ/ anyastu - eṣo'smādṛśasya sakhījanasya taṭasthasahṛdayalokasya cāśāsyatvena buddhau viparivartamānaḥ saṃniveśo bhavatoḥ saṃbandho'bhilāṣamātram/ tasyāṃ viṣaye'bhinavaḥ kenāpi virahiṇānanubhūtapūrvaḥ, ata eva vicitro vismayakārī kusumeṣorvyāpāraḥ/ tvāmuddiśya smareṇa balavadabhibhūyata ityarthaḥ/ tattasmādanyonyasamāgamenobhayorvaidagdhyasya sakalakalāparijñānasya kṛtārthatā bhavatu/ tvadīyavaidagdhyasāraṃ saiva loke veditumarhā tasyāśca tvamevetyanyonyasadṛśābhijñaviṣayalābhādubhayorvaidagdhyaṃ caritārthamastvirthaḥ[tyarthaḥ]/ vaidagdhyalakṣaṇaṃ ca - ``laukike vaidike vārthe tathā sāmayike'pi ca/ samyakparicayaprauḍhirvaidagdhyamiti gīyate//'' iti/ nirmāṇasya bhavadubhayasṛṣṭilakṣaṇasya vidhātṛvyāpārasya ramaṇīyatā sarvalokātiśāyisaundaryasaṃpādakatvena manoharatā bhavadubhayasamāgamenaiva phalena phalatu/ anyathedṛksṛṣṭiśramaguṇojjvalastrīpuṃsanirmāṇaprayāso vidhervyartha eva bhavediti bhāvaḥ/ tadeṣa tribhuvanaratnabhūtaḥ sarasaḥ sānurāgo rasikāgraṇīśca bhavāṃstasyāḥ kaṇṭhāvalambanena kaṇṭhāliṅganena mahārghatāṃ prāpnotu/ sā hyamūlyaratnabhūtaṃ bhavantaṃ svakaṇṭhāliṅganasaukhyena ratiramaṇaparīkṣake kalpitena mahatādhikena lāvaṇyadraviṇena daridrasvetarayuvatijanāsaṃpādyena mūlyena kretumarhā nānyā kāciditi bhāvaḥ/ atra kusumadāmaprārthanāvyājena pūrvaṃ niṣpannārthasya mālatīviraharūpasya bhāvinaśca nirvahaṇaparyantānuvartamānabakuladāmasākṣikasarvavyāpārasūcanātpatākāsthānakamidam/ yathā - ``atītānāgate kārye kathyete yatra vastunaḥ/ anyāpadeśavyājena patākāsthānakaṃ [hi] tat//'' iti/ ata eva cirakālāvasthāyinī mlānāpi saurabhyamayī bakulamālā saiva kavinā nirdiṣṭā na tvitarakusumamāleti// makarandaḥ/ vaidagdhyaṃ tatparicārikāyā evedṛśaṃ vacanakauśalaṃ citramityarthaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ anuyuktayā keyaṃ kasya sutā veti pṛṣṭayā/ kathitaṃ tayā - iyamityādi/ prasūtirapatyam/ na tu dattā saṃkarajātā vā/ tena vivāhayogyatā sūcitā/ prasādabhūmirvisrambhabhājanam/ tena mayi śaṅkā na kāryetyuktam/ dhātreyītyanullaṅghyavacanatvaṃ sakalatanmarmajñānaṃ cātmanaḥ sūcitam// kalahaṃsaḥ - saharṣamiti/ yayā likhitamasya praticchandakamānītaṃ mayā tasyāmeva mālatyāmāsakto'yamiti saphala idānīṃ mama vyāpāra iti harṣahetuḥ/ kiṃ nāma mālatīti/ anenāsyānurāgaviṣayabhūtā mālatītyucyate/ kiṃ nāmeti harṣātiśayasūcakā kākuḥ/ tarhi diṣṭyā vilasitaṃ vijṛmbhitaṃ bhagavatā devena kusumāyudhena/ jitamasmābhiḥ/ taddūtyavyāpṛtaiḥ sarvaireva kāryatatparairityarthaḥ/ svāminaḥ prāṇasaṃkaṭasamaye tadujjīvanauṣadhapraticchandakānayanena tatprāṇatrāṇādātmani gauravamāropyāsmābhiriti bahuvacananirdeśaḥ// makarandaḥ - svagatam/ amātyeti/ amātyātmajetyetāvanmātragauravaṃ tasyā aparyāptamityarthaḥ/ nahyamātyaduhitetyeva sā bahumānāspadam/ kiṃtu lokātiśāyilāvaṇyaprabhṛtiguṇayogenāpi/ tena daivādupanato'pyasya kāmavikāraḥ samucitaviṣayatvācchlāghanīya eveti bhāvaḥ/ gauravāparyāptimevopapādayati - api ceti/ atyādarasūcanārthaṃ vīpsā/ svayaṃ sadaiva enāṃ ślāghata ityarthaḥ/ tāṃ ceti/ rājāpi narmasuhṛde kanyāsahasraṃ kvacitsaṃpādya pradātuṃ śakto'pi tadabhilāṣāspadamālatīsadṛśayuvatisaṃpādanāśaktatvānnīcadūtyamaṅgīkṛtavān/ kimu vaktavyaṃ tasyāḥ ślāghanīyatvamiti bhāvaḥ// mādhavaḥ - tayetyādi/ anubadhyamānaḥ priyavacanaiḥ punaḥpunarabhyarthyamānaḥ/ priyopabhuktamuktatayā mālatīṃ prati ślāghyatāṃ srajo darśayitumātmanaḥ kaṇṭhādityuktam/ asāvityādi/ abhiniviṣṭayā duṣprāpavastuprāptestadekasaṃlagnayā dṛśā tāmeva srajaṃ bahumanyamāneti saṃbandhaḥ/ mālatīdarśanena manaso'nyatrāsaṅgātprastutakriyāvikalahastatayā vipamaṃ prāggrathitabhāgāpekṣayā virūpamaśobhanaṃ yathā tathā racitaikabhāgo mālatīdarśanāntaragrathito bhāgo yasyāstāmapi srajam/ saundaryāttatra tasyā na bahumānaḥ kiṃ tu madgrathitatveneti bhāvaḥ/ ata eva vakṣyati - ``premṇā madgrathiteti vā priyasakhīhastopanīteti vā'' iti/ anantaraṃ cetyādi/ paurasya naigamasya ca janasya/ puranigamabhedaḥ kathyate - ``adrauvane'thavā rāṣṭre
(
ṛ)sarvajanāvāsasaṃkīrṇam/ krayavikrayakairyuktaṃ puramuditaṃ yattadeva nagaramiti// cāturvarṇyasametaṃ sarvajanāvāsasaṃkīrṇam/ bahukarmakārayuktaṃ yattannigamaṃ samuddiṣṭam//'' iti/ saṃkulena saṃmardena vighaṭitāyāṃ nayanavighaṭitāyām/ nayanasaṃnikarṣādvibhraṣṭāyāṃ tirohitāyāmityarthaḥ// makarandaḥ idānīṃ mādhavenāpi sāmānyato jñātaṃ mālatyāḥ svasminnanurāgamātmanyapratyayādāśaṅkyamānaṃ liṅgairdṛḍhaṃ kṛtvā tanmano'vasthāpayitumāha - makarandaḥ - vayasyeti/ tasyā api tvadvacanāttvayyanurāgapratīteretatkāryaṃ sādhu saṃghaṭitamityarthaḥ/ na ca mama darśanātpūrvameva sā kapolapāṇḍimādyupetā vartate, tena madanyasminneva kvāpi yūni prāgavalokite baddhabhāveyaṃ na tu mayītyāśaṅkanīyam/ yatastvameva kutracitpūrvaṃ dṛṣṭastasyāścirādanubadhyamānasya manmathavikārābhiniveśasya hetuḥ/ kiṃ tu kva vā tayā prāgavalokito'bhūrityetāvadeva jñātavyamityāha - yohītyādinā/ ahameva tatra heturiti kuto'vadhāraṇamityata āha - na khalviti/ tādṛśyaḥ praśastakulaśīlaguṇagaṇojjvalā mahābhāgadheyajanmānaḥ prakṛṣṭasucaritaparipākalabdhajanuṣaḥ/ cakṣūrāgaścakṣuḥprītirāsāmastīti tathā/ yathā - ``kulīnā guṇavatyaśca kumāryo bhāgyabhūṣaṇāḥ/ īdṛśāstvayaśodoṣabhājanaṃ naiva jātucit// yadyanyāsaktacittā sā na cakṣustvayi pātayet/ mano'nyatra dṛganyatra ceṭīnāṃ nottamastriyāḥ//'' iti bhāvaḥ/ anyadapi tvayi pūrvānurāge liṅgamastītyāha - api ceti/ anyonyeti/ anyonyasaṃbhinnadṛśāṃ sapratyabhijñaṃ sa evāyamiti nirdhārayituṃ parasparamukhāvalokanena saṃmiśradṛṣṭīnāṃ tasyāḥ sakhīnāṃ kasyāpi janasya ko'pi dayita ihaiva vartata ityevaṃrūpaṃ niveditam/ svasakhyai prajñāpanaṃ caikaṃ tasyāstvayyevāsau pāṇḍimādisūcitaḥ prāganurāga ityasminnarthe cihnaṃ liṅgamityarthaḥ// atha mādhavaḥ svanirdhāritaṃ liṅgāntaraṃ lavaṅgikāśliṣṭavacanarūpamasāvudbhāvayiṣyati vā na vetyautsukyāttadvacanāvasarapratīkṣaṇamasahamānaḥ kasyāpi ko'pīti niveditaṃ ceti caśabdena kimanyatsamuccinoṣīti pṛcchati - mādhavaḥ - kiṃ ceti/ cakāraḥ/ kimanyatprāganurāgaliṅgaṃ dyotayatītyarthaḥ// makarandaḥ - dhātreyikāyā iti/ caturaṃ śliṣṭam/ prāganurāgacihnamiti śeṣaḥ//36//

kalahaṃsaḥ/ etacca/ idamapi prāganurāgacihnamiti prāptāvasaraścitramarpayatītyarthaḥ//  citralekhanaparyantasya mālatīvyāpārasya makarandenāpātato'dhigamātkeneti sāmānyato nirdeśaḥ// kalahaṃsaḥ/ punarvimarśapūrvakaṃ tenaiva vācayitumāha - yenaivāsya hṛdayamapahṛtamiti// makarandaḥ/ api nāmeti praśne/ likhitamiti śeṣaḥ// kalahaṃsaḥ/ atha kimityaṅgīkāre/ bāḍhaṃ mālatyaivetyarthaḥ// mādhavaḥ - vayasyeti/ prasannaprāyaḥ saṃdehalakṣaṇakāluṣyāpagamānnirmalībhūtastarkastvayyeva sānuraktetyūhaḥ/ atra citradarśanātsvasminmālatyanurāganiścayena sukhāgamapratīteḥ prāptirnāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktaṃ bhavati/ yathā - ``prāptiḥ ko'pi sukhāgamaḥ'' iti// makarandaḥ/ kalahaṃsaka, kasmādetattvayā prāptamityarthaḥ// kalahaṃsaḥ/ mama tāvanmandārikāhastāt/ tasyā api lavaṅgikāsakāśāt// kalahaṃsaḥ/ utkaṇṭhāvinodanamiti// yā kaumudīti/ yā tvannetracakorayoścandrikeva niratiśayasauhityakāriṇī tasyāḥ sujanmā tvamapi manorathabandhasya dṛḍhānurāgasya bandhurāśraya ityarthaḥ/ tatastasyāḥ saṃgamaṃ prati saṃdeho nāsti/ yato yasminsaṃgame vidhiranyonyānurūpanirmāṇenodbhūtaprayāso madanaścākasmādevotpāditagāḍhānurāgaḥ kṛtābhiyogaḥ etatkāryasaṃgame racitābhiniveśo baddhakacchaḥ/ tato na tadarthaṃ tvayā cintanīyamityarthaḥ//37//

 draṣṭavyeti/ atidhīrasyāpi te yā manovikāramutpādayitā
(trī) sā mayā draṣṭavyarūpā darśanīyākṛtiḥ/ anena mādhavasya saṃśayaṃ nirasya parasparānurāgasya bījasya sthāpanātsamādhānaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``bījāgamaḥ samādhānam''/ iti// draṣṭavyarūpā darśanīyākṛtiḥ/ keyaṃ kalāsu prāgalbhyaṃ prakāśitavatī, tadbhavānapi tadvilekhanena vyājena tasyai svavaidagdhyaṃ prakaṭīkarotviti pratītiḥ// mādhavaḥ/ vayasyāyeti/ ``rucyarthānām'' iti saṃpradānatvam/ tatkaromīti śeṣaḥ/ atha tattadavayavasādṛśyalekhanārthaṃ manasaiva mālatyāḥ sarvāvayavasamyaṅnirvarṇanena samupajātabāṣpādisāttvikabhāvo mādhavaścitrakriyāyāṃ svasya dṛkkarādisāmagryā ananukūlatāmupapādayannāha - vāraṃvāramiti/ likhāmi priyāmiti saṃkalpya yatkiṃcidbhrūnetrādyavayavasya smaraṇaṃ tatsamayasamudgato bāṣpapūro vāraṃvāraṃ kṣaṇe kṣaṇe/ ``vāraścāvasaraḥ kṣaṇaḥ'' iti vacanāt/ punaḥpunarunmṛjyamāno'pi dṛśau tirayatyāvṛṇoti/ anenāśrunāmakaḥ sāttvikabhāva uktaḥ/ yathā - ``aśrunetrodbhavaṃ vāri duḥkhakrodhapraharṣajam'' iti/ tasyāḥ saṃkalpena cintayopahitaḥ prāpto jaḍimā yena tadgātraṃ stambhamabhyeti/ jāḍyastambhau yathā - ``kriyāsvapāṭavaṃ jāḍyaṃ cintotkaṇṭhābhayādibhiḥ/ stambhaśceṣṭāpratīghāto bhayarāgāmayādibhiḥ//'' iti/ tathā sadyastadupasmṛtikṣaṇa eva svidyansvedajalārdro bhavannayaṃ me pāṇirlekhāvidhiṣu lekhanakriyāsvaviratotkampenānavaratavepathunā lolāścañcalā aṅgulayo yasya sa tādṛśo vartate/ tatkiṃkaromi/ idānīṃ citrakarmavaidagdhyaprakaṭanaṃ durghaṭamityarthaḥ/ svedavepathū yathā - ``vapurjalodgamasvedo ratidharmaśramādibhiḥ/ rāgarogabhayādibhyaḥ kampo gātrasya vepathuḥ//'' iti//38//

 tathāpītyādi spaṣṭaḥ// jagatīti/ jagati bhuvane te te navendukalādayo bhāvāḥ padārthā jayino manmathasāhāyyācaraṇena virahijanavaśīkaraṇaśīlāḥ/ prakṛtimadhurāḥ svabhāvenaiva manoharāḥ/ ``iha vaṭe yakṣaḥ'' iti nyāyena na kevalamandhaparamparayā prasiddhāḥ/ anye mālatīvyatiriktāḥ santyeva ye'nyeṣāmadṛṣṭamālatīmukhāmbujānāmavivekināṃ vā mano madayantyutsukayanti/ naitadapūrvam/ santu nāma/ taiḥ kimidānīṃ tadapekṣayā varākaprāyairiti bhāvaḥ/ ``bhinnarucirhi lokaḥ'' iti nyāyenānyeṣāṃ yathā tathā vāstu nāma/ mama punarvilocanacandrikā netrāhlādakāriṇī/ loke lokaprasidhdyā kevalamidamucyate, na tu svāpekṣayā/ mādhavasya mālatyapekṣayā candrikāderatitucchatvena mālatyāmutkarṣapratipādanārthaṃ candrikātvāropasya svavacovyāhatatvāt/ tena yathā lokasya candrikā netrayorāhlādakāriṇī evaṃ mama mālatītyetāvatā candrikātvāropaḥ/ na tūtkarṣapratipādanāyeti vyākhyeyam/ evaṃ ca vyāhatārthadoṣa iti tu parihṛtam/ iyaṃ mālatī nayanaviṣayam/ bhāvaprādhānyena nirdeśaḥ/ netragocaratāmāpanneti yatsa evāsmiñjanmani loke kṛtsne'pi prapañca eko'dvitīyo mahānutsavaḥ saukhyahetuḥ/ nānye candrakalādaya ityarthaḥ/ atiśayoktiralaṃkāraḥ//39//

 praviśya/ mandāriketi/ mādhavāya pradarśya śīghraṃ pratyānayeti kalahaṃsasya haste citramarpitavatī madanodyānātpratinivṛtya krīḍāsaudhavartinyāṃ mālatyāmadya viśeṣadarśanaprabalatarotkaṇṭhāvinodanāya kva citraphalakamiti vimṛśantyāṃ tadasaṃnidhānādupajātasaṃbhramā mandārikā janasaṃkule kalahaṃsamanviṣya tatrādṛṣṭavatī praviśya vadatīti bhāvaḥ/ dviruktiḥ saṃbhramasūcanārthā/ plutaśca ``nigṛhyānuyoge ca'' iti vihito deśabhāṣāsvapi vyavahriyate/ coretyāmantraṇaṃ madgṛhāccitraphalakaṃ tvayā coreṇānītamityupahāsaparam/ ata evāha - pādānusāreṇa labdho'sīti/ coro hi padanyāsaliṅgānusaraṇenaiva gṛhyate/ salajjamiti/ tatsvāmisaṃnidhau tena sahopahasanānaucityāt/ kathaṃ tāvapi sahānubhāvāvatraiva// ita āgamyatāmityādaroktiḥ/ tasyāḥ samanantarakāryopayogāttadanunayārtham// mandārikā/ kalahaṃsaka, upanaya citraphalakam// kalahaṃsaḥ/ gṛhāṇedam// mandārikā/ kalahaṃsakaṃ prati/ kena kiṃnimittaṃ vātra mālatyabhilikhitā// kalahaṃsaḥ/ ya eva yannimittaṃ ca mālatyā likhito mādhavastenaiva tadarthamevotkaṇṭhāvinodārthameva mālatī likhitetyarthaḥ// mandādi[ri]kā - saharṣamiti/ kāmandakīparijanasyāsmadāderdūtyavyāpāraḥ phalonmukha iti harṣahetuḥ/ diṣṭyā upadarśitaphalaṃ vijñānaṃ prajāpateḥ/ īdṛśayuvadvayanirmāṇaṃ vidhātuḥ śilpakauśalamanayoranyonyānurāgaprakarṣeṇa darśitaphalaṃ jātam/ pariṇayarūpā pariṇatirevetaḥparamavaśiṣṭeti bhāvaḥ// makarandaḥ/ atra praticchandakarūpe vastuni yadeṣa te patiḥ kathayati mālatyā likhitamutkaṇṭhāvinodanāyeti/ api praśne/ kiṃ tattathā satyamityarthaḥ// mandārikā/ mahābhāga, tattathā// makarandaḥ -
