kveti/ yena utkaṇṭhāvinodanāya tadrūpamalikhaditi bhāvaḥ// mandārikā/ lavaṅgikā bhaṇati vātāyanagateti// etadgavākṣamārgeṇa darśanamityarthaḥ/ mandārikā/ anumanyatāṃ mahābhāgaḥ/ yāvadidaṃ bhagavato'pratihatabhāvasya devasyāśakyasaṃghaṭanedṛśamithunasaṃghaṭanānuguṇavyāpārātmakakrīḍāvato madanasya sucaritamanyonyākṛtinirmāṇasetuparasparānurāgalakṣaṇaṃ lavaṅgikāyai nivedayiṣyāmi/ tayaivāhamatra niyuktā, tamimaṃ vṛttāntaṃ tasyai nivedayāmi/ sā punaryathocitamācariṣyatīti bhāvaḥ// makarandaḥ - prāpteti/ bhavatyā/ etanmādhavālokitasya dṛḍhīkṛtatadanurāgasya tadabhilikhitamālatīrūpasaṃpādayiṣyamāṇamālatīmanaḥpratyayasyāsya praticchandakasya lavaṅgikāyai nivedanaṃ prāpto'vasaro yasya tat/ avasarocitamityarthaḥ// saṃstyāyaṃ sadanam// dharmāmbha ityādi/ mugdhe sundare akṣiṇī yasyāstasyāḥ paricārikāṇāṃ vāravilāsinīnāṃ kapolakuṅkumāni kartṝṇi kapolayoranuliptāni kuṅkumāni/ anenānulepanaviśeṣa uktaḥ/ yathā - ``vaidagdhyenoparacitaṃ stanayorvā kapolayoḥ/ unmādanaṃ nayanayoryattatsyādanulepanam//'' iti/ dharmāmbhasaḥ svedajalasya visaro bindusamūhastasya vivartanaiḥ prasaraṇaiḥ/ tattadā mayā dṛṣṭaṃ prātaḥkāle vihitā yā vicitrā camatkārakāriṇī patralekhā patraracanā saiva vaidagdhyaṃ tatkarmabhūtaṃ jahati tyajantīti manye/ tarkayāmītyarthaḥ/ svedavaśātpramṛṣṭaracanāni vicchinnacāturyāṇi kapolakuṅkumāni madhyāhne syuriti bhāvaḥ//40//

 athonmādavijṛmbhaṇamahimnā vāyoracetanādapi tatsaṃgamaṃ prārthayannāha - unmīladityādi/ he pavana, tāmāliṅganneva, na tu tadanantaraṃ kiṃcidvilambya, mamāṅgamaṅgaṃ pratyavayavaṃ spṛśa/ priyatamāmāśliṣyatastavāṅgasaṃsparśo'pi priyatamāṅgasaṃsparśakalpa eveti bhāvaḥ/ yathāha - ``vāhi vāta yataḥ kāntā tāṃ spṛṣṭvā māmapi spṛśa/ tvayi me gātrasaṃsparśaścandre dṛṣṭisamāgame//'' iti/ tāmiti/ yādṛśī pūrvaṃ dṛṣṭā tāmeva smarati/ īṣatpracale vilāsavaśātkiṃciccañcale/ athavānimiṣadarśanena sakhyo jñāsyantīti tatpracchādanāya kiṃciccañcale locane yasyāstām/ pīvarakucabhareṇa virahaglānyā vā natamavanatamaṅgaṃ yasyāstām/ pavanaṃ viśinaṣṭi - unmīladiti/ unmīladbhirvikāsonmukhairmukulaiḥ korakaiḥ karālo danturaḥ/ ``karālo danture tuṅge viśāle vikṛte'pi ca'' iti darśanāt/ yaḥ kundakośaḥ kundalatāpratānaṃ tataḥ pracyotanto ghanā niviḍā ye makarandāḥ puṣparasāsteṣāṃ gandhasya bandho tasya sahacaretyanena vilāpākhyā madanāvasthā darśitā/ yathā - ``iha dṛṣṭamiha śliṣṭamihāgatamiha sthitam/ iha nirvṛtamatraiva śayitaṃ cāpyalaṃkṛtam// evamādīni vākyāni vilāpa iti kathyate// antarbahiḥ puraḥ paścāddūrādārātsamīpataḥ// kvacitpaśyati yātyeva kvacitkvāpyavatiṣṭhate/ āste kvacitkvacicchete kvacinnindati nandati// itaścetaśca rathyāyāṃ rauti bhrāmyati dhāvati/ evaṃ vilāpajā bhāvā manobhavavaśānugāḥ//'' iti//41//

 makarandaḥ - svagatam/ abhihantīti/ hanteti viṣāde/ ``hanta harṣe'nukampāyāṃ vākyārambhaviṣādayoḥ'' iti darśanāt/ abhihantyābhīkṣṇyena praharati/ anavagraho'pratibandhaḥ/ durnivāra iti yāvat/ kathamidamatyantānucitamārabdhamatinṛśaṃsena manmathahatakenetyarthaḥ/ acireṇotpattisamaya eva yathā cikitsānavasaraparo hataḥ syāt, tathā pravṛddha ityarthaḥ/ vikṛtaṃ doṣatrayam/ vikṛtasaṃbandhivivarto vaikṛtavivarta udriktadoṣatrayapariṇatirūpaḥ sāṃnipātiko jvaraḥ/ sa cāsau dāruṇaśceti vigrahaḥ/ kaṭhoro'titīvraḥ/ kūṭapākalo gajarogaviśeṣaḥ/ kūṭena kapaṭenākasmādāgatya tasminneva nipāyatīti kūṭapākalaḥ/ yathā - ``yathābhihanyātkūṭena mṛgayūthaṃ vanecaraḥ/ tathā pātātmako nāgaṃ hanti vai kūṭapākalaḥ// mṛgaḥ kūṭena śabarairhanyate dāruṇaṃ yathā/ tathā tena dvipaḥ sīdatyataḥ syātkūṭapākalaḥ//'' iti/ kecitkūṭībhūya saṃdhībhūya doṣatrayeṇa janito gajajvaraviśeṣaḥ kūṭapākala iti vyācakṣate/ anenonmādāvasthā sūcitā/ upamālaṃkāraḥ//42//

 tadatreti/ nānyā gatirastītyarthaḥ/ iyamarthasaṃprasāraṇarūpā yuktirmukhasaṃdheraṅgam/ yathā - ``saṃprasāraṇamarthānāṃ yuktirityabhidhīyate'' iti/ mādhavastu tāmeva gamanasamaye dṛṣṭāmidānīṃ sarvatrāpi vilokayannātmanyeva svakīyamunmādātiśayaṃ vimṛśannāha - paśyāmīti/ tāmeva gamanasamaye dṛṣṭāmidānīṃ bahudhā vivartamānām/ prātibhāsikākāro vivartaḥ/ pratibhāsamātraśarīratayā sphurantīm/ bāhuvidhyaṃ vyanakti - ita ita iti/ kadācitkarṇāntikamāgatya rahasyākhyānasaspṛhāmiva paryāyeṇa pārśvadvaye madīyabāhusaṃsparśaromāñcakañcukitaikapayodharaṃ niṣeduṣīm, anyadā purato'bhimukhamupaviśya ``kimityevamativiṣaṇṇaḥ khidyase/ sarvathā tvadāyattajīvitāham'' iti prauḍhavanitāvanmāmanunayantīm, kṣaṇāntare punaḥ paścānmāṃ mandasaṃcāramāgatya mannayane pidhāya karābhyāṃ ``kāhaṃ kathaya'' iti māṃ vinodayantīm, kadācidasau kimanyāṃ vā nirantaraṃ nidhyāyati māmeveti vicārayitumivāntaḥ praviśantīm, tatra ca svātmānamevopāsyāmiva paradevatāmākārāntareṇa madicchāparikalpitena bhāsamānāmavalokya vanitāntaraśaṅkayā ``nūnaṃ śaṭho'si/ nāhaṃ tvatsaṃnidhau vartitumutsahe'' ityasūyayeva bahirnirgatya celāñcale gṛhyamāṇāmapi balādapasarantīm, kadācidyoginīmiva parigṛhya bahūni śarīrāṇi paritaḥ parivṛtya māmanekaprakārairvilāsairānandayantīmiti svamanorathavijṛmbhaṇaprapañcanaprakāraḥ/ punaḥ kīdṛśīm/ vikasanmanoharakanakakamalasadṛśam/ āsaṅgena mayyāsaktatayā tiryagapavartitā tiryagvalitā dṛṣṭiryasya tattādṛśaṃ vaktraṃ vahantīmiti//43//

 evaṃ svātmani vimṛṣṭamevārthaṃ vayasyāya makarandāya nivedayannāha - prasaratīti/ ko'pyanirvācyaḥ kenāpyaviditanidānaḥ parimāthī parimathanaśīlaḥ sadya eva vipattiśaṅkākāryayaṃ dehadāho madanajvaraḥ prasarati sarvāvayavavyāpto vartate/ jvarākhyasya daśāviśeṣasya lakṣaṇamuktam/ pramohaḥ ``sukhaduḥkhādijanito mohaścittasya mūḍhatā'' ityuktarūpaḥ karaṇānāmindriyāṇāṃ grāhakatvaṃ svasvaviṣayagrahaṇaśaktiṃ tirayati nāśayati/ raṇaraṇakasyotkaṇṭhāyā vivṛddhiṃ bibhrat/ āvartamānaṃ madanānalena kvāthyamānam ``āvartanaṃ kvāthanaṃ syāt'' iti vacanāt/ hṛdayamantarjvalati/ yathā tailādikamatikvāthena prajvalati tadvaditi/ kathaṃ tarhīdānīṃ prāṇadhāraṇamityata āha - tanmayatvaṃ mālatītādātmyamevāmṛtaniṣekaprāyam/ saṃjīvanauṣadhamityarthaḥ//44//


cāpalaṃ yadakārīdaṃ bālenāpi satā mayā/

guṇaikagrāhiṇāmetatprītaye'stu mahātmanām//

iti śrīmadbhāradvājakulatilakaparvatanāthayāyajūkanandanatripurārisūriviracitāyāṃ mālatīmādhavabhāvapradīpikāyāṃ prathamāṅkavivaraṇam//

 dvitīyo'ṅkaḥ/

jayatyapūrvaṃ pāṇḍityamaho kusumadhanvanaḥ/

ā viriñcaprapañcaikasaṃpradāyapravartakam/

kenāpyanupadiṣṭeṣu pragalbhā yena bālikā//

kaviridānīmaṅkāntaramārabhamāṇo lavaṅgikāgamanānantarabhāvivṛttāntasūcanāyārthopakṣepakaṃ praveśakaṃ tāvatprastauti - tata ityādi/ praveśakastu - ``vṛttavartiṣyamāṇānāṃ kathāṃśānāṃ nidarśakaḥ/ praveśakastu nāṭyādau nīcapātraprayojitaḥ//'' iti/ ceṭyau dāsyau/ etādṛśānāmeva praveśakārhatvāt/ yadāha - ``parijanakathānubandhaḥ praveśako nāma vijñeyaḥ'' iti// ekā/ haleti sakhyā āmantraṇam/ saṃgītaśālāparisare'valokitādvitīyā bhagavatī kāmandakī kimapi mantrayantyāsīt/ prākṛte bahulagrahaṇādadyatanabhūte'pi laṅprayogaḥ/ avalokitayā tadā tvaṃ kimabhāṣiṣṭhā ityarthaḥ// dvitīyā/ sakhi, tena kila mādhavapriyavayasyena makarandena sakalo madanodyānavṛttānto bhagavatyai niveditaḥ/ makarandena niścitaṃ khalu kāmandakī naḥ śaraṇamiti/ tataḥ suhṛdvipatpratikārārthaṃ tatsamarthāyai kāmandakyai tatsarvaṃ niveditavānityavalokitāmukhācchrutamityarthaḥ/ tato bhartṛdārikāṃ draṣṭukāmayā bhagavatyā pravṛttinimittaṃ kva vā kathaṃ vā vartata iti bhartṛdārikāyāḥ samavasthāparijñānārthamavalokitānupreṣitā/ mayāpi tasyai avalokitāyai kathitaṃ yathā lavaṅgikādvitīyā vivikte bhartṛdārikā vartata iti// prathamā - sakhi, lavaṅgikā khalu kesarakusumānyavacinomīti tatraiva vilambitavatī nāsmābhirāgacchantībhiḥ saha madanodyānādevāgatā kiṃ sāṃprataṃ saṃprāptā// dvitīyā/ atha kim/ bāḍhaṃ saṃprāptetyarthaḥ/ tāṃ khalvāpatantīmeva haste gṛhītvāparijanā pratiṣiddhasakalaparicārikā bhartṛdārikoparyalindaṃ krīḍāgṛhabahirdvāraprāntavartinyā vedikāyā ūrdhvabhāgaṃ samārūḍhā// prathamā/ nūnaṃ tasya mahānubhāvasya saṃkathayātmānaṃ vinodayati/ tvayā kimuktaṃ tena ca kimuktamiti tatprasaṅgenaivotkaṇṭhāvinodanaṃ karotītyarthaḥ/ dvitīyā/ niḥśvasyeti/ mālatīmanorathāntarāyādhigamānniḥśvāsaḥ/ kutaḥ khalvasyā āśvāsaḥ/ etenādya saviśeṣadarśanenātibhūmiṃ parāṃ kāṣṭhāṃ khalu tasyā abhiniveśo'nurāgaprakarṣo gamiṣyati/ anyadapyabhiniveśavivṛddhau nimittaṃ sūcayati - kalye ca prabhātasamaya eva nandanasya kāraṇānmahārājo bhartṛdārikāṃ prārthayamāno'mātyena vijñaptaḥ// prathamā/ kimiti vijñaptam/ kathayeti śeṣaḥ// dvitīyā/ prabhavati nijasya kanyakājanasya mahārāja iti/ vijñapta iti śeṣaḥ/ ata āmaraṇaṃ khalu tasyā mālatyā mādhavānurāgaḥ śalyamanabhilaṣitavarapradānaśaṅkāviṣāktaṃ manmathanārācaphalatulyamiti tarkayāmi// prathamā/ api nāma bhagavatī kimapyatra bhagavatītvaṃ darśayiṣyati/ mālatyāmativatsalā kāmandakī tanmanorathasaṃpādane svakīyajñānavairāgyaiśvaryādisaṃpattirūpaṃ māhātmyaṃ prakaṭayiṣyatīti saṃbhāvyata ityarthaḥ// dvitīyā/ ayi asaṃbaddhamanorathe, ehi/ prakṛtakṛtyānusaraṇāyeti śeṣaḥ// evaṃ makarandena kāmandakyai mādhavāvasthānivedanam, lavaṅgikāprāptim, mālatīlavaṅgikayoḥ praveśam, tadanantaraṃ mālatyavasthāparijñānārthaṃ kāmandakyāgamanam, tayā ca prasajyamānaṃ nandanavṛttāntaṃ sūcayitvā ceṭyau niṣkrānte// praveśakaḥ// tata ityādi/ lavaṅgikādvitīyā vivikte vartata iti sūcitapraveśe mālatīlavaṅgike praviśata ityarthaḥ/ nanvatra nāyakapraveśābhāvātpratyakṣanāyakacaritavarṇanākhyāṅkalakṣaṇābhāvātkathamasyāṅkatvam/ netṛgrahaṇena nāyikāyā apyekaśeṣeṇa grahaṇādadoṣaḥ/ kiṃ cāṅkadvayasyāpyekadinavyāpāratvāt, ekasaṃdhyātmakatvācca pūrvāṅkaśeṣa evāyaṃ pātrapraveśanirgamasaukaryārthaṃ nāyakavyāpārānusaṃdhānaṃ parisamāpya nāyikāvyāpārānusaṃdhānātkasaukaryārthaṃ cāṅkāntaraṃ kṛtamiti mantavyam/ kathamaṅkadvayasyaikasaṃdhitvamiti ceducyate - ``anvitānāṃ kathāṃśānāṃ paramete prayojane/ saṃbandhaḥ saṃdhirityukto'vāntaraikaprayojane//'' ityevaṃrūpam/ tadatra mālatīmādhavayorvivāhalakṣaṇaparamaprayojanasamanvitānāmavāntaraikaprayojane tadanyonyānurāgasya bījasya dṛḍhīkaraṇalakṣaṇe pravṛttānāmadyatanakāmandakyavalokitādiprayatno madanodyānacitraphalakavṛttāntato nandanaprasaṅgodbhāvanena mālatīmanodṛḍhīkaraṇaṃ mādhavasya kulaśīlavayorūpādiprakhyāpanaṃ cetyeteṣāṃ kathāṃśānāṃ saṃbandhaḥ sāṅgo mukhasaṃdhiḥ/ ``mukhaṃ bījasamutpattirnānārtharasasaṃbhavā'' ityuktalakṣaṇāt/ ityalaṃ prapañcena// mālatī/ sakhi, tatastataḥ/ madanodyāne svavilambaṃ mādhavasamīpagamanaṃ caturairvacanairbakuladāmaprārthanaṃ cetyetāvaduktavatīṃ lavaṅgikāṃ pratyuparitanavṛttāntaśravaṇakautukotkarṣātsasaṃbhramapraśnaḥ/ ādarasūcanārthā vīpsā/ anantaravṛttāntaṃ kathayeti śeṣaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ tatastena mahānubhāvenopanīteyaṃ bakulamālā// mālatī/ nirvarṇya sādaraṃ vilokya/ sakhi, ekapārśvaviṣamapratibaddheyaṃ viracanā// lavaṅgikā/ atrāramaṇīyatve tvamevāparāddhāsi/ mālatī/ kathamiva/ mādhavakartṛkāyāḥ sragviracanāyā ekapārśvavaiṣamye kathamahaṃ nimittaṃ bhavāmītyarthaḥ/ lavaṅgikā/ yena sa dūrvāśyāmalāṅgastathā vihastīkṛtaḥ/ vastuto grathanakriyāyāṃ vikalahastaḥ kṛtaḥ/ tvameva svasaundaryasaṃpadā gambhīramapi tanmanaḥ samākarṣantī prastutakriyāvikalahastaṃ kṛtavatī/ tata idaṃ viracanāvaiṣabhyaṃ
{myaṃ
ṛ
} jātamiti bhāvaḥ// mālatī/ sakhi lavaṅgike, sarvathāśvāsanaśīlāsi/ matsamāśvāsanāya kevalamidaṃ tvayocyate/ tasya mayyanurāgo'saṃbhāvita iti bhāvaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, kātrāśvāsanaśīlatā/ pratyakṣasiddhe'rthe kiṃ mayyaviśvāsenetyarthaḥ/ nanu bhaṇāmi/ so'pi priyasakhyā pratyakṣīkṛta evetyantena saṃbandhaḥ/ na mayaiveti bhāvaḥ/ mandamārutapracalitapraphullapuṇḍarīkavibhramābhyāṃ prathamārabdhabakulāvalīviracanāpadeśena yatsaṃyamanaṃ tvadavalokanajanitākāravikriyāsaṃvaraṇaṃ tanmūlo yo balātkārastena vistṛtābhyām/ balāmoḍieti balātkāre deśīpadam/ locanābhyāṃ vijṛmbhamāṇavismayastimitadīrghaparyantapariyantraṇāvilāsollasitabhrūlatāvibhāvitānaṅgaśarasaṃrambhavibhramavidagdham/ vijṛmbhamāṇo'dbhutarasatāmāpādyamāno yo vismayastena stimitau niṣpandīkṛtau dīrghāvāyatau ca yau locanaprāntau tayoḥ pariyantraṇā niyamanaṃ saiva vilāsastenollasitābhyāṃ bhrūlatābhyāṃ vibhāvito yo'naṅgaśarasaṃrambhaḥ sa eva vibhramastena vidagdhaṃ yathā syāttatheti vigrahaḥ/ avalokayanpratyakṣīkṛta eva/ tasya sābhilāṣadṛṣṭibhireva tvayyanurāgo vibhāvyata iti bhāvaḥ// mālatī/ pariṣvajya/ svamanobhavavṛttāntapratyakṣasaṃvādapradarśanajanito harṣaḥ pariṣvaṅge nimittam/ āmeti saṃvāde/ aṅgīkāra iti kecit/ mama manasi vartamānameva tvayāpyuktaṃ tadevameva khalvityarthaḥ/ priyasakhi, kiṃ tāvattasya svābhāvikā eva te muhūrtasaṃnidhāyino janasya tatkṣaṇamātrasaṃnihitasya pāṃsurapādasya/ svabhāvaviśeṣānabhijñasyeti yāvat/ vipralambhayitṛkā vañcayitāro vilāsāḥ/ āhosvidyathā priyasakhī saṃbhāvayati/ kiṃ tasya sahajaireva vilāsairaparicayādvayaṃ vipralabdhāḥ, kiṃ vā tvaduktarītyānurāgasūcakā eveti bhāvaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ niścityāpi bhāvaguptaye kevalamiyaṃ saṃdigdha iti vihasya niścite'pyarthe mamāpi kimācchādyata iti sāsūyam/ āheti śeṣaḥ/ sāvajñamityavyayaṃ praṇayakrodhe/ tvamapi svabhāvenaiva tasminnavasare'saṃgītakaṃ strīgānaśūnyam/ ``strīṇāṃ raṅgopaviṣṭānāṃ gānaṃ saṃgītakaṃ matam'' iti vacanāt/ nartitāsi/ yadi tasya sahajavilāsaistvaṃ vañcitāsi tarhi tvadīyā api vilāsāḥ svābhāvikāstadvañcikā eva/ na tvanurāgaprabhavā iti/ pratibanditaḥ prakṛtārthanirdhāraṇametat// mālatī/ itaḥ paraṃ gopanāśakteḥ salajjam/ humiti praśne/ tatastataḥ vaktavyaśeṣaṃ brūhītyarthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ tataḥ pratinivartamānayātrājanasaṃkulenāntarite tasminmādhave mandārikāgṛhamupagatāsmi/ tasyāścitraphalakaṃ prabhāte hastīkṛtaṃ nyāsīkṛtamāsīt// mālatī/ kiṃnimittam// lavaṅgikā/ tāṃ khalu mādhavānucaraḥ kalahaṃsakaḥ kāmayate/ sā tasya darśayiṣyatīti hetostasyā haste dattamāsīdityarthaḥ/ tato mandārikā priyanivedikā saṃvṛttā// mālatī/ svagatam/ nūnaṃ tenāpi kalahaṃsakenaitatpraticchandakamātmanaḥ prabhordarśitaṃ bhaviṣyatīti/ kathamanyathā mandārikā priyanivedikā syāditi bhāvaḥ/ prakāśam/ sakhi, kimidānīṃ te priyam/ yanmandārikayā niveditamityarthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ etatkhalu saṃtāpitasya tvadaprāptyā saṃtapyamānasya tava saṃtāpakāriṇo durlabhe tvayyeva viṣaye yo manorathāveśastena yo duḥsahāyāsastena dahyamānasya cittasya kṣaṇamātranirvāpayitṛkaṃ tava praticchandakam/ mamedānīṃ priyamiti śeṣaḥ/ tvayā yathā taddarśanamantareṇa jīvitumapārayantyā citradarśanenāpi prāṇāndhārayituṃ praticchandakaṃ likhitaṃ tenāpyevameva tvatpratikṛtirlikhitetyetadeva me priyam/ anyonyānurāgaprakarṣasya prāptibījasya niścayāditi bhāvaḥ// mālatī/ saharṣocchvāsam/ samāśvāsaḥ priyasya svasyāmatyantānurāganiścayahetoḥ/ tallikhitātmapratirūpasya darśanāt/ harṣāśvāsau ``manaḥprasādo harṣaḥ syādiṣṭaprāptistavādibhiḥ'' iti lakṣaṇāt/ kvacit ``saharṣasādhvasam'' iti pāṭhaḥ/ tatra kumārījanānucitaparapuruṣānurāgapratyāyakametatkasya vā bandhujanasya dṛkpathaṃ gacchediti sādhvasahetuḥ aho, idānīmapi hṛdayasya me'nāśvāsaḥ/ yenedamapyāśvāsanaṃ vipralambha iti saṃbhāvyate/ kathamakṣarāṇyapi/ dṛśyanta iti śeṣaḥ/ jagatīti vivṛtaṃ prāk/ sānandamiti/ ślokārthānusaṃdhānamānandahetuḥ/ mahābhāga, sadṛśaṃ khalu te nirmāṇasya vacanamadhuratayā/ nirmāṇe vacane ca tulyaiva madhuratetyarthaḥ/ darśanaṃ punastatkālamanoharaṃ pariṇāme'saṃdarśanasamaye dīrghasaṃtāpadāruṇaṃ ca/ dhanyāḥ khalu tāḥ strī[stri]yo yāstvāṃ na prekṣante/ puṇyavaśātkathaṃcitpaśyantyo'pyātmano vā hṛdayasya prabhavanti/ yāstvāṃ na paśyanti tāḥ svajanmanaiṣphalyāddṛṣṭvāpi yāstvanyamanaskāḥ paśukalpatayā nādaraṇīyatvādubhaye'pyadhanyā iti vyatireko dhvanyate// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, evamapyāśvāsite'pi nāsti ta āśvāsaḥ// mālatī/ kathamiva/ kimidānīmananyathāsiddhamāśvāsananimittaṃ dṛṣṭamiti tayaiva vācayitumavagate'pi praśnaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ yasya kāraṇāttvamutkhaṇḍitabandhanamucchinnamūlaṃ kaṅkellipallavamaśokapallavamiva hṛdayaṃ dhārayantī klāmyannavamālikākusumaniḥsahātisukumārā kusumāyudhena parihīyase parihānīṃ[niṃ] tanutāṃ prāpyase, so'pi jñāpito bhagavatā manmathena saṃtāpasya duḥsahatvam/ tallikhitaślokavaśādbhavatyā iva tasyāpi gāḍhānurāgapratītirevāśvāsananimittamiti bhāvaḥ// mālatī/ sakhi, kuśalamidānīṃ tasya mahāprabhāvasya bhavatu/ mama punaḥ sudurlabha āśvāsaḥ/ mama punaridānīmeva caramāvasthā bhavatu/ madavasthāsadṛśāvasthatvaṃ tasya mā bhavatu/ tādṛśasya tribhuvanālaṃkārasya durlabhajanmatvāditi bhāvaḥ/ sāsram/ vedanāyā atiduḥsahatvātsadyo vipattiśaṅkayā sāsratvam/ saṃskṛtamāśrityeti/ yadāhuḥ- ``veśyānāmapsarasāmantaḥpurikāsu cāgryamahiṣīṇām/ saṃskṛtamapi yogyaṃ syātprastāvenāgataṃ kiṃcit''/ iti// mālatyāścāntaḥpurikāsvantaḥpātaḥ/ ata eva bhartṛdārikāvyavahāro'pi/ manoroga iti/ viṣamivāviratamanavarataṃ visarpansarvadhātuṣu vyāpnuvan/ pramāthī tīvraḥ sadyovipattikṛt/ ayaṃ mānmatho manorogo manmathavyathālakṣaṇo vyādhiḥ/ nirdhūmaṃ dhūmarahitaṃ yathā vidhutaḥ saṃdhukṣitaḥ pāvaka iva jvalati/ tathā garīyānsāṃnipātiko jvara iva pratyaṅgaṃ sarveṣvapyavayaveṣvita ito'ntarbahiśca hinasti pīḍayati/ tato māṃ dustarādasmādvyasanāttrātuṃ tāto'patyasnehaparavaśo'pi tāvannarapatimanorañjanapravaṇatvānna prabhavati/ pumapatyeṣvadhikatvādvātsalyasya pituḥ kanyakājane mātā niratiśayapremanighnahṛdayā yadyapi bhavati tathāpyambā bhartṛparādhīnā na māṃ rakṣituṃ samarthā/ jananyapekṣayāpi sahapāṃsukrīḍanādibhiḥ samaduḥkhasukhāḥ svamanonirviśeṣāḥ sakhyastathāpi bhavatī madviṣaye'svatantratvānna rakṣaṇakṣamā/ kiṃ tu yadi rakṣaṇe samarthaḥ syātsa evaiko'mṛtasaṃjīvanauṣadhiriva mādhavaḥ/ sa ca mama durlabha eveti maraṇamevedānīṃ śaraṇam, nānyatkiṃcidityarthaḥ/ viṣajvalanajvarākhyopamānatrayopādānaṃ naikenāyamupamātuṃ śakyata ityatiśayasūcanārtham/ saṃjvaronmādau cānenoktau/ tallakṣaṇaṃ prāgevoktam//1//

 lavaṅgikā/ evametat/ pratyakṣasaukhyadāyinaḥ parokṣaduḥkhaduḥsahāḥ sajjanasamāgamā bhavanti/ nedamāścaryaṃ yadatra saṃtāpaḥ/ kuta ityatra hetumāha - api ceti/ priyasakhīti/ yasya vātāyanāntaramuhūrtadarśanenāpi susamiddhahutavahāyamānapūrṇacandrodayā niṣkaruṇakāmavyāpārasaṃśayitajīvitā te śarīrāvasthā/ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ/ tasyaiva sāṃprataṃ saviśeṣadarśanenādya saṃtapyasa iti kimatra bhaṇitavyam/ tadatra priyasakhi, ślāghanīyaṃ durlabhaviṣaye yo manorathastasya phalaṃ tatsiddhirūpaṃ jīvalokasya prāṇivargasyaitadeva yadgurukānurāgasya garīyasaḥ parasparapremabandhasya sadṛśo'nurūpo mahābhāgavallabhasamāgama ityetāvajjānīmo vayam/ śarīraviyogānantaraṃ kasya kiṃ vā phalamiti kevalaṃ śivo veda/ jīvatāṃ punaḥ svajanmasāphalyamabhilaṣatāṃ prāgbhavīyasucaritātirekāttulyānurāgo mahābhāgaśca praṇayijano yadi labhyate, tarhi yathākathaṃcidapi tatsamāgama eva ślāghyaṃ manorathaphalamityeṣa sakalajanasaṃmato rāddhānta ityarthaḥ// mālatī/ sakhi, dayitamālatījīvite sāhasopanyāsini, apehi/ tava tāvanmadīyaṃ jīvitameva dayitaṃ priyatamam/ tena majjīvalobhena kanyakājanaviruddhamatyantagurubandhuvargānaṅgīkṛtāśayaṃ kulakalaṅkaṃ svayameva svapradānasāhasaṃ upanyasyasi/ naivamupadeṣṭavyam/ tataḥ sāhasopadeśādapasareti niṣedho vācyaḥ/ tavaiva me jīvitaṃ dayitam, na tu gurūṇāmanyeṣām/ tasmāttvayaiva prāṇasakhyā mama mano yathā śaṅkāśūkavihīnaṃ bhavati tathopadeṣṭavyamiti vidhirvyaṅgyaḥ/ sāsramiti/ snehātkevalaṃ tvayā yadyapi sāhasopadeśaḥ kriyate tathāpyupadeśamātreṇa na tatphaliṣyati/ kevalaṃ gurujanājñātikramaṇasāhasādhyavasāyaḥ kṛto bhavet/ tato mamāvinayasya phalaṃ mayaivānubhoktavyam/ kimatra tvayā kartavyamiti rudantyāhetyarthaḥ/ athavā/ ahameva vāraṃvāraṃ bahukṛtvo vilokayantī/ tamityadhyāhāraḥ/ palāyamānaṃ satpratiṣṭhāpitaṃ punarnirudhya sthāpitaṃ yaddhīratvaṃ tasyāvaṣṭambho'valambanaṃ yasya tādṛśenātmano hṛdayena dūraṃ vilīyamānalajjātvena durvinayalaghvyatrāparādhyāmi/ kulakumārījanamaryādāmatilaṅghya bahuśastamavalokayantyā mama naitadvyasanaṃ garīya iti bhāvaḥ/ vilokayantīti cakṣuḥprītiḥ, palāyamāneti cittāsaṅgaḥ, dūraṃ vilīyamāneti trapānāśaśca kathita iti draṣṭavyam/ tathāpi priyasakhi/ jvalatvityādi/ rātrau rātrau sarvāsu śuklakṛṣṇapakṣarātriṣu madvairātparityaktasvīyakālaniyamo'ta eva sarvadākhaṇḍakalo virahijanajīvitendhananicayajājvalyamānasaṃpūrṇakalācchalajvālājālajaṭilaḥ śaśī viprayuktābalājanavadhapātakenaiva śaśākhyena dhūmenāṅkito viparītalakṣaṇayā śiśirakarādivyapadeśyo dahano jvalatu/ yathecchaṃ māmevendhanaṃ dahatu/ tameva dahanamādāya madana eva dahatu/ ubhau caitau mṛtyoḥ pareṇa maraṇādadhikaṃ kiṃ vā vidhāsyataḥ kariṣyataḥ/ sa ca mṛtyurmayaivāṅgīkṛta ityaṅgīkṛtā glānirdoṣāyeti bhāvaḥ/ mama punaḥ ślāghyo nikhilajanaślāghanīyaguṇastāto dayitaḥ/ nirmalakulaprasūtā jananī dayitā/ amalinamiyantaṃ kālaṃ kalaṅkaleśenāpi vivarjitaṃ kulaṃ dayitam/ na punarayaṃ hṛdayavartijīvitādapi spṛhaṇīyatvāttato'pi prathamaparigaṇanīyo mādhavākhyo jano dayitaḥ/ tathā taṃ vinā varākaprāyaṃ tatparityāgasāhasavyavasāye'pyavilīyamānatayātikṛtaghnam/ ata eva paścādupāttaṃ jīvitamapi na dayitamiti vācyo'rthaḥ/ bhāvastu bahudhā parisphurannapi vistarabhayānna likhitaḥ sahṛdayairūhanīyaḥ//2//

 lavaṅgikā/ svagatam/ atredānīṃ ka upāyaḥ/ mayā sūcitaṃ sāhasopadeśaṃ na gṛhītavatī, saṃnihitā cāntimā daśā/ kimatra vidheyamityarthaḥ// atredānīṃ ka upāya iti vicārayantyā lavaṅgikāyā upaśrutivyājenopāyamivopadiśantī nepathyārdhapraviṣṭā pratihārī vadati - eṣā bhagavatī kāmandakīti// atredānīṃ ka upāya iti saṃbandhapratītyāvagata iti noktam/ ata eva mālatīlavaṅgike pṛcchataḥ - kiṃ bhagavatī saṃdiśati/ āgacchatītyarthaḥ// pratihārī/ bhartṛdārikāṃ draṣṭukāmāgatā - ubhe/ kiṃ tato vilambyate/ bahireva/ bhavatyeti śeṣaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ svagatam/ susamāhitaṃ khalu jātam/ bhadraṃ saṃpannaṃ yata idānīmeva bhagavatī samāgatetyarthaḥ// kāmandakī - sādhvityādi/ ubhayalokāviruddhamubhayorlokayorihalokaparalokayoraviruddhaṃ chaloktirūpatayā satyānṛtātmakam/ tathā hi sarvo hyasmatkuṭumbo mahārājasya parikaraḥ/ tena nijasya svakīyasya yatheṣṭaviniyogārhasya kanyakājanasya devaḥ prabhavatīti rājacittānurañjanenaihikasukhahetutvādihalokāviruddhatvam/ nijasyaiva kanyakājanasya prabhavati devaḥ/ na tu madīyasyeti satyasyaivoktatvātpūrvapravṛttasuhṛtsaṃbandhapratijñābhaṅgapratyavāyaparihārācca paralokāviruddhatvam/ ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - yadi rājñā yācito na dāsyāmīti mukhato niṣedhāddṛṣṭena rājājñollaṅghanadoṣeṇa daṇḍanīyo bhavet/ atha yadi rājadākṣiṇyena dadāmīti niścitya vadetsuhṛtsaṃbandhapratijñānirvāhāya paramukhapravṛttyā kṛtavivāhodyogaḥ sannanyathābhāṣaṇadoṣeṇa pratyavāyaḥ syāt/ evaṃ tu cchalena vadannubhayalokaviruddhamuktavāniti/ yadvā ubhayorlokayorvyavahārayoḥ kṛtrimavivāhanandanānusaraṇarūpayoraviruddham/ yadvā ubhayorlokayorjanayormādhavanandanavargyayoraviruddhamiti/ dṛṣṭasāmagrīmuktvā daivānukūlyamapyastītyāha - api ceti/ vyatikaro vinimayaḥ/ mālatīlikhitacitraphalakasya mādhavena darśanaṃ mādhavagrathitabakuladāmno mālatīkaṇṭhābharaṇatvamityevaṃ vyatikaraḥ parivṛttiḥ/ pramodaṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ harṣamullāsayati/ itaretarānurāgaprakarṣahetutvātsa eva ceha bījamityāha - itareti/ parārdhyaṃ śreṣṭham/ nirbandha āsaktiḥ/ ṛddhirmaṅgalodgamaḥ// avalokitā/ eṣā mālatī// kāmandakī - nikāmamityādi/ atyarthaṃ kṛśāṅgī/ kārśyaṃ nāma madanāvasthā darśitā/ sarasakadalīgarbhavatsubhageti mārdavaṃ saukumāryaṃ pāṇḍutā kārśyaṃ ca prakāśyate/ evamatikṛśatve'pi kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ kalāmātraśeṣā śaśino mūrtiriva nayanānandakāriṇī/ madanadahanena yadudbhavo dāhastena vidhurāṃ vihvalāmavasthāmāpannā prāptā/ kalyāṇī maṅgalalakṣaṇopetā/ iyaṃ no'smākaṃ mano ramayatyasmadvyāpārānukūlamādhavānurāgaprakarṣasūcakāvasthāśālitvādāhlādayati kampayati ca/ duḥsahamanmathavyathādūyamānatayā sukumāratayā ca kadācidaniṣṭāśaṅkayodvejayati cetyarthaḥ//3//


parīti/ pāṇḍupāṃsulau kriyādveṣeṇa kuṅkumānulepanādisaṃskāraśūnyatayā dhūsarau kapolau yasya tādṛśamānanaṃ dadhatyapi kathamiyamatiśayena manoharatvaṃ prāptā citramidam/ athavā nedamāścaryaṃ yato ramaṇīyajanmani sahacarasaundaryaśālini jane paribhramaṃstatra vyāpriyamāṇo lalitaḥ sukumāro mānmatho vidhiravasthāviśeṣo vijayate sarvotkarṣeṇa prakāśate/ sahajasaundaryaśālināṃ manmathavikāro'pi śobhātiśayaṃ puṣṇātītyarthaḥ//4//

 niyataṃ niścitam/ yataḥ saṃbhogānubhāvā dṛśyanta iti bhāvaḥ/ tānevāha - tathā hyasyā ityupaskāraḥ/ nīvītyādi/ nīvī nāmāntarīyanivasanagranthirucchayākhyaḥ/ tatsaṃbandhasyocchvasanaṃ ślathatvam/ upalabhyata ityadhyāhārya kriyāsaṃbandhaḥ sarvatra draṣṭavyaḥ/ bāhyasaṃbhogānantaraṃ ratārambhasamaye krameṇa kucakalaśadormūlanābhipradeśasaṃcāriṇaḥ kāñcīdāmākarṣaṇavyagrasya priyatamakarasya bhāvanayā tatsparśasukhanirbharodbhinnanitambabimbavisraṃsamānanīvīyamupalakṣyata iti bhāvaḥ/ ratārambhavidhānaṃ ratirahasye yathā - ``alikacibukagaṇḍaṃ nāsikāgraṃ ca cumbanpunarupahitasītkaṃ tālu jihvāṃ ca bhūyaḥ/ kalitalalitanābhīkakṣavakṣoruhoruḥ ślathayati dṛḍhadhairyaḥ kṣobhayitvātha nīvīm''// iti/ adharaspandanamityanena tritayamapi tadetatkanyakāyāṃ prayojyamiti kanyakājanavihitasya vikuñcitasphurītaghaṭitākhyacumbanatrayasya madhye sphuritaṃ nāma cumbanamuktam/ tallakṣaṇaṃ ratirahasye - ``sphuritamadhimukhāntanyastamoṣṭhaṃ jighṛkṣuḥ sphuradadharapuṭābhyāṃ yatra gṛhṇāti bhartā'' iti/ dorviṣādo bhujalatāśaithilyam/ anena nāyakasya ratitantracaturatayā pūrvameva nāyikāyā ratibhāvaprāptiruktā/ yadāha - ``snigdhatā vapuṣi mīlanaṃ dṛśormūrcchanā ca ratilābhalakṣaṇam''/ iti/ svedaḥ sāttvikabhāvaviśe ratijanyaḥ/ yadāha - ``vapurjalodmasvedo ratigharmaśramādibhiḥ'' iti// cakṣuścāsyā masṛṇaṃ mukulamākekaraṃ snigdhamugdhaṃ ca/ ``masṛṇamadhuram'' iti kvacitpāṭhaḥ/ sukhapāravaśyādupajāyamānadṛṣṭivikāraviśeṣā masṛṇādayaḥ/ tatrākekaradṛṣṭiryathā - ``ākuñcitapuṭāpāṅgasaṃgatārthanimeṣiṇī/ muhurvyāvṛttatārā ca dṛṣṭirākekarā smṛtā//'' madhuraṃ yathā - ``śītalīkriyate tāpo yena tanmadhuraṃ smṛtam''/ iti/ masṛṇamukulasnigdhamugdhānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ stimitālaseti ślokadvayavyākhyānāvasare prāgevoktam// gātrasya stambho rāgavaśānniśceṣṭatvaṃ sāttvikabhāvaḥ/ yathā - ``stambhaśceṣṭāpratīghāto bhayarāgamadādibhiḥ'' iti/ stanamukulayorullaṅghitaḥ prabandhaḥ prakṛṣṭo bandhaḥ prabandhaḥ sthairyaṃ yena tādṛśaḥ/ prakampo vepathuḥ sātvikabhāvaḥ/ yathā - ``rāgarogabhayādibhyaḥ kampo gātrasya vepathuḥ'' iti/ gaṇḍābhoge vistāriṇi kapolatale pulakapaṭalaṃ romāñcaḥ sāttvikabhāvaḥ/ yathā - ``rāgarogāṅgarāgādyai romāñco romavikriyā'' iti/ mūrchanā saṃjñāstaimityam/ pralayo nāma sāttvikabhāvaḥ/ yathā - ``astendriyatvaṃ pralayaḥ sukhaduḥkhabhayādibhiḥ'' iti/ astendriyatvamindriyavyāpāroparamaścetanā ca vartate/ tena nātyantikaḥ pralaya iti bhāvaḥ/ evamanubhāvanibandhaparipoṣātsamucitavyabhicāryālambanapratīteśca saṃbhogaśṛṅgāro vyāpāraḥ//5//

 lavaṅgikā/ cālayati/ priyasaṃbhogānubhavanimīlitanetrāṃ bodhayatītyarthaḥ/ mālatī/ bhagavati, vande// kāmandakī/ phalasya tādṛśavarasamāgamalakṣaṇasya// lavaṅgikā/ bhagavati, etatpavitramāsanam/ ``viviktam'' iti pāṭhe śuddhamityarthaḥ// mālatī/ kuśalaṃ bhagavatyāḥ// kāmandakī/ niḥśvasya/ kuśalamiva// lavaṅgikā/ svagatam/ prastāvanā khalveṣā kapaṭanāṭakasya/ kapaṭamasadṛśavarapradāne vārtopanyāsacchalenodvegamutpādya mālatīmanasi kṛtrimapariṇayādhyavasāyadṛḍhīkaraṇaṃ tadeva nāṭakam/ tasyaiṣā niḥśvāsasahakṛtā kuśalamiveti vāstavakuśalābhāvoktiḥ prastāvanā/ āmukhamityarthaḥ/ niḥśvāsevaśabdadyotitāṃ tasyā vaiyākulīmeva sphorayituṃ tāṃ pṛcchati/ prakāśam/ gurukabāṣpabharastambhamantharitakaṇṭhapratilagnanirgamamanyādṛśamivādya bhagavatyā vacanam/ tatkimidānīmudvegakāraṇaṃ bhaviṣyati// kāmandakī/ cīrairjīrṇavastrakhaṇḍairnirmitasya cīvarasya kanthāyāḥ tatparigrahopalakṣitasya/ parivrājakasyeti yāvat/ viruddho bhavādṛśena saṃsārijanena sahāyaṃ paricaya eva/ udveganimittamityarthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ kathamiva/ asmatparicayaḥ kathamudvegahetustadvaktavyamityarthaḥ// kāmandakī/ ayīti saṃbodhane/ tvamapi/ tvayā khalu prāṇasakhyā sarvaṃ mālatyā iṣṭamaniṣṭaṃ ca vicāraṇīyam/ tata īdṛśamaniṣṭamidānīmapi kathaṃ tvayā na jñātamityarthaḥ/ idamiti/ madanasya jayaśīlamāyudhamakṛtrimavilāsāspadamidaṃ mālatyāḥ śarīram/ anucitāya yena kenacidapi kulaśīlarūpayauvanādinā varaguṇena saṃbandhāyogyāya varāya yatsaṃpradānaṃ rājadākṣiṇyādbhūrivasunā cikīrṣataṃ tena śocyaṃ duḥkhajanakam/ ata eva samānurāgasadṛśavarasaṃprayogābhāvācca viphalaguṇātiśayaṃ bhaviṣyatīti hetorbālyādetasyāṃ duhitṛnirviśeṣaṃ kṛtavātsalyāyāṃ mahānudvega ityarthaḥ//6//

 mālatī/ vaicitryaṃ vailakṣaṇyam// lavaṅgikā/ astyetadyannarendravacanānurodhena nandanasya pratipannā mālatīti sakalo jano'mātyaṃ jugupsata iti/ sarvalokajugupsitatvena pitā bhūrivasuḥ parityājya iti bhāvaḥ// mālatī/ svagatam/ kathamupahārīkṛtāsmi rājñastātena/ yathā durgāyai paśūpahāraṃ datvā kaścitsvābhilaṣitaṃ sādhayati, tathā svakāryamātrapareṇāpatyasnehavihīnena tātena kathaṃ maraṇāyaiva rājādhīnāhaṃ kṛtetyarthaḥ/ yadvā saṃnihitavidūṣakagrāhyaṃ phalādikamivopāyanaṃ kathaṃ kṛtāsmītyarthaḥ// kāmandakī/ guṇāpekṣeti/ kularūpavayovidyāprabhṛtivaraguṇāpekṣayā śūnyamidaṃ nandanāya sutāpradānarūpaṃ vaiśasanaṃ kathamupakrāntamamātyena/ athavā kuṭilanayaḥ svajanavyayenāpi svakṛtyanirvāhopāyastatra niṣṇātaṃ kuśalam/ ``ninadībhyāṃ snāteḥ kauśale'' iti mūrdhanyādeśaḥ/ mano yeṣāṃ teṣāmapatyasnehaḥ kutaḥ/ tato nedamāścaryamityarthaḥ/ apatyavyayenāpi kāryaṃ sādhayatastasya ko'bhiprāya ityata āha - idaṃ tviti/ nṛpaternarmasacivaḥ sa bhavānpūjyo nandanaḥ sutādānānmitraṃ bhavatviti yadidaṃ tvaidaṃparyaṃ tātparyam//7//

 mālatī/ rājārādhanaṃ khalu tātasya gurukam, na punarmālatī// lavaṅgikā/ yathā bhagavatyājñāpayati/ kuto'patyasneha ityādi yaduktaṃ tattathaiva/ anyathā tasminvare durdarśane kurūpe'tikrāntayauvane kimiti na vicāritamamātyena// mālatī/ svagatam/ hā, hatāsmi samupasthitānarthavajrapatanā mandabhāgyā// lavaṅgikā/ tatprasīda/ bhagavati, paritrāyasvāsmājjīvanmaraṇātpriyasakhīm// tavāpyeṣā duhitaiva/ tvamapyasyā mātṛtvādabhimatāya varāya dātumīśiṣa iti bhāvaḥ// kāmandakī/ ayi sarale, ṛjuprakṛtike/ daivaṃ ceti/ janakamapyullaṅghya yadi karoti daivameva karoti nānya ityarthaḥ/ daivānukūlyamasti cenmayā nopekṣyata iti bhāvaḥ/ yacceti/ yacchakuntalādibhiḥ kṛtaṃ satsāhasamiti kṛtvā nopadeṣṭuṃ yogyam/ śāstralokaprasiddhyādibhiryanniveditaṃ tatra yadi svayameva svātmahitaṃ vicārya pravartate tadā sāhāyyamātramasmābhiḥ kartuṃ śakyam/ na tu kartavyamityupadeṣṭuṃ śakyamityarthaḥ/ atha ca sāhasamityevaṃ nopadeṣṭavyam/ kiṃ tu svajīvitavyaye prāpta idameva yuktamityevopadeṣṭavyamityapi pratītiḥ/ anena mādhavasya samāgamānuguṇaprotsāhanaṃ bhedayati/ sarvathetyupaskāraḥ/ rājña iti/ rājñaḥ priyāya premabhājanāya suhṛde sauhārdaśāline/ priyasuhṛdoḥ kiṃcidbhedādbhedenopādānam/ tadyathā bhāvaprakāśe - ``satyavāgārjavaratirupakurvanpriyaṃ vadan/ bhajate yaḥ svayaṃ prītiṃ priyaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ// duḥkhe vipadi saṃmohe kāryakālātyaye'pi ca/ hitānveṣī ca hitakṛdyaḥ suhṛtso'bhidhīyate//'' iti/ tenātīva visrambhasthānatvaṃ sūcitaṃ na kevalaṃ mitramātram/ api tu sacivāya svatulyāya nandanāyātmajāṃ kāryāddhetordattvāmātyo nirvṛtimānbhavatu/ saṃpradhāraṇe loṭ/ durdarśanādevānarthahetunānena nandanena saha dhūmagraheṇa dhūmaketurapi darśanādevānarthaṃ sūcayati/ vimalā candrakaleva/ iyamapi ghaṭatāṃ niyaterdurlaṅghyatvāditi bhāvaḥ/ idaṃ ca saṃprasāraṇaṃ mālatyā udvegātiśayajananena svavidheyatānayanārtham//8//

 mālatī/ svagatam/ hā tāta, tvamapi mama nāmaivamiti jitaṃ bhogatṛṣṇayā/ tvamapyapatyavatsalo vivekī ca mama nāma nānyadīyasya janasya snehaikālambanāyā duhiturapyevaṃ jīvitanirapekṣaḥ/ bhogatṛṣṇayetyukte kenānyena bhogatṛṣṇā jetavyā/ tasmāttayā jitam/ akuṇṭhitaprasaratvaṃ prāptamityarthaḥ/ evaṃ nandanasya duṣṭatāmamātyasya ca svakāryaikaparatāmuktvānantaraṃ mādhavasya kularūpavaidagdhyaprakāśanenānukūlyaṃ vivakṣantyā kāmandakyā abhiprāyajñāvalokitā gamanakālanivedanavyājena prasaṅgaṃ saṃpādayati// ā[a]valokitā/ cirāyitaṃ bhagavatyā ciraṃ vilambaḥ kṛtaḥ/ nanu bhaṇāmyasvasthacitto mahābhāgo mādhava iti/ tvaritaṃ gantavyamiti śeṣaḥ/ kāmandakī/ idaṃ gamyate/ idamiti gamanakriyāviśeṣaṇam/ pratyakṣatvakathanena saṃnidhānasūcā

/65

nārthamidam// lavaṅgikā/ janāntikalakṣaṇaṃ tūktam/ ubhayorabhiprāyajñā lavaṅgikā kāmandakyā vivakṣāṃ mālatyāḥ śuśrūṣāṃ jñātvā śanairmālatyāḥ karṇe vadatītyarthaḥ/ sakhi mālati, sāṃprataṃ bhagavatyāḥ sakāśāttasya mahānubhāvasyodgamamutpattisthānaṃ kulaṃ viśuddhamiti yāvajjānīmaḥ// mālatī/ janāntikameva/ asti me kautūhalam/ asminnarthe tatpṛcchetyarthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ prakāśam/ ka eṣa mādhavo nāma, yasminbhagavatyevaṃ snehagurukamātmānaṃ dhārayati// kāmandakī/ aprastāvinī prastāvanānupayuktā/ mahatī bahuvaktavyasadbhāvādvistṛtā/ śīghraṃ mayā gantavyam/ tadidānīmavasarābhāvādaprastutatvācca kimanayā/ śuśrūṣātiśayotpādanārthamanādaroktiḥ// lavaṅgikā/ tathāpyākhyāya bhagavatī prasādaṃ karotu// kāmandakī - śrūyatāmiti/ samagrasya kṛtsnasya puruṣaprakāṇḍacakrasya puruṣaśreṣṭhasamūhasya cūḍāmaṇiścūḍāratnam/ śreṣṭha ityarthaḥ/ sātīrthyaṃ sahādhyayanam/ ``satīrthyāstvekaguravaḥ'' ityamaraḥ/ vyatikariteti/ śvetamānairdhavalībhavadbhiryaśobhirvyatikaritā jātavyatikarā digantā yeṣāṃ te/ vyatikaro miśrībhāvaḥ/ ūrjasvalānāṃ prabalānām/ rāśībhūtānāmiti yāvat/ sukṛtavilasitānāṃ bhāgadheyavijṛmbhaṇānām/ ``prakṛtivilasitānām'' iti pāṭhe svabhāvotkarṣāṇāṃ sthānamāspadam/ agaṇitamahimāno'paricchedyānubhāvā maṅgalānāmabhyudayānāṃ ketanaṃ cihnabhūtā vā yatra te tatra maṅgalamiti/ tādṛśā asminbhuvane kathamiva saṃbhavanti/ na kathaṃcidityarthaḥ/ vyatikariteti/ vyatikaro vyāptirmasṛṇaṃ vāsya saṃjāto yebhyo yeṣu diganteṣu/ cetayanti cetayamānāni/ ketanaṃ cihnaṃ maṅgalānāmabhyudayānāṃ yatra tādṛśāḥ puruṣāstatra maṅgalamityāśayaḥ/ ūrjasvalānāṃ tejasvināṃ śūrāṇāṃ sthānamutpattiḥ/ etena sahajaśūro mādhava iti bhāvaḥ/ ata eva śmaśāne mālatīvipannistāre kṛtanibandhano bhavitā/ śūrānurāgiṇyo hi yuvatayo bhavantītyapi darśitaṃ bhavatīti vyākhyānāntaram//9//

 mālatī/ sakhi, taṃ khalu bhagavatyā gṛhītanāmadheyaṃ sarvathā tātaḥ smarati/ svayaṃ devarātanāmāgrahaṇe mālatyā bhāviśvaśuratvavyavasāyo bījam// lavaṅgikā/ samaṃ kila tayorbhagavatyā gurusakāśādvidyādhigamaḥ kṛta iti tatkālavedino mantrayante// kāmandakī - tata ityādi/ tato'smāddevarātādudayagirerivaika eṣaḥ/ pitrorekaputratayātīva premapadatvamuktam/ sphuritāḥ prakāśā guṇā gāmbhīryādaya eva dyutayastābhiḥ sundaraḥ/ kalāvāṃścatuḥṣaṣṭikalābhijñaḥ/ iha jagati nayanavatāṃ cakṣuṣmanmātrasya sarvasyāpi prāṇijātasya mahotsavasya heturāhlādakārī/ lāvaṇyasaṃpa[pā]danenoktā/ bālaścandra iva bālacandraḥ/ prathamayauvanamanenoktam/ udiyāyodbhūtaḥ/ śleṣopamāgarbho rūpakālaṃkāraḥ//10//

 lavaṅgikā/ api nāma mādhavo bhavet/ yatra tavānurāgaḥ sa eva mādhava evaṃguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ saṃbhāvyata ityarthaḥ// kāmandakī - asāviti/ asau devarātanandano vidyābhiḥ śālata iti vidyāśālī śiśuḥ sannapi bhavanādvinirgatya saṃpratīhāyātaḥ saṃpūrṇaśaraccandravadanaḥ/ yasyāvalokanapradeśe/ yatra sthitvā so'valokyate tatretyarthaḥ/ nārīṇāṃ paurasyoṣitāmunmādataralairakuṇṭhitaprasarairlolaiśca kaṭākṣaiḥ puramidaṃ kuvalayitavātāyanaṃ saṃjātakuvalayagavākṣamiva bhavati/ pūrṇacandradarśanena kuvalayollāso hi yuktaḥ/ sakalavanitālocanīyalāvaṇyasya tasyābhilāṣavatī bhavatītyaho te saubhāgyamiti bhāvaḥ//11//

 tadatreti/ tvadanurāgāviṣṭa eva vidyābhyāsavyājenehāgatya vartata iti bhāvaḥ/ sa iti/ ya eṣa kathito devarātātmajaḥ sa eva mādhava ityasmābhirnirdiṣṭa ityarthaḥ// mālatī/ sakhi lavaṅgike, śrutaṃ mahākulaprasūto mādhava iti// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, kuto vā mahodadhiṃ varjayitvā pārijātasyodgamaḥ/ pārijātakalpastādṛśādeva mahodadhikalpādutpattimarhati/ na tu kṣudrapalvalakalpānnīcādityarthaḥ/ tadanena ``niḥśvasya/ kuśalamiva'' ityādinā ``sa eṣa mādhavo nāma'' ityantena saṃdarbheṇa prāsaṅgikanandanavṛttāntasūcanam, tadguṇahīnatvam, pituḥ svakāryaparatayāpatyasneharāhityam, śakuntalāvṛttāntasya niṣedhamukhena sūcanam, tulyasaṃvidhānena mādhavasamāgamopāyanivedanam, tadguṇaprakhyāpanena tatrānurāgadṛḍhīkaraṇaṃ cetyetāvato'rthasya kāmandakyā taṭasthayaiva saṃvidhānacāturyeṇa kṛtatvātpatākāsthānamidam/ yathā - ``āgantukena bhāvena yadabhivyaktikārakam/ vastuno bhāvyavasthasya patākāsthānakaṃ tu tat// atītānāgate kārye kathyete yatra vastunaḥ/ anyāpadeśavyājena patākā sthānakaṃ tu tat//'' iti/ ata eva kāmandakī vakṣyati - ``avalokite, sādhu saṃprati mayā taṭasthayaiva mālatīṃ prati nisṛṣṭārthadūtyasya laghūkṛto bhāraḥ'' iti// kāmandakī/ aho kālātipātaḥ/ prastutakathāvyāsaṅgavaśādatikrāntaḥ kālo'pi na vicārita ityarthaḥ/ kālātipātamevāha - saṃprati hi/ kṣipanniti/ vihagamithunānāṃ cakravākadvandvānāṃ madanakalahacchedena manmathasaṃgaraparyavasānena subhagāṃ śrāntyatiśayavinodakatayā manoharāṃ nidrāmudrāṃ svāpāvasthāṃ prathamataḥ pramukha eva kṣipannapasārayan/ saṃdhyāśaṅkhadhvaninā hi kokadvandvānāṃ niśāsaṃnidhānapratīterbhāvivirahaduḥsahatvānusaṃdhānādutpannavepathūnāmiti viśeṣaṇam/ tathā gurutareṣu harmyamadhyadeśeṣu pratidhvānena ghanatāṃ bahulatāṃ dadhāno'sau rājñāṃ bhavaneṣu saṃdhyāsamayanivedanārthaṃ kriyamāṇaḥ śaṅkhadhvaniranibhṛto'mandaḥ khe vicarati/ prasaratītyarthaḥ//12//

 mālatī/ apavārya valitagrīvaṃ parāvṛtya/ mahākulaprasūtaḥ sa mahābhāgaḥ/ tanmahākulīnatve sānandatvena sarvathā sa eva me patirnānya ityadhyavasāyaḥ sūcitaḥ/ suṣṭhu bhaṇitaṃ priyasakhyā kuto vā mahodadhiṃ varjayitvā pārijātasyodgama iti/ api nāma taṃ punarapi prekṣiṣye/ itaḥ paramekavāraṃ taddarśanaṃ vā labhyate cettāvatāpi kṛtārthāhamityutkaṇṭhātiśayo darśitaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ ita etena saṃjavanenāvatarāvaḥ/ saṃjavanaṃ catuḥśālam/ ``saṃjavanaṃ tvidam/ catuḥśālam'' ityamaraḥ// kāmandakī/ apavārya/ ``nisṛṣṭārthadūtīkalpastantrayitavyaḥ'' iti yaduktaṃ prāk, tadidānīṃ nirvyūḍhamityavalokitayā sahāpavāryāhetyarthaḥ/ laghūkṛto bhūyānkartavyāṃśaḥ sādhitaḥ/ itaḥ paramalpamevāvaśiṣṭamityarthaḥ/ kṛtāśaṅkaṃ kartavyāṃśaṃ ca viśeṣato nirūpayati - vara iti/ anyasminvare nandane doṣaḥ/ pitari vicikitsāptatvasaṃdehamūlaṃ vaimukhyaṃ ca janitaṃ yadvātsyāyanasūtraṃ kanyāsaṃprayuktakādhikaraṇe nisṛṣṭārthadūtīkṛtyanirūpaṇaprakaraṇe'nyavarapitṝṇāṃ ca doṣānabhiprāyaviruddhānpratipādayediti mātāpitrośca guṇānabhijñatāmanapekṣatāṃ lubdhatāṃ śaṭhatāṃ capalatāṃ bāndhavānāṃ pratipādayediti pūrvasūtrādanuṣajyate/ śakuntalāvṛttāntādītihāsodghāṭanaiḥ kāryapadavī kartavyasaraṇiśca kathitā/ yacca sūtram - ``yāścānyā api samānajātīyāḥ kanyāḥ śakuntalādyāḥ svarucyā bhartāraṃ prāpya modante sma, tāścāsyāḥ pradarśayet'' iti, tathā vatsasya mādhavasyābhijanato'bhijātyāddhetoryanmāhābhāgyaṃ mahābhāgadheyatvaṃ guṇataśca rūpavidyāguṇebhyo yanmāhātmyaṃ tatprasaṅgata evānyathā pakṣapātapratīteḥ stutaṃ yadapi sūtraṃ tasyāḥ svarucyā nāyakaguṇānbhūyiṣṭhānparivarṇayediti/ evaṃ tāvanmādhavānurāgo dṛḍhīkṛtaḥ/ itaḥ paraṃ paricayo visrambhayogyatvamanucite'pi vyāpāre viniyujyamānā jhaṭityaviśaṅkaṃ yena karoti tādṛśaḥ praṇayo vidheyaścetyetāvanmātramavaśiṣṭamityarthaḥ/ anena karaṇaṃ nāma mukhasaṃdheraṅgamuktaṃ bhavati/ yathā - ``karaṇaṃ prakṛtārambhaḥ'' iti//13//


jayantyamṛtaniṣyandirasotkarṣānurañjitāḥ/

madhurodāttagambhīrā bhavabhūtikavergiraḥ//

iti śrīmadbhāradvājakulatilakaparvatanāthayāyajūkanandanatripurārisūriviracitāyāṃ mālatīmādhavabhāvapradīpikāyāṃ dvitīyāṅkavivaraṇam//

anaṅgaśāsanavadhūmanaṅgojjīvanauṣadhīm/

abhaṅgurakṛpārajyadapāṅgāmāśraye śivām//

kaviridānīmaṅkāntaramārabhamāṇastadarthasūcanārthaṃ praveśakaṃ prastauti - tata ityādinā/ asaṃnihitāmapyavalokitāmākāśabhāṣitena saṃbodhayantī buddharakṣitā vadati/ ākāśabhāṣitaṃ yathā - ``apraviṣṭaiḥ sahālāpo bhavedākāśabhāṣitam'' iti/ avalokite, api jānāsi kva bhagavatī/ vartata iti śeṣaḥ// avalokitā/ buddharakṣite, kiṃ pramugdhāsi mohaṃ prāptavatyasi/ ``vismṛtāsi'' iti pāṭhe kartari niṣṭhā/ vismṛtavatītyarthaḥ/ yaḥ ko'pi kālo bhagavatyāḥ piṇḍapāraṇāvelāṃ visṛjya mālatīmanuvartamānāyāḥ/ bhaikṣyābhyavahārakālamapyullaṅghya mālatyanuvartanārthaṃ gatāyā bhagavatyā mahānkālo jāta ityarthaḥ// buddharakṣitā/ humiti smaraṇe/ tvaṃ punaḥ kva prasthitāsi// avalokitā/ ahaṃ khalu bhagavatyā mādhavasakāśamanupreṣitā/ saṃdiṣṭaṃ ca tasya śaṃkarapurasaṃbandhi kusumākarodyānaṃ kāmodyānaṃ gatvā kuñjanikuñjaparyantaraktāśokagahane tiṣṭheti/ kuñjaṃ mālākusumam/ gataśca tatra mādhavaḥ/ buddharakṣitā/ avalokite, kimiti mādhavastatrānupreṣitaḥ/ avalokitā/ adya kṛṣṇacaturdaśīti jananyā samaṃ mālatī śaṃkarapuraṃ gamiṣyati/ tata evaṃ kila saubhāgyaṃ vardhata iti devatārādhananimittaṃ svahastakusumāvacayamuddiśya lavaṅgikādvitīyāṃ mālatīṃ tadeva kusumākarodyānamāneṣyati/ bhagavatyeveti śeṣaḥ/ tato'nyonyadarśanaṃ bhaviṣyatīti/ tvaṃ punaḥ kva prasthitāsi// buddharakṣitā/ ahaṃ khalu śaṃkarapurameva prasthitayā priyasakhyā madayantikayāmantritāhūtā/ ato bhagavatyāḥ pādavandanaṃ kṛtvā tatraiva gacchāmi// avalokitā/ tvaṃ khalu bhagavatyā yasminprayojane madayantikāmakarandasaṃghaṭanarūpe niyuktā tatra ko vṛttāntaḥ/ vartata iti śeṣaḥ/ buddharakṣitā/ mayā khalu bhagavatyāḥ samādeśena tāsu tāsu visrambhakathāsvīdṛśastādṛśa iti makarandasyopari priyasakhyā madayantikāyāḥ parokṣānurāgastathā dūramāropito yathaivamasyā manoratho'pi nāma taṃ paśyāmīti// avalokitā/ sādhu buddharakṣite, sādhu/ ehi gacchāvaḥ// iti niṣkrānte// praveśakaḥ// athāvalokitāsūcitapraveśā praviśati kāmandakī/ kāmandakī - tatheti/ tathā tena prakāreṇa gurujanocitabhaktiśraddhayopalakṣitena vinayena kulakanyakājanocitasvabhāvena namrā prahvāpi mālatī mayopāyataḥ satatatatsamīpāvasthānam, vidagdhabhaṅgyā kuntalaviracanam, stanamukulagaṇḍaphalakeṣu citrapatralekhanam, sahākṣakrīḍānarmālāpairvinodanam, apūrvavastūpaharaṇamityevamādibahuprakāreṇopāyena katipayairalpairevāhobhiḥ sakhīṣu lavaṅgikādiṣu yo visrambhaḥ sādhvasalajjāśaṅkāparityāgena svamanogatasarvasvanivedanaṃ maduktasya jīvitavyayasyāpyaṅgīkaraṇaṃ kṣaṇamapi madvirahāsahiṣṇutvamityādirūpasnehotkarṣastena sevyatāmanurañjanīyatām/ tatra bhajanamanurañjanarūpameva nītā/ prāpitetyarthaḥ//1//

 visrambhameva darśayati - vrajatīti/ no'smākaṃ virahe vaicitryaṃ cittavaikalyam, manaḥkhedamiti yāvat/ vrajati gacchati/ saṃnidhau punaḥ prasīdati nirvṛtāntaḥkāraṇā bhavati/ rahasyekānte ramate cirakālaṃ sakhībhyo'pi yanna kathitaṃ tādṛśanarmarahasyanivedanaiḥ krīḍati/ prītyā vācaṃ dadāti priyameva sarvadā bhāṣate, na tu kadācidapyanyathā/ ``deyaṃ dadāti'' iti pāṭhe deyaṃ karpūrādikaṃ prītidānaṃ dadātītyarthaḥ/ tathānuvartata ānukūlyenābhyutthānāsanapradānādinopacarati/ tathā svasamīpānmaṭhaṃ prati gamanāvasare madīyakaṇṭhāsaṅgivalayīkṛtabāhupāśā māṃ bahuśo nirudhya sarvathā gantavyamiti mayā nirbadhyamānā sapadi pratyāvṛttiṃ tasminnai[nne]va ca kṣaṇe punarāgamanaṃ śapathairidānīmeva yadi nāgacchasi tarhi guruṃ te svahastena māritavatyasi mamānyathābhāvamācaritavatyasītyevamādibhiranekairanatikramaṇīyaiḥ pramāṇavacanaiḥ praṇamya māmevaṃ yācate/ prārthayata ityarthaḥ//2//

 idaṃ ceti vakṣyamāṇaṃ ca sādhīyo dṛḍhataraṃ pratyāśāyāḥ sarvathā maduktamasau kariṣyatītyevaṃrūpāyā nibandhanaṃ nimittam/ kiṃ tadityata āha - śākuntalādīniti/ anyaparairanyaḥ svavyatirikto janaḥ parastātparyagocaro yeṣāṃ tairmukhato'nyoddeśena pravṛttavadavabhāsamānairvastutastu svopadeśāyaiva pravṛttairvacobhiḥ prastāvitānmayā matpreritena vā sakhījanena prasañjitāñśakuntalāvṛntā[ttā]ntapramukhānitihāsavādāñśrutvā madutsaṅganiveśitāṅgī yatheṣṭaviniyogārhastavaiṣa janastadasya maraṇaṃ jīvitaṃ vā tvadekāyattamityātmānaṃ nivedayantīva madaṅkavinipātitaśarīrā cirakālaṃ cintayā kathaṃ mayā śakuntalādivatkartavyaṃ ko vātrābhyupāya iti vicāreṇa stimitatvaṃ niśceṣṭatāmeti prāpnoti/ ityetatsādhīyaḥ pratyāśānibandhanamityarthaḥ/ sa eṣa ślokadvayārtho ratirahasye dūtīvaśībhūtanāyikārūpavarṇanasamaye kathitaḥ/ yathā - ``evaṃ prayujyamāne lakṣayitavyastadīya ākāraḥ/ dṛṣṭvā bravīti sasmitamantika evopaviśati// pṛcchati bhojanaśayane cākhyānaṃ diśati rahasi vā milati/ niḥśvasa[si]ti jṛmbhayati ca svavittamasyai dadāti kiṃcidvā// yāntīmeṣyasi punariti vadati tathā sāmavādinī bhavatī//'' ityādi//3//

 taditi/ mādhavasya samīpa evottaramuparitanaṃ kāryajātamadyopakramiṣyāmaḥ// ``nepathya-'' ityādi ``punaḥ prekṣiṣye'' ityantaṃ gatārthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, eṣa khalu madhureṇa madhurasenārdrāṇāṃ bhṛśaṃ klinnānāṃ mañjarīṇāṃ kavalanameva kelistayā kalo'tyantamadhuro yaḥ kokilakulakolāhalastenākulitātsahakāraśikharāduttrāsenoḍḍīnānāṃ caṭulānāṃ cañcarīkanikarāṇāṃ vyatikareṇa vimardenoddalitadalānāmūrdhvaṃ vikasitapatrāṇāṃ karālānāṃ danturāṇām/ ``karālo danture tuṅge viśāle vikace'pi ca'' iti vacanāt/ campakānāṃ cāmpeyakusumānāmadhivāsena manoharaḥ/ marālasya samavṛttasya/ ``marālaṃ samavṛttaṃ syāt'' iti vacanāt/ jaghanapariṇāhasya nitambaprāgbhāgavistārasyodvahanabhāreṇa mantharaṃ mandaṃ tathorubhareṇa visaṃsthulaṃ viṣamaṃ skhalitaṃ yaccaraṇasaṃcaraṇaṃ pādagamanaṃ tenopanītaiḥ svedaśīkaraireva sudhābindubhirujjvalasya mugdhamukhacandrasya tvadīyasya candanāyamānaḥ śītalasparśo yasya sa tvāṃ pariṣvajati kusumākarodyānamārutaḥ/ taditaḥ parikramāvaḥ// ``gataśca tatra mādhavaḥ'' iti sūcito mādhavaḥ praviśati/ mādhavaḥ/ hanteti harṣe/ parāgatābhimukhamāgatā/ paretyupasargo'trābhimukhyadyotakaḥ/ āvirbhavantīti/ priyāyāḥ prathamamāvirbhavantī prakaṭībhavantī bhagavatī priyatamāyāḥ punardarśananairāśyātsmṛtaprāyaṃ mamāntaḥkaraṇaṃ dayitāgamanasūcakatayā socchvāsaṃ sajīvaṃ karoti/ kathamiva nidāghasaṃtaptasya mayūrasya vṛṣṭeḥ purastādāvirbhavantī saṃnihitāṃ vṛṣṭiṃ sūcayantī/ aciraprabhā vidyudivetyupamā/ tatra mādhavasya mālatīdarśanābhilāṣo dvitīyāṅkagatamālatīvṛttāntanandanavṛttāntavicchede prāpte punardarśanahetutvenācchedakāraṇatvādbinduḥ/ yathā - ``avāntarārthavicchede binduracchedakāraṇam'' iti/ atra ca tṛtīyacaturthāṅkābhyāṃ punaranyonyasaṃdarśānātmakāvāntaraprayojanasaṃghaṭitaikabhāvikathāṃśanirūpakābhyāṃ nirdiṣṭasya bindoścaturthāṅke ``tatsarvathā saṃgamanāya yatnaḥ prāṇavyayenāpi mayā vidheyaḥ'' iti vakṣyamāṇasya yatnasya samanvayādupajātam ``tadāsyatām/ kiṃcidākhyeyamākhyātukāmāsmi'' ityārabhya ``yadi tadviṣayo'nurāgabandhaḥ'' ityantena kāmandakīlavaṅgikābhyāṃ mādhavamālatyormadanavyathātiśayavarṇanena lakṣyasya, tata upari śārdūlavṛttāntanandanavivāhaniścayaprasaṅgābhyāmantarāyabhūtābhyāmantaritatayālakṣyasya ca parasparānurāgasya bījasyodbhāvanātmakastatrodbhāvayiṣyamāṇavilāsādyaṅgasamanvitaḥ pratimukhasaṃdhirnirūpyate/ tallakṣaṇaṃ ca - ``lakṣyālakṣyatayā bījavyaktiḥ pratimukhaṃ bhavet/ binduḥ prayatnādudayādaṅgānyatra trayodaśa//'' iti//4//

 diṣṭyā lavaṅgikādvitīyā mālatyapi/ āgacchatīti śeṣaḥ/ āścaryamiti/ indīvarākṣyā vadanamevāmalaḥ kalaṅkaśūnya industasya sāṃnidhyataḥ sāṃnidhyāddhetormama manasā svābhāvikanaimittikadravatvavirahitenāpi parvatasaṃbandhinā jātyena viśuddhajātiprabhavena candrakāntamaṇinena muhurjaḍimānaṃ jāḍyaṃ ``kriyāsvapāṭavaṃ jāḍyam'' ityuktarūpaṃ bhāvaṃ jalaprakṛtikatva[tvaṃ] caitya prāpya dravamayo vikāro dravībhāvaḥ saṃdhāryate/ tadetanmanaso dravībhavanamāścaryamityarthaḥ/ mahīdharasya candramaṇinetyanena svasya mahīdharasāmyapratīteracalavaddhīrasyāpi mama mano dravībhavatītyāścaryahetuḥ//5//

 saṃpratīti/ pūrvadṛṣṭāvasthāpekṣayā virahavighnānāṃ sphuṭībhāvātteṣāmātmaviṣayatvasya niścitatvācca mālatyatīva ramaṇīyā ca vartata ityarthaḥ/ yata ityadhyāhāryam/ jvalayatīti/ parimṛditā mlānimupagatā yā campakāvaliścāmpeyakusumamālā tasyā vilāsa iva vilāso vilasanaṃ kāntiprakarṣastena lulitairāvilaiḥ/ abhivyāptairiti yāvat/ alasaiḥ kriyāsvanunmukhairaṅgairmanobhavāgniṃ jvalayati dīpayati/ hṛdayaṃ madayati harṣaparavaśaṃ karoti cakṣuḥ kṛtārthayati draṣṭavyāvadhidarśanena saphalaṃ karotīti mannimittatayā niścitāṃ virahāvasthāmanubhavantīyamānandamadanavedanābhyāṃ mamāntaḥkaraṇamākulayatītyarthaḥ/ kārakadīpakālaṃkāraḥ//6//

 mālatī/ sakhi, amuṣminkuñjanikuñje vāmanatayā svahastāpaceyakusume latāgulme kusumānyapacinuvaḥ// mādhavaḥ - prathameti/ aśrutapūrvapriyāvacanaśravaṇena sphuranpulako romodgamo yasya tena mayā saṃprati meghapaṅktinavajalabindusaṃsecanena ya utpannaḥ kṣaṇo harṣastena baddhāni kuḍmalāni yena tasya kadambatarorḍambaro'vasthāviśeṣo'valambyate/ anukriyata ityarthaḥ/ atra pratīyamānasaṃbhogābhilāṣānuguṇyāttadvacanaśravaṇakautukena ca tadantopasarpaṇādiceṣṭāyā gamyamānatvādvilāso nāma pratimukhasaṃdheraṅgam/ yathā - ``samīhā ratibhogārthaṃ vilāsaḥ parikīrtitaḥ'' iti//7//

 lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, evaṃ kurvaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ aparimeyānyaparicchedyānyāścaryāṇi yatra tadācāryakaṃ kāmatantropadeśakauśalaṃ bhagavatyāḥ/ yatprasādādidānīmihaikākinīṃ kusumāvacayasaṃbhramasraṃsamānāṃśukatayā sphuṭaṃ vibhāvyamānasarvāvayavasaundaryaśālinīṃ priyatamāṃ yo'hamantarhita eva niḥśaṅkamavalokayannamṛtasarasi nimagna iva bhavāmītyarthaḥ/ anena pūrvaṃ dṛṣṭāyāḥ paścādantaritadarśanāyā mālatyāḥ punaranusaraṇātparisarpo nāmāṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``parisarpastu bījasya dṛṣṭanaṣṭānusarpaṇam'' iti// mālatī/ sakhi, teneto'pyaparasminnavacinuvaḥ/ anyasminpradeśe// atha kāmandakī pracchannasthāpitamādhavasamīpaṃ mālatīmupāyenānīya tato'nyatra jigamiṣantīṃ tāṃ nirudhya tatraiva sthāpayitumāha - kāmandakī - ayīti/ niḥsahā paryaṭanāsamarthā jātā/ kuta ityata āha - skhalayatīti/ he subhru/ subhrūśabdasyovaṅsthānatvena nadīsaṃjñāpratiṣedhādapi ``ambārthanadyorhrasvaḥ'' iti hrasvāprāptāvapi ``vimānanā subhru kutaḥ piturgṛhe'' ityādi mahākaviprayogadarśanātsādhutvaṃ subhrūśabdasya jñeyam/ khedaḥ puṣpāvacayajanitaśramaḥ/ ``manaḥśarīrayoḥ khedaḥ kriyātiśayataḥ śramaḥ'' iti vacanāt/ tvayi vallabhālokanena vallabhakarmakeṇa gāḍhābhilāṣāyāḥ kāminyā avalokanena sarvathā sarveṇāpi prakāreṇa tulyaṃ yathā syāttathā vilasati/ vallabhamavalokayantyā ye'nubhāvāste śramavaśāttvayyapi dṛśyanta iti narmagarbhoktiḥ/ atha ca latāntaritavallabhakartṛkālokanena tvadgocareṇa tulyamekakālatayaikālambanatayā ca saha vartata ityapi pratītiḥ/ kiṃca vallabhakartṛkāvalokanajanitā evaite'nubhāvā na śramaprabhāvāḥ, tena vilokayantaṃ vallabhamajānānāyā api te tatkaṭākṣasaṃsparśamātreṇa candrakāntasyeva candrakarasaṃparkamātreṇaivaṃvidho vikāra ityaho te gāḍhānurāgitvamiti pratipādyāṃ mālatīṃ pratyapahnavātiśayoktidhvaniḥ/ evamiyaṃ rakṣijanasahasrarakṣitāpyupāyena mayaiva punarvallabhālokanagocaratāṃ nītā, na tu bhavatyeti lavaṅgikāṃ prati dhvaniḥ/ tvadālokanamārgamiyamānītā/ tadvisrambhamavalokayeti taṭasthaṃ mādhavaṃ pratyasyāstvanurāgeṇa tvayā vilokyamānāyāstvāmatra saṃnihitamajānānāyā apyete vikārā ityaho te yuvatijanahṛdayākarṣaṇaprāvīṇyamiti tameva prativyajyata iti bahudhā vyaṅgyasyodbhāvayituṃ pratiślokaṃ pratipadaṃ ca śakyatve'pi vistarabhayādasmābhirupekṣitam/ kathaṃ tulyatetyapekṣāyāṃ śramapriyadarśanayoḥ kāryasāmyamāha - te vacanaṃ skhalayati gadgadaṃ karoti/ śramādapi skhalitavāgbhavati/ priyatamadarśanādapi gadgadākhyaḥ sāttvikabhāvo bhavati/ yathā - ``madapramodavrīḍādervākstambho gadgadaṃ viduḥ'' iti// aṅgamaṅgaṃ pratyavayavaṃ saṃśrayati/ sarveṣvavayaveṣvabhivyāpto vartata ityarthaḥ/ vallabhālokanapakṣe tu aṅgaṃ kartṛ/ aṅgamavayavāntaraṃ karmabhūtaṃ śramavaśātsaṃśrayati/ śramākhyaḥ sātvikabhāva uktaḥ/ tallakṣaṇaṃ tūktam/ asminpāṭhe vallabhālokanena tulyamiti sahoktinirvāhakaṃ vallabhālokanasya kartṛtvena prakrāntasya parityāgena kartrantarasvīkārāt/ na ca vyaṅgyatvādasyārthasyāyamadoṣa iti vācyam/ tulyamiti sahārthatulyaśabdabalādubhayorapi vācyatvena vyaṅgyatvābhāvāt/ vallabhālokanajanitānubhāvābhivyakto hi śṛṅgāro vyaṅgyaḥ/ na tu vallabhālokanavyāpāraḥ/ tasmāt ``sraṃsayati'' iti pāṭhaḥ śreyān/ aṅgamaṅgaṃ sraṃsayati srastaṃ karoti/ aṅgasādo'nenoktaḥ/ sa cobhayatra sādhāraṇa eva/ mukhacandra udbhāsanaśīlānsvedakaṇāñjanayati/ anena svedo dvayorapi sādhāraṇo darśitaḥ/ yathāha - ``vapurjalodgamaḥ svedo ratigharmaśramādibhiḥ'' iti/ netre ca mukulayati/ yadāha - ``niśvāsasvedasītkārāḥ saṃkoco mukhanetrayoḥ/ ete śramasyānubhāvāḥ'' iti/ pakṣāntare ca sukhapāravaśyānnetrasaṃmīlanaṃ stambhākhyaṃ sātvikabhāvaṃ vyanakti/ sahoktidīpakayoḥ saṃkaraḥ//8//

 lavaṅgikā/ śobhanaṃ bhagavatyājñaptam/ mādhavaḥ/ parihāso narmavacanam/ anena narmākhyamaṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``parihāsavaco narma'' iti/ kāmandakī - tadāsyatāmityādi/ kiṃcidākhyeyamalpameva vaktavyam// cubukaṃ vadanādhobhāgaṃ lajjāvanatamunnamayya/ āheti śeṣaḥ/ śṛṇvityādi/ ratirahasyavacanapratīkopādānaṃ kāmandakyā vakṣyamāṇasya nāyakavirahātiśayasya dūtīvyāpāratayā śāstrasiddhatāṃ sūcayitum/ yadāha - ``utpādya saumanasyaṃ brūyātsubhage śṛṇuṣva yaccitram/ citraṃ kimayaṃ vyatikaramasau yuvā kusumasukumāraḥ// dṛṣṭibhujaṅgīdaṣṭastava sakhi saṃdehamārūḍhaḥ/ śvasiti svidyati muhyati saṃtāpo'pyasya ko'pi durvāraḥ// tvanmukhacandrasudhārasamaprāpya prāṇitā nāsau/ svapne'pi tasya nedṛkkadāpi subhage vikāro'bhūt//'' iti/ subhage iti saṃbodhanaṃ tvadīyasaubhāgyamīdṛśaṃ yenaikākī pitrorekaputro mādhavaḥ kulālambanaṃ śravaṇamātrādevātrāgatya vartata ityādi sphuṭam// mālatī/ avahitāsmi// kāmandakī/ prasaṅgato'svasthaśarīro mādhava ityavalokitoktiprasaṅgāt/ manaso bandhanam/ badhnātyanyatra gamanaṃ niruddhya svaikāyattaṃ karotīti bandhanaṃ viśrāntisthānam/ premabhājanamityarthaḥ/ lavaṅgikā/ smarāmaḥ// kāmandakī - sa iti/ durmanāyamāno duḥkhitamanaska ivācaran/ paravāniti paravaśaḥ/ madanodyānayātretyanena prāthamikānyonyadarśanapratīteścakṣuḥprītiruktā/ durmanāyamāna iti cittāsaṅgaḥ/ tvāmuddiśyāsau madanena tāstā daśāḥ prāpita iti nivedayitumavasthāntarāṇyapyāha - yadindāviti/ indāvamṛtamayaśarīre sakalalokāhlādake praṇayini niratiśayapremabhājane jane vā yadayamānandaṃ nirvṛtiviśeṣaṃ na bhajate tato'yamatidhīro'pi dhairyavānapi viṣamamatiduḥsahamantastāpaṃ vyanakti dyotayati/ aratisaṃtāpākhyamavasthādvayamanenoktam/ tathā priyaṅguḥ phalinīlatā tadvacchyāmāṅgaprakṛtiḥ svābhāvikī dehakāntiryasya sa tādṛśo'pi cāpāṇḍu virahajanitapāṇḍimavattathāpi madhuraṃ mādhuryaguṇavat/ ``sarvāvasthāsu ceṣṭānāṃ mādhuryaṃ mṛdukāritā'' iti mādhuryalakṣaṇam/ kṣāmaṃ kṣāmamatyantakṛśībhūtaṃ vapurvahati/ na cāyaṃ vyādhinimittaḥ kārśyapāṇḍutādirityāha - ramaṇīyaḥ kāntimātrāvaśeṣeṇa manoharākāraśca bhavati/ kārśyaṃ nāmāvasthoktā/ anumānavirodhayoḥ saṃsṛṣṭiḥ//9//

 lavaṅgikā/ etadapi tasminnavasare bhagavatīṃ tvarayantyāvalokitayā niveditamāsīt/ yathāsvasthaśarīro mādhava iti/ kāmandakī - yāvaditi/ manmathakṛtonmāda unmathanaṃ karṇākarṇikayā śrutvāhamapyetadevaṃ niracaiṣam/ anyathā taddhairyacalanāyogāditi bhāvaḥ/ anubhavamiti/ eṣa iti mālatīmukhaṃ cubukadeśe gṛhītvā nirdiśati/ vadanendurasya prakṛtatvena buddhisthasya latāntaritayā ca purovartino mādhavasyānubhavaṃ samupāgamaddarśanaviṣayatāṃ prāptaḥ/ niyataṃ niścitaṃ sarvathāsyaivaṃvidhaviṣamadaśāvipākahetubhūtaṃ tvadānanendudarśanaṃ jātamiti sādhyate/ yato mahātmano'tigambhīrāśayasya stimitasya nistaraṅgasya mahodadheḥ paya ivotkalikātaralamutkaṇṭhayā kallolaiśca taralaṃ satkṣubhitamapahṛtadhairyamudvelaṃ ca saṃjātam/ yathā payonidheḥ pura indudarśanādeva kṣubhyati na tūṣṇīmevaṃ mādhavamanaḥkṣobhe'pi tvanmukhendudarśanameva nimittamiti kāryātkāraṇamanumīyata ityarthaḥ/ rūpakopamābhyāmaṅgāṅgibhāvena saṃkīrṇamanumānamalaṃkāraḥ//10//

 mādhavaḥ - aho iti/ upanyāsasya pratipādanaprakārasya śuddhiḥ pūrvāparavirodhādidoṣarāhityam/ mahattvasyāropaṇe mayyavidyamānasyāpi samudrasāmyasaṃpādanādhyārope yatno nibandhanam/ athavā naitādṛśānāmidamāścaryam/ kuta ityata āha - śāstra iti/ śāstre pratiṣṭhā śaṅkāśūkarahitasamyagjñānarūpā pariniṣṭhitiḥ/ sahajaḥ svābhāviko bodho'nabhyasteṣvapi sūkṣmaprameyarahasyeṣu svārasikaprasaraṇaśālinī prajñā/ prāgalbhyaṃ prauḍhoktivaicakṣaṇyam/ ``niḥsādhvasatvaṃ prāgalbhyaṃ prayogeṣvapi sarvataḥ'' ityuktarūpaṃ vā/ tathābhyastāḥ prayatnasaṃpādyā guṇāḥ prasādamādhuryādayo yasyāḥ sā vāṇī/ kālānurodho vacanāvasaranirīkṣaṇam/ pratibhānavattvaṃ tādātmikanavanavollekhaśālipratibhānavatvamityete guṇāḥ kriyāsu sādhayitumiṣṭāsu kāmadughā manorathapūrakāḥ/ tenaitadguṇagariṣṭhāyā bhagavatyā upanyāsaśudhdyādikaṃ kimāścaryamityarthaḥ//11//

kāmandakī - yata iti/ jīvitādudvijamānena tvayā vinā jīvanamaśakyamiti tatrodvegavatā virahijanasya duṣkaramapi na kiṃcinna kriyata iti/ kiṃtu sarvamapi jīvitadveṣiṇā tena kriyata evetyarthaḥ/ tadevāha - tathā hi/ dhatta iti/ mukulini korakite korakāṇāmeva tīkṣṇāgrasmarasāyakatvena manmathaśaradhībhūte/ raṇatkokila ityanena kaṃdarpasainyakalakalāyamānapikakulālāpavācālatayā yāvacchabdaḥ śrūyate tāvatsaṃnihitapradeśagamanamapyaśakyaṃ kimuta darśanamityuktam/ tasminbālatayā manohare sahakāre vṛkṣaviśeṣe dṛṣṭiviṣasarpa iva cakṣurdhatte/ bhavitavyaṃ bhavatviti dṛṣṭiṃ nipātayatītyarthaḥ/ kiṃ ca kesarakusumaparimalamilitasya vāyoścandanācalamandasamīraṇasya mārge samarasamayasamāpatannārācadhārāsaṃpātasaṃnihitaśātravāvasthitapradeśa iva gātraṃ kṣipati/ kimanena duḥkhānubhavanirdayaśarīreṇeti sakṛtsaṃparāmṛśyata iti/ tathā tāpapraśamanārthaṃ parijanasthāpitārdrakamalinīpatramātraprāvaraṇaḥ/ tāmyantī glānimupayāntī mūrtiryasyāsau/ kenāpi śiśiropacāreṇānirvāpaṇīyo'yaṃ tāpastadadya davadahanajvālāsaṃkleśa eva nirvāpaṇauṣadham ``uṣṇamuṣṇena śāmyati'' iti nyāyādityevaṃ jhaṭiti śarīratyāgasādhakatamatvena tatkālāpekṣite dāve premṇā pakṣapātena virahijanadāvānalajvālāyamānāṃścandrapādānmṛtyave tatkṣaṇaprāṇanirgamanāya śrayati/ tadevamanapekṣitajīvano'yamatiduṣkaraṃ kurvannapi sāhasaṃ tvāmeva sakalaviṣānalādisaṃstambhanauṣadhīmanavaratamantaḥkaraṇe dhārayannamaṅgalaśaṅkābhājanaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ/ atraikasminmṛtyulakṣaṇe kārye kusumitabālasahakāradarśanādikriyāṇāṃ bahvīnāṃ sādhanatvena samuccitatvātsamuccayālaṃkāraḥ/ yadāha - ``guṇakriyāyaugapadyaṃ samuccayaḥ'' iti//12//

 mālatī/ evaṃ duṣkaraṃ karoti saḥ/ kāmandakī/ tadevamuktaprakāreṇa maraṇamapyanena śakyamanubhavituṃ yato'yaṃ prakṛtyā sukumāra uttamasaukumāryaśālī/ tadyathā - ``saukumāryaṃ tridhā bhinnaṃ śreṣṭhamadhyāyamakramāt/ prasūnapallavasparśāsahaṃ yatsyāttaduttamam//'' iti/ anubhūtapūrvatvādvikārasya duḥsahatvamuktaṃ kadāciditi/ tapasvyanukampanīyaḥ/ yadvā tvatsamāgamāyaiva mahate puruṣārthāya tvadvirahavyathānubhavalakṣaṇaṃ suduṣkaraṃ tapaścaratīti tapasvī/ kumāra iti prāgakṛtadāraparigrahatvaṃ pitroḥ kuśalitvaṃ ca vyajyate// mālatī/ sakhi, ātmanaḥ kāraṇānmartyalokālaṃkārabhūtasya tasya kimapyāśaṅkamānā bhūtāviṣṭeva na jānāmi kiṃ pratipatsyata iti/ kimapīti maraṇaṃ nirdiśati/ bhagavatīvacanasya kimuttaraṃ deyamityahaṃ na jānāmi/ tvameva yathocitaṃ madavasthānivedanādikamuttaraṃ dehītyāśayaḥ// mādhavaḥ - diṣṭyeti/ bhāgyavaśādeva madavasthāṃ prakaṭayantyā bhagavatyānukampito'smi/ yadvā janāntikamapi latāmātrāntaritatvādantikasthena mādhavena śrutaṃ vākyamiti tadvacanākarṇanaharṣavaśādiyamuktiḥ// lavaṅgikā/ bhagavatyevaṃvādinītyākhyāyate/ asmākamapi bhartṛdārikā bhavanāsannarathyāmukhamuhūrtamaṇḍanasya tasyaiva bahuśo'nubhūtadarśanā bhūtvā/ cakṣuḥprītiranenoktā/ ravikarāśliṣṭamugdhakamalinīkandasundarāvayavaśobhāvibhāvitānaṅgavedanāvyatikararamaṇīyāpi parijanaṃ dūnayati/ aṅgaglāniḥ pāṇḍimā ca kathitaḥ/ nābhinandati kalākrīḍāḥ/ nṛttagītādayaḥ kalāḥ/ aratiruktā/ kevalaṃ mlāyamānakāntahastaparyastagaṇḍamaṇḍalā divasāngamayati/ cintā sūcitā/ api ca vikasitāravindamakarandaviṣyandasundareṇa daradalitakundamākandamadhubindusaṃdohavāhinā bhavanodyānaparyantamārutenottāmyati/ uddīpanavibhāvanājanitavedanātiśayoktiḥ/ anyacca yataḥ prabhṛti tasmindivase nijamahotsavābhyudayadarśanārthaṃ pratipannarūpasya svīkṛtaśarīrasya bhagavato madanasyeva kāmakānanālaṃkāriṇastasya mādhavasya vividhairvibhramairvilāsairanurāgānubandhena samucitapriyajanagocarābhilāṣaprakarṣeṇa ca maharṣīkṛto'tiślāghanīyaḥ/ saṃpādanīya iti yāvat/ yauvanārambho yasya tattathā/ anyonyadṛṣṭivinipātavañcanāvasare jvaritaṃ kheditaṃ yaccittaṃ tasṃmistvaramāṇena kautūhalenollasiteṣu sādhvasajanitātstambhānmanthareṣu ceṣṭāvihīneṣvavayaveṣu pratilagnāḥ svedapulakakampāḥ sāttvikabhāvāstairānanditasakhījanaṃ parasparāvalokanasukhaṃ samāsāditaṃ mālatyā/ tataḥ prabhṛti saviśeṣaduḥsahāyāsavijṛmbhaṇoddāmadāruṇaṃ daśāpariṇāmamanubhavantī muhūrtasaṃprāptapūrṇacandrodayeva bālakamalinī parimlāyati/ tathāpi muhūrtamātrahṛdayavinihitanirmīyamāṇavallabhasamāgamā nirbharasalilāsārasicyamāneva medinī śītalāyata iti jānāmi/ yena prasphuritaradanacchadojjvaladdantamauktikapaṅktikāntisaviśeṣaśobhitaṃ nirantarollasitapulakapakṣmalakapolaghūrṇamānasaṃtatānandabāṣpastabakam/ ``bāhadhevam'' iti pāṭhe dhevo binduḥ/ īṣadviṣamaniṣpandamantharatārottānamasṛṇamukulāyamānanetranīlotpalamaviralodbhinnasvedajalabindusundaraniṭilacandralekhāmanoharam/ niṭilo lalāṭaḥ/ mugdhamukhapuṇḍarīkamudvahantī vidagdhasahacarīcittasaṃśayitakaumārabhāvā bhavati/ yena kāraṇena dantacchadasphuraṇaromāñcānandabāṣpadṛgvikāraviśeṣasvedaprabhṛtibhiḥ saṃbhogacihnairvidagdhasakhīnāṃ citte saṃśayaviṣayīkṛtaḥ kumārībhāvo yasyāḥ sā/ kathamiyaṃ kumāryapi vidagdhanāyakopabhukteva saṃbhogacihnālaṃkṛtetyevaṃ saṃdihyamānā bhavati, tena kāraṇena hṛdayanihitavallabhanirmāṇasaṃbhogasauhityanirvāpiteyamiti jānāmīti yojyam/ etatsarvamasmābhiḥ ``nīvībandha-'' ityādāveva vyākhyātamiti neha pratanyate/ kiṃ ca, uddāmaśaśimayūkhanikurumbacumbitapravṛttaniṣyandacandramaṇihāradhāriṇī pracurakarpūrasaviśeṣaśiśiracandanarasacchaṭāsāranikaradanturitabālakadalīpatraśayanā pādasaṃvāhanādivyāpāratvaramaṇasahacarīsārthaviracitopanītakamalinīdalajalārdraṃ tālavṛntaṃ yasyāḥ sā/ unnidreva rajanīrgamayati/ jāgarāvasthā darśitā/ kathamapyupalabdhanidrā ca nidraiva sukhaṃ svapne priyatamasamāgamaupadhāyakatvāttadevāha/ prakṣālitābhyāmaviralasvedajalakṣālitābhyāṃ pādapallavābhyāmudvamannudvāntaḥ piṇḍālaktakaraso yayā sā/ tharatharāyanteti deśīyapadaṃ kampavācakam/ kampamānapīvarorumūlapārśvādvisaṃvāditaṃ vigalitaṃ nīvībandhanaṃ yasyāḥ sā/ utkṣubhyamānahṛdayāntarottaraṅganiḥvāsairviṣamamanekaprakāramucchvasantau pulakapakṣmalau saromāñcau payodharau tayorupari vikṣiptavepamānabhujalatāveṣṭanabandhanā/ svedavepathunīvīsraṃsananiḥvāsaromāñcabhujalatāvalayaveṣṭanaiḥ svapnasamāgame priyatamagāḍhāliṅganasaukhyamanayānubhūtamityarthaḥ/ jhaṭiti pratibodhavelāvisarjitāpāṅgadṛṣṭivinipātena vijñānaśūnyaṃ tādṛśabāhupāśāntargatakaṇṭhāvalambitanāyakavirahitaṃ yacchayanīyaṃ tena saṃjāto yo mohastena mīlallocanaṃ yasyāḥ sā/ sasaṃbhramaḥ sodvego yaḥ sakhījanastasya prayatnairvyajanavījanahimavālukāsekādirūpaiḥ pratipanno yo mūrcchāvicchedastatsamaye saṃgalitaḥ saṃbhūya vinirgataścirakālanirodhādyo dīrghaniḥśvāsastena janitā jīvitāśā yasyāḥ sā/ kiṃkartavyatāmūḍhaṃ prathamaṃ prārthitanijajīvitāvasānaṃ durvāradaivadurvilasitopālambhamātravyāpāraṃ sakhījanaṃ karoti/ tatpaśyatu bhagavatī/ eṣu tāvallāvaṇyabhūyiṣṭhaṃ yannirmāṇaṃ tena paripeśaleṣu sukumāreṣvaṅgeṣu vijṛmbhitasya manmathasya kiyacciraṃ kiyataḥ kālasya kuśalāvasānatā kṣemaparyavasāyitvam/ bhaviṣyatīti śeṣaḥ/ idānīṃ mālatyaṅgeṣu dāruṇakarmaṇaḥ smarasyetaḥ paraṃ kadā vā priyatamasaṃghaṭanena kuśalakāritvaṃ bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ/ kathaṃ cemāni ramaṇena saha yaḥ kelikalahaḥ kopastenopanīto yo rāgaḥ koparāgastena pallavitau saṃjātapallavau yau keralīkapolau tadvatkomalā udvelā vimalā ca yā candrikā tayoddāmaṃ pragalbhaṃ dalitāni timirāvaraṇāni yeṣu tāni vibhāvarīmukhāni/ ime collasitadugdhadhārāpūradhavalojjvalajjyotsnāprakṣālitanabhoṅgaṇāḥ/ parimalitaṃ saṃjātaparimalaṃ yatpāṭalamukulaṃ tasya yannirmathanaṃ tena yo bahulaparimalasyotpīḍa udgārastena yatsaṃkalanaṃ miśrībhāvastena masṛṇaḥ snigdho māṃsalaḥ puṣṭo yo malayamārutastenoddhūtā daśadiṅmukhā anarthakāriṇo bhavanti rajanīpariṇāmāḥ priyasakhyāḥ/ īdṛśāni rajanīmukhāni īdṛśāśca rajanīpariṇāmāḥ priyasakhyāḥ/ kathaṃ kena vā prakāreṇānarthakāriṇo bhavantītyetadapi bhagavatī paśyatviti saṃbandhaḥ/ ``na bhavanti'' iti pāṭhe kathamanarthakāriṇo na bhavanti/ bhavantyevetyarthaḥ/ rajanīmukhapariṇāmayoranarthahetutvenaiva kṛtsnasya rātriṃdivasya tādṛśatvaṃ darśitam// kāmandakī - yadīti/ yadyayamanurāgabandho gāḍhānurāgastadviṣayo mādhavālambanastarhi sphuṭaṃ vyaktametadeva/ hiśabda evakārārthe/ guṇajñatāyāḥ phalamityevamanenānurāgasthairyeṇānanditaṃ prārabdhakāryānukūlyena jātānandamapi me hṛdayamasyā dāruṇayā tvaduktayāvasthayā vidīryate/ sukumāratayā jhaṭityaniṣṭopanipātamāśaṅkya vidīrṇamiva bhavatītyarthaḥ//13//

 mādhavaḥ/ abhyullāso'niṣṭāśaṅkayā hṛdayasyodvegaḥ/ sthāne yukta ityarthaḥ// kāmandakī/ pramādo'smadīyānavadhānatā/ aho āścaryam/ yadidānīmapi mayā saṃdihānenaiva prāṇavyayenāpi mālatīsamāgamaṃ prati vilambyata ityarthaḥ/ vilambanānaucityamevāha - prakṛtīti/ prakṛtyaiva lalitam/ lalitaṃ nāma yauvanārambhasaṃbhavaśṛṅgārānuguṇo gātrārambhaḥ/ yathā - ``sukumāro'ṅgavinyāsaḥ sabhrūnetrādharakriyaḥ/ anulbaṇaśca masṛṇaḥ strīṇāṃ lalitamīritam//'' iti/ tadyogāllalitametasyā vapuḥ saukumāryaikasāraṃ saukumāryamātrasthirāṃśam/ saukumāryamevāsya vapuṣo'valambanaṃ nānyaḥ sattvayoga ityarthaḥ/ ``prasūnapallavasparśāsahaṃ yatsyāttaduttamam'' ityuktamuttamasaukumāryamatra vivakṣitam/ ayaṃ punaḥ pañcabāṇo dāruṇo ghorataravyāpāraḥ/ astu nāmaitat/ kathamayaṃ kālo'pi mandamandasaṃcāricandanācalagandhavahakampitasahakārakusumanikarastuṣāravāridādyavagrahābhāvāccārucandra evāvataṃso yasya sa tādṛśa idānīmevopasthitaḥ/ kaṣṭamanarthaparamparā saṃmilitetyāśayaḥ/ anena vidhūtaṃ nāmāṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``vidhūtamaratiryūnoḥ suratāprāptisaṃbhavā'' iti//14//

 lavaṅgikā/ anyacca jñātaṃ bhavatu bhagavatyā/ evacca[etacca athavā evañca] mādhavapraticchandakasanāthaṃ citraphalakam/ mādhavānurāgaprakarṣadyotakamidamapyekaṃ bhagavatyā jñātavyamityarthaḥ/ stanāṃśukamiti kaṇṭhāvalambitasragavalokanārtham/ eṣāpi tasyaiva svahastaviraciteti kaṇṭhāvalambitā bakulamālā saṃjīvanaṃ priyasakhyāḥ/ tadevaṃ bhagavatyā mādhavaṃ prati mālatyanurāge na kiṃcidapi saṃśayitavyam/ yathā kathaṃcitsaṃghaṭanopāya eva jhaṭiti prayojya ityāśayaḥ// mādhavaḥ - jitamiti/ sakhi, pūrvaṃ matkaṇṭhāvalambinī bhūtvedānīṃ matpriyatamākaṇṭhapraṇayinītvena matprayojanasaṃpādanaikapravaṇatayā mama vayasyabhūte bakulāvali, iha bhuvane sarvasminnapi prapañce tvayaiva jitaṃ sarvātiśāyyutkarṣo labdhaḥ/ yato'syāḥ priyāyā vallabhā jātāsi/ mayā hi kathamasyā madanurāgasūcakasaspṛhāvalokanagocaro bhaveyam, kathametadīyaśarīrasaṃsparśasaṃjātasauhityanirvṛto bhūyāsam, kathaṃ kṣaṇamapyetatkaṇṭhāliṅganamahotsavacaritārthajīvanaḥ syāmiti saṃtataṃ yadāśāsyate tadetattvayā nirantaratatkucamaṇḍalīmaṇḍanāyamānayā saṃtataṃ saspṛhāvalokananirvarṇyamānayā mameyaṃ jīvitālambanabhūtā priyasakhītyanavaratamabhinandyamānayā sahasraguṇamadhigatamityaho te saubhāgyātiśaya iti bhāvaḥ/ idameva viśeṣaṇena darśayati - pariṇato yo bisadaṇḍastasya kāṇḍaḥ parvadeśastadvatpāṇḍuryaḥ stanapariṇāhaḥ kucābhogastasya yo vilāsa ujjṛmbhaṇaṃ tasya vaijayantī patākā utkarṣadyotikā/ anena mādhavasya svahastanirmitabakulamālikāyāḥ priyatamākaṇṭhābharaṇatvajñānena harṣapratīteraratyupaśamācchamo nāmāṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``aratyupaśamaḥ śamaḥ'' iti/ kāvyaliṅgamalaṃkāraḥ//15//

 evamanyonyānurāgavyañjakena parasparāvasthānivedanena śṛṅgāraṃ paripoṣya rasāntareṇa taṃ tirodhātuṃ cūlikākhyamarthopakṣepamāha - nepathya iti/ ``antaryavanikāsaṃsthaiścūlikārthasya sūcanā'' iti cūlikālakṣaṇam/ re re śaṃkarapuravāsijānapadāḥ, eṣa khalu yauvanārambheṇa bharitau pūṇag durviṣahau yāvamarṣaroṣāvakṣamākrodhau/ yadvā sthirakrodhatātkālikakopau/ yathāhuḥ - ``krodhaḥ kṛtāparādheṣu sthiro'marṣatvamaśnute/ roṣastātkālikaḥ kopaḥ'' iti/ tayorvyatikareṇa saṃmelanena yo balātkārastena pūrvaṃ vighaṭitaṃ bhañjitaṃ paścādudghāṭitamapasāritadvārabandhanaṃ yallohapañjaraṃ tatraiva pratilagnaḥ saṃsaktaḥ saṃgalitaḥ svacaraṇādvigalito nigalaḥ śṛṅkhalā yasya saḥ/ nijaḥ svakīyaḥ pañjaranigalabandhāpagamāccirasya labdho yo līlayā svācchandyena vilāsaścaṅkramaṇārūpo vihārastenodvella ūrdhvaprasārito vallabhaḥ priyatamastuṅga unnato yo lāṅgalo bāladaṇḍaḥ sa eva vikaṭavaijayantikā vikṛtākārapatākā tayā viṣamo durdarśo ḍāmaro bhīṣaṇa uddāmaḥ prācaṇḍyamaṇḍitaḥ śarīrasaṃniveśo yasya saḥ/ maṭhādapakramya tatkṣaṇe'pakramaṇa eva satṛṣṇaṃ yathā syāttathā kavalitānāmanekadehidehānāmavayavāsteṣāṃ madhye ye niṣṭhurāḥ paruṣā asthikhaṇḍāsteṣāṃ khaṇḍanena dantairniṣpīḍanena janito yaṣṭaṃkārastena kaṭakaṭāyamānā kaṭakaṭāśabdaṃ kurvāṇā karapatravatkrakacavatkaṭhinā yā daṃṣṭrā tayā karālo danturo mukhakandaro vadanakuharaṃ yasya saḥ/ tathā vikaṭaṃ yadvijṛmbhaṇaṃ viharaṇaṃ tenoddāmadāruṇaḥ pracaṇḍabhīṣaṇo yaścapeṭo vistṛtāṅguliḥ karaḥ/ ``pāṇau capeṭapratalaprahastā vistṛtāṅgulau'' ityamaraḥ/ tenāmoṭitaṃ marditam/ ``muṭa pramardane'' iti dhātoretadrūpam/ parito militaṃ yannaraturaṅgasya jāṅgalaṃ saraktaṃ māṃsaṃ tasya ya udgāra ādhikyādvamanaṃ tena bharitā paripūrṇā galaguhā tasyā garbhe gambhīro ghargharo yasya/ rallīti deśī padaṃ[deśīpadaṃ] dīrghamadhuradhvanivāci/ dīrghamadhuro gallūraṇaśabdo māṃsabhakṣaṇasamayakupitaśārdūlajātiprayuktaṃ kaṇṭhagarjitaṃ tasya saṃdarbho vipulatvaṃ tena paripūritaṃ nabhastalaṃ yena saḥ/ tathā kecinnihatāḥ, kecinniṣpeṣitāścūrṇīkṛtāḥ, kecinnaṣṭā adarśanaṃ gamitāḥ, kecinniṣṭhāpitā niṣṭhāṃ nirbhayasthānaparyantaṃ gamitā ye'śeṣajanāsteṣāṃ nivaho yasya saḥ/ kvacit ``bhīṣitanaṣṭaniṣṭhāpitā'' iti pāṭhaḥ/ tatra pūrvaṃ bhīṣitāḥ, paścānnaṣṭā dṛgviṣayamatītāḥ, paścānniṣṭhāpitā nirbhayasthānaṃ prāpitā iti vyākhyeyam/ tathā kaṭhoranakharakarparadalitākṛṣṭajantugātrāvayavapravṛttaraktakardamitagatipatho duṣṭaśārdūlaḥ kṛtāntalīlāyitaṃ karoti/ tatparirakṣata yathāśaktyātmano jīvitamiti// buddharakṣitā/ paritrāyadhvam/ eṣā naḥ priyasakhyamātyanandanasya bhaginī madayantikaitena duṣṭaśārdūlena hatavidrāvitaparijanābhibhūyate/ mālatī/ sakhi lavaṅgike, aho mahānpramādaḥ/ ciramatra vijane vane'smābhirekākinībhiḥ sthitamanucitaṃ kṛtam/ idānīṃ kaḥ paritrāteti bhāvaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ asau kutra vartate/ idānīmeva nihatya bhavatsakhīṃ mocayiṣyāmītyarthaḥ// mālatī/ saharṣasādhvasamiti/ lavaṅgikāniveditasvakīyavirahavedanācitraphalakabakulāvalīvṛttāntaḥ sarvo'pyatraiva vartamānenādhigata iti harṣaḥ, vivikte māmenaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā kaścittātāya nivedayiṣyatīti sādhvasaṃ ca/ ahmahe aho pramādaḥ/ ``cintāyāṃ sapramādāyāmahmahe iti kalpitam/ śabdarūpaṃ viśeṣeṇa prayoktavyaṃ prayoktṛbhiḥ//'' iti vacanāt/ eṣo'pyatraiva/ mādhavaḥ/ yadityādi ślokopaskāraḥ/ aviraleti/ atarkitopanatamaśaṅkitameva haṭhādupāgataṃ yaddarśanaṃ madviṣayaṃ tenollasitayāścaryaharṣaparavaśatayānayā kartryā sphāritena vistāritena/ ``āyataṃ visphurattāraṃ visphāritamudāhṛtam'' iti lakṣaṇāt/ cakṣuṣā karaṇena pauṇḍarīkeṇa sitāmbhojanirmitena dāmnāviralaṃ naddho baddha iva/ ahamiti śeṣaḥ/ dāmatvaṃ ca kaṭākṣāṇāṃ paṅktībhūyāviralanipātanāt/ naddhatvena niṣkrayatvamātmano darśayanstambhākhyaṃ sātvikabhāvamabhivyanakti/ tathā nirbhareṇa saṃpūrṇena dugdhasrotasā payaḥpravāheṇa snapita iva/ kṣīrapūrasnapanenātmanaḥ sauhityaprakarṣaṃ vyanakti/ kṛtsnaḥ samasta evāhaṃ kavalito nigīrṇo viṣayamādhuryotkarṣāccakṣuṣā svāntaḥpraveśita iva/ tasyāścirakāladarśanenāpyatṛptimanena sūcayati/ amṛtavarṣiṇā meghena sāndreṇa sikta iva/ anena dhārāsāranyāyena kaṭākṣapravṛtterlolākhyo dṛṣṭivyāpāra uktaḥ/ yathā - ``dhārāvāhikasaṃcāro yasya tallolamucyate'' iti mālopamālaṃkāraḥ//16//

 buddharakṣitā/ mahābhāga, eṣa khalūdyānabāhyarathyāmukhe// mādhavaḥ/ sāṭopaṃ sasaṃbhramam/ apramattaḥ sāvadhānaḥ// mālatī/ lavaṅgike, saṃśayaḥ khalu jātaḥ śārdūlamṛtyuvaktrānnirgamiṣyatyasau na veti saṃdeho jātaḥ/ kaṣṭaṃ dhigityarthaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ sabībhatsaṃ sajugupsam/ āheti śeṣaḥ/ ahahetyadbhute/ saṃsakteti/ nakhāyudhasya vyāghrasya mārgaḥ prācaṇḍyamatibhayaṃkaratāṃ vahati dhatte/ kīdṛk/ saṃsaktāni kvacillatādau prathamaṃ lagnāni tatastruṭitāni chinnāni tataśca vivartitāni bhūmau tatra vikṣiptāni yānyantrajālāni tairvyākīrṇaḥ saṃkulastathā sphurantaḥ prakāśamānā apavṛttā viparyastā ruṇḍakhaṇḍāḥ śiraḥkapālacchedā yatra saḥ/ tathā kīlālasya raktasya vyatikareṇāmiśrībhāvena gulphadaghnaḥ paṅko yatra sa iti//17//

 aho pramādaḥ/ madayantikāparijanasyānavadhānatvamāścaryaṃ yadiyamaśakyapratikāre saṃkaṭe nipatitetyarthaḥ/ aśakyapratikāratāmevāha - vayamiti/ vayaṃ tāvaddūre vartāmahe/ kanyakā tu paśoḥ kramagatā/ ekenaiva padavinyāsena labhyadeśe vartata ityarthaḥ/ hāśabda iṣṭajanavipattidyotakaḥ/ mādhavasya saharṣatvaṃ vyāghrahananādvanitājīvanācca/ kāmandakyāḥ sākūtatvaṃ makarandena madayantikāprāṇarakṣaṇopakārasya tadubhayasaṃghaṭanopayogitvāditi/ kathaṃ makarandaḥ sahasaiva saṃbhramādetya tadavapātitāttena śārdūlenāvapātitānnihatāt/ kasmāccidāyudhahastātpuruṣādityadhyāhāraḥ/ adhigataṃ prāptamāyudhaṃ yena saḥ/ tannihatabhaṭahastagatamāyudhamāhṛtyetyarthaḥ/ madhye vyāghramadayantikayorantare sthito'tarkitopanato'sāvasyā ālambanaṃ jāta ityarthaḥ// itarāḥ/ sādhu/ pauruṣaṃ mahatkṛtamiti śeṣaḥ/ dṛḍhamiti/ dṛḍhaṃ yathā syāttathā makarandaḥ paśunā hato'bhihataḥ/ amunā makarandena cāsau paśurvyasurvigataprāṇaḥ kṛtaśca pramathitaśca/ ``daṃṣṭrāyudhaḥ'' iti kvacitpāṭhaḥ/ khaṇḍitaḥ/ vyāghra ityarthaḥ//18//

 itarāḥ/ atyāhitaṃ mahābhītiḥ/ upasthitamiti śeṣaḥ// kāmandakī/ sākūtatve heturuktaḥ/ vyālaḥ krūrajantuḥ/ kṣititale viṣaktālambanayaṣṭiriva saṃlagnaikakoṭiryā khaḍgalatā tadavaṣṭambhena tadavalambanena niścalo mahāśūratayā balavatprahṛto'pi svāyudhamevāvalambya sthitaḥ, na tu patita ityarthaḥ/ saṃbhrāntayodvignayā madayantikayāvalambito dattahastāvalambaḥ// itarāḥ/ hā dhik/ gāḍhaprahāratayā klāmyati mahābhāgaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ kathaṃ makarandaḥ pramugdho mohaṃ prāpta eva/ bhagavati, paritrāyasveti/ mitravyasanātsvayamapi pramugdha iva bhavannevaṃ vadatīti jñeyam// kāmandakī/ atikātaro'si/ kimarthametāvataiva bibheṣi/ nanvityāmantraṇam/ ehi makarandaṃ paśyāmaḥ/ kathaṃ vartata iti drakṣyāma ityarthaḥ/ idaṃ cāṅkāvataraṇamuttarāṅkārthasya pūrvāṅkānte nipātanāt/ aṅkadvayasya saṃgatārthatvāt/ yathā - aṅkāvatārastvaṅkānte pāto'ṅkasyāvibhāgataḥ/ pūrvāṅkasyāvasānoktakathāvicchedapūrvakam// praveśo bhāvino'ṅkasya soṅkāvatara iṣyate//'' iti//

bhavabhūtivacogumpharasāsvādanacāturī/

yadi vidyeta keṣāṃcidviralānāṃ vipaścitām//

iti śrīmadbhāradvājakulatilakaparvatanāthayāyajūkanandanatripurārisūriviracitāyāṃ mālatīmādhavabhāvapradīpikāyāṃ tṛtīyāṅkavivaraṇam//

caturtho'ṅkaḥ/

namāmi madanānandavidhānavihitādarām/

śaṃbhuvāmāṅgavāmākṣīṃ kāmākṣīṃ kāmitāptaye//

kaviridānīmaṅkāvatāreṇaiva pūrvamaṅkānte niveśitānāmaṅkapātrapraveśakatadvṛttāntasya sūcitatvādaṅkamevārabhate - tata ityādi/ mādhavasyāpi mitravyasanena pramugdhatvānmālatyā saṃnihitayāvalambanaṃ draṣṭavyam// madayantikā/ prasīda bhagavati, paritrāyasva madayantikānimittaṃ saṃśayitajīvitaṃ vipannānukampinaṃ mahābhāgam/ madayantikānimittamityarthāntarasaṃkramitavācyo dhvaniḥ/ lokottarapuruṣadhaureyajīvitāpahāralakṣaṇāvayavaśobhājanakatvaṃ dyotyate// itarāḥ/ hā dhik/ kimidānīmatra prekṣitavyamasmābhiḥ// makarandaḥ/ vayasya, atikātaro'si/ kimetat/ dhīrasyāpi te'yuktamityarthaḥ/ nanvityāmantraṇe/ svastha evāhamasmi na pramugdhaḥ// madayantikā/ aho, pratibuddhamidānīṃ makarandapūrṇacandreṇa/ pūrṇacandratvāropaṇena svacittāhlādakatvādanurāgaprakarṣaṃ sūcayati// mālatī/ mādhavasya lalāṭe hastaṃ dattvetyaṅgasaṃsparśenāmṛtāsecanakalpena jhaṭiti pramohamapasārayituṃ lalāṭe hastanikṣepaḥ/ na caitāvatā mālatyā dhārṣṭyamāśaṅkanīyam/ īdṛśe vipatsamaye sarvatra sarveṣāmucitopacārakaraṇasya loke dārṣṭyarūpatayānudbhāvanāt// kāmandakī/ śirasyāghrāṇaṃ gurujanasyāpatyeṣu vātsalyadyotakam/ jīvantau vatsau vātsalyaviṣayāvapatyakalpau yasyāḥ sā// itarāḥ/ priyaṃ naḥ saṃvṛttam// buddharakṣitā/ sakhi madayantike, eṣa eva saḥ/ yo mayā pūrvaṃ niveditastvayi niratiśayānurāgo makarandaḥ sa eṣa evetyarthaḥ// madayantikā/ sakhi, jñātameva mayā/ eṣa eva mādhavo'yamapi sa jana iti/ bhartṛtvādhyavasāyānnāmānupādānam/ tvaduktākṛtiviśeṣasaṃvādena svajīvitanirapekṣatayā matprāṇatrāṇapravaṇatayā kāmandakyādivyavahāreṇa ca madanurakto'yaṃ makā[ka]randa iti jñāta evetyarthaḥ// buddharakṣitā/ api satyavādinyaham// kiṃ maduktamasya saundaryādiguṇojjvalatvaṃ pratyakṣato dṛśyate na vetyarthaḥ// madayantikā/ na khalvasma[mā]dṛśīṣu yuṣmādṛśyaḥ pakṣapātinyo bhavanti/ sakhi, mālatyā api ramaṇīyo'sminmahānubhāve'nurāgapravādaḥ/ tanmadīyabhrātṛmanorathasaṃpattirdurlabheti hṛdayam// kāmandakī/ ramaṇīyaṃ saṃbhramasrastāṃśukaṃ samyagvibhāvya parasparāvayavasauṣṭavagrāhitvena sādhvasavyājāddṛḍhāliṅganamahotsavānubandhitvena ca manoharam/ tathorjitaṃ balavaddustaravipannistaraṇopakāramūlatvena daivena, natvasmadādiprayatnādīdṛksaṃpādanasya duḥsādhakatvāt/ saṃnidhāpitaḥ saṃnihitīkṛtaḥ// makarandaḥ/ parāpatanneva tvaritamāgacchanneva/ avaskando'bhibhavaḥ/ abhijātakanyakāmākṛtiviśeṣādyanumitamahākulaprabhavakumārībhāvāmabhyupapannavānabhyupagatavānasmi// mālatīmādhavau vimṛśataḥ/ cittodvegahetumubhayoḥ sādhāraṇatvātsukhaduḥkhayorubhāvapi vimṛśataḥ ityarthaḥ// kāmandakī mādhavacittodvegātiśayakarīṃ vārtāṃ nirūpayati - kāmandakī/ vṛttānteneti/ rājñānandanāya mālatīpradānaṃ niṣpannaprāyaṃ bhavitumarhatītyarthaḥ/ atha niścityavārtāṃ śīghramantaḥpuramākārayitumatrāgamiṣyanti, tataḥ prāgeva mālatīmanaḥpratyayajananārthaṃ mādhavena svābhiprāyaṃ svata eva vācayiṣyāmītyabhiprāyeṇāha - vatsa mādhava, mālatyā vardhito'si pratyāhṛtacaitanyo'si/ so'yamavasaraḥ prītidānasya pāritoṣikasya/ taddīyatāmityarthaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ yaditi/ iyaṃ mālatī yato vyālena krūrasattvena vraṇitasya vikṣatagātrasya suhṛdaḥ pramohena mūrchayā pramugdhaṃ māṃ kāruṇyādgatavyathaṃ kṛtavatī/ tasmātkāmaṃ svecchayā vinaiva mamānumatiṃ pūrṇapātravṛttyā pūrṇapātraprakāreṇa mama hṛdayaṃ ca jīvitaṃ ca svīkartuṃ prabhavati yatheṣṭaviniyogārhaṃ kartumīṣṭe/ pūrṇapātraṃ nāma priyanivedakena balādākṛṣya gṛhyamāṇamalaṃkāravastrādikaṃ kathyate/ prāgeva hṛdayaṃ jīvitaṃ ca tadadhīnameva vartata iti tanmayā deyam/ tato'dhikaṃ nāsti kimidānīṃ dātavyamityāśayaḥ/ etadyuktottarapradānātpragamanaṃ nāmāṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``yuktottaraṃ pragamanam'' iti//1//

 lavaṅgikā/ pratīṣṭaḥ khalu naḥ priyasakhyāyaṃ prasādaḥ/ ayameva mahānprasādo yatpriyasakhyadhīne tvanmanojīvite iti kimataḥ paraṃ ślāghanīyamityarthaḥ// madayantikā/ jānāti mahānubhāvo'yaṃ jano ramaṇīyaṃ mantrayitum/ ramaṇīyaṃ manogatābhiprāyaprakaṭanaṃ bāhyopacāranaipuṇyapradarśanaṃ yathā bhavati tathā manoharam// mālatī/ kiṃ nāma makarandenodvegakāraṇaṃ śrutaṃ bhaviṣyati/ mādhavasyeti śeṣaḥ// praviśyetyādi/ jyāyāñjyeṣṭhaḥ/ parameśvareṇānatikramaṇīyaśāsanena rājñā paraṃ viśvāsamasmadīyaṃ sarvasvam/ tasya yatheṣṭaviniyogārhaṃ tadīyamasmākamityanyonyasya niratiśayaṃ manaḥsaṃmelanaṃ prakaṭayatetyarthaḥ/ pratipāditā dattā/ saṃbhāvayāvaḥ/ pramodena bahumanyāvaha ityarthaḥ/ idaṃ ca prastutaprāptinirodhānnirodho nāmāṅgam/ yathā - ``hitarodho nirodhaḥ syāt'' iti// makarandaḥ/ iyaṃ seti/ yā tvayodvegakāriṇī vārtā śrutā saiveyamityarthaḥ/ vaivarṇyaṃ vivarṇabhāvamapratisamādheyavipatprāptijanitatāpahetukaṃ sāttvikabhāvarūpam/ yadāha - ``vaivarṇyaṃ varṇabhedaḥ syāttāpaśaṅkābhayādibhiḥ'' iti// madayantikā/ sakhi mālati, tvaṃ khalvekanagaranivāsena pāṃsukrīḍanātprabhṛti priyasakhī bhaginī ca sāṃprataṃ punarbhrātṛbhāryātvenāsmākaṃ gṛhasya maṇḍanaṃ jātāsi// kāmandakī/ vardhasa iti sopahāseyamuktiḥ// madayantikā/ yuṣmākamāśiṣāṃ prasādena/ kevalaṃ bhavadāśīrvādānmālatīlābho na tu daṃpatyoranyonyānurāgātiśayādityāśayaḥ/ sakhi lavaṅgike, bharitāḥ saṃpūrṇā manorathāḥ/ yuṣmākaṃ lābhena saparijanāyā eva mālatyā lābhādyuṣmākamiti bahutvam// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, asmākamapyetanmantrayitavyam/ pūrṇā no manorathā yuṣmākaṃ sadṛśaślāghyasaṃbandhānāṃ lābhenaivetyasmābhirapi vaktavyamityarthaḥ/ atha ca yuṣmākaṃ saparijanāyā bhavatyā makarandaprāptirūpeṇa bhāvinā lābhenāsmākaṃ pūrṇā manorathā bhavitāra ityasmābhirevaitanmantrayitavyam/ na tu tvayā saparijanamālatīlābhasya bhavatsaṃkalpamātrasya niścitatvādityapi pratītiḥ// madayantikā/ sakhi buddharakṣite, ehi tāvat/ mahotsavaṃ saṃbhāvayāvaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ yathā hṛdaye bharitau pūrṇāvata evodvamantau yau vismayānandau tābhyāṃ sundaraṃ yathā syāttathā ghūrṇitā bhrāmitā dhīrā ākāragopanārthamavalambitadhairyāḥ paryante'pāṅgadeśe manoharāśca paryasyante paritaḥ kṣipyante'lasavalitādiprakāravaicitryeṇa pravartante/ madayantikāmakarandayordalitanīlotpalamāṃsalacchavayo dṛṣṭisaṃbhedāḥ saṃmukhapravṛttyā miśrībhūtāḥ kaṭākṣavikṣepāḥ, tathā manye manorathanirvṛttasamāgamāvetāviti/ dṛṣṭivikṣepā yathā yena prakāreṇa pravartante tathā tena prakāreṇaivamunnīyata ityarthaḥ// kāmandakī/ nanvityādi/ mānasaṃ mohanaṃ saṃkalpanirmitasaṃbhogam/ tadvyañjakaṃ liṅgamāha - tathā hīti/ īṣaditi/ anayordṛṣṭaṃ darśanaṃ vyaktaṃ yathā syāttathā śaṃsati/ saṃkalpanirmitaṃ saṃbhogaṃ kathayatītyarthaḥ/ kīdṛśaṃ dṛṣṭam/ īṣattiryagvalanena manāktiryagudañcanena viṣamaṃ vakram/ vakrākhyo'yaṃ śṛṅgāravikāraḥ/ yathā - ``valito vāṅgasaṃcāro yatra tadvakramucyate'' iti/ kūṇitaṃ prāntekūṇitaprāntam/ apāṅgadeśe bhāgatrayeṇa saṃkucitamityarthaḥ/ kūṇitaṃ yathā - ``bhāgatrayasya saṃkoco vikāsastvaparasya ca/ yasyā dṛṣṭervilakṣeṇa tadvikūṇitamucyate//'' iti/ ``purastribhāgasaṃkoce premṇā tatkūṇitaṃ bhavet''/ iti/ etaditi purovartitvena nirdiśati/ premṇo'nurāgasyodbhedena prakaṭībhāvena stimitaṃ niṣpandaṃ lalitaṃ premārdraṃ ca/ niṣpandalalite yathā - ``niṣpandaṃ tadyadanyatra dṛṣṭānna spandate kvacit/ premārdramantarvikasattāraṃ lalitamīritam//'' iti/ kiṃcidākuñciteṣaducchvasantī bhrūryatra taccaturākhyo bhrūvikāraḥ prasannadṛṣṭivikāraścoktaḥ/ tallakṣaṇaṃ tūktaṃ prāk/ antarunmādasyāntarānandasyānubhavena masṛṇaṃ premṇānurañjitam/ ``masṛṇaṃ tattu vijñeyamanurāgakaṣāyitam'' ityetadapyuktaṃ prāk/ srastānyavasannāni niṣkampāni niścalāni pakṣmāṇi yatra tat/ ākekare akṣiṇī/ ``ākuñcitapuṭā yāṅgasaṃgatārthanimīlitā/ muhurvyāvartatārā ca dṛṣṭirākekarā matā//'' ityākekaradṛṣṭiruktā/ kecittu ``dṛṣṭamākekarākṣamityākekaradṛṣṭireva dṛṣṭaśabdenoktā/ ākekaradṛṣṭisvarūpameveṣattiryagityādi viśeṣaṇairnirūpitam'' iti vyācakṣate/ aciramiti darśanakriyāviśeṣaṇam//2//

 madayantikā/ sakhi buddharakṣite, api punardrakṣyata eṣa jīvitapradāyī puṇḍarīkalocanaḥ/ api punarityautsukyātiśayo dyotyate// buddharakṣitā/ yadi daivamanukūlayiṣyati/ atha mādhavaḥ samadhigatamālatīpradānaniścayī nairāśyasaṃjātamudvegātiśayaṃ vivṛṇvannāha - cirāditi/ cirādanuvṛtto bisinīsūtravanmṛṇālatantuvadbhiduraḥ svameva bhidyamāna āśātantustatprāptyāśaiva tanturidānīṃ truṭatu chinno bhavatu/ priyatamāsamāgamāśā durbalaiveyantaṃ kālamanuvṛttā sedānīṃ chinnetyarthaḥ/ tathedānīṃ mahānādhirmānasī vyathā sa eva vyādhirniravadhirniravasaraḥ prasaratu/ mālatīprāptilakṣaṇamavadhiṃ pratīkṣya kathaṃcidādhiḥ soḍha idānīṃ tasyāvadherabhāvaniścayānnirmaryādo vijṛmbhatāmityarthaḥ/ tathā pāriplavasya cittacāñcalyasyāratirūpasya dhurotkarṣo mayyavyājaṃ niṣkapaṭam/ visrambheṇeti yāvat/ pratiṣṭhāṃ sthitiṃ vrajatu/ mālatīprāptyāśayā ālambanabhūtāyā apagamādvātyākṣiptatūlavadapratiṣṭhāṃ prāpnotvityarthaḥ/ tathā vidhiḥ sthairyaṃ dhattām/ madanaśca kṛtakṛtyo bhavatu/ ubhābhyāmapi kartavyasya mahataḥ prayojanasya mahatā prayatnena nirvartitatvādityarthaḥ/ jīvanamitaḥ paraṃ kṣaṇamātramapi durlabhamiti bhāvaḥ/ ekasminkārye'nekakāraṇasamuccayātsamuccayālaṃkāraḥ//3//
