 atha veti/ svayameva svātmānaṃ samāśvāsayati - samāneti/ samānapremāṇaṃ tulyānurāgamasulabhaṃ parādhīnatvena duṣprāpaṃ janaṃ prārthitavato mama vidhau vāmārambhe vakrodyoga eṣā pariṇatiriyaṃ vipattirūpā daśā samucitaiva/ tato na mayā vaiklavyamaṅgīkāryamityeva yadyapi hṛdayaṃ vyavasthāpayāmi tathāpyasmindānaśravaṇasamaye nandanāya rājñā mālatī dattetyetadvārtākarṇanasamaye pravigalatprabhaṃ tatkṣaṇaprakṣaritakānti ata eva prātaścandradyuti asyā vadanaṃ māmantardahati/ vārtāśravaṇamātreṇaivāsyā īdṛśyavasthā/ niṣpanne tu tatprayojane kimasyā bhaviṣyatītyeṣa eva mama vyathāheturnāsmadvipattiniścaya ityarthaḥ//4//

 kāmandakī/ durmanāyamāno durmanasko bhavan/ vatsā mālatī ca/ nirāśā prāṇitīti yattadeva duṣkaram/ tādṛśī kāpyavasthā vartate yajjīvanameva maraṇāpekṣayā kutsanīyam/ aśakyapratīkāraścāsāvanarthaḥ kapaṭasaṃvidhānamantareṇa/ tacca duṣkaramiti mamaiva cittavyākṣepo balavānityarthaḥ/ bhavatu tāvat/ dhārṣṭhyāvalambanena samāśvāsayāmīti prakāśamāha - pṛcchāmīti/ āyuṣmanniti saṃbodhanena mayi vartamānāyāṃ jīvanasaṃdeho na kartavya iti sūcayati/ pūrvāpekṣayedānīmudvegātiśayasya kiṃ vā kāraṇamiti pṛcchāmi/ etāvantaṃ kālaṃ bhūrivasureva mahyaṃ sutāṃ dāsyatīti niścitya kimevaṃ prāṇadhāraṇaṃ kṛtaṃ tadetatpṛcchāmi kathaya/ tathā cedidānīṃ yuktaḥ paritāpātiśayaḥ/ tenaivānyasmai tasyāḥ pradānāditi bhāvaḥ// mādhavaḥ - salajjamiti/ bālacāpalena kevalaṃ bhavānasyāmanuraktaḥ/ nahi bhūrivasunā svasutāṃ dāsyāmītyāhūto bhavānatrāgata iti kāmandakīvacanābhiprāyamavagamya salajjamāhetyarthaḥ/ nahi nahīti kutastasya dāsyāmīti vacanam/ sa tāvadasmannāmāpi na jānātītyāśayaḥ// kāmandakī/ tarhi pūrvāvasthāyā bhūrivasurna parihīyate nānyathā bhavati/ pūrvamapi bhūrivasurdāsyatīti na tava pratyāśā/ idānīmapi sā nāstīti/ kimidānīmudvegādhikye kāraṇam/ bhavatoranyonyānurāgo'pi pūrvāvasthāto na parihīyate/ yadvaśādubhayorjīvitadhāraṇamiyantaṃ kālaṃ tato nedānīṃ kiṃcidapi nūtanamasti/ kimiti saṃtāpa iti bhāvaḥ// makarandaḥ/ yadyapi bhūrivasunā mādhavāya sutāṃ dāsyāmīti noktaṃ, na tadvacanāśayā cānena sthitaṃ, tathāpi mālatyāḥ pūrvaṃ pitrā kasmaicidapyapratipādanādātmanaśca yogyatāsaṃbhāvanādāśāmātraṃ sthitam/ idānīṃ tu pūrvaṃ rājñā yācito nandanāya dattavāniti vārtāṃ śrutvā bhagnāśatvādaniṣṭamāśaṅkyata ityasti pūrvottarayoḥ kālayorvaiṣamyamityarthaḥ// kāmandakī - jānāmīti/ astyevaiṣā vārtā/ tathāpi mālatīṃ dattavato rājñaḥ parasvapratipādane'dhikārābhāvādadhikāravatā ca pitrā manaḥpūrvakamadattatvādidānīmapi pūrvavadāśāstyeveti naitāvatā bhetavyamityarthaḥ/ kathaṃ pitrā niścitaṃ na dattetyāśaṅkya tadvacanaparyālocanayāpi varītuṃ tāvadastyeṣā prasiddhirityāha - idaṃ tāvaditi// makarandaḥ/ astyetat/ mayāpi śrutamityarthaḥ/ adyatano'pi vṛttānto rājñā svayameva mālatī dattā na tu pitrā tatpradānādhikāriṇetyevaṃrūpaḥ śruta evetyāha// kāmandakī - adya ceti/ nanu yadyapi svayamevādya rājñā dattā na bhūrivasunā tathāpi pūrvameva prabhavatītyādivākyena tasyāpyanumatiḥ pratīyata ityāśaṅkya tadvacanasya chalātmakatayā vañcanārthaṃ prayuktatvānna tena vyavahāraśuddhiriti vaktuṃ prathamaṃ lokavyavahārasya pramāṇatvaṃ darśayati - tadvatseti/ dehināṃ vyavahārarūpāṇi tantrāṇi kuṭumbakṛtyāni/ ``tantraṃ kuṭumbakṛtye syāt'' iti halāyudhaḥ/ vāci pratiṣṭhā sthitiryeṣāṃ tāni/ vacanamātranibandhanānītyarthaḥ/ puṇyāpuṇyahetavo vyavasthāḥ ``satyaṃ vadennānṛtaṃ vadet'' ityādayaḥ śāstrīyā maryādā vācyevāyatante vāgāyattā eva bhavanti/ vāṅmūla eva sarvo'pi śubhāśubhaheturvyavahāra ityarthaḥ/ iyaṃ tu bhūrivasorvākchalātmikā na tu śuddhetyāha - sā ceti/ anṛtātmikaivopacārokticāturyeṇāpātataḥ satyavatpratibhāse'pi vicāryamāṇe paramārthadṛṣṭyā mithyābhūtaiva/ kuta ityata āha - na khalviti/ īśvaratvāttasya parakīyakanyakāsvapi svātantryamastītyata āha - kanyaketi/ nṛpatayaḥ pramāṇaṃ paricchettāraḥ/ svatantrakartāra iti yāvat/ ityevaṃvidho dharmācāro dhārmikāṇāmācārastasya samayastatpravartakaṃ śāstram/ kvacidapi na dṛṣṭamiti śeṣaḥ/ tasmātpitrā cāpradānādrājñaścānadhikārādrājakartṛkapradānaṃ lokaśāstraviruddhamityetadavasthitamityāha - tasmāditi/ tadasyā vārtāyāḥ paspaśaprāyakatvena naitāvatā nairāśyaṃ bhāvanīyamityāśayaḥ/ kiṃca mayi jīvantyāmapi kathaṃ bhavadvyatiriktagāminī syāditi viśvāsajananārthaṃ sāhaṃkāramāha - kathaṃ ceti/ apramattaivāhaṃ bhavatsaṃghaṭane varte/ tannāniṣṭamāśaṅkanīyamityarthaḥ/ tadevāha - mā vāmiti/ asyāṃ tvayi ca yatpāpamaniṣṭaṃ viśaṅkyaṃ yuvābhyāmanyonyamasmadādibhiśca ``prema paśyati bhayānyapade'pi'' iti nītyā premavaśādasthāne'pyaniṣṭaśaṅkibhirāśaṅkanīyaṃ tadvāṃ yuvayoḥ sapatneṣvapi mābhūt aniṣṭaṃ hi śatrugāmitveneṣyate/ idaṃ tu teṣvapi nāpekṣituṃ yuktamityavasthādausthyātiśayoktiḥ/ tasmātsarvathāntato gatvā prāṇavyayenāpi bhavatsaṃgamanāya yatno mayā vidheyaḥ/ kārya ityarthaḥ/ iyaṃ ca yatnākhyā dvitīyāvasthā/ yathā - ``apaśyataḥ phalaprāptiṃ yo vyāpāraḥ phalaṃ prati/ paraṃ tvautsukyagamanaṃ sa yatna iti kathyate//'' iti//5//

 makarandaḥ/ api cetyādi/ saṃsārādvirataṃ rāgadveṣavāsanāvivarjitamapi cittaṃ dravayati kṛpārdraṃ karoti/ dayeti/ dayānukampā, snehaḥ premākhyo bhāvaḥ/ ato dayāsnehaparavaśatvāttvadantaḥkaraṇasya pravrajyāsamaye saṃnyāsasaṃpradāye sulabho yo'ṣṭāṅgayogābhyāsādirācārastasya vimukho virodhī/ ayaṃ yatnaḥ prasakto balādāpatitaḥ/ aparaṃ punaritaḥ paraṃ tu daivamanayorbhāgadheyaṃ prabhavati/ tvayā tu yāvatkartavyaṃ tāvatkriyata ityarthaḥ/ idaṃ cānunayarūpaṃ paryupāsanaṃ nāmāṅgam/ yathā - ``anunītiḥ paryupāstiḥ'' iti//6//

 nepathye/ bhagavati kāmandaki, eṣā devī vijñāpayati yathā mālatīṃ gṛhītvā tvaritamāgaccheti// mālatīmādhavau sakaruṇaṃ parasparāniṣṭaśaṅkayā sānurāgamanyonyabhāvādhigamenāvalokayataḥ// mādhavaḥ/ mādhavasya duḥkhaikabhājanasya mametyarthāntarasaṃkramitavācyatā/ mālatyā samaṃ lokayātrā darśanasaṃlāpādirūpo'nuraktajanavyavahāra etāvatīyatyeva parisamāptā/ tadetatkaṣṭamityarthaḥ/ aho āścarye/ nu vitarke/ khalu prasiddhau/ bhoḥ saṃbodhane/ he hṛdaya, āścaryaṃ khalu vidherdurvilasitamiti vitarkayāmītyarthaḥ/ tadeva durvilasitamāha - suhṛdiveti/ asau vidhiḥ prathamaṃ suhṛnmitramivaikarasāṃ tadekasvabhāvāṃ prakārāntaraśūnyāmanukūlatāṃ prastutakāryānuguṇyaṃ prakaṭīkṛtya punaḥ sa evākāṇḍe'navasare yadvivartanaṃ viruddhāvṛttiḥ prātikūlyena vyavaharaṇaṃ tena dāruṇo vidhiḥ/ manorujaṃ cittarogaṃ praviśinaṣṭi/ prayojanaṃ vighaṭayyādhimātramavaśeṣayatītyarthaḥ/ vidhinindayā viṣādo vyajyate//7//

 mālatī/ mahānubhāva locanānandakara, etāvaddṛṣṭo'si/ na punardṛśyase mama jīvanābhāvādityāśayaḥ/ locanayorevānandastvayā kṛtaḥ/ manasastu mahatī vyathaiva saṃpāditetyasya dhvaniḥ/ idaṃ ca pratyakṣaniṣṭhuratvādvajraṃ nāmāṅgam/ yathā - ``pratyakṣaniṣṭhuraṃ vajram'' iti/ lavaṅgikā/ hā dhik/ śarīrasaṃśayameva naḥ priyasakhī samāropitāmātyena/ tadidānīṃ bhagavati, tvameva gurujanaspṛhāṃ vihāya mālatīprāṇatrāṇe tvaritaṃ yatasvetyāśayaḥ// mālatī/ pariṇatamidānīṃ jīvitatṛṣṇāyāḥ phalam/ nirvyūḍhaṃ ca niṣkaruṇatayā tātasya kāpālikatvam/ kāpālika eva paraṃ strībālādivadhe niṣkaruṇaḥ/ tena pañcamāṅkakṛtyaṃ ca kiṃcitsūcitam/ pariniṣṭhitaḥ parisamāptiṃ gamito daivahatakasya dāruṇasamārambhapariṇāmaḥ/ kamupālabhe mandabhāgyā/ kaṃ vāśaraṇāśaraṇaṃ pratipadye/ anena/ mādhavaṃ kāmandakīṃ ca prati kathaṃ yuvayoḥ satorapyahamīdṛśīṃ daśāmanubhavāmīti tadupakramyatāṃ yathocitaṃ prayatna iti sūcyate/ etatsūcanārthamevādhīrayāpi mālatyā mādhavasaṃnidhāne'pi nistrapībhūyākranditamiti mantavyam// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, ita itaḥ/ āgamyatāmiti śeṣaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ nūnamiti/ kathaṃ vā rājani pratipakṣe tadvirodhena bhagavatī kāryaṃ niṣpādayet/ tadasyā vacanamāśvāsanārthamevetyarthaḥ/ saṃśayitaṃ janmasāphalyaṃ mālatīprāptilakṣaṇaṃ yasya tādṛśaḥ saṃvṛtto'smi/ atra mālatīprāptireva jīvitasāphalyamityanena tadanurāgatadguṇātiśayaprakhyāpanātsātiśayavacanarūpaṃ puṣpaṃ nāmāṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``puṣpaṃ sātiśayaṃ vacaḥ'' iti/ idānīṃ jātyanucitenāpi kṣipraphalapradena bāhyāgamaprasiddhena sāhasikajanakṛtyena mahāmāṃsavikrayeṇāpi prayojanaṃ sādhanīyamiti vicintya nirdhārayati - na khalviti/ mahāmāṃsaṃ puruṣamāṃsam/ iyamapi dvitīyāvasthā draṣṭavyā/ vayasyeti/ api praśne/ utkaṇṭhata utsuko bhavati// makarandaḥ/ atha kim/ bāḍhamutkaṇṭhito'hamityarthaḥ/ tadevāha - taditi/ sarasaprahāraṃ saśoṇitaśārdūlakarajakṣataṃ māmālokyāvicāritavigalatkucāvaraṇāṃśukā/ anena tātkālikī tasyāḥ kāntiruktā/ trasto ya ekahāyanakuraṅgastasyeva vilolā dṛṣṭiryasyāḥ sā/ anena tasyā apyudvegapratīteranurāgātiśayaḥ prakhyāpyate/ amṛtasaṃvalitaiḥ sudhārasāplutairivāṅgaiḥ/ anena tadaṅgasaṃsparśasya paramasauhityahetukatvamuktam/ āśliṣṭavatyāliṅganaṃ kṛtavatīti yattadāliṅganaṃ kartṛ me manaḥ kṣipati/ vyathayatītyarthaḥ//8//

 mādhavaḥ/ neti/ buddharakṣitāyāḥ priyasakhī tvayā na durlabheti makarandamāśvāsayati/ adurlabhatvamevopapattyā nirdhārayati - api ceti/ pramathyeti/ maraṇasamaye tasyāḥ prāṇavipattikāle kravyādaṃ māṃsāśanaṃ vyāghraṃ pramathya nihatya rakṣitavatastādṛśavyasane prāṇatrāṇalakṣaṇamupakāraṃ kṛtavatastava pariṣvaṅgaṃ svayaṃgrahāśleṣaṃ labdhvā kathamivānyatra tvadvyatirikte ramatāmanuraktacittā bhavet/ tathā hi tadānīmeva svajīvitapradāyinastava madīyaṃ jīvitaṃ śarīraṃ ca samarpitamiti svayaṃgrahāliṅganena kathitam/ atastvadarpitajīviteyaṃ nānyatrānurajyata iti bhāvaḥ/ yathā mayā niścitaṃ tathā ca tadanurūpa eva tasyā nayanayorvyāpāro vyaktasnehaḥ prakaṭīkṛtapremātiśayastathā stimitatayā viṣayāntarāsaṃsparśena tatraiva nimagnatayā ramaṇīyo manoharastvayi viṣaye ciramabhūt/ sasnehāvalokanādeva niścīyate tasyāstvayyevāsaktirityarthaḥ/ idaṃ copapattyā nirṇayarūpamupanyāso nāmāṅgam/ yathā ``upapattikṛto yo'rthaḥ sa upanyāsa iṣyate'' iti//9//

 varadeti/ varadā sindhuśceti nadyau/ tayoḥ saṃbhedaḥ saṃgamaḥ// makarandaḥ/ vyatikaro'pi sa eva/ ya eṣa iti/ yo'yaṃ nadyoḥ saṃbhedaḥ/ jaleti/ puṇyatīrthatvādavagāhanārthamāgatābhirvadhūbhiḥ sakalapaurāṅganābhiḥ parigatataṭabhūmirvyāptatīrapradeśaḥ/ dṛśyata iti śeṣaḥ/ vadhūrviśinaṣṭi - snātamātrotthitābhiḥ snānasamanantarameva nirgatābhiḥ/ ata eva jalena nibiḍitamatyantasaṃśliṣṭaṃ yadvastraṃ tena vyaktāḥ samyagvibhāvyamānā nimnonnatapradeśā nābhimadhyakucamaṇḍalanitambajaghanādyavayasaṃniveśā yāsāṃ tāḥ/ tathā ramaṇīyakāñcanakalaśakāntimanta ābhogena paripūrṇatayā tuṅgāśca ye stanāstatra vinihitāni nyastāni hastasvastikāni vyatyastasaṃkucitasvastikākārahastayugalāni yāsāṃ tābhiriti tatkṣaṇasnātotthitavadhūsvabhāvoktiḥ/ atha ca snānārthamāgatābhirvadhūbhirityanena nānājātinagarāṅganāpratītervarṇasaṃhāro nāma pratimukhasaṃdheraṅgamiti draṣṭavyam/ yathā - ``varṇasaṃhāra ityukto nānājātisamāgamaḥ'' iti//10//


vicitravyañjanāprāṇā ślāghyaśabdārthabandhurāḥ/

jayantyabhinavollāsā bhavabhūtikavergiraḥ//

iti śrīmadbhāradvājakulatilakaparvatanāthayāyajūkanandanatripurārisūriviracitāyāṃ mālatīmādhavabhāvapradīpikāyāṃ caturthāṅkavivaraṇam//

 pañcamo'ṅkaḥ/

pañcabāṇasuhṛdvairivāmavāmetarāṅgakam/

vīraśṛṅgārayoraikyaṃ hṛdi sphuratu tanmahaḥ//

atha pūrvāṅkasūcitamahāmāṃsavikrayopayogivṛttāntaṃ sūcayitumekapātraprayojyaṃ śuddhaviṣkambhamārabhate - tata iti/ ekenāpi madhyapātreṇa śuddhaviṣkambho bhavatyeva/ yathāha - ``śuddho'nekena vaikena madhyapātreṇa yojitaḥ'' iti/ ākāśayāneneti yoginītvena khecaragamanādisiddhisaṃpatteḥ/ bhīṣaṇo bhayaṃkara ujjvalo dīptaśca veṣo yasyāḥ sā/ kapālakuṇḍaletyanvarthaṃ nāma// ṣaḍadhiketi/ śaktīnāṃ jñānecchākriyopalakṣitasvabhāvaparispandarūpāṇāṃ nāthaḥ prayoktā/ ata eva tābhiḥ pariṇaddho vyāptaḥ/ vicitraviśvaparispandasvatantranirmāteti yāvat/ yadvā śaktīnāṃ brāhmyādīnāṃ nāthaḥ/ ata eva tābhiḥ pariṇaddhaḥ svaprāṇeśvarabuddhyā praṇayādveṣṭitaḥ kāpālikadarśanapratipādyabhūtanātho jayati sarvotkarṣeṇa prakāśate/ tādṛśasyaiva namaskāryatvāt/ kīdṛśo'sāvityata āha - ṣaḍadhiketi/ ṣaḍadhikā daśa ṣoḍaśa yā nāḍyastāsāṃ yaccakraṃ maṇḍalaṃ piṇḍarūpaṃ tasya madhyaṃ hṛdayaṃ tatra sthita ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya saḥ/ nāḍīnāmanantatve'pi pradhānanāḍīnāmiḍādīnāṃ ṣoḍaśasaṃkhyātvātṣaḍadhikadaśetyuktam/ iḍādayo nāḍyaśca/ ``iḍā ca piṅgalā caiva suṣumnā ca parā smṛtā/ gāndhārī hastijihvā ca pūṣā vasuvaśā tathā// alambuṣā kuhūścaiva śaṅkhinī daśamī smṛtā/ tālujihvā ca jihvā ca vijayā kāmadā parā// amṛtā bahulā nāma nāḍyo vāyusamīritāḥ//'' iti/ aparimitasūkṣmanāḍīsahitapradhānaṣoḍaśanāḍīsamūha eva rudhiravasāmāṃsādidhātuparipūritaṃ tvagācchannaṃ piṇḍaśabdavācyaṃ śarīramiti yogamārgaḥ/ yadvā/ ṣaḍbhyaścakrebhyo'dhikasya/ saptamasyetyarthaḥ/ daśanāḍīcakraṃ daśanāḍyātmakaṃ cakraṃ nābhikamalarūpam/ yadvā/ ṣaḍadhikadaśanāḍīnāṃ ṣoḍaśanāḍīnāṃ cakraṃ ṣoḍaśanāḍyātmakaṃ cakraṃ kaṇṭhavilavartyanāhatākhyam/ tatra sthita upāsanāviśeṣārthaṃ saṃnidhāpita ātmā svarūpaṃ yasyetyarthaḥ/ tadyathā cakrāṇi yogaśāstre - ``caturdalaṃ syādādhāre svādhiṣṭhāne ca ṣaḍdalam/ nābhau daśadalopetaṃ sūryasaṃkhyadalaṃ hṛdi// kaṇṭhe syātṣoḍaśadalaṃ bhrūmadhye'śītisaṃmitam/ sahasradalamākhyātaṃ brahmarandhre tu paṅkajam// etāni sapta padmāni cakrasthāni pracakṣate//'' iti/ nāḍīcakrātmakakamalānāṃ nāḍya eva dalāni/ anena cāṇuraṅguṣṭhaparimāṇo vā jīva iti tanmatam/ tadvidāṃ tādṛgātmasvarūpadarśināṃ yogināṃ hṛdi vinihitaṃ rūpaṃ yasya saḥ/ tathāvidhaḥ sansiddhido bhuktimuktipradaḥ/ avicalitamanobhirviṣayāntaraparityāgena svaikatānamanaskaiḥ sādhakaiḥ svopāsakairyogibhirmṛgyamāṇo'nviṣyamāṇa iti/ atra nāḍīcakrādipadānāṃ kevalayogaśāstraprasiddhatvādapratītatvamapi svātmavimarśane na duṣṭatvaṃ kiṃtu guṇatvamevetyāhuḥ//1//

 iyamityādi/ nityamiti/ nityaṃ pratidinaṃ japopakramasamaye'nuṣṭheyamantrāṅgatvena yannyastaṃ tattatsthāneṣu ``hṛdayāya namaḥ, śirase svāhā'' ityādi ṣaḍaṅganyāsaprakāreṇa vinyastaṃ/ ṣaḍaṅgānāṃ hṛdayaśiraḥśikhākavacanetrāstrāṇāṃ cakraṃ saṅghastatra nihitastanmadhyagatatvena dhyātaḥ/ ``māṃ ko'pi mantrādhidevatātmakatvenātmānaṃ dhyātvā mantramanutiṣṭheta'' ityāgamaḥ/ ata eva hṛtpadmasyahṛdayakamalasya madhya uditaṃ prakāśamānaṃ śivarūpiṇamātmānaṃ paśyantī/ iyamahaṃ layavaśādūrdhvamākṛṣṭasya vāyosteṣu teṣu nāḍīniveśeṣu vṛttinirodho gaganagamanahetubhūto yogaśāstraprasiddho layaḥ/ tadvaśāddhetornāḍīnāṃ pārthivāpyāgneyavāyavyanābhasākhyānāṃ pañcabhūtaprakṛtikānāṃ nāḍīviśeṣāṇāmudayasāmyāvasthātirodhānakrameṇa tattadbhūtāṃśākarṣaṇasādhanena jagatojaṅgamasya jantuvargasya yānyārambhakāṇi pañcāmṛtāni na mriyante nātyantaṃ vinaśyanti kāryakāraṇarūpeṇa sarvadāvasthānādityamṛtāni pañcamahābhūtāni teṣāmākarṣaṇāt/ yadvā - ``viṭ ca mūtramasṛṅmajjā śukraṃ pañcāmṛtaṃ smṛtam'' iti/ teṣāmākarṣaṇādaprāptotpatanaśramānutpannākāśodgamanaprayāsā/ satyata evāgrenabho nabhaso'gre'mbhomuco meghānvighaṭayantyapasārayantīdānīmabhyāgatā/ uddeśyaṃ karālāyatanaṃ prāptetyarthaḥ//2//

 uddhṛtteti/ udvṛttordhvaṃ vikṣiptā tataḥ skhalitā ca yā kapālaireva grathitā kaṇṭhamālā grīvālaṃkārastatra saṃghaṭṭaḥ kapālānāṃ parasparābhighātastena kvaṇitāḥ saṃjātakvaṇāḥ/ śabdāyamānā iti yāvat/ karālā danturāḥ kiṃkiṇyaḥ kaṇṭhamālāsthāpitāḥ kṣudraghaṇṭikā yatra saḥ/ gaganatalaprayāṇavego mayi paryāptaṃ paripūrṇaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ manoharaṃ ḍāmaratvamaudbhaṭyaṃ saṃdhatte/ saṃpādayatītyarthaḥ//3//

 tadeva ramaṇīyaḍāmaratvaṃ prakaṭayati - viṣvagiti/ viṣvaksarvato vṛttiravasthānaṃ yasya sa tādṛśo jaṭānāṃ bhāro dṛḍhataragranthibandho'pi vegavaśātpracalati/ tathā khaṭvāṅge naddhā ghaṇṭā saṃskāreṇa śabdajanakābhighātādijanyenābhihanyamānagatenātiśayena kāryaikaparikalpena janito yaḥ kvāṇo raṇaraṇadhvanistena dīrghaṃ yathā tathā/ paṭu/ kṛtā āvṛttiḥ punaḥpunarabhyāso yathā syāttatheti kriyāviśeṣaṇatrayam/ raṭati dhvanati/ tathā vivṛtāni yāni śavaśirāṃsi teṣāṃ yā śreṇī tasyāḥkuñjeṣu sarandhrakuhareṣu guñjañśabdāyamāna uttāla udbhaṭaḥ kiṃkiṇīnāmanavarataraṇatkārasyāvicchinnanikvāṇasya heturvāyurvegotpanno'nilaḥ patākāṃ khaṭvāṅgāvabaddhāmūrdhvaṃ yathā syādevaṃ dhūnoti kampayati//4//

 parikramyetyādi/ idaṃ ca tatkarālāyatanam/ kīdṛśam/ śmaśānavāṭasya pitṛvanapradeśasya nedīyo'tisaṃnihitam/ śmaśānavāṭaṃ viśinaṣṭi - purāṇena nimbatailenāktasya paribhṛjyamānasya rasonakasya laśunasya/ ``laśunaṃ gṛñjā[ja]nāriṣṭamahākandarasonakāḥ'' ityamaraḥ/ yo raso niryāsastasya gandha iva gandho yeṣāṃ taiścitādhūmairgaganaṃkaṣairadhastādbhūtale vibhāvitasyānumitasya mahato vistīrṇasyeti/ yatra karālāyatane paryavasitamantrasādhanasya parisamāpitapuraścaraṇasya saviśeṣaṃ pūrvāpekṣayā viśeṣasahakṛtaṃ yathā syāttathā pūjāsaṃbhāraḥ pūjopakaraṇakalāpaḥ saṃnidhāpanīya upasthāpanīyaḥ/ viśeṣaḥ ko vetyapekṣāyāmāha - kathitamiti/ prāgupayācitaṃ pūrvaṃ prārthitaṃ siddhe'sminmantre bhagavatyai strīratnamupahārīkariṣyāmīti saṃkalpitam/ upahartavyamānetavyam/ tatstrīratnaṃ cātraiva saṃnihite nagare viditaṃ sarvajanaprasiddhamāsta iti// sakautukamiti/ etādṛśasukumāramadhurākārasya kathaṃ niśāyāmatra nirnimittaṃ saṃcaraṇamiti sakautukam/ atigambhīramadhurākṛtirgāmbhīryamādhuryaguṇaśālinyākṛtiryasya saḥ/ ``bhīśokakrodhaharṣeṣu gāmbhīryaṃ nirvikāratā/ sarvāvasthāsu mādhuryaṃ priyāṇāṃ mṛdukāritā//'' uttambhito jūṭīkṛtya baddhaḥkuṭilaḥ karālaḥ kuntalabhāraḥ keśakalāpo yasya saḥ/ kṛpāṇapāṇiḥ khaḍgahastaḥ śmaśānamavatarati/ tatko'pyayaṃ mṛduprakṛtirapi kutaściddhetoraciraparigṛhītasāhasavyāpāraḥ/ tadeṣa vicāraṇīya ityāśayaḥ/ tameva nirvarṇayati - ya eṣaḥ/ kuvalayeti/ svabhāvata indīvaradalanīlo'pi virahādinimittasaṃskārarāhityātparidhūsaraṃ vivarṇamaṅgaṃ dadhadbibhrāṇaḥ tathā mahānāyakatayā vinayagāmbhīryadhairyaśālitvenātrāpi śmaśānapradeśe lalito gāmbhīryamanoharo niḥśaṅkaścaraṇavinyāso yasya saḥ/ śrīmānmahābhāgyadyotakakāntiviśeṣayukto mṛgāṅkanibhānanaḥ pūrṇacandramaṇḍalasaṃnibhavadano ya eṣa paridṛśyate'to nedamasya samucitaṃ karma/ kiṃtu ``na sāhasamanāruhya naro bhadrāṇi paśyati'' iti nyāyena jīvitavyayenāpi sādhanīyaṃ kiṃcidabhilaṣitaṃ sādhayitumevaṃ sāhasamavalambitamiti bhāvaḥ/ yasyāsyaprakṣaradrudhirakardamo lalannarajāṅgalo vilasannaramāṃsakhaṇḍaḥ prakaṭīkṛtasāhaso vāmaḥ pāṇireva kevalaṃ vinayaṃ haratyavinayaṃ dyotayati/ samāṃsakhaṇḍavāmakaradarśanādevāsya sāhasaṃ pratīyate/ tadatiriktāvayavadarśane tu praśānto vinītākāra eva pratīyate/ na sāhasamityarthaḥ//5//

 nirūpya niścitya/ sa eṣa iti/ yaḥ kāmandakīsuhṛdo devarātasya kuṇḍineśvarasacivasya putro bāla evāsmābhirdṛṣṭaḥ sa evāsau kāryavaśādihāgataḥ kiṃcidatiduḥsādhakaṃ kāryaṃ sādhayitumavalambitasāhaso mahāmāṃsasya vikretā bhūtveha saṃcarati/ yujyate cāsyaitanmanuṣyaduṣkaraṃ karma/ yadasaumahāyoginyāḥ kāmandakyāḥ suhṛtputra  iti bhāvaḥ/ paṇayitā vyavahartā/ vikreteti yāvat/ ``paṇa vyavahāre stutau ca'' iti dhātostṛci rūpaṃ paṇayiteti/ tatkimanena/ ayamapi svakṛtyaṃ nirvartayatu/ ahamapi madiṣṭaṃ saṃpādayāmītyarthaḥ/ gurvājñānuṣṭhānasya kālo'pyanuguṇo'yaṃ prāpta ityāha - vigalitaprāya iti/ nirvṛttakalpa ityarthaḥ/ vyomna iti/ vyomnaḥ paryantā avanītalaprānteṣvavanatāstiraskariṇīvatpratibhāsamānā viyattaṭāḥ/ tamovallarībhirandhakāralatābhistāpicchagucchāvalibhiriva tamālastabakapaṅktibhiriva vriyanta ācchādyante/ tathā pṛthivīprāntavṛttyā paritaḥ paryantadeśanimajjanakrameṇa nūtane pratyagratayā kaluṣe payasi majjatīva/ andhakārāvṛtā payorāśinimagnevetyutprekṣate/ tathā triyāmā rātriḥ prārambhe svapraveśasamaye'pi vātyānāṃ maṇḍalākāravātānāṃ samyagvegena viṣvaksarvato vitatā vistāritā tato valayitā saṃjātavalayākārā sphītā pracurā yā dhūmyā dhūmamaṇḍalī tasyāḥ prakāśo yatra taṃ nijaṃ svakīyaṃ nīlimānaṃ taruṇayati/ pramukha eva saṃtamasanailyaṃ yauvanadaśāṃ prāpayatītyarthaḥ//6//

 evaṃ mahāmāṃsavikrayārthaṃ mādhavapraveśaṃ, karālāyatane pūjārthamaghoraghaṇṭasya saṃnidhānam, tatra mālatyānayanaṃ ca sūcayitvā kapālakuṇḍalā niṣkrāntā// iti śuddhaviṣkambhaḥ// tata ityādi/ yathānirdiṣṭaḥ kapālakuṇḍalayā nirūpitarūpaḥ// mādhavaḥ - sāśaṃsamiti/ yaduddeśena mahāmāṃsavikrayākhyaṃ karma cikīrṣitaṃ tatprayojanāśaṃsāpūrvakamāhetyarthaḥ/ premeti/ śṛṅgārasya sthāyībhāvasya ratirūpasya prakarṣalakṣaṇāvasthāviśeṣaḥ premetyucyate/ premamānapraṇayasnehānurāgā raterevāvasthābhedāḥ/ yaduktam - ``iyamaṅkuritā premṇā mānātpallavitā bhavet/ sakorakā praṇayataḥ snehātkusumitā bhavet// rāgātphalavatī seyamanurāgeṇa bhujyate//'' iti ``parasparāśrayaghanaṃ nirūḍhaṃ bhāvabandhanam/ yadekāyattatopādhi tatpremeti nigadyate//'' iti/ tenārdrāḥ sarasāḥ ānukūlyaśālinya iti yāvat/ uktaṃ ca - ``yadyadasyāḥ priyo vakti tasya tasyāśukāritām/ yogyatāmārdratāmāhurmanaḥkāluṣyanāśinīm//'' iti/ tathā praṇayaṃ spṛśanti tenānurajyanta iti praṇayaspṛśaḥ/ praṇayo yathā - ``upacārairmitho yūnoryadbāhyābhyantarābhidhaiḥ/ prema nītaṃ prakarṣaṃ cetsa eva praṇayaḥ smṛtaḥ//'' iti/ tathā paricayāttasyaiva praṇayasya punaḥpunaravalokanasaṃbhāṣaṇasparśādibhiḥ paripoṣāt/ ``videśasthe mṛte vāpi durbale pratiyogini/ dharmiṇi kleśakārī yaḥ saprauḍhasneha ucyate//'' ityevaṃrūpaprauḍhasnehātmakatāpattau satyāṃ tata udgāḍhaḥ prauḍhaḥ kadācidapyanuparamo māñjiṣṭhākhyo rāgaścittānurañjakasnehaprakarṣo yastasya udaya udreko yāsu tāḥ/ rāgo yathā - ``sa eṣa cedguṇadravyadeśakālādibhirhṛdi/ rajyate dīpyate citte sa rāga iti kathyate//'' māñjiṣṭho rāgo yathā - ``atīva śobhate yastu nāpaiti kṣālito'pi san/ sa eva kavibhiḥ sarvairmāñjiṣṭho rāga ucyate//'' ayameva ca śreṣṭho rāgaḥ/ yathāha - ``śreṣṭho māñjiṣṭharāgaḥ syānnīlīrāgastu madhyamaḥ/ kusumbharāgaḥ kavibhiradhamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ//'' iti sthiro'pyaśobhano nīlīrāgaḥ/ āpātaramaṇīyaḥ kusumbharāgaḥ kṣaṇikaḥ/ nisargamadhurāḥ svabhāvaramaṇīyā mugdhadṛśastasyāstāstāstathātathānubhūtāḥ kevalamanubhavaikasārāśceṣṭāḥ kaṭākṣanikṣepabhrūkarmagātravalanaprabhṛtayaḥ śṛṅgārānuguṇā gātrārambhāḥ yathā - ``līlāvilāso vicchittirvibhramaḥ kilakiñcitam/ moṭṭāyitaṃ kuṭṭamitaṃ vivvokalalite tathā// vihṛtaṃ ceti vijñeyā yoṣitāṃ daśa gātrajāḥ//'' ityevaṃ sūkṣmadṛṣṭyā parigaṇitā mayi bhaveyurityāśaṃsāyāṃ liṅ/ yāsu ceṣṭāsvāśaṃsāmātreṇa parikalpitāsvapi kathametadīyapremārdrakaṭākṣagocaro bhaveyam, kathamamṛtarasamadhuramākūtagarbhamasyāḥ praṇayavacanamākarṇayeyam, kathametadīyagāḍhāliṅganasauhityasudhārasasarasyātmānaṃ nimajjayeyamityādiprakāreṇa manorathamātropasthāpitāsvapi na tu paramārthato'nubhūyamānāsvantaḥkaraṇasya layo vilīnatā tadekanimagnatvam/ taptāyaḥpiṇḍapāthonyāyena tanmayībhāva iti yāvatsa bhavati/ kimu vaktavyaṃ vastuto'nubhūyamānāsviti bhāvaḥ/ kīdṛśo layaḥ/ bāhyakaraṇānāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ yo vyāpāraḥ svasvaviṣayagrahaṇahetuśaktirūpastaṃ roddhuṃ śīlamasyāstīti tathā/ antaḥkaraṇasya ceṣṭāsveva vilīnatvāttadadhiṣṭhānābhāvāduparatavyāpārāṇīndriyāṇi bhavantyeva/ mūrchādilayādvaiṣamyamāha - ānandasāndra ānandaikapracuraḥ pratyagātmana iva brahmaṇyānandātmake vilayo mokṣadaśāyāṃ tadvadityarthaḥ/ atra cābhilāṣavipralambhaśṛṅgāraḥ prādhānyena vyajyata ityabhidhāmūlasaṃlakṣyakramavyaṅgyorasadhvaniḥ//7//

 atra ca pūrvaṃ śaṃkarapurodyānavṛttāntena dṛṣṭasya samanantaraṃ nandanapradānaniścayena naṣṭasya bījasya ``premārdrāḥ'' ityārabhya ``līneva pratibimbiteva'' ityantena tataḥ ``upapannastāvat'' ityādinā tatratatrāṅkaparisamāptiparyantaṃ muhurmuhuranveṣaṇādanusaṃdhānarūpāyāḥ ṣaṣṭhāṅke mālatyāḥ prathamāvalokasamayādārabhya ``vistāriṇaḥ'' ityādināṅkasamāptiparyantaṃ nirūpyamāṇāyāḥ prāptyāśāyāḥ ``vatsa makaranda, mālatīnepathyena parivṛtaḥ pravartasvātmanaḥ pariṇayāya'' ityādinā sa prathamāṅkaparisamāptiparyantaṃ nirūpyamāṇāyā mālatīrūpadhāriṇo makarandasyopanāyakasya tasyādhikārikavṛttāntopayogivyāpārarūpāyāḥ patākāyāśca samanvayātkṛtrimapariṇayalakṣaṇāvāntarakāryasamanvitānāmaṅkatrayapratipādyānāṃ mahāmāṃsavikrayamālatīprāṇatrāṇāghoraghaṇṭasaṃharaṇanagaradevatāgarbhagṛhavṛttāntakṛtakamālatīrūpamakarandavyāpārarūpāṇāṃ kathāṃśānāṃ saṃbandharūpastatratatrodbhāvayiṣyamāṇabhūtāharaṇādyekādaśāṅgasamanvito garbhasaṃdhirnirūpitaḥ kavineti draṣṭavyam/ tallakṣaṇāni - ``garbhastu dṛṣṭanaṣṭasya bījasyānveṣaṇaṃ muhuḥ/ samanvayādbhavedeṣa prāptyāśāyāḥ patākayā// aṅgānyekādaśaitasya garbhasaṃdheryathākramam//'' iti/ ``upāyāpāyacintābhyāṃ prāptyāśāprāptisaṃbhavaḥ/ nāyakasya kathāmadhye tatsamānasya yā kathā/ aphalodayasaṃbandhā sā patāketi kīrtitā'' iti saṃkṣepaḥ/ idānīṃ sarvāsvapi ceṣṭāsu śreṣṭhāmāśleṣarūpāmeva ceṣṭāṃ prārthayate - atimuktaketi/ atimuktakairvāsantikākusumaiḥ/ ``atimuktaḥ puṇḍrakaḥ syādvāsantī mādhavīlatā'' ityamaraḥ/ antarāntarā niveśitairgrathitā yā kesarāvalī bakulakusumapracurā mālā tasyāḥ satatādhivāsena subhagāvarpitau stanau madīyorasi niveśitau stanau yasminkarmaṇi taditi kriyāviśeṣaṇam/ priyayā kaṇṭhajāhe madīyagrīvāmūle/ ``tasya pākamūle pīlvādikarṇādibhyaḥ kuṇabjāhacau'' iti karṇāditvātkaṇṭhaśabdānmūle jāhacpratyayaḥ/ tatra viniveśitaṃ svakīyamānanaṃ yasya so'ham/ āliṅganasamaye strīṇāṃ mugdhānāṃ svabhāvo'yaṃ yatpriyakaṇṭhamūle svaśiro bhugnaṃ nidadhata iti tadaṅgena madaṅgasya parivṛttiṃ vinimayaṃ prāpnuyām/ āliṅganavaśāttadaṅgaṃ madadhīnaṃ madaṅgaṃ ca tadadhīnaṃ bhavedityarthaḥ/ parivṛttirevālaṃkāraḥ/ kecit ``avimuktaka-'' iti paṭhitvā ``avimuktakā anujjhitā kadācidapi mayā grathitatvenādarādyā kesarāvalīti vyācakṣate//8//

 athaveti/ ``manorathānāmagatirna vidyate'' iti nītyā kevalaṃ prārthyate/ atidurlabhatvānna saṃbhāvyate/ idaṃ tu saṃbhāvyata iti prathamaṃ tadeva prārthaye/ kiṃ tadityata āha - saṃbhūyeti/ yatra priyatamāmukha ālokapathāvatāriṇi dṛṅmārge nipatite sati sukhāni loke yāvanti sukhasādhanāni santi tatsarvānubhavajanmānandaviśeṣāḥ saṃbhūyeva rāśībhūyeva cetasi paraṃ niratiśayaṃ bhūmānamanyonyamelanajanitamatibahutvamātanvate vistārayanti/ netrayordraṣṭuścakṣuṣorutsavo harṣaheturyadviṣayo vyāpāraḥ/ ``utsūte harṣamityeṣa utsavaḥ parikīrtitaḥ'' ityuktatvāt/ ratimicchāviśeṣātmikāṃ śṛṅgārasthāyinīṃ cittavṛttiṃ prastautyutpādayati/ ``ratiricchā bhavedyūnorubhayaprārthanātmikā'' ityuktatvāt/ darśanamātreṇaiva niratiśayānando ratiśca nāyikābhilāṣarūpā bhavatītyarthaḥ/ kiṃca yanmukhaṃ bālendoḥ kalaṅkarāhityasaukumāryamanoharasya kalāḥ pratimāsamekaikakrameṇotpādyamānāstāsāmuccayo rāśistasmādupacitaiḥ saṃgṛhītaiḥ sāraiḥ śreṣṭhāṃśairutpāditamivetyutprekṣā/ niratiśayāhlādahetutvena nairmalyaprasādādiguṇayogāttadanaṅgasya maṅgalagṛhaṃ tadadhiṣṭhānenānaṅgasya tribhuvanavijayalakṣaṇamaṅgalaprāpteḥ/ tadetattasyāḥ priyāyā mukhaṃ bhūyo'pi paśyeyam/ puṇyaparipākavaśādidaṃ tāvadastu tāvatāpi caritārtha evāhamityāśayaḥ/ atiśayoktyutprekṣārūpakāni vācyālaṃkārāḥ/ prasiddhasukhasādhanābhāve'pi kāraṇāntarādeva tatsamastasukhotpattervibhāvanā saṃbhūyevetyatra vyajyate/ nedaṃ mukhamitaraśarīravadbhautikopādānakaṃ kiṃ tvanyadeva lokottaramasyopādānamityapahnavaḥ/ kalaṅkaśūnyasārāṃśanirmitatvādasya candramaṇḍalādhikyapratītervyatirekaḥ/ candrāderapyumānatvāyogyatvādanyasya cābhāvādasyedamevopamānamityananvayaśca yadbālendukaletyatra vyajyata ityādi nirūpaṇīyaṃ sahṛdayaiḥ/ rasadhvanistūkta evetyalaṃ prapañcena//9//

 nanu bhāvanāvaśādanavaratamadhyakṣamīkṣamāṇāyāṃ priyatamāyāṃ kimiti punaḥ saṃdarśanaṃ tvayā prārthyata ityāśaṅkya bhāvanāprasūtātsaṃdarśanātprārthanīyasaṃdarśanasya viśeṣamāha - yaditi/ yadyapi yādṛśī preyasī pūrvamanubhūtā tādṛśyevānyūnātirekeṇedānīmapi dṛśyate/ tatra kaścidviśeṣastathāpyadhunā saṃdarśanamidānīṃtanaṃ bhāvanāprasūtaṃ darśanaṃ satyaṃ pāramārthikaṃ na bhavati/ prārthanīyaṃ tu satyamityetāvānsvalpo'pi viśeṣaḥ prārthanīyasaṃdarśanasyedānīṃtanasaṃdarśanādbhedo'stīti yataḥ/ tasmāttatprārthyata ityarthaḥ/ nanu kathametāvāneva viśeṣo darśanasyaivedānīmabhāvāditi śaṅkāṃ svasaṃvedanasiddhatanmayībhāvopanyāsena śamayati - mama hi saṃpratīti/ sātiśayo dṛḍhatarasaṃskārādhānasāmarthyalakṣaṇātiśayasahito yaḥ prāktana upalambhaḥ pūrvānubhavaḥ/ āpātadarśanaṃ hi kadācinna saṃskāramādadhyāt/ āhito vā saṃskāro dārḍhyābhāvānna smṛtiṃ janayet/ bubhutsāpūrvakaṃ tu saṃpāditaḥ sādhīyānanubhavaḥ saṃskārotpādanāvyabhicārī tadutpāditaśca saṃskāro dṛḍhatvādavaśyaṃ smṛtiṃ janayatīti sātiśayetyuktam/ tena saṃbhāvitaḥ saṃjanita ātmā svarūpaṃ yasya tādṛśasya saṃskārasya bhāvanākhyasyānavarataprabodhādudbodhahetościntāyā nirantarānuvṛttinimittako yo'navarataprabodha udbodhaḥ svakāryajananaunmukhyaṃ tasmātpratāyamāno dhārāvāhikarītyā vistāramadhirohan/ atra tādṛśa eva saṃskāraḥ pūrvānubhavena sātiśayotpāditaḥ/ yo naikāṃ smṛtiṃ janayitvā svayaṃ naśyati kiṃ tu ghaṭādyanuraṇanaśabdanimittavatpravāharūpeṇa kāryamutpādayati/ yathā līlāvatīkārapakṣe sātiśayaḥ prāthamikakarmajanyaḥ prāthamika eva śarādigato vegaḥ samanantarabhāvinīmāpatanātkarmasaṃtatiṃ janayati tadvaditi vā/ saṃskārasyeti jātyabhiprāyamekavacanam/ tena dhārāvāhikasmṛtijananasamarthaḥ karmabhāvī saṃskārarāśirutpannaḥ kramikakāryadarśanādeva krameṇaivodbodho'dṛṣṭavaśātparikalpanīya iti vyākhyeyam/ punaḥ kiṃbhūtaḥ/ tadvisadṛśairmālatīsmṛtijātīyairviṣayāntarāvagāhibhiḥ pratyayāntarairjñānaiḥ/ vibhinnaviṣayāvagāhitvameva jñānānāmanyonyaṃ vaisādṛśyam/ atiraskṛto'nantaritaḥ pravāho yasya/ vijātīyapratyayāsaṃvalita ityarthaḥ/ priyatamāviṣayāḥ smṛtaya eva pratyayāsteṣāmutpattisaṃtānaḥ saṃtanyamānatvenotpadyamānāḥ smṛtipratyayā iti yāvat/ mama caitanyaṃ cidrūpamātmānaṃ tanmayaṃ mālatīmayamiva tattādātmyāpannamiva karoti/ kutaḥ/ vṛttisārūpyataḥ/ vṛttermālatīgocarāyāḥ smṛtirūpāyā antaḥkaraṇavṛtteḥ sārūpyaṃ mālatyākārakāritatvaṃ tasmādayamarthaḥ/ vedāntinaḥ khalvevamāhuḥ - indriyārthasaṃnikarṣe satyantaḥkaraṇaṃ pariṇāmisvabhāvaṃ vṛttyākāreṇa pariṇamate/ antaḥkaraṇāvacchinnaṃ ca pramātṛcaitanyaṃ vṛttāvapi pratiphalati/ tadeva vṛttipratiphalitaṃ caitanyaṃ pramāṇamityucyate/ sā ca vṛttirviṣayadeśaṃ gatvā viṣayākārakāritā bhavantī viṣayādhiṣṭhānacaitanyāvaraṇakamajñānaṃ virodhitvātpradīpatamonyoyena nivartayati/ tataśca viṣayādhiṣṭhānacaitanyaṃ vṛttipratiphalitapramātṛcaitanyābhedena kūpakulyālavālasalilanyāyenaikyamāpannaṃ sphurati/ adhiṣṭhānacaitanyasya cāntaḥkaraṇatādātmyādhyāsābhāvādahamiti na pratītiḥ/ kiṃ tvidamarthena tādātmyādhyāsādviṣayākāreṇedamityeveti/ tathā ca prakṛte'pi smṛtermālatyākārakāritvāttatpratiphalitacaitanyaṃ viṣayacaitanyenaikyamāpannaṃ viṣayākāreṇa sphuratīti/ nanvevaṃ tarhi sā mālatītyevaṃ pratītiḥ syānna tu tanmayatvam/ satyam/ kiṃtu saṃtānapadaṃ paryālocitaṃ tvayā nairantaryeṇa smaryamāṇatattāṃśavigalaneneyamiti purovartitvapratyakṣe pratibhāti/ tato'pi cirakālaṃ tathaiva bhāvanayedaṃtayāpi pravilayātpramātṛcaitanyatādātmyāpannaiva pratīyata iti bhāvanāyā māhātmyametadeva/ evaṃ hi kāmāturakāminīsākṣātkāraḥ prasiddhaḥ śāstreṣviti/ ata evevaśabdaprayogaḥ sarvatra tanmayamiva līnevetyādāvapāramārthikatvadyotanāya/ tena satyapyevaṃvidhe darśane pāramārthikadarśanaprārthanaṃ yuktameveti bhāvaḥ/ tanmayameveti yaduktaṃ tadeva viśadayati - līnevetyādi/ yā madanodyāne madviṣayaprāganurāgadyotakānanubhavānprakāśitavatī sā priyā no'smākaṃ tādṛśe strīratne matto'pi prāgeva mayyanurakta āsaktacitta ityaho me saubhāgyamityātmani gauravamāropya bahuvacanam/ cetasi lagnā/ kathaṃ lagneti jijñāsāyāṃ lagnatvaprakāraviśeṣānutprekṣate - līneveti/ layaṃ gateva pāṃsurāśiparipatitapayaḥkaṇavaccite'ntarhitevetyarthaḥ/ nanu tarhi tatsphuraṇameva na syādityāśaṅkyāha - pratibimbateveti/ vimalasalilāśaye candramaṇḍalamiva maccetasi pratibimbiteva/ antaḥsphuraṇamupapannamirtthaḥ/ nanu tarhi bimbāsaṃnidhau kathaṃ pratibimba ityata āha - likhiteveti/ maccittabhitau manmathenaiva citrakāreṇa cintātūlikayānurāgavarṇakena likhiteva/ ataḥ sākṣātsphuratītyarthaḥ/ nanu tarhi citrasya rekhoparekhāsaṃsthānaviśeṣātmakatvena tadrūpatvābhāvānmālatīcitrameva lagnaṃ na tu mālatī syādata āha - utkīrṇarūpeveti/ yathā śilāyāmeva śilāputrikā śilpinā ṭaṅkenotkīryate, evaṃ mama citte madanaśilpinā śaraireva ṭaṅkairutkīrṇarūpamaccittameva priyākāreṇotkīrṇamityarthaḥ/ nanvaṇīyaso manaso niravayavatvātkathaṃ priyā tatrotkīrṇetyata āha - pratyupteveti/ yathā kanakavikāreṣu vajrādikaṃ ghaṭitaṃ bhavati, evaṃ manmanasi virahānalasaṃdīpitatvādujjvalasuvarṇapiṇḍasaṃnibhe'ta eva dravībhūte strīratnaṃ madanasuvarṇakāreṇa sthāpitamityarthaḥ/ nanu prativāpasya saṃyogaviśeṣatvādvibhāgo'pi syādityataḥ saṃyogadārḍhyārthaṃ prakāraviśeṣamāha - vajreti/ guḍamāṣarasādidravyabhāvitaḥ sudhālepo vajralepaḥ/ taddhaṭitaṃ vastu yathā dṛḍhataraṃ bhavati tadvatpriyāpītyarthaḥ/ nanu tarhi manasaḥ priyāyāśca saṃsparśo na syāt/ manasa upari vajralepastadupari tasyā ghaṭitatvādityata āha - antarnikhāteveti/ bhūtale nidhānavanmanogarte yathā yatṃkicinnikhātyate tadbatpriyāpi nikhātetyarthaḥ/ tarhi tadvadeva pratītirna syādityata āha - kīliteveti/ yathā dārudvayamayaḥśalākāviddhaṃ kīlitaṃ bhavati tadvatpañcabhirapi pañcabāṇairatitīkṣṇairubhayatrānuvṛttatayā protairmama manasi priyatamā kīlitevetyarthaḥ/ atra svacittasya kaṭhinatvādayaḥśalākāvedhavyathāsahiṣṇutve'pi priyāyāḥ saukumāryātiśayaśālinyāḥ kusumasaṃsparśenāpi khidyadaṅgaṃ kathaṃ śalākāvedhavyathāṃ soḍhumarhatītyarucyā prakārāntaramāha - cinteti/ cintāsaṃtatiḥ kathaṃ tatprāptiḥ syādityevaṃrūpaiva tantujālaṃ tena niviḍaṃ syūtā baddhevālagneti sarvatrānvayaḥ/ atra līnetyatrātyantābhedasyopakramāttasya cottarottaraṃ bhedapratīteḥ patatprakarṣatvādarthasya patatprakarṣātiśayasyaitadudāharaṇaṃ syāditi bhītyā ``pāṭhakramādarthakramo balīyān'' iti nyāyena sopānāvarohaṇarītyā cintāsaṃtatītyārabhya līnevetiparyantaṃ cottarottarasaṃśleṣotkarṣapratīteḥ patatprakarṣatvaṃ na bhavatīti kecidvidagdhā vyācakṣate/ atra lagnatayā nirdiṣṭāyāḥ prakṛtāyāḥ priyāyā layādikriyānimittakālīnādyaprakṛtavastvātmakatvādhyavasāyapradhānakriyānimittakadravyasvarūpabhāvotprekṣeti niṣkarṣaḥ/ yatsūtram - ``aprakṛtaguṇakriyāsaṃbandhādaprakṛtatvena prakṛtasya saṃbhāvanamutprekṣā'' iti/ ekasyānekadhādhyavasāyācceyaṃ mālotprekṣā//10//

nepathye kalakalaḥ/ piśācānāmutpanna iti śeṣaḥ/ kauṇapāḥ rākṣasāḥ/ raudratā bhīṣaṇatā/ atra hi śmaśānapradeśe/ paryanteti/ tamaḥ kartṛ citājyotiṣāmaujjvalyaṃ parabhāgato varṇotkarṣātprakaṭayati/ tamorūpasaṃnidhau hi tejorūpamujjvalaṃ prakāśate/ kīdṛśaṃ tamaḥ/ paryante jyotiṣāṃ prāntapradeśe pratiroddhuṃ śīlamasya tattathā/ jyotiḥ samīpaparyantamāvṛtya vartamānamityarthaḥ/ tayā meduraṃ māṃsalaṃ ghanaṃ nibiḍaṃ styānaṃ piṇḍībhūtam/ sūcībhedyamityarthaḥ/ tathā bhogena vistāreṇa bahudeśavyāpitvena bhīmaṃ bhīṣaṇam/ kiṃcaitesaṃsaktāvicchinnākulā keliryeṣāṃ te/ uttālā udbhaṭāḥ/ kaṭapūtanā nāma piśācaviśeṣāstatprabhṛtayaḥ piśācāḥ saṃmadātkilakilārūpaiḥ kolāhalaiḥ sāṃrāviṇaṃ sarvato'pi visṛtvaramākrośaṃ kurvate/ saṃpūrvāt ``ru śabde'' ityasmāddhātoḥ ``abhividhau bhāva inuṇ'' itīnuṇpratyayaḥ/ ``tadantādaṇ'' iti taddhito'ṇpratyayaḥ svārthikaḥ//11//

 āghoṣayāmi/ uccairvikrayārthaṃ vikreyavastu nivedayāmītyarthaḥ/ bho bhoḥ saṃbhramādāmantraṇe/ aśastreti/ śastreṇāspṛṣṭham/ aśastrakṣatamityarthaḥ/ śastraspṛṣṭaṃ hi pavitratvātpiśācaiḥ spraṣṭumaśakyamiti tadabhāvasteṣāṃ tasya sugrahatvapratipādanāyoktaḥ/ avyājamakapaṭaṃ vastuto vikrayārthamevānītaṃ na tu māṃsavikrayavyājena viśvāsamutpādya prahartumānītamityarthaḥ/ yadvā vyājaḥ kṛtrimo gaḍvādirmāṃsadūṣako rogaviśeṣastadrahitaṃ yatpuruṣasyāṃgaṃ tenopakalpitam/ gaḍvādikṛtrimadoṣadūṣitaṃ hi māṃsaṃ rasahīnatvādanāsvādyaṃ syāt/ strīmāṃsāpekṣayā puruṣamāṃsasya praśastatvadyotanāya puruṣagrahaṇam/ uktaṃ hi kāpālikāgame - ``aśastrasaṃchinnamayoṣidīyaṃ nṛmāṃsamārdraṃ galadasrabindu yat''/ anye tu - ``ātmasiddhiṃ paṇīkṛtya sāhasādyadupārjitam/ aśastrapūtamavyājaṃ nṛmāṃsaṃ parikīrtitam//'' ityāhuḥ/ etadeva mahāmāṃsaśabdenocyate/ gṛhyatāṃ gṛhyatāmiti vīpsā durlabhatvādetādṛśasya prathamamāgatasyaivāsti na paścādāgatasyetyādarātiśayasūcanārthā//12//

 sarvataḥ samuccalannuttālo bhīṣaṇastumulaḥ saṃkīrṇo vyaktaḥ sphuṭaṃ vibhāvyamānaśca yaḥ kalakalastenākula āvirbhavadbhistadaiva prakaṭībhavadbhirbhūtaiḥ saṃkaṭaḥ saṃkīrṇaḥ śmaśānapradeśaḥ pracalita iva/ piśācagatasaṃcaraṇakriyāyāstadgatatayā pratīteḥ kampamāna iva vartata ityarthaḥ/ karṇābhyarṇeti/ lakṣyaṃ mukhagatolkādīptivaśādīṣallakṣyamandhakāravaśātsphuṭamalakṣyaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ śuṣkaṃ kṛśaṃ dīrghamatyunnataṃ ca vapuryeṣāmulkāmukhānāmanvarthānāṃ piśācaviśeṣāṇāṃ mukhairnabha ākīryata ākīrṇaṃ bhavatīti/ kīdṛśaiḥ/ karṇayorabhyarṇamantikaṃ yāvadvidīrṇe vipāṭite te sṛkkaṇī āṣṭhaprāntau tābhyāṃ vikaṭaṃ yadvyādānaṃ vadanakuharavivaraṇaṃ tena dīptaḥ prakāśamāno'gniryeṣu taiḥ/ daṃṣṭrāṇāṃ koṭibhiragrairviśaṅkaṭairākulaiḥ/ itaścetaśca dhāvadbhiḥ/ pradīptolkāsaṃbandhena mukhamātradarśanātteṣāmapasaraṇamuktam/ taḍitsamūhasaṃnibhakeśādibhirānanaiḥ//13//

 etaditi/ etatpūtanānāṃ piśācaviśeṣāṇāṃ cakramālokyate dṛśyate/ kīdṛśam/ paritaḥ sarvata ādardaramīṣadghargharaṃ yathā tathā/ ``āghargharam'' iti kvacitpāṭhaḥ/ krandata ākrandanaṃ kurvāṇānvṛkānmāṃsāhārānmṛgaviśeṣānakrameṇātitṛṣṇayā yaugapadyenaikīkṛtya gṛhīto yo grāsaḥ kavalapiṇḍastasmādardhamuktairādhikyādvadanavivare'dyamānādbhūmau patitairnṛmāṃsasya vighasairbhuktaśeṣaiḥ/ ucchiṣṭairiti yāvat/ utpuṣṇatpoṣaṇaṃ kurvāṇam/ kharjūradrumadaghnā tatpramāṇā jaṅghā yasya tattathā/ asitayā tvacā naddhāḥ parītāḥ viṣvaksarvatastatā vyāptāḥ snāyugranthayaḥ sirāgranthayastābhirghanaṃ nibiḍamasthipañjaraṃ raktamāṃsamajjādibhirvihīnatayā kevalaṃ pañjarībhūtānyasthīni yasya tādṛkca jarato'ticirakālajīvanājjarājīrṇāḥ kaṅkālāḥ śarīrāsthīni yasya tattādṛkceti viśeṣaṇasamāsaḥ/ ``kaṅkālaḥ puṃsi kāyāsthni'' iti viśvaḥ//14//

 samantādavalokya vihasya ca/ āheti śeṣaḥ/ vihasanena ca tādṛśabhayaṃkaradarśane'pi vītaśaṅkatāsūcanānnāyakasyātimanasvitā kathitā/ pṛthucaleti/ viśīrṇā viśeṣeṇa śīrṇā vraṇakiṇagaṇḍādibahūpadravavaśādviśakalitāḥ śuṣkā māṃsarudhirādiśūnyatayā nīrasā dehā yeṣāṃ te piśācāḥ pṛthu viśālaṃ calayā kampamānayā rasanayogram ``pṛthutararasanogram'' iti pāṭhe'tisthūlatayā rasanayogramāsyagartaṃ gartamivāsyam/ upamitasamāsaḥ/ ata eva samāsasāmyāttadvācilope luptopamā/ tadvidārya vipāṭya sthitāḥ santaḥ/ caladbhiścañcalaiḥ ``galat'' iti pāṭhe koṭarānnirgacchadbhirajagarairmahāsarpairdhorāṇi bhīṣaṇāni koṭarāṇi yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ tādṛśānāṃ purāṇānāṃ ciraṃtanānām/ jīrṇatvaśuṣkatvasuṣiropetatvādisūcanāya purāṇatvoktiḥ/ rohiṇānāṃ candanānām/ ``rohiṇaścandanadrumaḥ'' iti viśvaḥ/ dyutiṃ śobhāṃ dadhati/ candanagrahaṇaṃ teṣāṃ svabhāvataḥ puṣpādiśūnyatvānnīrasatvādajagarādisamāśrayatvāccāsyagartasya koṭareṇa calantyā rasanāyā ajagareṇa sādharmyaṃ vivakṣitam/ tena nidarśanamalaṃkāraḥ/ na hyanyagatāṃ dyutimanyo dadhātīti candanatarugatadyuteḥ piśāceṣvasaṃbhavānnibandhanatayārthādbimbapratibimbabhāvenopameyatvādyākṣepāt/ yaduktam - ``asaṃbhavaddharmayogādupamānopameyayoḥ/ pratibimbakriyā gamyā yatra sā syānnidarśanā//'' iti//15//

 atibībhatsam/ atijugupsālambanamityarthaḥ/ utkṛtyeti/ pretaraṅko'tyantadīnaḥ piśācaviśeṣaḥ/ prathamaṃ kṛttiṃ tvacamutkṛtyotkṛtya chittvāthānantaraṃ pṛthunotsedhena vapurunnatyā bhūyāṃsi pracuratarāṇi/ aṃsayorbhujaśikharayoḥ sphijorjaghanābhogayoḥ pṛṣṭhapīṭhādiṣu ca māṃsaleṣvavayaveṣu sulabhānyanāyāsaprāpyāṇyugrapūtīni duḥsahadurgandhīni māṃsāni jagdhvā bhakṣayitvā samanantaramāttāni snāyvāntranetrāṇi yena sa tādṛk/ snāyurvasā, āntra[ntraṃ] purītat/ snāyvādīnāmādānaṃ tadantargatamāṃsagrahaṇārtham/ tadanantaraṃ cāṅkasthātsamīpe vartamānātkaraṅkānnirmāṃsāsthipañjarācchavaśarīrādasthisaṃsthamasthnāmanmargataṃ sthapuṭagataṃ nimnonnataviṣamapradeśasthitamapi kravyaṃ māṃsam/ prakaṭitā viṣamakaṭhinasthānasthitaṃ māṃsaṃ saṃniṣkraṣṭuṃ prakāśitā daśanā yena sa tādṛśaḥ san/ avyagraṃ tvarārahitaṃ yathātathāttīti/ atra jugupsāyāḥ paripoṣādbībhatsaraso dhvanyate//16//

 imameva rasaṃ prakārāntareṇa nirvarṇayati - api ca/ niṣṭāpeti/ kuṇapabhujaḥ śavaśarīrābhyavahāriṇaḥ piśācāḥ/ niṣṭāpena niḥśeṣeṇa tāpo niṣṭāpastena samyaktāpena svidyanti kṣaranmajjānyasthīni yeṣāṃ tān/ tathā kvathanenāvartanena parito galatsraṃsamānaṃ medo yeṣāṃ tānevaṃvidhānsaṃsaktadhūmāṃścitāsu dahyamānānpretakāyānbhūyasībhyaścitābhyo mṛtaśarīradahanendhanarāśibhyaḥ kṛṣṭvākṛṣya/ utpakvamutkarṣeṇa pakvamata eva sraṃsi vigalanmāṃsaṃ yasya tat/ pracalatkāyāpṛthaktvena svatantrakriyam/ ubhayato mūlāgrabhāgayoyag saṃdhī tābhyāṃ tayorvā nirmuktaṃ jaṅghānalakaṃ jaṅghākāṇḍamārātsamīpe niṣkṛṣya śarīrātpṛthakkṛtya/ ``niścūṣya'' iti pāṭhe prathamaṃ dravībhūtagalanmāṃsatvānmedādivaccūṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā paścāttata udayinīrniṣpratibandhaṃ niḥsarantīrmajjadhārā āsthisuṣirapūrakadhātuviśeṣadhārāḥ pibanti/ niḥśabdapānāsecanamityatrāsecanamīṣatsecanaṃ tadvyatiriktenāsecaneneṣatva[ttva]vidhānānniṣṭāpana iti ṣatvam/ ``niṣṭaptaṃ rakṣaḥ'' ityādau niḥśeṣatāpasyaiva vivakṣitatvāt/ pūrvaśloke māṃsabhakṣaṇasyoktatvādatra pānakriyāvarṇanena bībhatsarasaparipoṣaḥ//17//

 prādoṣika iti/ rajanīmukhasamudbhūtaḥ pramodaḥ prakṛṣṭo harṣaḥ/ teṣāṃ rātriṃcaratvātpradoṣasamaye prāpte yuktaḥ pramoda iti bhāvaḥ/ āntrairiti/ pūrvaṃ piśācavṛttānta uktaḥ/ idānīṃ samṛddhavadhūkānāṃ teṣāṃ vijṛmbhaṇamucyate/ āntraiḥ kalpitā maṅgalārthaṃ pratisarā raktasūtrādinirmitavalayā yāsāṃ tāḥ/ strīṇāṃ mṛtānāṃ hastā eva raktotpalāni tānyeva vyaktānuttaṃsānbibhratīti tathoktāḥ/ śoṇitapaṅka eva rudhirakardama eva kuṅkumaṃ tajjuṣanti sevanta iti tathābhūtāḥ piśācāṅganāḥ sahasā hṛdbhireva puṇḍarīkaiḥ kṛtasrajaḥ/ ``pinahya śirasā'' iti pāṭhe svakīlena śirasā dhṛtvā/ evamalaṃkṛtāḥ kāntaiḥ samaṃ kapālaiḥ śavaśiraḥkaroṭibhireva caṣakaiḥ pānapātrairasthisnehamajjānyeva surāṃ prītāḥ satyaḥ pibantītyekadeśavartisāvayavaṃ rūpakamatrālaṃkāraḥ/ piśācānāṃ saṃbhoganāyakatvasya tadaṅganānāṃ tannāyikātvasya ca śrutyāropaṇābhāvādarthātpratīteḥ/ yadāha - ``tadrūpakamabhedo ya upamānopameyayoḥ'' iti/ ``śrautā ārthāśca te yasminnekadeśavivarti tat''iti ca ``āntraiḥ kalpitamaṅgalapratisarāḥ'' ityatra tu pariṇāmaḥ/ āntrapratisaratvasya prakṛtopayogitvenāropaṇāt/ yathāha - ``āropyamāṇasya prakṛtopayogitve pariṇāmaḥ'' iti/ ``āropyamāṇamāropaviṣayātmatayā sthitam/ prakṛtasyopayogitvātpariṇāma udāhṛtaḥ//'' iti/ prakṛtopayogitvādityanena rūpakādbheda uktaḥ/ tatra prakṛtenoparañjakamāropyamāṇasya/ atra tu tadupayogitvamātramiti/ atra priyatamaiḥ saha madhupānapravṛttasrakcandanādyalaṃkṛtanāyikāpratīteḥ saṃbhogaśṛṅgārarasaḥ pratīyamānaḥ pradhānasya cārthabhūtasya bībhatsarasasyāṅgamiti rasavadalaṃkāraśca/ aṅgāṅgibhāvāpatteśca śṛṅgārabībhatsayorvirodhaparihāraḥ/ ``ayaṃ sa raśanotkarṣī'' ityevamādau karuṇāṅgatvātkaruṇaśṛṅgārayoriva/ yadāha dhvanikṛt - ``vivakṣite rase labdhapratiṣṭhe tu virodhinām/ bādhyānāmaṅgabhāvaṃ vā prāptānāmuktiracchalā//'' iti/ atra ``premārdrāḥ'' ityārabhya ``līneva'' ityantaṃ paripoṣitasya śṛṅgārasya ``āntraiḥ'' ityanena bībhatsena saha virodha iti nāśaṅkanīyam/ madhye ``itastataḥ parivartamāna-'' ityādināścaryasya, ``karṇābhyarṇa-'' ityādinā cādbhutarasasya nairantaryakṛtavirodhaparihārāyaiva niveśanāt/ ``rasāntarāntaritayorekavākyasthayorapi/ nivartate hi rasayoḥ samāveśe virodhitā//'' iti ekavākyasthayorapi rasāntaravyavadhāne virodho nivartate kila/ kimuta bhinnavākyasthayorityapiśabdārthaḥ/ na cātraikāśrayatvakṛto virodhaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ śṛṅgāriṇo mādhavasya bībhatsarasālambanatvānupapatterna virodhaśaṅkāpīti prāgeva nirūpitam/ na ca yāvatpradhānarasaparipoṣaḥ kṛtastāvānatra paripoṣastenāpi virodhābhāvaḥ/ yadāha - ``avirodhī virodhī vā raso'ṅgini rasāntare/ paripoṣaṃ na netavyastathā syādavirodhitā//'' iti/ paripoṣābhāve kathaṃ rasatvamiti vācyam/ yato yāvānaṅgino rasasya paripoṣaḥ kṛtastāvānatra na kartavya ityasyā dhvanikārikāyāstātparyamityalaṃ prapañcena//18//

 ``punaraśastra-'' ityādipāṭhaḥ paścādāgatānāmapi prajñāpanārtham/ atibhīṣaṇā vibhīṣikāḥ parasya bhayamutpādayituṃ ye vikārā vikṛtaveṣabhāṣādayo yeṣāṃ taiḥ piśācairjhaṭityapakrāntaṃ satvaraṃ palāyitam/ niḥsattvā nirvīryāḥ/ sanirvedamiti/ piśācaiḥ sarvairapi bhītabhītairapasṛtaṃ na tu dhārṣṭyamavalambya madantikamāgatya māṃsaṃ vikrītam/ akrīte cāsminna me kāryasiddhiriti nirvedahetuḥ/ sarvo'pi śmaśānavāṭaḥ śmaśānapradeśo vicito'nviṣṭaḥ/ netaḥ paraṃ pitṛvanabhāgabhūmirasti yatra kretāro labhyante/ tadanucite'pi sāhase pravṛttasya me na kāryasiddhirityaho daivasya mayi prātikūlyamiti bhāvaḥ/ tataḥ paraṃ śmaśānapradeśasyābhāvamevāha - iha khalu purata eva pāreśmaśānaṃ saridvartata ityanenānvayaḥ/ guñjaditi/ pāreśmaśānaṃ śmaśānasya pāre paryante/ ``pāre madhye ṣaṣṭyā vā'' ityavyayībhāvaḥ/ sīmabhūtā saritparyanta eva vartate/ ato netaḥ paraṃ śmaśānabhūmirastīti bhāvaḥ/ kīdṛśī sarit/ guñjatāṃ kūjatāṃ kuñjakuṭīreṣu latādipihitodarapradeśeṣu sthitānāṃ kauśikānāmulūkānāṃ yā ghaṭāstāsāṃ dhūtkāraiḥ saṃvellitaḥ saṃvardhito yaḥ krandatāṃ pheravāṇāṃ jambukānāṃ caṇḍo bhīṣaṇo dhātkṛtiranukaraṇaśabdastena bhṛto bharaṇaśīlaḥ prāgbhārastena bhīmairbhīṣaṇaistaṭairupalakṣitā/ tathāntaḥkīrṇānāṃ karaṅkāṇāṃ nirmāṃsāsthipañjaraśavaśarīrāṇāṃ yāni karparāṇi kapālāni teṣu taradata eva saṃrodho'syāstīti saṃrodhi tata evaikadeśe saṅghībhūya vegātiśayena pravahaṇātkūlaṃkaṣaṃ taṭabhedakam/ ``sarvakūlābhrakarīṣeṣu kaṣaḥ'' iti khacmumāgamaśca/ tādṛśaṃ yatsrotastasya nirgamastena ghoro bhīṣaṇastathāvidhau ghargharaḥ śravaṇakaṭū ravo yasyāḥ sā tādṛśīti/ atrolūkādibhī rasābhāve'pi vācyāyāḥ sarito bhīṣaṇatvāttadanuguṇā paruṣavṛttiriti draṣṭavyam//19//

 nepathya iti/ itthaṃ vāṇī samutthiteti śeṣaḥ/ hā tāta niṣkaruṇa, eṣa idānīṃ te narendracittārādhanopakaraṇaṃ janovipadyate/ tāta, janaka evāsi kevalam, na tu janakānurūpavyāpāro'sīti bhāvaḥ/ ato niṣkaruṇo mayi kṛpārahito mādhavāya māmadattvā nandanāya prayacchatā tvayaivāhaṃ niṣkaruṇena māritā/ śramastu maraṇasya kevalaṃ vyapadeśabhāk/ anyathā maraṇasya niścitatvāditi bhāvaḥ/ eṣa ityātmanirdeśaḥ/ jano yaḥ kaścijjanturityeva mayi buddhiḥ na tvapatyamiti snehaleśo'pīti bhāvaḥ/ narendrasya cittānurañjanopakaraṇadravyatayaivāhaṃ tavopayuktā maṇisuvarṇādivat/ na tu punarātmajetyādyatra draṣṭavyam// mādhavaḥ/ sākūtaṃ sābhiprāyam/ nāda iti/ vikalā bhayodvignā yā kurarī krauñcavadhūstasyāḥ kūjitavatsnigdho masṛṇastāraśca mālatīdhvaniriva cittākarṣī manohāryayaṃ nādastāvatprathamaṃ paricita iva pūrvamanubhūta iva śrotrasaṃvādaṃ pūrvaṃ vyāghrāvaskandanasamaye makarandavipattivihvalāyā mālatyā yo dhvanirmayānubhūtaḥ sa evāyamiti pratyabhijñāgocaratvameti gacchati/ kiṃca nādaśravaṇānantaraṃ madīyaṃ hṛdayamantarmadhye bhinnaṃ vidīrṇaṃ sadbhramatyanavasthitaṃ vartate/ tathāṅgamaṅgaṃ sarvamapyavayavajātaṃ pāṇipādādikaṃ vihvalati viṣaṇṇaṃ bhavati/ tathā gātrasya mahāvayavinaḥ śarīrasya stambho niśceṣṭatvaṃ gatiṃ gamanaṃ skhalayati/ tataścāyamatrārtanādasamudbhavaprakāraḥ ko vā bhavet/ kimetam/ kiṃ mālatyeva kenaciddurlabhasiddhisādhakena māyāvinā vividhajīvopahārapriyatvaprasiddhāyāḥ sakalasādhakābhimatasiddhipradānadakṣāyāḥ saṃnihitāyatanavartinyāḥ karālākhyāyā mahākālyā upahārīkartuṃ māyābalādānītā saṃnihitapathā karuṇaṃ krandati, uta, tannādasadṛśo vānyadīyo vāyaṃ dhvaniriti prakāre vitarkaḥ/ yadyanyadīyo'yaṃ dhvanistarhi mamotpannaṃ manovidalanabhramaṇapāṇyādyavayavaviṣādagātrastambhādikaṃ kiṃnimittakaṃ syāt/ na hyanyadīyavyasanānmamaiṣā duḥsahavedanā bhavitumarhati/ tena ``pramāṇamantaḥkaraṇapravṛttayaḥ'' iti nyāyena priyāyā evaiṣa ārtadhvaniriti manye/ sā hi mama bahiścarāḥ prāṇā iti tadvipattisaṃbhāvanayaiva mamaiṣā daśeti niścayagarbho mādhavasya vitarko yujyata evaiṣaḥ//20//

 priyāyā eva kenacitkālyā upahārīkṛtāyā eṣa dhvaniriti kuta ityata āha - karāleti/ karālākhyāyāḥ kālyāḥ sthānāduccarannudgacchankaruṇadhvaniriva vibhāvyate/ tatkhalvīdṛśānāṃ jīvopahārādīnāmaniṣṭānāṃ sthānam/ tasmātsaṃbhāvitamevaitadityarthaḥ//21//

 evaṃ vimṛśya kartavyaṃ niścinoti - bhavatu mayā yathābhyūhitaṃ tathaiva bhavatu/ tathāpi paśyāmi kālīsthānaṃ karuṇadhvanimūlaṃ ca pratyakṣīkaromi/ yadi vastutaḥ priyaiva tanmūlam, tadā matprāṇavyayenāpi yadi sā vipado nistārayituṃ śakyate, tarhi tatprāṇatrāṇena saṃpūrṇasaṃprāptajīvitaphalo bhaveyam/ yadi na śakyate, tadāpi tatraiva visṛjya prāṇānpriyatamāṃ lokāntare vā samāsādayeyamityabhiprāyaḥ// mālatī/ hā tāta niṣkaruṇa, eṣa idānīṃ te narendracittārādhanopakaraṇaṃ jano vipadyate/ hā amba, hṛdaye hatāsi durvāradaivadurvilasitena/ ``matsnehamaye'' iti hṛdayaviśeṣaṇaṃ kvacitpaṭhyate/ tatra tāta, tvaṃ niṣkaruṇaḥ kiṃtu 
(ambā) madvatsaleti madviyogaśokajvalanānna jīviṣyatītyetadeva me duḥkhanimittam, na tu matprāṇaviyogaḥ/ tasya mādhavamalabhamānāyā mama saṃmatatvāditi bhāvaḥ/ hā mālatīmayajīvite, mama kalyāṇasādhanaikasukhasakalavyāpāre/ matpariṇayaniṣpādanamevaikaṃ sukhaṃ sādhyatvena phalaṃ yasya sa tādṛśaḥ sakalo vyāpāro yasyā iti vigrahaḥ/ bhagavati kāmandaki, cirasya jñāpitāsi duḥkhaṃ snehena/ mayi sneha eva tava duḥkhaheturjāta ityarthaḥ/ hā priyasakhi lavaṅgike, svapnadarśanamātrāhaṃ te saṃvṛttā/ mādhavaḥ/ hanteti khede/ satyaṃ priyatamaiva vadhārthamānītā kenaciditi majjīvitatarormūle kuṭhāro'narthāya vyāpārita iti bhāvaḥ/ ``hanteti harṣe'' iti vyākhyānamasaṃgatam/ jīvitasarvasvabhūtāyāstasyā vadhyaśūlāropaṇasamaye ko'tra harṣasyāvakāśaḥ/ na ca priyā mayā mocayiṣyata iti niścayastu tadānīmasti jīvanasyaiva saṃdigdhatvādata evāha - api nāma jīvantīṃ saṃbhāvayeyamiti/ sarvathā tanmaraṇenāpi mamāpi prāṇatyāgo niścitaḥ/ tathāpi jīvantīmeva tāṃ saṃdarśanasaṃbhāṣaṇarakṣaṇaprayatnavyāpārairyadi saṃbhāvayāmi tadā jīvitasāphalyaṃ syādityāśaṃsā/ api nāmeti nipātābhyāmapi saṃbhāvanautsukyaṃ dyotyate/ yaccātra nipātasamudāyasyāvadhāraṇaparatvamuktvā jīvantīmevaināṃ saṃbhāvayeyamiti jīvantyeva vartata iti vyākhyānam, tadapi prakṛtānanuguṇatvāddhyeyam/ na hīdānīmapi tasyā jīvanaṃ niścitaṃ tanniścaye vā saṃbhāvayeyamityāśaṃsāyāṃ liṅśabdaḥ kathaṃ ghaṭata ityalam// kāpālikau/ kapālakuṇḍalāghoraghaṇṭau/ ``pumānstriyā'' ityekaśeṣaḥ/ devītyādi/ raktopahāraṃ purataḥ saṃnidhāpya stutinamaskārapūrvakaṃ nivedayediti devyāḥ stutiḥ kriyate - sāvaṣṭambheti/ sāvaṣṭambhaḥ sāvalepo yo niśumbhaḥ pādaghātalakṣaṇaparibhavastatsaṃbhrameṇa/ ``niśumbhanirbhara-'' iti pāṭhe niśumbhena nirbharamatimātraṃ namadyadbhūgolaṃ bhuvaḥ saṃsthānaṃ tena yanniṣpīḍanamatibhāratvena durvahatayā vahanaśaktyavasādanaṃ tena nyañcannavanamankarparaḥ pṛṣṭhāsthikaṭāho yasya tādṛśasya kūrmasya pṛṣṭhādvigalantī tatkampena sraṃsamānā brahmāṇḍakhaṇḍasya sthitiryasminkrīḍite tattathā/ pātālasya pratimallayoḥ pratibhaṭayoḥ/ adhobhuvanakuharasadṛśayorityarthaḥ/ gallavivarayoḥ/ kapolasuṣirayoḥ prakṣiptāḥ saptārṇavā yatra tattādṛśam/ nanditasya saṃtoṣitasya nīlakaṇṭhasya bhūtanāthasya pariṣadi vyaktaṃ prasiddhaṃ tava krīḍitaṃ lāsyātmakaṃ vilāsaṃ vande/ atrāpi vikaṭabandhocitarasābhāve'pi vācyāthagcityānuguṇā paruṣavṛttiriti na pratikūlavarṇatvākhyo doṣaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ/ evamuttaratrāpi draṣṭavyam/ yadyapi gallapadaṃ kevalaṃ laukikasaṃketasiddhatvādgrāmyamityuktam tathāpi pāṣaṇḍoktau guṇatvameva grāmyasya na doṣatvam/ yadāha - ``adhamaprakṛtyuktiṣu grāmyatvaṃ guṇaḥ'' iti//22//

 pracaliteti/ bho devi, va iti pūjāyāṃ bahuvacanam/ tāṇḍavaṃ no'smākamariṣṭyā aśubhābhāvāya hṛṣṭyai harṣāya ca bhūyādityāśīrvacanam/ kīdṛktāṇḍavam/ pracalitāṅgavikṣepasaṃbhramavaśādvikṣiptā yottarīyabhūtā karikṛttirgajacarma tatparyanteṣu cañcantaścañcalā ye nakhāsteṣāmāghātena bhinnādvidīrṇādindorniṣyandamānasya sravato'mṛtasya ścyotena kṣaraṇena jīvatāṃ labdhajīvānāṃ kapālānāṃ maulimālyagrathitānāmāvalyā paṅktyā muktaiścaṇḍāṭṭahāsaistrasadbhyo bibhyadbhyo bhūribhyo bhūtebhyaḥ pravṛttā stutiryasya tattādṛśam/ tathā śvasatāmatikhedātphūtkurvatāmasitabhujaṅgānāṃ kṛṣṇasarpāṇāṃ bhogā evāṅgadagranthayaḥ keyūragranthayaḥ keyūrabandhanāni teṣāṃ niṣpīḍanenotphullā viśālāḥ phullantyo vikasantyo yāḥ phaṇāstāsāṃ pīṭhāt/ kaṇṭhāditi yāvat/ niryato nirgacchato viṣajyotiṣa ujjṛmbhaṇoḍḍāmarāṇāṃ bhayaṃkarāṇāṃ vyastānāṃ vikṣiptānāṃ vistāriṇāṃ prasāritānāṃ doṣṇāṃ khaṇḍena saṅghena paryāsitāḥ parito vikṣiptāḥ kṣmādharā yasmiṃstat/ tathā jvalatānalena piśaṅgānāṃ netracchaṭānāṃ nayanaraśmipravāhāṇāṃ bhāreṇa bhīmaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ yaduttamāṅgaṃ śirastasya bhramirbhramaṇaṃ tena prastutā pravṛttā yālātacakrasya kriyā/ pradīptaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ vegena bhrāmyamāṇaṃ cakravadābhāti tadalātacakramityucyate/ tasyāṃ syūta iva syūto digbhāgo dikcakraṃ yatra tat/ tathottuṅgasya khaṭvāṅgasya śṛṅgaṃ śikharameva dhvajastasyoddhūtibhiruddhūnanairvikṣiptā viśīrṇāstārāgaṇā yatra tat/ tathā pramuditānāṃ kaṭapūtanānāṃ piśācaviśeṣāṇāmuttālānāṃ pracaṇḍānāṃ vetālānāṃ bhettālānāṃ ca tālaiḥ karataladvayāsphālanaiḥ sphuṭatkarṇā vidalacchravaṇakuharā ata eva saṃbhrāntā yā gaurī tasyā ghanāśleṣeṇa gāḍhāliṅganena hṛṣyanmanasastryambakasyānando'trāstīti tattādṛśamiti daṇḍakaḥ/ ``nau raḥ'' iti vacanāt//23//

 mādhavaḥ/ mālatīmeva vadhārthamānītāṃ dṛṣṭvā hā dhigiti/ pramādaṃ pitroḥ kāmandakyādīnāṃ cānavadhānatāṃ dhik/ yadiyamanena nṛśaṃsena kairapyanālakṣitā vadhārthamānītā vadhyaveṣabhūṣitā ceti bhāvaḥ/ tadevāha - nyasteti/ nyastamanuliptamalaktakaṃ yasyāḥ sā cāsau raktamālyavasanā ca pāpānāṃ pāpakāriṇāmārambho māraṇaṃ karmānayorastīti tadvatoḥ/ pāṣaṇḍau bauddhāveva caṇḍālau/ caṇḍālakarmakāritvāttatsahakāritvācca/ tayoḥ/ ``pumānstriyā'' ityekaśeṣaḥ/ vṛkayorvyāghrādikrūrasattvayormṛgī hariṇīva gocaraṃ viṣayaṃ gatā/ bhīruḥ svabhāvenaiva/ vasorivāṣṭavasuṣvanyatamasadṛśasya/ yadvā vasoragneriva parākramasaṃpannasya devatulyasyāpi bhūrivasoramātyasya sutā mṛtyormukhe vartate/ hā khede/ dhigiti/ mametaḥ paraṃ jīvitāśāmiti śeṣaḥ/ kaṣṭamidamaniṣṭamāpatitam/ astakaruṇaḥ kṛpāhīno vidheḥ prakrama ārambhaḥ ko'yam/ kathamatyantānucitamevārabdhaṃ daivahatakenetyarthaḥ/ hā dhikkaṣṭamaniṣṭamiti padānāmatīvārthabhedābhāve'pyanukampātiśayadyotanārthatvānna paunaruktyaśaṅketi dhyeyam/ yadāha - ``anukampādyatiśaye yadi kaścinnibadhyate/ na doṣaḥ punaruktyādau pratyuteyamalaṃkriyā//'' iti//24//

 kapālakuṇḍalā - taṃ bhadra iti/ atra maraṇasamaye yastava dayito'bhīṣṭo devo'bhūttaṃ smara/ maraṇadaśāyāmiṣṭadevatāsmaraṇamamutrābhyudayāyetyāgamaḥ/ adyeti/ mṛtyuḥ saṃnihita ityarthaḥ// mālatī/ hā deva mādhava, paralokagato'pi smartavyo'yaṃ jano yuṣmābhiḥ/ devetyanena tvameva mameṣṭadevatāsvarūpaḥ/ tanmaraṇadaśāyāmapi tvāmeva smariṣyāmi/ janmāntare vā tvatsamāgamo me bhūyāditi bhāvaḥ/ na khalu sa uparato yasya vallabho janaḥ smarati/ yasyetīyaṃ karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī/ priyatamasmṛtigocarasya janasya nirantaraṃ tanmanasi sthityā noparatirityarthaḥ// hanteti viṣāde/ tapasvinyanukampārhā/ iyaṃ mādhave'nuraktā satī/ tadidānīṃ so'traiva mahāmāṃsasya vikrayārthaṃ paryaṭati/ daivādāgatya mocayedvā/ tadā kaṣṭaṃ mantrasādhanapratyūhaḥ saṃbhāvita iti viṣādaḥ// ``taṃ bhadre'' iti ślokasyottarārdham - cāmuṇḍe bhagavatīti/ mantrasādhanasyādāvupakramasamaya uddiṣṭāṃ saṃkalpitāmidānīṃ tatparyavasāna upanihitāṃ samarpitāṃ pūjāṃ strīratnopahāraṃ bhajasveti//25//


  mādhavaḥ/ khaḍgaṃ kāpālikasyodyataṃ prakoṣṭhena maṇibandhena vikṣipyāpasārya/ apehyapasara/ pratihato'si vaiparītyena tvameva hato'si// mālatī/ paritrāyatāṃ mahābhāgaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ maraṇasamaya iti/ tyaktāśaṅkā yasminkarmaṇi tattathā/ pralāpe'narthakavacojalpane idānīmevainaṃ saṃhariṣyāmītyevaṃrūpe nirargalo niṣpratirodhaḥ/ mahānubhāvāḥ khalūpakāraṃ kurvantyeva na tu vacanapūrvakaṃ kurvantīti vacanasyānarthakyam/ yadyapyevaṃ kariṣyāmīti na vaktavyaṃ/ kevalaṃ kartavyameva/ tathāpīdṛśyāṃ durdaśāyāṃ priyāyāḥ prāṇatrāṇena majjanmano'tīva sāphalyamāsāditamiti harṣabharavaśena manasyavadhāritatvāllāghavaśaṅkāmapi visṛjyānarthakameva kiṃcitpralapitamiti bhāvaḥ/ tathā prakaṭito nijaḥ sahajaḥ sneho yena so'yaṃ te sakhā purata eva vartate/ ataḥ sutanu, utkampaṃ bhayanimittaṃ vepathuṃ visṛja/ saṃpratyevāsau pāpaḥ pāpakārīhaiva tavaiva purataḥ pratīpaṃ viparītaṃ yathā tathā vipākaḥ phalārambho yasya tādṛśasya pāpmanaḥ phalamanubhavati/ tvadvadhodyamenāyaṃ svayameva hata ityarthaḥ//26//

 aghoraghaṇṭaḥ/ antarāyaḥ pratyūhaḥ/ kapālakuṇḍalā/ asyāḥ snehasya bhūmirekālambanamityanenāvaśyaṃ rakṣaṇīyeyamiti sūcitam/ kāmandakīsuhṛtputra ityanena mahāyoginyāstasyāḥ prasādādasminnāsmākaṃ maṇimantramāyādikaṃ prabhavatīti sūcitam/ mahāmāṃsasya paṇayitetyanenātiśūratvānna pauruṣeṇāpyasau sādhya iti darśayati/ mādhavaḥ - sāsramiti/ ānandaviṣādābhyāṃ sāsratvam/ kimetat/ tatkathamasāvanarthopanipāta ityarthaḥ// mālatī/ mahābhāga, ahamapi na jānāmi etāvajjānāmi/ uparyalindameva prasupteha pratibuddhāsmi/ yūyaṃ punaḥ kva/ samāgatā iti śeṣaḥ// mādhavaḥ - salajjamiti/ svasyānucitaṃ svenācaritaṃ karma nṛmāṃsavikrayarūpaṃ nivedanīyaṃ jātamiti lajjāhetuḥ/ tvatprāptipratyāśayānucitamapi kṛtamiti dhārṣṭyamavalambyāha - tvaditi/ tvatpāṇipaṅkajaparigraheṇa dhanyajanmā bhūyāsamityanenābhiniveśenābhiṣaṅgeṇa kadarthyamāno dhairyamapasārya cāpalāpādanena vitathīkriyamāṇo'haṃ nṛmāṃsapaṇanāya vikrayāya paretabhūmau śmaśāne bhrāmyan/ he bhīru, tava ruditānyākarṇya samāgato'smīti/ bibhetestācchīlye ``bhiyaḥ kruklukanau'' iti krūṅ/ ``ūṅutaḥ'' ityukārāntānmanuṣyajātivācina ūṅ/ tataḥ ``ambārthanadyorhrasvaḥ'' iti hrasvaḥ/ bhīruśabdasya niyamena strīvācitvādrūḍhyā manuṣyajātivācitvam/ saṃjñāyāmiti vā ūṅpratyayaḥ//27//

 mālatī - apavāryeti/ ``apavāritakaṃ kāryaṃ tripatākena pāṇinā'' ityuktavrīḍāharṣātiśayagopanātmakasya vyabhicāriṇo'nubhāvo'pavāraṇam/ kathaṃ mama kāraṇādevaita ātmanirapekṣaṃ parikrāmanti/ mama strīmātrasyaiva kṛte mahatkāryamuddiśya kartumātmanirapekṣametatkarma kathamanenārabdham/ tadatrāhamevāparādhinī yā sāhasenāpi nainaṃ samāgatetyaho me dāruṇaṃ cittamiti bhāvaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ aho nu khalu bhoḥ/ etadvyākhyātaṃ prāk/ tadetaditi/ tadetatpriyāyā darśanaṃ kākatālīyaṃ nāma/ daivātkākāgamanasamaye tālaphalapatanasadṛśamityarthaḥ/ svarūpasiddhistu ``ive pratikṛtau'' itīvārthaprakaraṇe ``kuśāgrācchaḥ'' ityataścha ityanuvartamāne ``samāsācca tadviṣayāt'' iti tacchabdenevārthaḥ parāmṛśyate/ ivārthaviṣayātsamāsādivārthe cho bhavatīti sūtrārthaḥ/ nanu samāsenaiva tadviṣayeṇevārthābhidhānātkiṃ pratyayeneti cenmaivam/ samāsaviṣayādivārthādanyasminnivārthe pratyayotpattiḥ/ tathā hi/ yādṛcchikaṃ kākāgamanaṃ tālapatanaṃ ca/ tena patatā tālena kākavadhaḥ kṛtaḥ/ mādhavasya yadṛcchayā tatrāgamanam, mālatyā api tatra tathaivāgamanaṃ ca/ kākāgamanatālapatanamiveti vigṛhyāsmādeva vacanājjñāpakātsamāsaḥ/ vṛttiviṣaye ca kākatālaśabdāvāgamanapatanakriyāvācinau bhavataḥ/ tataḥ kākatālaśabdātpatatā tālaphalena kākavadhasadṛśaṃ yadṛcchāgatena mādhavena kāpālikaṃ hatvā priyārakṣaṇamasminnivārthe cchaḥ/ kākatālīyamivātarkitopanataṃ citrakaraṇamiti śabdārthaḥ/ supsupeti samāsaḥ/ anyasya viśeṣavidhānasya cābhāvāttasya caivaṃvidhā gatiranekaviṣayatvādityalam/ saṃpratītyupaskāraḥ/ rāhoriti/ rāhorānane caratītyānanacarī/ tām/ tanmukhāntargatāmityarthaḥ/ ``careṣṭaḥ'' ityadhikaraṇa upapade ṭapratyayaḥ/ ṭittvānṅīp/ tādṛśīṃ candrakalāmiva preyasīm/ priyaśabdāt ``dvivacanavibhajyopapade tarabīyasunau'' itīyasun/ tasminparataḥ ``priyasthira-'' ityādinā priyaśabdasya pretyādeśaḥ/ ``ugitaśca'' iti ṅīp/ daivādyadṛcchayā samāsādya prāpya dasyoścorasyāsya kāpālikāpasadasya kṛpāṇapātaviṣayāttatkhaḍgapatanagocarādācchindataḥ prasahyāpaharato me mama ceta ātaṅkātkathamiyaṃ rāhorivātikrūrasya dasyoḥ kṛpāṇapātaviṣayamindukaleva saukumāryātiśayaśālinī priyatamā prāptā/ yadi mama kṣaṇamātramitaḥ paraṃ vilambo'bhaviṣyat, mahānanartha āpatsyata ityevaṃrūpādbhayānakarasasthāyībhāvāt/ ``bhayaṃ cittasya vaiklavyaṃ raudrasaṃjanitaṃ viduḥ'' ityuktalakṣaṇādbhayādvikalamitikartavyatāmūḍham/ tathā karuṇayā kṣaṇavyavadhāne'pi parāsureveyaṃ tanmṛtaiva punarutpannetyevaṃ priyāyāstādṛgavasthāvibhāvanājanitayā karuṇayā śokarasasthāyībhūtayā drutaṃ vilīnam/ tathā vismayādaho daivavilasitasyācintyamahimatvaṃ yadadyaiva mahāmāṃsavikrayārthamāgamanaṃ tatraiva kākatālīyanyāyenehāgamanaṃ priyāsaṃprāptiriti mahadidaṃ citramityevaṃrūpādadbhutarasasthāyinaḥ/ ``vismayaścittavistāraḥ padārthātiśayādibhiḥ'' ityuktalakṣaṇādvikṣobhitaṃ janitādbhutarasam/ tathā krodhena kathamayaṃ pāpakārī niraparādhinīṃ tribhuvanaratnabhūtāmimāmapīdānīṃ hantuṃ samudyataḥ, tasmādavaśyameṣa hantavya ityevaṃrūpādraudrarasasthāyinā/ ``pratikūleṣu taikṣṇyasya prakarṣaḥ krodha iṣyate'' ityevaṃlakṣaṇena jvalitaṃ raudrarasenoddīpitam/ tathā mudā matprāṇebhyo'pi garīyasīṃ priyāṃ parirakṣya kṛtārtho bhavāmītyādibahuprakārayā ratyā manaḥprasādarūpayā pradhānaśṛṅgārarasasthāyinyā vikasitaṃ rasāsvādena vistāramupagatam/ kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa vartate/ kathaṃcidetādṛśī daśāsyeti nirūpayituṃ na śakyata iti bhāvaḥ/ atra ca prabandhapravṛttā mādhavasya mālatīgocarā ratiḥ preyasīmiti nirdiṣṭenālambanavibhāvena vibhāvyamānā tadavalokanādibhiruddīpitā manovikāsapriyavacanatadrakṣaṇavyāpārādibhiranubhāvairvāṅmanaḥśarīrārambharūpairanubhāvyamānā taddarśanajanitairmadaśramaharṣacintāsmṛtyādibhirvyabhicāribhirupacitā rasatāmāpannā prādhānyena dhvanyate/ tadaṅgatayā tu rāhorānanacarīṃ candrakalāmivetyupamāsūcitabhīṣaṇākārakāpālikālambanastatsvarūpavibhāvanoddīpita ātaṅkapadanirdiṣṭabhayasthāyī vikalapadasūcitamanovaiklavyatadanuguṇaśarīravāgārambhānubhāvastanniyatakampaśaṅkānirvedaglānijāḍyadīnatāvegaviṣādonmādacintāmohādibhirvyabhicāribhiḥ pratīyamānairupacito bhayānakarasaḥ/ tathā dasyoḥ kṛpāṇapātaviṣayāditi sūcitamṛtaprāyapriyatamālambanastaddurdaśāparibhāvanoddīpitaḥ karuṇayeti nirdiṣṭaḥ śokasthāyībhāvo drutamiti nirdiṣṭacittadravībhāvataducitaśarīravāgārambhānubhāvatanniyatāśruvaivarṇyavaisvaryajāḍyāvegamohonmādaviṣādādibhirvyabhicāribhiḥ pratīyamānaiḥ paripoṣaṃ gataḥ karuṇarasaśca/ tathā daivādāsādyeti sūcitayādṛcchikasamāgamālambanataddhetuvidhivaicitryapariśīlanoddīpito vismayāditi nirdiṣṭādbhutasthāyībhāvo vikṣobhitamiti manovikṣobhataducitaśarīravāgārambhānubhāvastanniyatastambhapralayasvedaharṣagarvasmṛtivitarkavibodhacintādibhirvyabhicāribhirupacito'dbhutarasaśca/ tathācchindata iti sūcitaḥ priyāvadhodyatahantavya ityālambanastaddaurātmyavimarśanoddīpito jvalitamiti manojvalanataducitavāgārambhānubhāvatanniyatastambharomāñcasvedaharṣāvegabhramonmādamadaharṣacāpalāsūyāśramāmarṣādibhirvyabhicāribhiḥ sarvatra kathaṃ vartata ityanena sūcitairupacito raudrarasaścetyete rasā aṅgabhāvāpattyeva dūrato nirastaparasparavirodhāḥ pradhānarasāpekṣayāvirodhāḥ/ pradhānarasaparipoṣāpekṣayā nyūnarasaparipoṣatvena ślāghanīyaucityanibandhanāvyatikarākhyayā śobhanākhyayā ca melanagatyā śṛṅgāreṇa saha saṃkīrtyante/ yathāha - ``samakālasamutpannaistribhirdvābhyāmathāpi vā/ rasaścedvyatikīryeta samavyatikaraḥ smṛtaḥ// virodhe mitraśatrūṇāṃ rasānāṃ saṃkare'pi ca/ mahimnā śobhate svena yaḥ sa śobhana īritaḥ//'' ityalamatikathanena//28//

 aghoraghaṇṭaḥ/ brāhmaṇaḍimbha/ vipratvācchiśutvācca bhīrusvabhāvatayā pauruṣaśūnyetyarthaḥ/ vyāghrāghrātetyādi/ vyāghreṇāghrātāyāṃ gṛhītāyāṃ mṛgyāṃ hariṇyāṃ kṛpākulaḥ kṛpāluryo mṛgo hariṇastannyāyena hiṃsāruceranavaratahiṃsāparasya prāṇināmupahārasya sādhakaiḥ kriyamāṇasya ketanamāsthānaṃ karālāsthānaṃ tajjuṣaḥ sevamānasya me gocaraṃ viṣayaṃ prāpto'si gato'si/ yathā mṛgyāṃ dayālurmṛgo vyāghreṇa hanyate, tathā tvamapyasyāṃ mayopahārārthamānītāyāṃ dayālurmayā vadhyasa iti bhāvaḥ/ pāpeti prārabdhakarmapratyūhakāritvānmādhavasya saṃbodhanam/ so'haṃ pūrvaṃ khaḍgāhatyā vyastaskandhaṃ vikṣiptaskandhaṃ yatkabandhaṃ vigataśiraskaṃ tasya randhrebhyo rudhiraprāgbhāraṃ raktapravāhaṃ niṣyandate varṣatīti tathoktena bhavataiva bhūtānāṃ jananīṃ mātaraṃ bhadrakālīmṛdhnomi prīṇayāmi/ paścādanayeti śeṣaḥ//29//

 mādhavaḥ/ asāramiti/ aye durātmanpākhaṇḍacaṇḍāla, kathamevaṃ vidhātuṃ vyavasito'syudyukto'si/ kiṃ vidhātumityapekṣāyāmāha - saṃsāramākīṭamāvirañcaṃ devatiryaṅmanuṣyādivividhavicitraprāṇivargasya jananajīvanamaraṇatattaddeśakālocitaśarīraparigrahatattaccharīrānurūpasukhaduḥkhādyanubhavādivyāpārapravāhādicchedena saṃtanyamānaṃ prapañcamasāraṃ parārdhyavastuśūnyaṃ kartuṃ kathaṃ vyavasitavānasīti sarvatra saṃbadhyate/ ayaṃ hi saṃsāraḥ satsvapi candanacāmīkarādiṣu padārtheṣu tairna sāravān/ kiṃtvanayaiva sārabhūtayā/ asyāṃ ca tvayā nihatāyāṃ kevalamṛjīṣakalpo'yaṃ niḥsāra eva syāt/ tadimāṃ hantumudyatastvaṃ saṃsāramevāsāraṃ kartumudyato'sītyarthaḥ/ atra viṣayanigaraṇena mālatyāṃ sakalasaṃsāraikasāravastutvasyādhyavasitatvāt ``nigīryādhyavasānaṃ tatprakṛtasya pareṇa yat'' ityuktalakṣaṇādhyavasitaprayatnātiśayoktiḥ siddhavannirdiṣṭāyā vyavasāyakriyāyāḥ sarvatrānvayātkriyādīpakaṃ ca vācyālaṃkārau/ candanacāmīkarādīnāṃ na vastutaḥ sāratvam, kiṃtu pāmarajanaprasidhdyaiva/ iyameva sārabhūtetyapahnavaḥ/ sarvasāravastūpamānāni yāni tebhyo'pi sārabhūteyameveti vyatirekaśca vyaṅgyāviti sarvatra draṣṭavyam/ tathā tribhuvanaṃ trayāṇāṃ bhuvanānāṃ samāhārastribhuvanam/ ``pātrādibhyaḥ pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ'' iti vacanāt ``dvigoḥ'' iti ṅībna bhavati/ parimuṣitaṃ luṇṭhitaṃ ratnaṃ yasya tatkartum/ satsvapi kaustubhakanakādiṣu na tai ratnavattribhuvanam/ na ca tāni vastuto ratnāni/ avivekināṃ tu tatra ratnatvavyapadeśaḥ samīcīnavastūpamānatvaprasiddhiśca/ iyameva tribhuvanasya sakalaratnātiśāyiguṇaśālinyekaṃ ratnamiti nigīryādhyavasānam/ tāṃ hantuṃ pravṛttaḥ/ ratnaśūnyameva jagatkartuṃ pravṛtto'sītyarthaḥ/ alaṃkārayogaḥ pūrvavat/ tathā lokaṃ pramāṇaṣaṭkapathapathikaṃ prapañcaṃ nirālokaṃ prakāśaśūnyaṃ kevalamandhakārākrāntaṃ kartum/ na khalu saurādibhirālokaiḥ sāloko'yaṃ lokaḥ/ teṣāṃ jaḍarūpāṇāṃ prakāśāntaraprakāśyatvenābhāsatvāt/ anayaiva punarānandaikarasasaṃvinmayaprakāśātmikayā tribhuvanabhavanalokottararatnapradīpakalikayā samudbhāsito'yaṃ sasūryacandrāditejasko lokaḥ/ tadimāṃ nirvāpayitukāmaḥ/ jagadandhaṃ kartukāmo'sītyarthaḥ/ tathā bāndhavajanaṃ sakalamapi bandhuvargametadekajīvitaṃ maraṇameva śaraṇametadvipattijanitaśokanirākaraṇasamarthaṃ yasya tattādṛśaṃ kartum/ idaṃ cāvāntaravākyaṃ sahacarabhinnam/ iha hi mahāvākyarūpe śloke'sāraṃ saṃsāramityādibhiravāntaravākyairmālatyāḥ sakalalokātiśāyilāvaṇyādiguṇaśālitvena sarvapadārthotkarṣatvaṃ tadanvayavyatirekābhyāmeva lokasyopādeyatvānvayavyatirekau cetyetāvadbhaṅgivaicitryeṇa prauḍhoktiyuktyā ca nirūpyate/ tatra cānena vākyena mālatyā utkarṣo na pratipādyate/ bāndhavā hi kāṇakubjaduḥśīlāpatyanāśe'pi maraṇaśaraṇā bhavanti/ na ca tadanvayāllokasyopādeyatā kadācit/ nāpi tadvyatirekādanupādeyatvam/ bahutaraśreṣṭhavastupracuratvādeva tadupādānasiddheḥ/ tataścātyutkarṣapratipādakavākyamadhye taddvijātīyatayā sahacarairbhinnaṃ tannikṛṣṭatvādviruddhamidaṃ vākyaṃ ṣāṇmāsikamuktāphalagrathitahāralatāmadhye varāṭikeva nāyakatvena niveśanaṃ nārhati prakramabhaṅgaśca syāt/ prakrāntātiśayoktyādyalaṃkārāṇāmuparyupari nibaddhānāmakāṇḍe vicchedāt/ kecittu janamityudāsīnavācinā janaśabdena mādhavaḥ preyasīvināśaduḥkhottaraṇopāyāntaramapaśyantaṃ maraṇameva tadupāyatvenādhigacchantamātmānaṃ nirdiśati/ tena tannāśe svasya maraṇameveti tallāvaṇyātiśayaprakāśanānnoktadoṣa iti vyācakṣate/ tadasat/ bāndhavajanamityukte svanirdeśasya kathaṃcidapyapratīteḥ pratītau vā naitāvatā tasyā utkarṣaḥ/ sarvo rāgāndhaḥ svahṛdayābhimatameva vastvadhamabhūtamapi sarvalokottaramityabhimanyate/ tathā hi - ``yā yasyābhimatā loke sā tasyādhikarūpiṇī'' iti/ ata eva cakravāko'pi svapriyāvirahe jagadeva śūnyamākalayaṃstāneva lokottarasaundaryaśālinīṃ manyata iti tiryakṣvapi prasiddham/ na cātrāpi mādhavābhimānamātrasiddhameva tasyā lokottaratvamiti vācyaṃ mā bhūt/ prabandharasāsvādayitṝṇāṃ sahṛdayānāmanyathābhāvapratītyānaucityasphuraṇādrasāsvādabhaṅgaḥ/ na ca svābhimānena tasyā ślāghyatve'pi na tāvatā tatsattvāsattvābhyāṃ jagato'sti kaścidviśeṣa ityupekṣaṇīyamevaitat/ ato ``vidhivilasitaṃ sarvamaphalam'' iti paṭhanīyamiti kuntakaprabhṛtayaḥ kāvyatattvajñāḥ sahṛdayāḥ/ evaṃ hi nāvadyaleśo'pi/ anādau hyasāre saṃsāre manoharataravastusṛṣṭau pravṛttasya vidheryo vilāso vyāpāro na tasya candrādinirmāṇaṃ phalam/ teṣāṃ tucchatayā tādṛśavidhiprayāsaphalatvāyogāt/ apitviyameva tasya phalamiti nigīryādhyavasānam/ tadetatphalamakāṇḍe hantumudyuktastvam/ vidhātṛvyāpārameva niṣphalaṃ kartumudyata ityarthaḥ/ tathā kandarpamadarpaṃ darpaśūnyaṃ kartum/ tasya hi satsvapi mandānilacandanacandrikādibhāveṣu na tānavalambya trilocanāvadhikāṃ trilokīmahaṃ jeṣyāmīti darpasteṣāṃ tatsahakāraśaktyabhāvāt/ kiṃtvimāmevāpratihatāṃ nijāṃ śaktimavalambya na me kaścittrilokyāṃ pratibhaṭo'sti, ahameka eva vīra iti tasya darpaḥ/ tāmimāṃ hantukāmaḥ kandarpameva darpaśūnyaṃ nirutsāhaṃ kartukāmo'sītyarthaḥ/ tathā janānāṃ nayananirmāṇamaphalaṃ phalahīnaṃ kartum/ niratiśayasaundaryaśālivastudarśanameva hi nayananirmāṇasya phalam/ na ca vastvantaradarśanaṃ phalaṃ tasya/ etādṛśasaundaryaśālino'nyasyābhāvāt/ iyameva lāvaṇyāsārasīmā tasya phalamiti tadvināśe netranirmāṇameva vandhyaṃ syādityarthaḥ/ tathā jagajjīrṇaṃ jaracchuṣkakaṇṭakakoṭarādiduṣprekṣavṛkṣamaraṇyaṃ krūrasatvarākṣasapulindādibhīṣaṇaṃ kāntāraṃ kartum/ idaṃ hi jagadvanamanayaiva hi pramadavanavasantaśriyātisukumāravividhatarulatākusumavāsanāsurabhīkṛtaṃ sakalasaṃtāpanivārakamānandānubhavaikabhūmiḥ pramadodyānamabhūt/ na tu bhāvairanyairanīdṛśaguṇagaṇojjvalaiḥ/ tadimāṃ kathāvaśeṣitāṃ kartumicchatā jagadidamatyudvegadāyi bhayaṃkaramaraṇyameva kevalaṃ cikīrṣitamityarthaḥ/ atra caikena vākyenotkarṣapratipādanāttasyaiva vākyāntarairapi pratipādanārthaṃ paunaruktyamāśaṅkya rasākṣiptavaktrā tasyaivārthasya prauḍhoktivaicitryena gambhīraviśeṣaṇopādānānna paunaruktyamiti bhojarājaḥ parijahāra/ yathāha - ``viśeṣaṇaṃ tu pūrvoktaṃ yadi bhūyo'pi kīrtyate/ tadekārthaṃ rasākṣiptaṃ tattasmāttu na dūṣyate//'' iti/ chekānuprāso'tra śabdālaṃkāraḥ/ ityalamalaṃkāragranthagauraveṇa//30//

 praṇayeti/ praṇayasakhīnāṃ premāspadānāmālīnāṃ salīlo yaḥ parihāsaḥ krīḍāviśeṣaḥ kusumastabakaprahārādistatra rasena rāgeṇādhigataiḥ svecchayaiva labdhairlalitairatisukumāraiḥ śirīṣakusumairhananairapi kiṃ punaranyena kaṭhinadravyena yadvapustāmyati sadya eva mlāyate tatra vapuṣi vadhāya śastramupakṣipataḥ pātayatastava śirasyakasmādeva nipātī yamadaṇḍaḥ kāladaṇḍa ivaiṣa bhujo vāmabāhuḥ patatviti samuddīpitakrodhasya mādhavasya raudrarasocito vāgārambhaḥ//31//

 aghoraghaṇṭaḥ/ prahara prahareti/ paśyāmi tava pauruṣamityupahāsagarbhoktiḥ/ ayaṃ na bhavasīti mādhavasyeva saṃnihitatvenopacārātpurovartitvadharmiprādhānyenedaṃśabdenocyate/ anyonyamanyonyavadhasya niṣpannaprāyatayā niścitatvādvartamānasāmīpyena laḍubhayatrāpi// mālatī/ prasīda nātha sāhasika, dāruṇaḥ khalvayaṃ hatāśaḥ/ tatparitrāyasva mām/ nivartatāmasmādanarthasaṃkaṭāt/ na mama strīmātrasya kṛte sakalalokamaulinā tvayā sāhasamācaraṇīyam/ yato'yaṃ dāruṇaḥ kiṃ kuryādvā/ tannivartasveti bhāvaḥ// kapālakuṇḍalā/ apramattaḥ sāvadhānaḥ// mādhavo mālatīmaghoraghaṇṭaśca kapālakuṇḍalāmekenobhābhyāṃ pāṭhyenobhayatrāpi tulyārthena ślokena samāśvāsayantau yugapadevāhatuḥ - mādhavāghoraghaṇṭau / ayi bhīru, iti saṃbodhanamidamubhayorubhe prati/ dhairyamiti/ he bhīru strīsvabhāvasulabhasādhvase, hṛdaye dhairyaṃ nidhehi, mā kātaryaṃ gamaḥ/ hata eṣa pāpa ityubhābhyāmubhayornirdeśaḥ/ hata eva/ kathamatrāpi mama pramādamāśaṅkase/ paśya kadācitkenacidvā sāraṅgasaṃhṛtividhau hariṇasaṃharaṇakṛtya ibhakumbhānāṃ kūṭasya saṅghasya kuṭṭākaḥ kuṭṭanaśīlaḥ/ ``jalpabhikṣakuṭṭaluṇṭhavṛṅaḥ ṣākan'' iti tācchīlye ṣākanpratyayaḥ/ pāṇikuliśasya pāṇireva kuliśaṃ kuliśamiva pāṇiryasya tādṛśasya hareḥ pramādaḥ kimanvabhāvi/ na kenacidanubhūtamityarthaḥ/ atra kṣudravipakṣapathe mama pramādo na saṃbhavatīti tadasaṃbhavalakṣaṇe vākyārthe hariṇapaterhareḥ pramādāsaṃbhavalakṣaṇo vākyārtho'dhyāropyamāṇaḥ sāraṅgavadhe siṃhasya pramādasaṃbhavena mamāpyetadvadhe pramādasaṃbhāvanetyupamānopameyabhāvaṃ parikalpayatīti ``asaṃbhavadharmasaṃbandha upamā parikalpitā'' ityuktalakṣaṇo nidarśanālaṃkāraḥ/ na cātra śleṣabhrāntiḥ kartavyā/ vākyadvayasyaiva yugapadubhābhyāṃ pāṭhāditi//32//

 kalakalaḥ kolāhalaḥ/ āśvāsayantī suśakunādinā duḥkhaṃ ślathayantī/ apratihatā kuṇṭhitā[apratihatākuṇṭhitā] prajñaiva cakṣuryasyāḥ sā bhagavatī kāmandakī samādiśati samājñāpayati/ kimiti/ paryavaṣṭabhyatāṃ pariveṣṭyatāmetatkarālāyatanam/ kimarthamityata āha - neti/ dāruṇādbhīṣaṇādaghoraghaṇṭādanyasmāttadvyatiriktādetanmālatyapaharaṇarūpaṃ karma nābhūt/ nāpi tadīyasya karmaṇo mantrasādhanārthaṃ karālopahārīkaraṇādanyaccāpi phalaṃ na vibhāvyate/ tasmāttenaiva kapālakuṇḍalayopahāritā devī balyarthamiti tadeva sthānaṃ vimṛśyatāmityājñāpayati bhagavatītyarthaḥ//33//

 kapālakuṇḍalā/ bhagavan, paryavaṣṭabdhāḥ parivṛtā bhaṭaiḥ/ kimataḥparaṃ kartavyamiti kātaryoktiḥ// aghoraghaṇṭaḥ- saṃpratīti/ rakṣaṇārthamāgatānāmevāgrata evaṃ vyāpādayiṣyāmi/ tanna bhetavyaṃ tvayetyāśvāsanoktiḥ/ mālatī/ hā tāta, hā bhagavati// mādhavaḥ/ bāndhavānāṃ rakṣaṇārthamāgatānāṃ samāje samūhe sukhena sthitāmenāṃ vidhāya paścānnaiścintyena yatnātteṣāmevāgrato haniṣyāmi/ yathā eka eva mālatīprāṇatrāṇanimittamasmatpurata evāsāvetaṃ durātmānaṃ hatvā mahāntamupakāraṃ kṛtavānityamātyāya nivedya tasya madarthaṃ mālatīpradāne rucimutpādayeyuriti bhāvaḥ/ ekameva vākyamubhāvubhayoddeśena prayuñjāte - re re iti/ kaṭhoreti/ kaṭhorāḥ kaṭhinā ye'sthigranthayaḥ kīkasakūṭāni taiḥ saha vyatikareṇa tadabhighātena janito yo ghaṇātkāro'nukaraṇaśabdastena mukharaḥ/ tathā kharāṇāṃ snāyūnāṃ chedena kṣaṇamātraṃ janito vegasya vyuparamo viśrāntiryasya saḥ/ tathā piśitakhaṇḍeṣvasthisnāyurahiteṣu māṃsakhaṇḍeṣu paṅkeṣviva nirātaṅko niṣpratirodho niṣpatannevaṃvidho'siḥ sapadi te gātraṃ gātraṃ pratyaṅgaṃ lavaśaḥ khaṇḍaśaḥ kṛtvā vikiratu dikṣu vikṣipatu/ bībhatsopaskṛto raudraraso vyajyate/ pratyakṣayuddhavarṇanasya niṣiddhatvānnoktam/ tatphalamagre sūcayiṣyate/ yaduktam - ``dūrādhvānaṃ vadhaṃ yuddhaṃ rājyadeśādiviplavam/ saṃrodhaṃ bhojanaṃ snānaṃ surataṃ cānulepanam// ambaragrahaṇādīni pratyakṣāṇi na nirdiśet//'' iti//34//

 yuddhārthaṃ niṣkrāntāḥ//

yathāmati mayākhyāto bhavabhūterihāśayaḥ/

tatsarvārthaparijñāne samarthāstu sacetasaḥ//

iti śrīmadbhāradvājakulatilakaparvatanāthayāyajūkanandanatripurārisūriviracitāyāṃ mālatīmādhavabhāvapradīpikāyāṃ pañcamāṅkavivaraṇam//

 ṣaṣṭho'ṅkaḥ/

anāhāryaguṇodārāmanāgantukabhūṣaṇām/

ayatnasiddhasārasyāmāśraye sahajāṃ giram//

kaviridānīmaṅkāntaramārabhamāṇo vṛttavartiṣyamāṇārthasūcanāyaikapātraprayojyaṃ śuddhaviṣkambhākhyamarthopakṣepakaṃ prastauti - tata ityādi/ kapālakuṇḍalā/ mālatīnimittaṃ vinipātito'smadgururyena tādṛśeti/ tādṛśena saṃbodhanena yuddhe mādhavenāghoraghaṇṭavadho, mālatyarthaṃ hi tvayāsmadgururnihatastanmālatyapaharaṇenaiva pratyupakariṣyāmīti kapālakuṇḍalābhiprāyaśca sūcyate/ tasminnavasare yuddhasamaye nirdayaṃ nighnatī strījātiprayuktaṃ bhīrutvaṃ parityajya guruvadhakopānniṣkaruṇaṃ praharantyahaṃ tvayā strītyavajñātāvadhīritā/ tasmādavaśyaṃ madavajñāyāḥ phalamanubhaviṣyasīti/ asmadguruvadhapātakasya mayi baddhavairāyāṃ jāgratyāṃ kutaḥ sukhamityeva vākyārthe tadavaśyamityādivākyoktervākyārthāntaramasaṃbhavadvastusaṃbandhātmakamupamānopameyaparikalpitaṃ nidarśanālaṃkāracchāyayādhyāropayannāha - śāntiriti/ tasya bhujaṅgaśatrorāśīviṣasya hantuḥ śāntiḥ saukhyaṃ kutaḥ/ yatra yasminnibaddhānuśayā dṛḍhatarabaddhavairātitīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrāgrā viṣodvamanabhīṣaṇā bhujaṅgī daṃśārthaṃ jāgarti sāvadhānā vartate/ tasyāḥ saukhyāsaṃbhavāttavāpi saukhyaṃ na saṃbhavatīti bhāvaḥ//1//

 vartiṣyamāṇaṃ sūcayati - bhorājāna iti/ he rājānaḥ sakaladigantaviśrāntaśāsanasya mahārājasya pādamūlasevārthamaṅgīkṛtakiṃkarabhāvāḥ sāmantamahīpatayaḥ caramavayasāṃ dṛṣṭaparāparavyavahārāṇāṃ vṛddhānāmājñayā kartavyavidāmājñayā kartavyeṣu prayojaneṣu saṃcaradhvam/ viprāśca śruteḥ śravaṇasya subhagaṃ yathā syāttathā paṭhantu/ āśīrvādamantrāniti śeṣaḥ/ nānāvacananivahaiḥ saṃskṛtaprākṛtādinānābhāṣāviśeṣaiḥ sahitaṃ citramāścaryakāri gītavādyanṛttādikaṃ maṅgalebhyo maṅgalārtham/ tādarthye caturthī/ ceṣṭyatāṃ vyāpriyatām/ tato janyānāṃ varasaṃbandhināṃ janānām/ ``janyāḥ snigdhā varasya ye'' ityamaraḥ/ yātrāyā varagṛhādvadhūgṛhagamanakriyāyāḥ praveśaḥ prāptistvarayati/ tvarāṃ janayatītyarthaḥ//2//

 na parāpatanti na samāgacchanti/ gṛhītaṃ saviśeṣamaṇḍanaṃ yena/ samyagalaṃkṛta ityarthaḥ/ ānuyātriko mālatyā anucaro janaḥ pratīkṣyatāṃ paripālyatām/ śīghramāhūyatāmityarthaḥ/ yadvānuyātriko varānugāmī tatsuhṛjjanaḥ/ gṛhītaṃ saviśeṣamaṇḍanamābharaṇavastrādikaṃ yasmātsa tādṛśaḥ pratīkṣyatāmabhyudgamanādinā saṃbhāvyatāmiti// kapālakuṇḍalā - bhavatvityādi/ tena nedānīṃ mālatyapaharaṇaṃ śakyamiti bhāvaḥ/ ākulājjanasaṃmardānmādhavasyāpakāro mālatyapaharaṇarūpastaṃ pratyabhiniviṣṭā dṛḍhataraprayatnā bhavāmi/ iti niṣkrāntā/ iti śuddhaviṣkambhaḥ// kalahaṃsaḥ/ ājñapto'smi nagaradevatāyā garbhagṛhavartinā makarandasanāthena mādhavena jānīhi tāvaditomukhaṃ pravṛttā mālatī na veti/ tadyāvadenamānandayiṣyāmi/ itomukhaṃ pravṛttā mālatīti priyanivedaneneti śeṣaḥ/ anena vākyena mādhavamakarandayoḥ kṛtrimapariṇayārthaṃ chadmanā nagaradevatāgarbhagṛhe pūrvameva praviśya nilīyāvasthānasūcanādabhūtāharaṇaṃ nāma garbhasaṃdhyaṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``abhūtāharaṇaṃ tatsyādvākyaṃ yatkapaṭāśrayam'' iti/ idameva tayoḥ praveśakaṃ ca// mādhavaḥ - mālatyā iti/ madanāyāsaprabandhasya smaravyathānubandhasya sarvathādya khalvito mālatīlābhānyatarakoṭiparicchedena paryavasānaṃ bhaviṣyatītyeṣa tattvārthakīrtanānmārgo nāma garbhasaṃdheraṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``tattvārthakīrtanaṃ mārgaḥ'' iti/ kathamatrānyatarakoṭipariccheda ityapekṣāyāṃ bhagavatīprayuktopāyasiddhyasiddhibhyāmityāha - bhagavatyāḥ kāmandakyā nītiḥ kṛtrimavivāharūpa upāye kalyāṇaṃ mālatīpāṇigrahaṇarūpaṃ vā vidadhātu/ tathā sati tatprāptyaiva madanāyāsasyānto bhaviṣyati/ athavā madbhāgyavaikalyādviparyetu viparītā bhavatu/ tathāpi prayuktopāyabhaṅgādupāyāntarābhāvācca tadalābhaniścaye dhruvaṃ mama vipattirityāśrayāntarābhāvādeva madanāyāsasyānto bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ/ kīdṛśasya madanāyāsasyetyapekṣāyāṃ tāvadāha - mālatyāḥ prathamadarśanakṣaṇādārabhya vistāriṇaḥ pravardhamānasya/ tathā mṛgadṛśastasyāḥ snehasūcakairviceṣṭitaiḥ sābhiprāyāvalokanādibhiḥ parāṃ niratiśayāṃ koṭiṃ prakṛṣṭatāṃ nītasyeti yojyam/ eṣā prāptyāśā nāma tṛtīyā kāryāvasthā/ yathā - ``upāyāpāyacintābhyāṃ prāptyāśā prāptisaṃbhavaḥ'' iti//3//

 makarandaḥ/ viparyeṣyati viparītā bhaviṣyati// kalahaṃsaḥ/ nātha, diṣṭyā vardhase/ pravṛttā khalvitomukhaṃ mālatī/ mādhavaḥ/ apisatkim/ satyamitomukhaṃ pravṛttetyasaṃbhāvayataḥ praśnaḥ// makarandaḥ/ aśraddadhāno viśvāsarahitaḥ/ asmākamiti/ marutā prabalapavanena vikīrṇaṃ viśakalitaṃ yajjīmūtajālaṃ tadrasitānukārī gambhīro yo maṅgalārthaṃ mṛdaṅgasahasrajanmā ninādaḥ so'smākamekapada eva svavyatiriktaśabdaśravaṇasāmarthyaṃ nirasyati/ matsamīpamāgatyāhetyarthaḥ//4//

 kalahaṃsaḥ/ nātha, paśya/ ime tāvanmaṅgaladhavalātapatranivahā dṛśyanta ityanvayaḥ/ kīdṛśāḥ/ utpatitānāṃ rājahaṃsānāṃ vibhrameṇeva vibhrameṇābhirāmāṇāṃ cāmarāṇāṃ samīraṇenodvelordhvaṃ carantī yā kadalikāvalī patākāpaṅktistayā taraṅgitaṃ saṃjātataraṅgaṃ yaduttānaṃ gaganāṅgaṇaṃ nabhobhāgeva sarastatra nirantarāṇāṃ sāndrāṇāmuddaṇḍapuṇḍarīkāṇāṃ yo vibhramastaṃ vahanto vibhrāṇāḥ/ atra gaganāṅgaṇasyottānatvaṃ gāmbhīryadyotanārtham/ gambhīre sarasi puṇḍarīkāṇāmuddaṇḍakāṇḍā bhavanti nānyatra/ savilāseti/ savilāsaṃ kavalitena tāmbūlenābhipūritasya kapolamaṇḍalasyābhogena vistāreṇa yo vyatikaraḥ saṃparko gītākṣarāṇāṃ tena skhalitaṃ yanmadhuraṃ maṅgalārthamudgītaṃ gaurīkalyāṇādi gānaṃ tena baddhaḥ kolāhalo yena taiḥ/ vividhānāṃ ratnālaṃkārāṇāṃ kiraṇāvalībhirviḍambitā anukṛtā ye mahendracāpavicchedāstairvicchuritaṃ vyāptaṃ nabhaḥsthalaṃ gaganasthalaṃ yeṣāṃ tairvārasundarīkadambakairadhyāsitāḥ kvaṇatkanakakiṃkiṇīraṇitajhaṇajhaṇatkāriṇyaḥ kariṇyaḥ/ dṛśyanta iti śeṣaḥ// makarandaḥ/ preṅkhaditi/ diśa unmukhānāmūrdhvaṃ prasṛtānāṃ maṇijyotiṣāmanekavarṇānāṃ vitānairvistārairevaṃvidhā bhavanti/ kiṃvidhāḥ/ unmeṣiṇāṃ prasaraṇaśīlānāṃ cāṣāṇāṃ kuliṅgānāṃ marutā ākulā ye chadāḥ pakṣāsteṣāṃ chāyābhiḥ saṃvalitairmiśrībhūtairvivartibhirvisṛmaraiḥ preṅkhatāṃ pracalatāṃ bhūrīṇāṃ bahūnāṃ mayūrāṇāṃ mecakacayaiścandrakarasamūhaiḥ prānteṣu paryantapradeśeṣu paritaḥ samantādāvṛtā iva bhavanti/ tathā vyaktākhaṇḍalakārmukā iva bhavanti/ tathā likhitacitrāṇāṃ cīnadeśodbhavānāmaṃśukānāṃ prastāreṇa saṃstareṇa sthagitā ācchāditā iva bhavantīti ratnālaṃkārabāhulyaṃ sūcitam/ tasmādatipuraṃdaravaibhavamamātyabhūrivasoraiśvaryamiti bhāvaḥ//5//

 kalahaṃsaḥ/ kathaṃ sasaṃbhramamāgatānāmanekeṣāṃ pratihārāṇāṃ vetrahastānāṃ maṇḍalenāvarjitābhiravanamitābhirujjvalena kanakena kaladhautena rūpyeṇa cāntarāntarā ghaṭitena citrābhirvetralatābhiḥ parikṣiptā bhūmau likhitā yā rekhā sā na laṅghanīyeti sīmātvena parikalpitā/ tayā racitamaṇḍalaḥ pṛthagavasthitaḥ sa tādṛśo jano dūrata eva sthitaḥ/ eṣā ca bahulasindūranikareṇa saṃdhyārāgeṇevoparaktamukhe vadane prādurbhāve ca madhuraṃ manoharaṃ yathā syāttathā ghūrṇamānā prakāśamānā nakṣatramālā hāraviśeṣo'śvinyāditārāpaṅktiśca tadevābharaṇaṃ dhārayatīti dhāriṇī tāṃ kareṇurajanīm/ rajanīmiva kareṇumityupamitasamāsenopamā vācyā/ alaṃkurvatīta eva kautūhalotphullamukhasamastalokadṛśyamānamanoharāpāṇḍuraparikṣāmadehaśobhāvibhāvitānaṅgavedanā/ ata eva viśeṣaṇadvayasya sāmarthyātprathamacandralekhāyāḥ pratipaccandrakalāyā vibhramaṃ vahantī kiṃcidantaraṃ parijanamaṇḍalādapasṛtyānyadeśaṃ prasṛtā mālatī/ kathamanyavibhramamanyā vahedityasaṃbhavadharmayogātsaṃdhyārāgoparaktatārāvalīvibhūṣitarajanīprārambhoditā svabhāvādaprauḍhatvācca pāṇḍukṣāmakautūhalonmukhasamastalokadṛśyamānā pratipaccandralekheva sindūroparaktahārālaṃkṛtakareṇumalaṃkurvatī virahabhāvanātpāṇḍukṣāmā sarvalokaprekṣaṇīyā mālatīti bimbapratibimbabhāvenārthāntarādhyāropānmukhanakṣatramālāśabdopāttaśleṣopaskṛtasindūrasaṃdhyārāgakareṇurajanīśabdopāttopamānugṛhīto nidarśanālaṃkāraḥ// makarandaḥ/ iyamiti/ iyaṃ varārohottamā nāyikā/ avayavaiḥ sahajalāvaṇyamayairvirahavaśātpāṇḍukṣāmaireva pratyutālaṃkṛtāni maṇḍanāni bhūṣaṇāni yasyāḥ sā/ alaṃkaraṇairna kaścidatiśayastadavayavānām/ kiṃtu tadgatatvena teṣāmeva śobhātiśayo dṛśyata ityarthaḥ/ tādṛśī satī māṅgalyamaṇḍanasanāthatayā ramyāṃ vivāhamahotsavaśriyamaupayikīṃ gurunirbandhādāhṛtāṃ bahiḥ kevalaṃ vahati/ tathātyarthamudayinīmidānīṃ pratikṣaṇamupacīyamānāṃ manorujaṃ manmathavedanāmavayavagatapāṇḍimakṛśimādibhireva vyanakti/ antargatamanupādhikaṃ duḥsahamanmathavyathāvikāraṃ dyotayatītyarthaḥ/ kathamiva/ bahiḥkalitakusumāntaḥkīṭādidoṣavaśātpariśoṣiṇī bālalatevetyupamālaṃkāraḥ/ dhīrā ceyaṃ nāyikā//6//
