 niṣāditopaveśitā// tataḥ praviśatīti/ pūrvavākyenaiva sūcitatvādatra mādhavamakarandayoḥ kathānubandhena kāmandakyādipraveśasūcanāttayośca kāmandakīṃ prati śiṣyatayā parijanatvāt/ ``parijanakathānubandhaḥ praveśako nāma vijñeyaḥ'' ityuktalakṣaṇapraveśako'yaṃ granthabhāga iti kaścidvyācaṣṭe/ sa vaktavyaḥ/ kathaṃ pratyakṣābhinayarūpāṅkamadhye kevalābhinayasūcanaikapātrasūcyārthasūcakasya praveśakasya niveśaḥ/ kathaṃ vā pradhānanāyakasya vastutaḥ śiṣyasyāpi praveśakapātratvaparijanatvādiparikalpanamanaucityāpādakamiti/ kathaṃ ca svapaṭhitasyottaraśloko na dṛṣṭaḥ/ ``aṅkacchedaṃ kṛtvā māsakṛtaṃ varṣasaṃcitaṃ vāpi/ tattatra sūcanīyaṃ varṣādūrdhvaṃ tu na kadācit//'' iti/ tathā ``divasāvasānakāryaṃ yadyaṅkenopapadyate sarvam/ aṅkacchedaṃ kṛtvā praveśakaistadvidhātavyam//'' ityādi bharatagrantheṣu tadanantaraślokeṣu bahupraveśakānabhidheyārthasūcakatvena pṛthageva niveśābhidhānāt/ ``eteṣvaṅkabahirbhāvaḥ syādviṣkambhapraveśayoḥ/ cūlikāyāḥ kvacidbāhye kvacinmadhye niveśanam''// iti bhāvaprakāśe'bhihitatvācca tasyāṅkamadhye niveśanaṃ pradhānanāyakasya naicyāṅgīkaraṇaṃ ca lokaśāstraviruddham/ atha praveśayatīti vyutpattyā praveśakatvasūcakamātreṇa praveśakatvavācoyuktistarhi na tasya parijanetyādikaṃ lakṣaṇam, kiṃtu viṣkambhakādipañcakāntargatasyeti tadudāharaṇamanugatam/ cūlikādīnāṃ tu trayāṇāṃ pātrapraveśasūcanārthameva svīkārāttathaiva lakṣitatvācca yujyate kvacitteṣāmaṅkamadhye pāta ityāstām// kāmandakī - saharṣamiti/ svaprayuktopāyasya sidhyaunmukhyaṃ harṣahetuḥ/ ``śreyāṃsi bahuvighnāni'' iti saṃbhāvitavighnavighātāyeṣṭadevatāṃ prārthayate - vidhāteti/ vidhātā brahmā/ anyonyānuguṇayogānmanoharāya vidhaye mālatīmādhavavivāharūpāya prayojanāya no'smākaṃ bhadraṃ vitaratu/ atra sarvajñāyā api bhagavatyā akārapraśleṣaṇādabhadramiti no iti ca niṣedhavācinā nipātena cāmaṅgalārthadyotakaṃ vākyaṃ kāryavyagratāhaṭhānnirgataṃ bhāvyaniṣṭamuttarāṅkasthaṃ dyotayatīti kecit/ devāḥ paramāmutkṛṣṭāṃ ramaṇīyāṃ pariṇatiṃ samucitavadhūvarasamāgamarūpāṃ vidheyāsuḥ/ ``erliṅi'' ityetvam/ priyasuhṛdapatyayorupanayataḥ saṃghaṭṭanādahaṃ kṛtārthā bhūyāsam/ uktaṃ hi prāk - ``prāṇaistapobhiḥ'' ityādi/ ayaṃ ca sarvaḥ suhṛdapatyapratijñāmārabhyaitadantaḥ prayatno'dya mālatīmādhavavivāharūpeṇa phalena phalatu/ śivatātiḥ śivaṃkaraśca bhavatu/ śivaśabdāt ``śivaśamariṣṭasya kare'' iti tātinpratyayaḥ/ ``kṣemaṃkaro'riṣṭatātiḥ śivatātiḥ śivaṃkaraḥ'' ityamaraḥ//7//

 atha mālatī muhūrtānantaraṃ nandanapāṇigrahaṇalakṣaṇavajranipātasamamutprekṣamāṇā durviṣahatatpāṇigrahaṇalakṣaṇaduḥkhātyantocchedakatvena maraṇameva śaraṇaṃ niḥśreyasaṃ manyamānā tamupāyamapi durlabhaṃ matvā vitarkayati - kena punaḥ sāṃpratamupāyena maraṇanirvāṇasyāntaramavasaraṃ saṃbhāvayiṣyāmi/ etadvitarkapratipādanādrūpaṃ nāmāṅgam/ yathā - ``vitarko rūpamucyate'' iti/ maraṇamapi mandabhāgadheyāyā me'bhimatamatidurlabhaṃ bhavati// lavaṅgikā/ atikleśitā khalu priyasakhyetenānukūlasya priyasya vipralambhena// praviśya/ āheti śeṣaḥ/ paṭalakamābharaṇadhāraṇapātram/ pratihārī/ amātyo bhagavatīṃ bhaṇati/ etena narendrānupreṣitavivāhanepathyena devatāyāḥ purato'laṃkartavyā mālatīti// kāmandakī/ māṅgalikaṃ maṅgalaprayojanamiti ṭhak// pratihārī/ etattāvaddhavalapaṭṭāṃśukayugalam/ etaccottarīyavarṇāṃśukam/ ime ca sarvāṅgikā ābharaṇasaṃyogā ābharaṇānyeva saṃyojyante sthāpyante yeṣvavayaveṣviti saṃyogāḥ/ karmaṇi ghañ/ sarvāṅgikāḥ sarvāṅgasthāpyāḥ/ ime ca mauktikahārāḥ/ etaccandanam/ eṣa sitakusumaśekhara iti// kāmandakī/ ramaṇīyamiti/ anena prasādhanena prasādhitaṃ mālatīrūpadhāriṇaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ makarandaṃ jano vipralabdho mālatībuddhyā kṣaṇāntaramavekṣyatītyarthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ bhagavatī punaḥ/ kva yāsyatīti śeṣaḥ// mālatī/ lavaṅgikāmātraparivārā tāvatsaṃvṛttā/ tadidānīṃ yathākathaṃcidenāṃ lavaṅgikāmupanīya śakyaṃ prāṇāṃstyuktamiti bhāvaḥ/ inaṃ devatāmandiradvāram/ tatpraviśatu priyasakhī// stambhāntaritau stambhāpavāritau// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, ayamaṅgarāgaḥ/ imāḥ kusumamālāḥ// mālatī/ tataḥ kim/ martukāmāyā mana[ḥ] kimebhirityāśayaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, asminpāṇigrahaṇamaṅgalasyārambha upakramasamaye kalyāṇasya vivāhamaṅgalasya saṃpattiravighnena niṣpattistannimittaṃ tadarthaṃ devatāṃ pūjayetyambayānupreṣitāsi/ tadetairdevatāmarcayeti śeṣaḥ/ mālatī/ kasmādidānīṃ dāruṇasamārambhasya daivasya yo durvilāso durvyāpārastatpariṇāmarūpeṇa duḥkhena nirdalitaṃ kheditaṃ mānasaṃ yasyāstāṃ punarapi marmacchedaduḥsahāṃ mandabhāginīmupatāpayasi/ idaṃ cātmagatameva lavaṅgikāṃ saṃbodhyopālambha iti draṣṭavyam// lavaṅgikā/ ayi kiṃvaktukāmāsi/ tadvadeti śeṣaḥ// mālatī/ kimidānīṃ durlabhaviṣayo yo'bhiniveśaḥ prayatnaviśeṣaratatra yo manorathastatra visaṃvadadviparītībhavadbhāgadheyaṃ yasya tādṛśo jano mantrayate/ priyatamasamāgamarūpamanorathavisaṃvāde maraṇaṃ mayāpekṣitaṃ tadapīdānīṃ bhāgyavaikalyānmama durlabhaṃ jātamiti kimidānīṃ mayā vaktavyamityāśayaḥ/ etacca prastutamaraṇotkarṣadyotakatvādudāharaṇaṃ nāmāṅgam/ yathā - ``yattu sātiśayaṃ vākyaṃ tadudāharaṇaṃ bhavet'' iti// makarandaḥ/ sakhe, śrutamityatra kākuḥ// mādhavaḥ/ asaṃtoṣastviti svanimittatvasyāsphuṭatvāt// mālatī/ paramārthabhagini priyasakhi lavaṅgike, eṣedānīṃ te priyasakhyanāthā maraṇe vartamānā āgarbhanirgamanirantaropārūḍhavisrambhasadṛśaṃ pariṣvajyābhyarthayate/ tatra prastutopayogisāmavacanatvātsaṃgraho nāmāṅgam/ yathā - ``saṃgrahaḥ sāmadānoktiḥ'' iti/ yadi te'hamanuvartanīyā tato māṃ hṛdayena dhārayantī samagrasaubhāgyalakṣmīparigrahaikamaṅgalaṃ mādhavasya śrīmukhāravindamānandamasṛṇaṃ yathā syādevaṃ pralokaya/ anena mannirviśeṣayā tvayā māṃ smarantyā priyatamo dṛṣṭaścenmayaiva dṛṣṭa iti pratītermādhavaviṣayaḥ premaiva parāṃ koṭimārūḍhaḥ/ sarvathā tadalābhaniścayānmaraṇaṃ niścitamiti vyajyate/ ānandamasṛṇamityanena ca yena prakāreṇāhaṃ tamavalokayāmi tenaivāvalokaya, na tu svocitena tāṭasthyenetyuktam// mādhavaḥ/ svaviṣayatāṃ premabandhasya niratiśayatāṃ ca jñātvāha - mlānasyeti/ diṣṭyā bhāgyena mayā tādṛśasaubhāgyānarheṇāpi vacomṛtānyetānyadhigatāni prāptāni/ kīdṛśāni/ priyāprāptyasaṃbhāvanayā glānimupagatasya jīvasyaiva saukumāryātkusumasya vikāsanāni/ tathā saṃtarpaṇāni samyaktṛptijanakāni/ tena hi mālatyāḥ śataśo darśane'pīdṛśavacanaśravaṇādyādṛśī tṛptistādṛśī na bhavatīti vacanānāṃ saṃtarpakatvam/ tathā sakalendriyāṇāṃ mohanāni/ pralayākhyasāttvikabhāvahetutvāt/ indriyāstamayohi pralayaḥ/ tathānandanāni niratiśayānandasyātmasaṃvedyatvādānandajanakāni/ tathā hṛdayasya virahaduḥkhaśoṣitasarvarasādidhātukasya rasāyanāni rasādidhātusaṃpādakatvena rasajanakāni/ ``nīrasānāṃ hi dhātūnāṃ lābhopāyo rasāyanam'' iti rasāyanam//8//

 mālatī/ yathā tasya jīvitapradāyino'hamavasitā mṛteti māṃśrutvā saṃtapyamānasya tattathāvidhaṃ śarīraratnaṃ na parihīyate, yathā ca lokāntaragatāmapi māmuddiśya sa janaḥ smaraṇakathāmātrapariśeṣāṃ kālāntareṇāpi lokayātrāṃ na śithilīkaroti tathā kuru/ atra mādhavamaraṇaśaṅkābījaṃ svātmani mādhavānurāgotkarṣaḥ/ yathāhaṃ tadalābhāttadanurāge jīvitaṃ tyakṣyāmi tathāsāvapi madanurāgotkarṣācchmaśānapradeśavṛttāntāvagamācca mayyavasitāyāmasau svajīvitanirapekṣastyajedapi śarīram/ yathā tu jīvati tathā kurviti/ tvatprayatnavaśātkathaṃcidaparityaktajīvito'sau kālakrameṇa yathā māṃ na vismarati tadapi tvayaiva kartavyamiti bhāvaḥ/ evaṃ te priyasakhī mālatī sakāmā bhavati// makarandaḥ/ hanteti premātiśayamaraṇodyogapratīterharṣaviṣādadyotakaḥ// mādhavaḥ - nairāśyeti/ nairāśyena matpāṇigrahaṇapratyāśābhāve taddhetukasvajīvitapratyāśābhāvena kātarā cakitā dhīryasyāstasyā hariṇekṣaṇāyā nikāmaṃ bhṛśaṃ karuṇaṃ maraṇodyogasūcanācchokātiśayajanakam/ atha svaviṣayānurāgaprakarṣapratītermanoharaṃ ca vātsalyena ratibhāvena/ yadyapi vātsalyaśabdo'patyaviṣayāṃ ratimācaṣṭe, tathāpyatra lakṣaṇayā ratisāmānyamātramāha, na tu vātsalyaṃ nāma daśamaṃ rasāntaram/ prasiddhālaṃkāraprasthāneṣvavyutpādanāt/ pratyuta niṣedhādeva gurunṛpaputrādiviṣayāyā raterbhāvatvameva hi vyavasthāpitam/ ``ratirdevādiviṣayā vyabhicārī tathā jitā''/ tasmāt ``tamaṅkamāropya śarīrayogajaiḥ'' ityādau putragocarāyā rateḥ paripoṣādasaṃlakṣyakramavyaṅgyabhedo bhāvadhvanireva rasāntaram/ na ca bharatenātra pṛthaksthāyī pradarśitaḥ/ nāpi ratirevāsya sthāyī/ tasyāḥ śṛṅgāraikasthāyitvāt, asya rasāntaratvābhāvaprasaṅgācca/ nāpi śṛṅgārabheda evāyam/ tadbhedeṣu saṃbhogādiṣvaparigaṇanāt/ mātrādīnāṃ putrādau ``śṛṅgārasyātyantānaucityācca/ astu vā vātsalyaṃ rasāntaram/ tathāpi prakṛte tanna saṃbhavati/ tasyāpatyamātrālambanatvāt/ na hi mālatyā mādhave vātsalyaṃ yuktamityarthaḥ/ alaṃ kutsitena vicāraṇena/ tasmādatra ratisāmānyaṃ lakṣyate/ tena mohena ``mohaścittasya śūnyatā'' ityuktena vyabhicāriṇā ca yatparidevitaṃ vilāpavacanaṃ tatra tadgatacintayā madalābhādiyaṃ prāṇānapi tyaktumudyatā/ anayā kimidānīṃ kartavyamityevaṃrūpayā yo viṣādo viṣaṇṇatvameva vipat/ tāṃ ca mahotsavaṃ ca/ aho me saubhāgyaṃ yadetādṛśasya strīratnasya mayyevaṃvidhānurāgabandhastadetallābhābhāve'pyetāvataiva labdhajanmasāphalyo'hamityevamātmano harṣotkarṣaḥ/ taṃ ca vahāmi/ uktaṃ ca - ``parasparaprāptinirāśayorvaraṃ śarīranāśo'pi samānarāgayoḥ'' iti/ śokaharṣābhyāṃ karuṇaśṛṅgārasaṃkīrṇaṃ mano vartata iti bhāvaḥ//9//

 lavaṅgikā/ ayi, pratihataṃ te'maṅgalam/ ito'pyaparaṃ na śroṣyāmi// mālatī/ sakhi, priyaṃ khalu yuṣmākaṃ mālatījīvitam/ na punarmālatī// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, kimiti bhaṇitaṃ bhavati/ mālatījīvitaṃ priyaṃ na tu mālatīti vacanasya vyāhatatvāditi bhāvaḥ// mālatī/ yena pratyāśānibandhanairvacanasaṃvidhānairjīvayitvaitāvantaṃ kālamīdṛśaṃ mahābībhatsārambhamanubhavitāsmi/ tadanenātijugupsitamapi mama jīvitameva yuṣmākaṃ priyaṃ yathā tathā vā jīvati cedalamiti na tu duḥkhavivarjitā mālatī kathaṃ sukhena tiṣṭhatīti yuṣmākaṃ na vicāra ityasya svavākyasyābhiprāyo varṇito bhavati/ mahābībhatsārambhamatijugupsitaṃ nandanapāṇigrahaṇodyogamityarthaḥ/ sāṃprataṃ punarmama manoratha etāvānena[va]/ yattasya devasya/ jīvitapradāyino mādhavasyeti yāvat/ parakīyatvena parādhīnatvenāparāddhamātmānaṃ parityajya nirvṛtā bhaviṣyāmīti jīvitaparitrāṇājjīvitasya sa eva svāmīti tadīyasyāsya jīvitasya gurupāratantryādanyāyattīkaraṇodyogo'parādhaḥ/ tasya ca tadīyajīvitaparityāga eva prāyaścittamanyādhīnatāsaṃpādanakalaṅkāpākaraṇāditi bhāvaḥ/ asminprayojane priyasakhī me'paripanthinyapratikūlā bhavatu// makarandaḥ/ paramā sīmā parākāṣṭhā/ netaḥparaṃ snehasyātiśaya iti bhāvaḥ/ atha pūrvameva vihitasaṃketā lavaṅgikā stambhāntaritaṃ mādhavaṃ pratyavasaro'yaṃ tvadāgamanasyeti hastasaṃjñayā bodhayatīti// mādhavaḥ/ paravānasmi parādhīnaḥ kṛto'smi sādhvasena vepathustambhādinā sāttvikena, na tu bhayena/ tasyedānīmanucitatvāttena śaṅkāmātraṃ sādhvasaśabdena vivakṣitam/ śaṅkayā ca stambhādayo lakṣyanta iti grāhyam/ mālatīsamīpe lavaṅgikādhiṣṭhite pradeśe sthātuṃ paravānadhīno'smīti// makarandaḥ - iyamiti/ nedīyasāmatisaṃnihitānāṃ cirābhilaṣitānāmabhyudayānāṃ priyāprāptiprabhṛtīnāmabhīṣṭānāmiyameva prakṛtiḥ svabhāvaḥ/ yatstambhapralayavepathuprabhṛtisāttvikāvirbhāvakṛtaṃ pāravaśyamityarthaḥ/ etacca prakṛtopayogitvena vañcanādadhibalaṃ nāmāṅgam/ yathā - ``ceṣṭayā sātisaṃdhānaṃ vadantyadhibalaṃ hi tat''// mālatī/ sakhi, anukūlatayā prasādaṃ kuru/ maraṇamabhijānīhītyarthaḥ/ atha mādhavo lavaṅgikātvamivāpannastasyāśca svasya ca dhāraṇaṃ prākṛtasaṃskṛtayoḥ samānarūpaṃ vākyaṃ prayuṅkte - sarala iti/ sarale ṛjubuddhe/ rambhoru/ ``ūrūttarapadādaupamye'' ityūṅ/ sāhase maraṇodyoge rāgamicchāṃ parihara parityaja/ saṃrambhaṃ muñca/ yatastava virahāyāsaṃ viyogaduḥkhaṃ virasamatyantavināśarūpaṃ mama hṛdayaṃ soḍhumasahamakṣamamiti tatsamatvādeteṣāṃ śabdānāṃ prākṛtasaṃskṛtayoḥ samānaṃ rūpam/ yathāha - ``tatsamaṃ tadbhavaṃ deśītyanekaḥ prākṛtakramaḥ'' iti/ na cātra bhāṣāśleṣabhrāntiḥ kāryā/ vācyabhedābhāvācchleṣatvānupapatteḥ/ na ca lavaṅgikāmādhavayorvaktrorbhedādvācyabhedo'pyastyeveti vācyam/ mādhavasyātra vaktṛtvātkiṃtu haṭhādeva lavaṅgikābuddhitirodhānena svaprajñāpanena vailakṣaṇyānniḥśaṅkaṃ svamanogatasarvasvanivedanaṃ na kuryāditi lavaṅgikāvācyamevārthaṃ taducitaireva śabdairmādhavaḥ pratipāditavānityekameva vākyameka evārtha iti na śleṣāvakāśa iti//10//

 mālatī/ alaṅghanīyaste mālatyāḥ praṇāmaḥ// pūrvavadeva mādhavastatsamaireva śabdairlavaṅgikevāha - kiṃ veti/ vicchedenātyantaviprayogeṇa dāruṇo ya āyāso maraṇodyogātmakaṃ duṣkaraṃ karma tatkāriṇīti saṃbodhanam/ īdṛśīṃ tvāṃ kiṃ vā bhaṇāmi kathaṃ mriyasvetyanujñāṃ dadyām/ na kathaṃcinmaraṇānujñāṃ kartuṃ śaknomītyarthaḥ/ tasmāttvameva ``īpsitārthasthiraniścayaṃ manaḥ'' iti nītyā kāmaṃ svābhilaṣitaṃ kuru/ he varārohe, mama parirambhaṇaṃ dehi/ māmāliṅgetyarthaḥ/ atha ca mādhavaucityena kāmaṃ manorathaṃ kuruniṣpādaya/ varārohe cirasya prārthitaṃ parirambhaṇaṃ dehītyapi pratīyate/ lavaṅgikayā praśaṃsārthaṃ varārohe iti sakhīsaṃbodhanaṃ yuktameveti//11//

 atha mālatī kāmaṃ kurviti sakhyā anumatiṃ matvāha - mālatī/ kathamanugṛhītāsmīti/ sakhyeti śeṣaḥ/ iyamāliṅgāmi/ darśanaṃ bāṣpotpīḍanenāśrūdgamena priyasakhyāḥ pratyakṣaṃ na labhyate/ evaṃ lavaṅgikābhrameṇa mādhavamāliṅgyāmṛtasarasīva nimagnā niratiśayaṃ nirvṛtiviśeṣamanubhavantyāha - sakhi, kaṭhorakamalagarbhapakṣmalo'nyādṛśa eva te'dya nirvāpayati māṃ śarīrasparśaḥ/ kaṭhorakamalagarbhavatpariṇatapaṅkajabījakośavatpakṣmalo romāñcakañcukitaḥ/ idānīṃ kṛtamaraṇaniścayā sakhyā cānumatiṃ kṛtāṃ manyamānā priyatamaṃ prativaktavyaṃ vācikaṃ saṃdiśati - kiṃ ca/ mauliviniveśitāñjalirmama vacanena vijñāpaya taṃ janam/ taṃ janamityanena tasya patitvaṃ niścitya sākṣānnāmagrahaṇānaucityaṃ sūcayati/ tena ca nāhaṃ kanyaiva saṃsthitā manmathenaiva mādhavāya dattā tatpatnyeva kevalam, tādṛśena patyā saha saṃsārasaukhyānyanubhavituṃ nāsti me bhāgyamiti, ata eva śarīraṃ tyajāmīti vyajyate/ vijñāpanāprakāramevāha - yathā na mayā mandabhāgyayāvikasacchatapatralakṣmīvilāsahāriṇo mukhacandramaṇḍalasya te svacchandadarśanena saṃbhāvitaściraṃ locanamahotsavaḥ/ mudhā manorathairavirataṃ vijṛmbhamāṇadurvāraduḥkhāvegavyatikarodvartamānabandhanaṃ dhāritaṃ hṛdayam/ gamitāśca vāraṃvāraṃ saviśeṣaduḥsahāyāsadhūpāyitasakhījanāḥ śarīrasaṃtāpāḥ/ kathamapyativāhitāścandrātapamalayamārutapramukhā anarthaparamparāḥ/ sāṃprataṃ punarnirāśā saṃvṛttāsmīsi[] vijñāpayetyanvayaḥ/ udvartamānabandhanamunmūlitamūlamativāhitā duḥkhena gamitāḥ/ eṣā ca mādhavaśrīhastanirmāṇamanoharā bakulamālā mālatīnirviśeṣaṃ priyasakhyā draṣṭavyā sarvadā hṛdayena dhāri[ra]ṇīyā ceti vadantī svakaṇṭhādunmucya mādhavahṛdi vinyasyantī stanaśūnyatvavyūḍhoraskatvādinā puruṣamadhigamya puruṣasya cānyasya tatrātyantamaśaṅkitatvānmādhavasyaiva kāmandakīprayatnena saṃnidhānasaṃbhavāttaditaraśarīrasparśe tādṛśaparamanirvṛtisāttvikalajjāśaṅkātrāsādivyabhicārisaṃvalanonmeṣābhāvāttadaucityena sahasāpasṛtya sādhvasenotkampaṃ vepathumanubhāvaṃ ca nāṭayatītyarthaḥ/ sādhvasaṃ trāsaḥ/ tallakṣaṇaṃ ca - ``trāsaścittacamatkāra ākasmikabhayādibhiḥ'' iti/ anena saṃbhramo nāmāṅgamuktam/ yathā - ``śaṅkātrāsau ca saṃbhramaḥ'' iti// mādhavaḥ/ ekīkṛta iti/ gāḍhāliṅganavaśānnirbhugnapīnakucakuḍmalayānayāvapīḍya gāḍhamāliṅgya me tvaci karpūrādisaṃtāpanirvāpakaśītaladravyavarga ekīkṛta ekarasatāṃ prāpto niṣikta iva/ āliṅganaṃ kṛtavatyā karpūrādirasaniṣekaḥ kṛta ivetyāliṅganakriyāyāṃ niṣekakriyādhyavasāyātpratīyamānasaṃtāpanirvāpaṇanimittakriyayā svabhāvotprekṣālaṃkāraḥ/ eṣa saṃcintyamānārthaprāptirūpaḥ kramaḥ/ yathā - ``kramastu cintitāvāptiḥ'' iti//12//

 mālatī/ aho, lavaṅgikayā mālatī vipralabdhā vañcitā/ svābhilaṣitasaṃpādane'pyākāragopanārthaṃ vañcanamityuktam/ anenāvahitthākhyo vyabhicārī sūcitaḥ/ yathā - ``avahitthā tu lajjāderharṣādyākāragopanam'' iti// mādhavaḥ/ ayīti saṃbodhane/ svamanovyathāmeva tvaṃ prakaṭayasi na tu parajanamanorujaṃ jānāsi/ yato madāliṅganena vañcitamātmānaṃ manyase/ na punaryathā me tathaivāsyāpi samaḥ suduḥsahaḥ saṃtāpaḥ/ tanmadāliṅganenāsya bhavatu saṃtāpanirvāpa ityanukampā tvayi dṛśyate/ tasmāttvaṃ purata evopālabhasa ityarthaḥ/ svavyathāmevāha - uddāmeti/ uddāmaḥ prauḍhodehaparidāha eva mahājvaro yeṣu tāni/ tathā manorathamātrakalpitenāpi tvatsamāgamena vinoditāpanoditā vedanā tvadvirahajanyā vyathā yeṣu tāni/ tathā ``asti mālatyā mayi snehaḥ'' iti saṃvedanamātreṇāvalambitaṃ kathaṃcittvatsnehaḥ kadācitphaliṣyatītyāśayā vidhṛtaṃ jīvitaṃ yeṣu tādṛśāni dināni mayāpi tvayeva na kimativāhitāni duḥkhena gamitāni/ yādṛśī tava virahavyathā tādṛśyeva mamāpi/ tattu kevalaṃ tvayā viditametanna viditamityarthaḥ/ etattu parasparānurāgabījasamutkṣepādākṣepo nāmāṅgam/ yathā - ``garbhabījasamutkṣepādākṣepaḥ parikīrtitaḥ'' iti//13//

 lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, upālambhanīyamupālabdhāsi/ yuktamevānena tvadupālambhanamityarthaḥ// kalahaṃsaḥ/ aho sarasaramaṇīyatā saṃvidhānasya// makarandaḥ/ etanmādhavenoktamevaṃ satyameva/ tadevāha - tvaṃ vatsaleti/ vatsalā premavatī svasminnityetāvataiva kathamapyatikleśenāvalambitātmā vidhṛtajīvito'yaṃ jana iyato divasānanaiṣīdativāhitavāniti satyameva tvatsnehādanyo nāsya prāṇadhāraṇe kaściddhetuḥ/ atastvayaivāsya jīvitarakṣaṇe niḥśaṅkaṃ pravartitavyam, na kāmandakyādiṣu bhāro nikṣeptavya iti bhāvaḥ/ etadevāha - ābaddhakaṃkaṇasya karasya yaḥ praṇayaḥ parigrahaḥ sa eva prasādo'nugrahastamāsādya prāpyāsau nandatu/ tathā cirāya cireṇa kāmā me manorathāścirakālakāṅkṣitā yuvayoḥ phalantviti//14//

 lavaṅgikā/ mahānubhāva, hṛdaye'pyapratihatasvayaṃgrāhasāhaso'yaṃ janaḥ kimidānīṃ karagrahaṇe vicārayati/ yena mādhavena hṛdaye svayameva gṛhyamāṇena nivāritavatī kevalamayaṃ mahānpuruṣārtha ityaṅgīkṛtavatī sā kareṇa kare svayaṃ gṛhyamāṇe'pi na vicārayet, tasmānniḥśaṅkaṃ svayamevāsyāḥ karaṃ gṛhṇātvityāśayaḥ// mālatī/ hā dhik, kanyakājanaviruddhaṃ kimapyupanyasyati/ kimapītigurujanānanumataṃ svakaragrahaṇaṃ vyajyate// kāmandakī/ praviśya/ vadatīti śeṣaḥ/ putrīti tatpariṇayane mātṛtvādastyeva mamādhikāra iti vyaṅgyam/ kātare kulakanyakājanaviruddhaṃ kathaṃ kuryāmiti duḥśaṅkodvignamānase kathaṃciddaivānugrahādupanate'vasare śīghravidheye kṛtya etadvilambanaṃ kiṃnimittam/ avicāryaiva śīghramasya pāṇiṃ gṛhāṇeti bhāvaḥ// pura iti/ yasya tvayi viṣaye yatra yasminviṣaye ca tava pūrvaṃ cakṣuḥprītistadanu cittāsaṅgastadanu saṃkalpādikrameṇa tanuglāniścetyādyā madanāvasthāḥ prādurāsan/ atra manaḥsaṅgakārśyāni vācyāni manaso'nanyaparatvena taccintaikavyāpṛtatvena saṃkalpajāgarakārśyatanuglānyaratayo vyajyante, sa evāyaṃ yuvā preyāniti vayasā premṇā cānurūpatvaṃ dhvanyate tasmādihāsminsuvadane sarvadā suvadane jaḍatāmapratipattiṃ muñca/ vidhāturvaidagdhyaṃ bhavadubhayanirmāṇanaipuṇyaṃ bhavadubhayasamāgamenaiva vilasatu saphalaṃ bhavatu/ madanaśca sakāmo bhavatu/ jāḍyaṃ vihāya yāvadunmadādyavasthāntarāṇi nollasanti tāvadeva preyāṃsaṃ vṛṇīṣveti bhāvaḥ/ jāḍyaṃ ca vyabhicāribhāvaḥ/ yathā - ``kriyāsvapāṭavaṃ jāḍyaṃ cintotkaṇṭhābhayādibhiḥ'' iti//15//

 lavaṅgikā/ bhagavati, kṛṣṇacaturdaśīrajanyām/ tasyāmeva sāhasakarmaprasiddherāgamoktatvāt/ smaśānasaṃcāreṇa nirvyūḍho nirvartito mahāmāṃsavikrayarūpo viṣama āryajanānucito vyavasāyo yena/ anena mālatyāṃ mādhavasya prakṛṣṭo'nurāgabandha uktaḥ sa cāsau niṣṭhāpitaṃ nirāghāṭitaṃ pracaṇḍapāṣaṇḍadordaṇḍasāhasaṃ mālatīvadharūpaṃ karma yena sa ityanena jīvitatrāṇenopakārātiśaya uktaḥ/ sāhasikaḥ khalveṣaḥ/ ataḥ priyasakhyutkampitā/ atra lavaṅgikā tasyāṃ niśīthinyāmekākī bhūtvātyantajugupsitanṛmāṃsavikrayasāhasarasiko bhīṣaṇapākhaṇḍavadhādraudraprakṛtiravimṛśyakārī cāsau svayamevānayā dṛṣṭaḥ, tadasminbālāyā yuktaiva jaḍatā kampamānatā ceti mādhavamuddiśya narmoktivyājena niratiśayapremṇā paramopakāriṇi cāsminnucitamevāsminnavasare jāḍyādikamasyā iti vaiparītyenopālabhata hati voddhavyam// mālatī - hā tāteti/ evaṃvidhānucitakāriṇyahaṃ kathaṃ punarapi tvanmukhaṃ vilokayeyamiti śeṣaḥ// kāmandakī - iyamiti/ aśeṣetyanena niratiśayaiśvaryasaṃpannasyāpatyaṃ na daridrasyeti tasyāpyekatvenātīva premabhājanaṃ tadapi ratnamiva ratnaṃ saundaryādyanekaguṇaśālyapatyamiyaṃ mālatītyanenāsyāmanādarastvayā na kathaṃcidapi kartavya iti sūcayati/ seyaṃ prathamaṃ sadṛśayoranyonyānurūpayoryuvayoḥ saṃyogarasikena vedhasā sa khalūtpādayanneveyamasmā ayaṃ cāsyā iti nirṇayaṃ bhālatale likhitavān/ tadanantaraṃ manmathenākasmādeva dvayormanoghaṭakena mayā ca taducitamānuṣaprayatnavidhāyinyā tubhyaṃ dīyate// rodanaṃ ca kanyāpradānānantaraṃ mātṛtvādvātsalyācca// bāṣpāyitaṃ bāṣpodvamanaṃ kṛtam/ ``bāṣpoṣmabhyāmudvamane'' iti ṇijantādbhāve ktaḥ// vijñāpayāmītyuktiḥ kāmandakyāḥ kanyāpradānameva tatkaiṅkaryamiti nītyā vinayadyotanārtham// mādhavastu gurujanaucityenāha - ājñāpayeti// kāmandakī - pariṇatīti/ tvadvidhānāṃ mahākulīnatvādiguṇaśālināṃ mahānāyakānāṃ prītayaḥ snehāḥ pariṇatiramaṇīyāḥ paripākena manoharāḥ na tu kṣudrajanasnehavatpariṇativirasāḥ/ ato na mayā kiṃcidapi vaktavyam/ tathāhamapi taistairhetubhirnimittaiḥ pitṛsnehavātsalyādibhirmānyānullaṅghyavacanā/ tattasmādihāsyāṃ suvadanāyām/ he tāta/ vātsalyātiśayādapatye'pi tātaśabdaḥ prayojyaḥ/ sa tādṛśaḥ paricayo gāḍhasneha eva karuṇā kṛpā tasyāḥ sakāśānmā viraṃsīrvirato mā bhūḥ/ asyāṃ snehānubandhenaiva mayi tvayā kṛpā kṛtetyarthaḥ/ viraṃsīriti ``vyāṅparibhyo ramaḥ'' iti parasmaipadam/ ramiśca śyani paṭhyate manirityanij tkam
(
ṛ)/ māṅyogādaḍāgamapratiṣedhaḥ/ vināvyavadhānādaḍāgamapratiṣedho nāstītyaśābdikoktaṃ heyam/ ``mā tāta sāhasaṃ kārṣīrmama pūrvaparigrahaḥ/ praśnānuktvā tu kaunteya pibasva ca harasva ca//'' ityādau bahuvyavadhāne'pi pratiṣedhadarśanāt//16//

 iti pādayoḥ patitumicchati// mādhavaḥ/ vātsalyādapatyapremṇaḥ prasaṅgaḥ prastāvo'tikrāmatyaucityamullaṅghayate/ yatsakalalokavandyā bhagavatī svayaṃ matpraṇāmaṃ kartumicchati/ anucitakarmaṇi pravartata iti bhāvaḥ// makarandaḥ - bhagavatīti/ ślāghyeti/ ślāghyānvayā mahākulaprasūteti hetoḥ/ tathā nayanotsavakāriṇīti niratiśayasaundaryaśālinīti/ tathā nirvyūḍho nirvāhaṃ gamitaḥ sauhṛdarasaḥ sauhārdasārabhūtā ratiryayā sā tādṛśīti/ tathā guṇairanyaiḥ sauśīlyādibhiraparigaṇanīyairujjvalā nirmaleti/ eteṣvekaikamevānyanirapekṣaṃ garīyo'natikramaṇīyaṃ vaśīkaraṇametadāyattībhāvasādhanam/ tadupari yuṣmākaṃ nirupādhikahitācaraṇapravṛttānāṃ sarvalokamānyānāmapīyamevaṃvidhā dṛśyate/ vātsalyātiśayācchiśormādhavasya praṇāmaścikīrṣita ityukte'tha kimahaṃ bravīmi/ itaḥ paramasyāmādarātiśayānubandhane kiṃ vaktavyamityarthaḥ//17//

 athobhayoḥ sādhāraṇaṃ dharmyamupadeśamāha - preya iti/ preyaḥ priyatamaḥ/ mitraṃ sakhā/ tathā samagrā sakalā bandhutā bandhusamūhaḥ/ sarve'khilāḥ kāmyanta iti kāmā viṣayāstathā/ śevadhirnidhiḥ/ kiṃ bahunā jīvitaṃ vā strīṇāṃ bhartaiva, puṃsāṃ ca dharmadārāḥ/ evaṃ snigdhamitrāṇītyetaddharmarahasyaṃ lokaśāstrasiddhaṃ vatsayorjñātaṃ manogataṃ sarvathāstu/ etāvatsarvadānusaṃdheyamityarthaḥ/ vatsayorityatra ``pumānstriyā'' ityekaśeṣaḥ/ ubhayorvātsalyasya tulyatvādvatsaśabdaprayogaḥ//18//

 makarandaḥ/ atha kim/ evamevaitadityarthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ yathā yūyamājñāpayatha// kāmandakī/ evamatra vivāhamaṅgalaṃ nirvartyetaḥ paraṃ nandanavandanārthaṃ kṛtakamālatīveṣaṃ makarandaṃ madayantikālābhalobhādadabhyupagatastrībhāvaṃ sajjīkurvantyāha - vatseti/ ita ārabhya makarandavṛttāntaḥ patāketi pūrvamevoktam/ mālatyarthaṃ rājñā preṣitena nepathyena maṅgalālaṃkāreṇa/ pariṇayāya nandanapariṇayāya madayantikāpariṇayāya cetyarthaḥ/ citrayavanikāṃ citrapaṭanirmitāṃ tiraskariṇīm/ antardhānaṃ strīsaṃnidhau vastrādiparidhāne strījanocite'pāṭavātparidhānacalanaśaṅkayā// mādhavaḥ/ sulabhamapi bahvanarthakamanekapratyavāyamatisaṃkaṭaṃ suduṣkaraṃ karma nandanavañcanātmakaṃ vayasyasya/ ``śubhamapi'' ityapi pāṭhaḥ// kāmandakī kastvamiti/ asyāṃ cintāyām/ prabhūtānarthakaṃ suduṣkaraṃ cedaṃ karma, kathamasya rakṣisahasrākule nandanabhavane praveśanirgamau, yadi rājā daivādvijānīyāttadānarthaḥ, kiṃ vā tava bhavedityevaṃrūpāyām/ kastvaṃ ko vā tava bhāro mayi vartamānāyāmiti bhāvaḥ/ gāḍhotkaṇṭheti/ gāḍhayā pravṛddhayotkaṇṭhayā priyānavāptijanitena smaradaśānimittenautsukyena vyabhicāriṇā kaṭhoraḥ prauḍho yaḥ keralavadhūnāṃ kapolastadvadacchāḥ pāṇḍuvarṇāśchadāḥ parṇāni yeṣāṃ taistāmbūlīnāṃ nāgavallīnāṃ paṭalaiḥ samūhaiḥ pinaddhāḥ saṃbaddhāḥ phalitā ata eva vyānamrāḥ phalabhārāvanatāḥ pūgadrumāḥ kramukavṛkṣā yeṣu tathā/ kakkolīphalānāṃ jagdhena bhakṣaṇena/ bhāve ktaḥ/ mugdhā manoharā ye vikirāṇāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ vyāhārā rutāni tadvantastathā/ preṅkhitāścalitāḥ phalāpādanārthaṃ mātuluṅgavṛtayo bījapūrasamūhā yeṣu tādṛśāstadbhuvo'smadvihārikāyā bhuvo bhāgā avakāśā vāṃ yuvayoḥ preyaḥ prītiṃ vidhāsyantīti/ atra nāgavallīlatāpūgadrumasaṃśleṣaṇaṃ mithunāntaradarśanamuddīpanaṃ cāyatnasiddhasaṃbhogasādhanatāmbūlalābhaśca pakṣiṇāṃ kolāhalena nānyairbhavadvacanākarṇanaṃ bījapurakuñjasaṃkaṭatvādanyeṣāmapraveśayogyatvamadṛśyatvena tenāvasthānaṃ ca bhavatītyādi vyajyate//19//

 madayantikāgamanaṃ yāvaditi/ tatra yuvayormadayantikāmakarandayośca yugapadeva vivāho nirvartayiṣyata ityāśayaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ kalyāṇāntarāvataṃsā kalyāṇāntarālaṃkārā kalyāṇasaṃpanmālatīprāptilakṣaṇamaṅgalasamṛddhiḥ// kalahaṃsaḥ/ diṣṭyā idamapi priyaṃ no bhaviṣyati/ yannistīrṇavipanmadayantikāsahito makarando'smābhirdraṣṭavyaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ śra[śru]taṃ priyasakhyā/ bhagavatyā vihārikāgamanāya makarandāgamanapratīkṣaṇāya ca yaduktaṃ tacchrūtaṃ kim/ tadetatkuruṣvetyarthaḥ// mālatī/ sakhi, tvayāpi gantavyam/ māṃ visṛjyeti śeṣaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ sāṃprataṃ khalu vayameva śiṣṭā bāhyāḥ/ tvaṃ bhartrā saha sukhamāssveti bhāvaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ āmūleti/ anaṅga eva nidāgho gharmasamayaḥ saṃtāpakāritvāt/ tena tapto'yamahamidānīṃ nidāghatapto dvirada ivāmūlaṃ kaṇṭakito romāñcakañcukitaḥ saṃjātakaṇṭakaśca komalo bāhunālo nālasadṛśo bāhuryasya tam/ tathārdrāḥ svedākhyasāttvikabhāvavaśātsalilasaṃbandhavaśāccāṅgulyo dalānīva dalasadṛśyo'ṅgulyo yasya tam/ asyā mālatyāḥ sārasyā iva kāntaṃ karamāraktapaṅkajaṃ kokanadamiva kareṇa hastena śuṇḍādaṇḍena cākalayāmi gṛhṇāgīti/ upamālaṃkāraḥ//20//


bhavabhūtikavervācaḥ svadante rasarañjitāḥ/

sumanobhyaḥ sudhāsyandabandhamādhuryabandhurāḥ//

iti śrīmadbhāradvājakulatilakaparvatanāthayāyajūkanandanatripurārisūriviracitāyāṃ mālatīmādhavabhāvapradīpikāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhāṅkavivaraṇam//

 saptamo'ṅkaḥṣṭapārvatīcumbitaṃ śaṃbhorjayatyalikalocanam/

jiṣṇormanasijādyena lajjayeva nimīlitam//

kaviridānīmaṅkāntaramārabhate - tata ityādinā/ kāmandakyā hi nisṛṣṭārthadūtīvyāpāraṃ mālatīmādhavakṛtakapariṇayalakṣaṇaphalaparyavasitaṃ saṃpādya kāmandakīniyuktāyā buddharakṣitāyā api tadvyāpāre ``niyuktaiva hi mayā tatra priyasakhī buddharakṣitā'' iti prāksūcitaṃ madayantikāmakarandasaṃghaṭanena saphalayituṃ kaveraṅkāvatāra iti draṣṭavyam// buddharakṣitā/ suśliṣṭākṛtimattvena nāśaṅkanīyā mālatīnepathyalakṣmīstayā vipralabdhena nandanena kṛtaḥ karagraho yasya saḥ/ amātyabhūrivasumandire bhagavatīvacanasaṃvidhānaiḥ ``samāgateyaṃ mālatī samabhyarcya nagaradevatām'' ityādivacanairmālatīsamucitatattadupacārasaṃpādanādivyāpāraiśca kṣemeṇa kenacidapyalakṣitatvādanupadraveṇa gopāyito rakṣito rājñastadanucarebhyaḥ paritrāto makarandaḥ/ yathā vijñāya tairna prahriyate tathā kṛta ityarthaḥ/ anena ca kṛtakamālatīnandanapariṇayaniṣpattiḥ sūcitā/ adya vayaṃ nandanāvāsamupagatāḥ/ madayantikāsakhītvena svasya varasaṃbandhādvidvadvaravargāṇāṃ ca bahutvādvayamiti bahuvacanam/ vivāhārthaṃ varamanusṛtya vayaṃ janyāśca vivāhaṃ nirvartya varagṛhameva pratyāgatā ityarthaḥ/ ato bhagavatī nandanamāpṛcchyāmantrya gatāvasathaṃ svāvāsam/ ayaṃ ca navavadhūgṛhapraveśārthaṃ viracito yo'kāla āśvayujapaurṇamāsīvyatiriktakāle kaumudyā abhāvātpaurṇamāsyāṃ kartavyo yo mahotsavastena pramatto'navahitastathā paryākulaḥ kāryāntaravyāsaṅgādvikṣiptacetasko'śeṣaparijano yasminsa tādṛśaḥ pradoṣo rajanīmukhamanukūlayiṣyatyabhimatasiddhisāhāyyakānukūlyena siddhyunmukhaṃ kariṣyati/ adya no'smākaṃ vyavasitaṃ madayantikāmakarandasaṃghaṭanaviṣayadūtyavyāpāraṃ mālatīveṣadhāriṇo makarandasya kṛtrimanirvartitamadayantikāpariṇayasya madayantikāṃ gṛhītvā nirgantuṃ kālo'pyeṣa evānukūlo vartate/ yato madīyāḥ sarve'pyapramattā rakṣiṇaḥ sarve pramattāḥ paryākulāśca/ tannirgamaprajñānatannivāraṇādāvasamarthā vartanta ityarthaḥ/ anena ca vartamāno'rthaḥ sūcitaḥ/ sāṃprataṃ ca tvaramāṇakāmo navavadhūsaṃbhogotkaṇṭhāsamunmīlitamanmathāvegaḥ kāmayituṃ māṃ kāmayasvetyevameva bahuśaḥ sapādapatanamabhyarthya punarbalātkāreṇābhidravanprasahya svayaṃgrāhasāhasapravṛttaḥ sanniṣṭhuraṃ paruṣaṃ yathā syānmakarandena kapaṭamālatyā pratihato nirākṛto jāmātā varo nandanaḥ/ sa ca vailakṣyaroṣāveśaskhaladakṣaro mantharavacanaḥ/ tathāvaruditanayanaṃ prasphuracca vadanaṃ yasya saḥ/ na me sāṃpratamanayā kaumāravardhakyā kaumāre kumārībhāve kanyātva eva vardhakīcalitacāritratayā/ mādhavaikānurakteyaṃ mālatīti pūrvameva nandano'pi śrutavānityevamupālambhaḥ/ prayojanamiti saśapathaṃ pratijñāṃ kṛtvā vāsabhavanānnirgataḥ/ tasmādanena prasaṅgena bhrātṛkāryānukūlyārthamanunayavyājaprastāvena madayantikāmānīya makarandena saha saṃyojayiṣyāmi/ iti vṛttaṃ vartiṣyamāṇaṃ ca saṃsūcya niṣkrāntā buddharakṣitā/ ayaṃ ca granthakāṇḍaḥ praveśako vṛttavartiṣyamāṇoditalakṣaṇayogāt/ na caikapātraprayogānupapattiḥ tallakṣaṇe'nekapātratvaniyamāpratīteḥ/ asya viṣkambhavadekenāpyupapatteśceti// tata ityādi/ śayyāgata iti vāsabhavane navavadhūvarasaṃbhogārthaṃ racite talpe śayitaḥ prārthanābhaṅgatiraskāraniṣkāsinandanaḥ/ mālatīrūpadhārītyarthaḥ// makarandaḥ/ api nāmeti saṃbhāvanāyām/ buddharakṣitāsaṃkrāntā bhagavatyaiva niveśitā bhagavatīnītiḥ kṛtyaṃ madayantikāṃ prati na buddharakṣitābuddhidoṣeṇa pratihatā phalatīti saṃbhāvyate/ ``na viparyeṣyati'' iti pāṭhe buddharakṣitāyā svaprajñābalenāpramattena doṣeṇa vaiparītyaṃ na saṃbhāvyate/ api tu bhagavatyaiva pravartitatvātphalaparyavasāyinī nītirbhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ kaḥ saṃdeho mahābhāgasya/ saṃbhāvanāyā ulbaṇaikakoṭisaṃdeharūpatvādapināmaśabdasūcitā saṃbhāvanaiva saṃdehaśabdenoktā/ kathaṃ tarhi saṃdehaleśasyāpyavakāśa ityarthaḥ/ kiṃ bahunā/ yathaiṣa mañjīraśabdaḥ śrūyate, tathā tenaiva nūpuradhvaniśravaṇaprakāreṇa hetunā tarkayāmi tena vyapadeśenālīkabhrātṛbhāryāyāstavānunayavyājenānītā buddharakṣitayā madayantiketi/ taduttarīyāpavāritaḥ suptalakṣaṇaḥ supta iva lakṣyamāṇastiṣṭha// madayantikā/ sakhi, satyameva parikopitaḥ parikupitaḥ kṛto me bhrātā mālatyā// buddharakṣitā/ atha kim/ satyamevetyarthaḥ// madayantikā/ aho, atyāhitaṃ mahābhītiḥ/ kupitā bhrāturmālatī samīpamupanītaivetyarthaḥ/ tadehi/ vāmaśīlāṃ vakrasvabhāvāṃ mālatīṃ nirbhartsayāvaḥ// buddharakṣitā/ idaṃ vāsabhavanam// madayantikā/ sakhi lavaṅgike jñāyate prasuptā te priyasakhīti// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, maināṃ pratibodhaya/ eṣā ciraṃ durmanāyamānā duḥkhitamanaskā bhavantī/ ``bhṛśādibhyo bhuvi-'' ityādinā kyaṅ/ idānīmeveṣanmanye prasupteti/ ataḥ śanairihaiva śayanārdha upaviśa// madayantikā/ durmanāyate kathamiyaṃ vāmaśīlā/ svayameva bhartuḥ pratikūlakāritvenāparādhaṃ kṛtvā kathamātmanaiva durmanaskā bhavatītyarthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ kathaṃ nāma navavadhvā visṛmbhaṇe visrambhajanane ya upāyā kāmatantroktāstaccittavṛttyanurodhastanmanorañjanaṃ tadanivāritārdhāṅgīkṛtānāṃ lajjayā kevalaṃ bahiralīkameva kiṃcinniṣiddhānāmapyantaranumoditānāmavayavasparśādisaṃbhogavyāpārāṇāṃ mandamandamupakramānnātiprasahya kiṃcidācaraṇamityādayasteṣāmabhijñamiti viparītalakṣaṇayoktiḥ/ yato'sau brahmacaryārthaṃ śayanādiniyamabāhye vaivāhika eva trirātre svacittavṛttyanabhijñāṃ kiṃcidapyaśithilīkṛtabāhubharāmatyantāparicayādaherivodvijamānāṃ balādenāṃ bālāṃ mukhata eva pāmarabāliśajanarītyā nirdayaṃ niṣpīḍayitumārabdhavān/ ato na kāmatantravārtāmapyasau jānātītyarthaḥ/ laḍaho vidagdho madhurabhāṣī/ laḍahaḥ sundaraḥ vidagdhaḥ/ suratakalānipuṇaḥ/ atrāpi saundaryagandhavihīnaṃ suratakalāvārtānabhijñamatiparuṣabhāṣiṇamiti vaiparītyenoktiḥ/ aroṣaṇamakopanamatimūrkhamityanyathoktiḥ/ bhrātaraṃ te bhartāramāsādya priyasakhī na durmanāyate/ yuktamevaivaṃvidhabhartṛsamāgame kleśenāsyā durmanaskatvamityarthaḥ// madayantikā/ paśya buddharakṣite, vipratīpamupālabdhāḥ smaḥ/ asmābhirupālabdhavye pratyutāsmānevopālabhata ityarthaḥ// buddharakṣitā/ vipratīpaṃ na vā vipratīpam/ āpātato vipratīpamiva pratibhāsamānamapi tattvanirūpaṇenaiva vipratīpaṃ vayamevopālabdhā ityarthaḥ// madayantikā/ kathamiva/ tatkathamiti praśnaḥ// buddharakṣitā/ yattāvaccaraṇapatito bhartā na bahumānito'nullaṅghitapraṇāmo'yamati gauravaviṣayo bharteti na vijñātaḥ/ atra na kiṃcidasyā dūṣaṇaṃ pratibhāti/ kiṃtu kulakanyakājanocitā navapariṇayasamayasulabhā lajjaiva paramārthataḥ ślāghanīyaguṇabhūtāpi kāmāndhasyāvivekino'smadbhrāturdoṣatvena sphuritā/ tena lajjādoṣeṇaiva janopālambhanīyaḥ/ yadyapyabhinavavadhūviruddhaṃ lokasiddhanavoḍhāsaṃbhogakramaviparītaṃ rabhasopakramaskhalanaṃ prasahyānucitaprakramarūpaṃ skhalanaṃ tena yadvailakṣyaṃ tena viccharditaṃ mahānubhāvatvaṃ yasyeti/ viccharditaṃ vivarjitam/ prakaṭitamaurkhyasyeti yāvat/ bhrātṛste kimapi śrotumapyanarhaṃ vācāgatamapratiṣṭhānaṃ na me tvayā kaumāravardhakyā prayojanamiti garhitavacanacāpalam/ tena vayamevopālambhanīyā iti pratibhāti/ śāstraviruddhaṃ caitadityāha - kiṃceti/ yoṣicchabdo'tra navavadhūparaḥ/ yathā kusumaṃ mlāyate tathā mṛdurītyā sukumārakrameṇopakramyamāṇaṃ saurabhamaśeṣamudgirati, na tu prasabhameva niṣpīḍyamānam/ evaṃ tatsamānadharmāṇo navoḍhā api sukumāreṇaiva saṃbhogakrameṇopakramyamāṇā anuraktāḥ sukhahetavo bhavanti/ tāstvanadhigato viśvāso navoḍhābhiprāyo yaiste tathā/ yadvānadhigato navoḍhayā paricayābhāvādaprāpto viśvāso visrambho yeṣu te tathoktāḥ/ tairapyetanmanovedibhiraparicitaiśca prasabhaṃ balādevātyudvegakāribhirvyāpārairupakramyamāṇāḥ prayogasya saṃprayogasyaiva vidveṣiṇyo bhavanti/ na kevalaṃ tādṛśapuruṣeṇānurajyante/ api tu ripusamāgamavadviṣīdantītyarthaḥ/ evaṃ kila kāmasūtrakārā mantrayante/ yadāhuḥ- ``rabhasena hyupakrāntā kanyābhāvamavindatā/ bhayaṃ cintāsamudvegaṃ sadyo dveṣaṃ ca gacchati//'' iti// lavaṅgikā/ gṛhe gṛhe puruṣāḥ kulakanyakā udvahanti/ na ca ko'pi lajjāprasādhanamanaparādhamugdhasvabhāvaṃ kulakumārījanaṃ prabhavāmīti samartho'hamityetāvatā vāganalenāvācyavacanarūpeṇānalena prajvalayati/ ete khalu te hṛdayaśalya nikṣepā āmaraṇaṃ saṃbhriyamāṇā vismartumaśakyatvena manasi saṃtataṃ dhāryamāṇā ata eva duḥsahāḥ paragṛhanivāsavairāgyakāriṇo mahāparibhavāḥ/ yeṣāṃ kṛte strījanmalābhaṃ jugupsante bāndhavāḥ// madayantikā/ buddharakṣite, atimlānā priyasakhī lavaṅgikā/ atimahānko'pi me bhrātrā vāgaparādhaḥ kṛtaḥ// buddharakṣitā/ atha kim/ kṛta evetyarthaḥ/ tadevāha - śrutamevāsmābhiryathā na me sāṃpratamanayā kaumāravardhakyā prayojanamiti saśapathaṃ pratijñāṃ kṛtvā vāsabhavanānnirgataḥ// madayantikā/ aho atikramo maryādollaṅghanam/ aho pramādo'navadhānatā/ sakhi lavaṅgike, asamarthāsmi te mukhaṃ sāṃprataṃ draṣṭum/ tathāpi prabhavāmīti mayyatisnigdhāyāṃ tvayi prabhutvamastīti kimapi sakhitvamevāvalambya tadanurūpaṃ kiṃcinmantrayiṣye// lavaṅgikā/ svādhīnaste'yaṃ janaḥ/ tadvisrabdhaṃ mantrayasveti bhāvaḥ// madayantikā/ tiṣṭhatu tāvanmama bhrāturduḥśīlatāpratiṣṭhānaṃ ca/ yuṣmābhirmālatyāptakoṭibhirapīdṛśo'pyeṣa sāṃprataṃ yathācittamanuvartanīyaḥ/ cittaśabdenātra tatsādhāraṇo vyāpāraḥ saṃkalpākhyo lakṣyate/ tena tadicchāpratikūlaṃ nācaraṇīyamityāśayaḥ/ yena bhartaiṣa iti/ nirguṇo doṣavānapi bhartā bhartṛtvena strībhiranuvartanīyaḥ/ na tu tasya prātikūlyaṃ kulavadhūnāmucitamiti bhāvaḥ/ na ca yūyamasyānabhijātākṣarādhikṣepopālambhasyānabhijātāni kitavajanayogyānyakṣarāṇi yatra sa tādṛśo'dhikṣepo dharṣaṇaṃ chidropālambhasya yanmūlaṃ tanna jānītha// lavaṅgikā/ kathaṃ vayamasantaṃ jānīmaḥ/ etadupālambhasya bījaṃ mālatīgataṃ kiṃcidanucitamasti cetkathaṃ vayaṃ na jānīmaḥ/ nāstyevaitat/ anyattu kāraṇamasaṃbhāvitamevetyāśayaḥ/ anyadeva tanmūlamāha - madayantikā/ yadidānīṃ tasminmahānubhāve mādhave kimapi kila mālatyā vāṅmātraṃ mādhavānurakteyaṃ mālatīti pravādamātramāsītsa eva sarvalokasyātibhūmiṃ gataḥ parāṃ koṭimārūḍhaḥ pravādaḥ/ ``tārāmaitramāsīt'' iti pāṭhe tārāmaitraṃ darśanamātrasnehaḥ/ tasminsarvalokasyeti pāṭhaḥ/ tatkhalvetadvijṛmbhate/ upālambhamūlatvenetiśeṣaḥ/ tatpriyasakhi, yathaiṣa bharturupekṣābhiniveśo mālatyā bhartṛviṣayaudāsīnyāgraho niravaśeṣo hṛdayādudbhriyate tathā kuru/ anyathā mahānpramāda iti jñātaṃ bhavatu/ niṣkampadāruṇāsvānukūlyaṃ[niṣkampadāruṇā svānukūlyaṃ] pratipadya kevalaṃ niśceṣṭībhūtāsvata[niśceṣṭībhūtā svata] eva dāruṇāsu kulakanyakāsu viṣayabhūtāsu dūnayati hṛdayaṃ manuṣyāṇāmīdṛśāddurabhiṣaṅgāditi/ durabhiṣaṅgo duṣṭānurāgabandhaḥ/ tanmā bhaṇa madayantikayā kathitamiti mālatyupālambhabījametadasmadbhrāturmanogataṃ bhavatīṣu snehānmayā gūḍhamapi kathitam, tadetanmayoktamiti na kvacidapi prakaṭanīyamityabhiprāyaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ ayi asaṃbaddhalokapravādamohite, apehi/ na tvayā saha mantrayiṣye// madayantikā/ sakhi, prasīda/ athavā na yūyaṃ sphuṭaṃ bhaṇitāstiṣṭhatha/ kiṃca vayaṃ satyameva mādhavaikamayajīvitāṃ mālatīṃ jānīmaḥ/ kena vā kaṭhoraketakīgarbhavibhramāvayavadaurbalyānnirvartitasundaratvaviśeṣaṃ mādhavasvahastanirmitabakulāvalīviracitakaṇṭhāvalambanamātrasaṃjīvanaṃ mālatyā mādhavasya ca prabhātacandramaṇḍalapāṇḍuraparikṣāmaramaṇīyadarśanaṃ na vibhāvitaṃ śarīram/ kiṃ ca tasmindivase kusumākarodyānaparyantarathyāmukhasamāgame savibhramollasitakautūhalotphullaparisarodvellamānasavilāsamasṛṇasnigdhasaṃcaraṇacārutārakāvijṛmbhamāṇānaṅgaśṛṅgārācāryasarvāgamopadeśanirmitavaidagdhyamugdhamanoharā mayā na nirūpitā etayordṛṣṭisaṃbhedāḥ/ kiṃ ca mama bhrāturdānavṛttāntaṃ śrutvā tatkṣaṇodvṛttagambhīrodvegavyatikarāndhakāritamlāyamānadehaśobhayorudvartamānamūlabandhanamiva na lakṣitaṃ hṛdayam/ kiṃ ca mayaitadaparaṃ vismṛtam// lavaṅgikā/ kimidānīmaparam// madayantikā/ yatkhalu mama jīvitapradāyino mahānubhāvasya cetanāpratilambhapriyanivedikāyā mālatyā bhagavatīvidagdhavacanopanyāsacoditena hṛdayaṃ jīvitaṃ ca mādhavena pāritoṣikatvena svayaṃgrāhe niyuktam/ atha lavaṅgike, tvayā khalvevaṃ bhaṇitaṃ pratīṣṭaḥ khalu naḥ/ priyasakhyā ayaṃ prasāda iti/ atra mahānubhāvasyeti makarandasyābhidhānaṃ tasya bhartṛtvena madayantikayā svamanasaiva niścitatvādbharturnāmagrahaṇasya kulastrīṇāmanucitatvāditi grāhyam/ atha lavaṅgikāpracchanno makarandaḥ svasyānurāgasya vyañjakāni svapriyatamāvacanāni svayameva śṛṇotviti jānānāpyajānānaiva pṛcchati// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, katamaḥ punaḥ sa mahānubhāva iti vismṛtamiva mayā// madayantikā/ sakhi, smara/ yena tasmindivase vikaṭaduṣṭaśvāpadavinipātagocaraṃ gatāśaraṇā sulagnasaṃnihitena pīvarabhujastambhena saṃbhāvitā niṣkāraṇabāndhavena sakalabhuvanaikasāranijadehopahārasāhasaṃ kṛtvā parirakṣitāsmi/ yena ca dṛḍhavikaṭamāṃsalottānapariṇāhivakṣaḥsthalalāñchanajarjaritajapāpīḍadhāriṇā/ rudhirārdratayā vakṣaḥsthalanihitahāralataiva japākusumavarṇatvājjapāpīḍatvenoktā/ taddhāriṇā karuṇādhanena matkṛte'pi nimajjatsakalanakhanikāyavajrapañjaraprahāro māritaśca sa duṣṭaśvāpadamahārākṣasa iti// lavaṅgikā/ huṃ, makarandaḥ/ humityaṅgīkāradyotakamavyayaṃ mālatīmadayantikayoḥ samānādhikāraviṣayānurāgaṃ vyanakti/ yathā kila tvaṃ śvāpadāpaskanditā vinihataśārdūle prāṇadāyini tasmingāḍhamanuraktā, evaṃ mālatyapi svalābhanimittameva jīvitaspṛhānirapekṣaṃ nirvyūḍhamahāmāṃsavikrayasāhase kāpālikamāritakalpātmaprāṇarakṣake mādhave nirupādhikaśabdagāḍhānurāgeti kathaṃ tāṃ tvamupālabhasa iti bhāvaḥ// madayantikā/ sānandam sakhi, kiṃ bhaṇasi/ priyatamanāmadheyaśuśrūṣātiśayādatyādareṇa praśnaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ nanu bhaṇāmi makaranda iti/ evaṃ makarandanāmagrahaṇādevāvirbhūtaromāñcasvedādisāttvikabhāvāṃ madayantikāṃ dṛṣṭvā sasmitamasyāḥ śarīraṃ spṛśantī saṃskṛtamāśritya/ vadatīti śeṣaḥ/ vayamiti/ kumāryeva satī mālatī mādhave'nurakteti yattvamāttha tattathā nāma tādṛśya eva vayaṃ bhavāmaḥ/ vayamiti kulaśīlādisūcanayā mālatyā samamātmano'bhinnāyā bahuvacananirdeśaḥ/ atra kiṃ bhaṇāmi tvāmātmaparadoṣānabhijñāṃ tvām/ ekākinīmiti śeṣaḥ/ ayaṃ tviti madayantikāṃ spṛśantī tāmeva nirdiśati/ viśuddhaḥ parapuruṣānurāgakalaṅkarahito mugdho madanavyāpāravārtānabhijñaḥ kulakanyakājanaḥ/ sābhiprāyairvacainarvaiparītyaṃ[sābhiprāyairvacanavaiparītyaṃ] dyotyate/ kathāntare goṣṭīmadhya eva, na tu taddarśanādau/ vikalaḥ svavyāpārākṣamaḥ sankadambagolakasya nīpatarusaṃsthānasyākṛtimudyanmukulālaṃkṛtamākaraṃ kathamāśritaḥ/ romāñcakañcukaṃ kathaṃ jātamityarthaḥ/ tvameva tannāmagrahaṇamātrāvirbhūtamanmathavikārā prasiddhakaumāravārdhakībhūtvā kathamitaropālambhe pravartasa iti bhāvaḥ//1//

 madayantikā/ salajjam/ sakhi, kiṃ māmupahasasi/ nanu bhaṇāmi/ nirvāpayati/ ``sukhayati'' iti pāṭhaḥ/ tādṛśasyātmanirapekṣavyavasāyinaḥ kṛtāntakavalīkriyamāṇajīvitabalātkārapratyānayanagurūpakāriṇo janasya saṃkathāmātrasya nāmagrahaṇaṃ smaraṇaṃ ca/ tathā ca tvayāpi gāḍhagurunakhaprahāravedanārambhavihvalitaśarīrasaṃgalitasvedasalilodgamo mohamukulīkriyamāṇanetranīlotpalayugalo bhūmivigalitāsiyaṣṭiviṣṭambhadhairyapratidhāritaśarīrabhāraḥ pratyakṣīkṛta eva madayantikāmātraviccharditamahārghajīvito mahānubhāva iti// buddharakṣitā/ asvasthaśarīre, kiṃ vācā/ darśitaṃ śarīreṇa makarandasamāgamautsukyam/ svedādibhirvyabhicāribhiriti śeṣaḥ// madayantikā/ salajjam/ kanyakājanānucitasvamanovṛttivyaktībhāvo'tra lajjāhetuḥ/ sakhi, apehyapehīti cāvahitthākhyavyabhicārivyañjakaṃ vacanam/ yathāha - ``avahitthā tu lajjāderharṣādyākāragopanam'' iti/ udbhinnāsmi sahavāsinyā mālatyā/ bālyātprabhṛti sahapāṃsukrīḍādibhirviśvāsasthānabhūtayā mālatyaiva svayaṃ nirupādhikajīvitadāyini mahānubhāve kumārītve'pi pravṛttaprakṛṣṭānurāgayāhamapyudbhinnāsmi tādṛśopakārināyakasamāgamaṃ prati darśitasvaprasarāsmi/ tāṃ dṛṣṭvāhamapi tathaiva pravṛttā, na tu svātantryeṇeti bhāvaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi madayantike, vayamapi jñātavyaṃ jānīmaḥ/ kimataḥ paraṃ tadācchādaneneti bhāvaḥ/ tatprasīda prasannāntaḥkaraṇā bhava/ na punarasmattaḥ śaṅkitavyamiti bhāvaḥ/ virama vyapadeśāt/ kapaṭavacanairākṛtigopanaṃ na śakyamiti bhāvaḥ/ ehi/ visrambhagarbhakathāprabandhasarasaṃ sukhaṃ tiṣṭhāmaḥ/ asmāsu visrabdhā bhūtvā niḥśaṅkaṃ svahṛdayaṃ nivedaya/ tatsiddhyupāyamupadekṣyāma iti bhāvaḥ// buddharakṣitā/ sakhi, śobhanaṃ lavaṅgikayā bhaṇitam/ viditasvābhiprāye jane na vañcanaṃ yuktam/ kiṃtu tannivedanena tadupāyo'nveṣṭavya iti bhāvaḥ// madayantikā/ vidheyāsmi sāṃprataṃ sakhīnām// lavaṅgikā/ yadyevamasmadvidheyāsi tatkathaya kathaṃ nu te kālo gacchatīti/ tādṛśakāṣṭhāprāptānurāgaviṣayaṃ dayitamalabhamānā kathaṃ divasānativāhayasīti bhāvaḥ// madayantikā/ niśāmaya śṛṇu priyasakhi, mama buddharakṣitāpakṣapātapratyayena prathamameva tasmiñjane'viralakautūhalotkaṇṭhāmanoharaṃ hṛdayamāsīt/ nāsthāne buddharakṣitāpakṣapātaṃ karotīti tatsnehādeva saṃbhāvitasakalanāyakagaṇagarīyāniti pratyayo viśvāso'styeveti bhāvaḥ/ tato vidhiniyojitaciranirvṛttadarśanā bhūtvā durvāradāruṇāyāsaduḥkhasantāpadahyamānacittavighaṭamānajīvitāśā dūravijṛmbhitāpūrvasarvāṅgaprajvalanamadanahutavahoddāmadāhaduḥsahāyāsadurmanāyamānaparijanā pratyāśāvimokṣamātrasulabhamṛtyunirvāṇapratikūlabuddharakṣitāvacanavivardhitāvegavyatikaravisaṃsthulemaṃ jīvalokaparivartamanubhavāmi/ makarandapratyāśānivṛttimātreṇa sulabho mṛtyureva nirvāṇaṃ nirvṛtistatpratikūlaṃ buddharakṣitāvacanaṃ saṃnihitamakarandaprāptipratipādakaṃ tena saṃvardhito ya āvega udvegastena vihvaletyarthaḥ/ saṃkalpacintāyāṃ svapnāntareṣu ca manorathonmādamohitā paśyāmi taṃ janam/ tathā ca priyasakhi, muhūrtamudūḍhavismayavisaṃsthulodvellavistāriprāntanālaraktanetrapuṇḍarīkatāṇḍavodbhaṭaprarūḍhamaireyamadaghūrṇanaśīlaṃ yathā syāttathā nirvarṇayati sābhilāṣaṃ māmeva paśyati/ saṃkalpādyupanato'sāviti śeṣaḥ/ nirvāpayati māṃ sukhayatīti vā/ maireyo madyaviśeṣaḥ/ vistāriṇaḥ prāntasya netra vibhāgasya dīrghībhūyamānā mālākāreṇa prasaraṇānnālatvanirūpaṇaṃ svabhāvata eva netrapuṇḍarīkasya madhumadaghūrṇamānatvamiva yathā syādityuktam/ kiṃ ca kavalitāravindakesarakaṣāyakaṇṭhakalahaṃsaghoṣaghargharaskhalitagambhīrabhāratībharitakarṇavivaraṃ priye madayantike iti māṃ vyāharati/ svapnādāviti yāvat/ atha prabhavanniva svāmīvottarīyāñcalāvalambanaparābhavena sasaṃbhramottaraṅgadhamadhamāyamānahṛdayāṃ samuttrāsayati/ māmiti śeṣaḥ/ saṃbhrama āvegaḥ/ ``ādarātiśayāccetasyāvegaḥ saṃbhramo mataḥ'' iti lakṣaṇāt/ dhamadhamāyamānamutkampamānaṃ kumārībhāvasulabhaśālīnatvena svavacanamanaṅgīkṛtyānyato vrajantīṃ māmuttarīyāñcale gṛhītvā kiṃ vānyatra vrajasīti śikṣaka iva trāsayatītyarthaḥ/ sahasā visarjitāpasṛtatatkṣaṇakaṭhorakamaladaṇḍāyamānabāhubandhanāpavāritapayodharodgamam/ āliṅganānantaraṃ tatprathamāliṅganajanitatrāsavailakṣyaśaṅkāvibhramābhirāmanāyikāṅgadarśanakutūhalena sahasā visarjitamanantaraṃ cāpasṛtaṃ yadudbhinnaromāñcatayā komalakamalanālasadṛśābhyāṃ bāhubhyāṃ bandhanamāliṅganaṃ tenāpavāritaḥ payodharodgamo yathā syādevamityarthaḥ/ vighaṭamānavihvalamekhalāvalayasaṃdhāryamāṇapīvarorupratiṣidhyamānavipratīpagamanām/ gāḍhāliṅganasaukhyātiśayatatkṣaṇapravṛddhānnitambādvighaṭamānaṃ viśliṣyadvihvalaṃ visrastaṃ ca yanmekhalāvalayaṃ tena saṃdhāryamāṇābhyāṃ pīvarorubhyāmeva pratiṣidhyamānapratikūlagamanāmityarthaḥ/ tathā pratikūlavādinīmapi sarvādaraprayatnanirvartitamuhūrtakopoparāgaduḥkhaparuṣīkṛtahṛdayām/ māmityarthaḥ/ tathā snigdhapunaruktaparyastalocanavibhāvitāśeṣacittasārāṃ premārdravilocanenaiva mithyaiva kupiteyaṃ na tu vastuto'syāḥ kopaḥ/ pratyuta sarvametanmacceṣṭitamanumatameveti nirṇītākūtasarvasvārthāmupahasya/ tvadīyaṃ kopamahaṃ jānāmyeva, tvayi pratikūlagamanāyāmapi mekhalaiva matsakhī tvāmanyatra gantuṃ na prayacchatītyādinarmavacanaiḥ parihāsyetyarthaḥ/ dviguṇabāhudaṇḍāveṣṭananiśceṣṭaniyamitāṃ priyasakhi, prarūḍhaśārdūlakaṭhorakararuhaprahāravikaṭapattrāvalīprasādhanamuttānaṃ ca yadvakṣaḥsthalaṃ tatra yanniṣṭoraniveśanaṃ tena niḥsahāṃ kṛtvā sāvegavidhūtamastakāpaviddhakabarīnihitakaraparigrahapuñjīkṛtonnamitaniścalamukhāvayavasvacchandavilasitavidagdhavadanakamalaḥ/ śārdūlanakhaprahārā eva vikaṭapatrāvalī saiva prasādhanaṃ yasyeti pūrvopakārasmaraṇam/ sāvegaṃ sāṭopaṃ yadvidhūnanaṃ tenāpaviddhabandhā visrastabandhanā yā kabarī tasyāṃ nihitena kareṇa yaḥ parigrahastena puñjīkṛtaiḥ/ kacairiti yāvat/ unnamitā niścalāḥ spaṣṭaṃ vibhāvyamānā ye manmukhādyavayavāsteṣu svacchandavilasitaṃ svecchayā vilasanaṃ kurvadvidagdhaṃ vadanakamalaṃ yasya sa ityarthaḥ/ vāmagaṇḍamūlaciravinihitaprasphuratpuñjitādharasamudgamamanoharasahajasārasvatamanoharotkarṣitaśarīraśobhāmullasitasādhvasānandaviṣamasaṃbhramamanoharasaṃvalanamantharabhramaccetanāṃ kimapi kimapi durvinayasāhasānurūpavyavasāyo māmabhyarthayate/ evaṃ nāma priyasakhi, samakṣaṃ pratyakṣaṃ sarvamanubhūya tato jhaṭiti pratibuddhā śūnyāraṇyasaṃnibhaṃ punarapi mandabhāgyā vibhāvayāmi jīvalokamiti// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi madayantike, sphuṭamākhyāhi/ api tasminnavasare snehavibhramorjitahāsavikasadbuddharakṣitālocananirūpitamāsanamayūrakaṃ parijanādgopanīyaṃ bhavati vā kiṃ na veti/ āsanārthaṃ nirmitaṃ vastu tūlapūritamiti tanmayūrākāramāsanaṃ mayūrakam/ tvadīyamāsanaṃ mayūrakaṃ svapnasamāgamanimittacaramadhātukṣaraṇārdrīkṛtaṃ sadvidagdhabuddharakṣitayā sasnehaṃ saparihāsaṃ ca svāpniko'pi te priyasaṃbhogaḥ pāramārthika eva te jāta iti dṛṣṭyaiva sūcyamānaṃ parijanātparihāsaśaṅkayā gopanīyaṃ ca bhavati vā na vetyarthaḥ/ mālatyapekṣayā madayantikāyā nikṛṣṭatvadyotanāya ceṭījanocitajugupsitanarmavacanapravṛttiriti grāhyam/ madayantikā/ ayi asaṃbaddhaparihāsaśīle, apehi// buddharakṣitā/ sakhi madayantike, mālatyāḥ priyasakhyeveyamīdṛśāni jānāti/ īdṛśāni mantrayituṃ na khalu mādṛśī tvatsakhīti bhāvaḥ/ mālatyāmevaṃ dṛṣṭavatīti kṛtvā tatsakhītvenaivameṣā vadatīti madayantikāyāṃ snehātiśayo buddharakṣitāyā darśitaḥ// madayantikā/ mā khalvevaṃ mālatīmupahasa// buddharakṣitā/ sakhi madayantike, pṛcchāmīdānīṃ kimapi/ yadi me viśvāsabhaṅgaṃ na karoṣi// madayantikā/ kiṃ punarapi praṇayabhaṅgena kṛtāparādho'yaṃ jano yenaivaṃ mantrayase/ priyasakhi, tvaṃ lavaṅgikā ca sāṃprataṃ me hṛdayam// buddharakṣitā/ yadi te kathamapi makarandaḥ punarapi darśanapathamavatarati tadā kiṃ tvayā kartavyam// madayantikā/ ekaikāvayavanisargalagnaniścale ciraṃ locane nirvāpayiṣye// buddharakṣitā/ atha sa manmathabalātkārito yadi kaṃdarpajananīṃ tvāṃ rukmiṇīmiva puruṣottamaḥ svayaṃgrāhasāhasena sahadharmacāriṇīṃ karoti tadā kīdṛśī pratipattiḥ kiṃ pratipattavyam/ manmathabalātkāraḥ saṃjāto yasmiṃstādṛśaḥ/ kaṃdarpajananīṃ pradyumnamātaraṃ manmathavikārahetuṃ ca/ puruṣottamo hariḥ puruṣaśreṣṭhaśca// madayantikā/ kasmādetāvadāśvasitāsmi/ manorathamātramidaṃ na saṃbhāvyata iti bhāvaḥ// buddharakṣitā/ sakhi, kathaya/ vyaktīkṛtyeti śeṣaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, kathitameva hṛdayāvegasūcakairdīrghaniḥśvāsaiḥ/ iṣṭamevaitadyadi siddhyediti kathitamevetyarthaḥ/ tasmānmakarandānayanopāyaścintyatāmiti bhāvaḥ// madayantikā/ sakhi, kāhametasya tenaivātmānaṃ paṇīkṛtya mṛtyukavalādākṛṣṭasya tasyaiva parakīyasya kṛtyakiṃkarasyātmanaḥ śarīrasya// lavaṅgikā/ sadṛśaṃ khalu mahānubhāvatāyāḥ/ tvaduktamiti śeṣaḥ// buddharakṣitā/ smariṣyasyetadvacanam// madayantikā/ kathaṃ dvitīyayāmavicchedapaṭahastāḍyate/ tadyāvannandanaṃ nirbhartsya sapādapatanaṃ vābhyarthya mālatyā uparyanukūlayiṣyāmi// madayantikā/ sakhi mālati, pratibuddhāsi/ priyatamadarśanaṃ harṣahetuḥ, kulakanyakājanānucitavyāpārāsaktiśca sādhvasahetuḥ/ aho ityāścaryam/ idamanyadeva vartate/ prastutamālatīnandanasaṃghaṭanādanyadeva makarandamadayantikāsaṃghaṭanasaṃdhānaṃ vartata ityarthaḥ/ atha makarando lajjāvailakṣyaśaṅkānirāsenonmukhīkartuṃ priyatamāṃ cāṭūktyā prasādayati - rambhorviti/ he rambhoru kadalīstambhavatpīvaroru, akāṇḍe bhayaṃ ko'sāvaparicita iti matsādhvasaṃ tyaja/ utkampinaḥ sādhvasajanitotkampataralasya stanayugalasya vikāraṃ vikṣobhamatikanīyānmadhyabhāgo na sahate/ yato'paricitāddhi bhītirbhavati/ eṣa iti svātmānaṃ nirdiśati/ ayaṃ janastu nāsaṃstutaḥ kiṃtvitthaṃ pūrvaṃ tvaduktena prakāreṇa tvayaiva kathitapraṇaya eva prasādo yasya sa tādṛśaḥ saṃkalpasamāgameṣu paricito dāsa eva/ tatra kathaṃ te sādhvasavailakṣyamityarthaḥ//2//

 atha buddharakṣitā kumārījanasulabhalajjāvaśāditikartavyatāmūḍhāṃ madayantikāṃ pratibodhayantī tasyā mukhamunnamayyāyameva trapāṃ śithilīkṛtya makarandavaraṇasamaya ityatra hetūnupanyasyati-preyāniti/ yaḥ pūrvaṃ tvayaiva manorathasahasrairvṛtaḥ sa eṣa preyānniratiśayapremāspadaṃ janaḥ saṃnihito vartate/ etaccāmātyagṛhaṃ suptānavahitarakṣitajanam/ tenātra kaścitpaśyediti śaṅkāpi nāsti/ prauḍhaṃ tamaḥ/ tena nagarapālakebhyo'pi śaṅkā nirastā/ tasmātkṛtajñatayaiva bhadraṃ makarandapāṇigrahaṇamaṅgalaṃ kuru/ anantaramūrdhvamutkṣiptamaṇinūpuraṃ yathā syādevaṃ yāmaḥ/ ehi/ nūpuradhvaniranyairyathā na śrūyate, tathordhvamutkṣipya kaiścidapyalakṣitā yatra mālatīmādhavau tatraiva yāmastvaritaṃ mā lajjasvetyarthaḥ//3//

 madayantikā/ sakhi buddharakṣite, kva punaridānīmasmābhirgantavyam// buddharakṣitā/ yatraiva mālatī gatā// madayantikā/ kiṃ nirvṛttasāhasā mālatī/ sāhasaṃ svayamevātmapradānam// buddharakṣitā/ atha kim/ nirvṛttasāhasaivetyarthaḥ/ anyacca tvaṃ bhaṇasi/ ``kāhametasya'' ityādi paṭhati/ tvayā yaduktaṃ tadanurūpaṃ vidhehītyarthaḥ// madayantikā gurujanavidhīyamānasaṃpradāna iva svayamaśrūṇi pātayati// buddharakṣitā ānandāśruvimocanenaiva tasyāḥ svātmapradānamadhigamyāha - mahābhāga, dattaḥ khalu svayamātmā priyasakhyā/ ānandāśrupūrvakamiti bhāvaḥ// makarandaḥ - adyeti/ adya priyatamālābhānmayorjitaṃ balaṃ yathā syādevaṃ vijitameva sarvottaratvaṃ saṃpāditameva/ kimanyaditaḥ paramāśvāsyamavaśiṣṭam/ na kiṃcidityarthaḥ/ tathā phalavato dayitāprāptyaiva saphalasya me navayauvanasyādyotsavo maṅgaladinam/ yanme prasādasumukhena makaradhvajena mādhavābhilaṣitaṃ saṃpādya madabhilaṣitamapi saṃpādayatā bāndhavadhurābandhukṛtyamudyatocitapadaṃ prāpitā//4//

 pakṣadvāreṇa pārśvadvāreṇa sādhayāmo gacchāmaḥ// samīhitaṃ niśīthe niḥsaṃcāreṇa rājamārgasya ramaṇīyatāsmatprayāṇānuguṇyaṃ vartate// prāsādānāmiti/ saudhānāmuparibhāgeṣu valabhījālagavākṣamārgeṣu praviśya prāptāmodaḥ/ ``bhrāntyāvṛttaḥ'' iti pāṭhe gavākṣamārgeṇa harmyataleṣu paribhramya punarāvṛttaḥ/ ata eva pariṇatamadyagandhajanitavāsanānuviddhaḥ/ mālyāmodī punaḥ punaranuṣaktasphuradghanasāragandhī vāto yūnāmabhilaṣitaṃ yuvatīsaṃnidhānaṃ vyanaktīti madyapānamālyādibhiḥ suratādibhogo vyajyate//5//


madvyākhyāmanthamathitādbhavabhūtivacombudheḥ/

rasāmṛtamabhivyaktaṃ svādayantu budhāściram//

iti śrīmadbhāradvājakulatilakaparvatanāthayāyajūkanandanatripurārisūriviracitāyāṃ mālatīmādhavabhāvapradīpikāyāṃ saptamāṅkavivaraṇam/

 aṣṭamo'ṅkaḥ/

vibudharatnāvalī/

atha mālatīmādhavayoḥ kāmandakyāvasathasthitayoḥ kṛtyaveśasaṃpādanānuguṇavyāpārārthamavalokitāpraveśaḥ/ avalokitā/ vanditā mayā nandanāvasathapratinivṛttā bhagavatī/ tadyāvanmālatīmādhavayoḥ sakāśaṃ gacchāmi/ parikramya/ paśyatīti śeṣaḥ/ etau tau parinirvartitagrīṣmadivasāvasānamajjanau dīrghikātīraśilātalamalaṃkurutaḥ/ tadupasarpāmi// mādhavaḥ/ niśīthasya vakṣyamāṇacandrodayatvena manmathaprauḍhasuhṛdaḥ/ manmathasuhṛttā ca manmathasādhyānukūlakaraṇāt/ tatra samaye/ ``sānandasādhvasāḥ suratavyavasāyinyo bhavanti'' iti vātsyāyanaḥ/ tathā hīti kṛtvā tasyā eva niśīthayauvanaśriya utkarṣanimittaṃ vyanakti - dalayatīti/ udgacchannaindavaḥ prakāśastadvacchuṣyattālīpattrapāṇḍuvarṇastimirastomaṃ bhinatti/ marudvegodbhedaketakīparāga iva sphārasphāramunmukhībhūya pracalita iva prakāśopamā//1//

 kulakumārītvena tathā pratyavasitacāritratvena ca tadabhiprāyānukūlaṃ gamyamityācarantīṃ vāmaśīlāmiva vāmaśīlāmupāvartaye svābhimatasiddhipraguṇacittāṃ karomi/ bhavatvityupāyasmaraṇe/ evaṃ tāvatkāmasūtroktena kanyāvisrambhaṇopāyenopāvartanārthaṃ yatiṣya ityabhiprāyeṇa matiṃ vyavasthāpya prakāśamāha - priya iti/ atra nidāghasaṃtāpaśāntaya ityuktyā saṃtāpapratīkāradhiyā na rāgitayaiva tvadaṅgasaṅgaprārthanārthaṃ vijñāpayāmīti premagarbhaṃ vinayapradarśanam/ niścyotanta iti/ sutanviti saṃbodhanenāliṅganātyantopayuktasya nāyikāśarīrasyātiramaṇīyakapradarśanaṃ kimitīdṛśyāṃ daśāyāṃ darśanārhaṃ māṃ na saṃbhāvayasi/ mayā tvaṃ yasminnavasare smaśānabhuvi saṃbhāvitāsi tatsadṛśa evāyaṃ samaya ityāśayaḥ/ niścyotanta ityātmanepadaṃ cintyam/ adya snānavimuktabandhakabarīsaṅgino jalabindavo yāvadeva patanti, yāvadvā stanadvandvāntarbhāgaḥ snānajalārdro vartate, yāvacca snānaśaityātsāndrapulakatvaṃ gatā tāvadevāśleṣaṃ dehi, prasannā bhava/ etena mālatyevaṃ jānātu yatkila madaṅgavartimajjanajanitaśaityamevāsya sparśasaṃtāpaśāntaye satyabhīṣṭam, na punaḥ kāmandakīlavaṅgikādyabhiprāyānapekṣameva pariṇayavidhānamanādṛtya kiṃciccikīrṣuriti vimṛśya mālatī mahyaṃ kāpālikavadhopakārabuddhyā vitaratviti mādhavasyāśayaḥ/ vastutastu jalamajjanajanyāṃ tātkālikīṃ kāntiṃ śobhāmiti sa tatra kāntigatālaṃkāratvena bharatamunisaṃmatāmavalokyātirāgādevāśleṣaprārthanāyāṃ pravṛttiḥ/ ``rūpayauvanalāvaṇyādupabhogopabṛṃhitaiḥ/ alaṃkaraṇamaṅgānāṃ śobheti parikīrtitā/ kāntirityucyate sāpi śobhaivāpūrṇamanmathā//'' tathā/ ``uttiṣṭhantyā ratānte bharamuragapatau pāṇinaikena kṛtvā dhṛtvā cānyena vāso vilulitakabarībhāramaṃse vahantyāḥ/ bhūyastatkālakāntidviguṇitasurataprītinā śauriṇā vaḥ śayyāmāliṅgya nītaṃ vapurativicaladbāhu lakṣmyāḥ punātu//'' iti//2//

 atha kāmasūtravinmādhavastadvacasyapi ``anuttarāṃ copalakṣyāt''
(
ṛ) ityādikāṃ dvitīyabhūmikāmadhiropayannāha - ayīti/ niranukrośe iti roṣodbhāvanena mālatyā vibhīṣikā darśitā/ yathāha vātsyāyanaḥ - ``evaṃ cittānugo bālāmupāyena prasādayet/ tathā cetsānurāgā ca visrabdhā cāpi jāyate// nātyantamānulomyena na cātiprātikūlyataḥ/ siddhiṃ gacchati kanyāsu tasmānmadhyena sādhayet// ātmanaḥ prītijananaṃ yoṣitāṃ mānavardhanam/ kanyāvisrambhaṇaṃ vetti yaśca tāsāṃ priyo bhavet// atilajjānvitetyevaṃ yastu kanyāmupekṣate/ so'nabhiprāyavedīti paśuvatparibhūyate//'' ityādi/ jīvayanniti/ māṃ gatajīvitaprāyaṃ jīvayanniva dhṛtasādhvasasvedarūpasāttvikabhāvo bāhuradhikaṇṭhaṃ kaṇṭhe madīye svayamevārpyatām/ yathā prārthita eva bhavadupari prakoṣṭho mayā kāpālikāpasaraṇāyārjitastathetyāśayaḥ/ aindavamayūkhacumbanena yaḥ sravaṇaśīlaścandrakāntamaṇihārastadvilāsastatsadṛśaḥ/ so'pi saṃtāpaśāntyai kaṇṭhe samarpyate/ atra cāṅkapālīṃ dehi tathādhikaṇṭhaṃ bāhurarpyatāmiti ca prārthanādvayena kanyānudvegakaramupakramaṃ kāmasūtroktaṃ smārayati/ ``pūrvakāyena copakramo viṣahyatvāt''//3//

 athavā kaṇṭhabāhvarpaṇānurūpaṃ dūre tāvadatyantadurlabhatvāt/ mandapuṇyaḥ kathaṃ kena hetunālāpasaṃvibhāgasyānurāgavyañjakavākyāmṛtasyāpyabhājanamasthānam/ dagdhamiti/ mama vapustāvacciraṃ malayānilādibhiḥ smaroddīpanavibhāvaistvatprāptyarthameva yatamānasya dagdhamiva/ dagdhaṃ yadasti tatparirabhya na nirvāpitaṃ na nirvṛttiṃ nītameva/ tadeva tāvanmahadanaucityaṃ tadanucitaṃ mayā na gṛhītam tarhi śrutirmadīyā madāndhakokilakuhūkāreṇātyantamadanoddīpanena vyathitā saṃjātaduḥkhā/ he kiṃnarakaṇṭhi, te vācaṃ hṛdyāṃ hṛdayahāriṇīṃ pibatu/ tuśabdāvatra krameṇa punarathag/ nāmaśabda ivārthaḥ/ kiṃnaramadhurakaṇṭhatvena kokiladhvanistvadvacanādahṛdya iti cāṭukāritvam//4//

 avalokitā/ ayi anirvahaṇaśīle, yadidānīṃ muhūrtamātrāntaritamādhavā/ muhūrtamātrāntaritaścāsau mādhavaśceti vigrahaḥ/ durmanāyamānā mama purato bhaṇasi/ cirāyata āryaputraḥ/ api nāma kiyatā cireṇa prekṣiṣye/ yena punarvivardhitāśeṣasādhvasāvismṛtanimeṣavighnaṃ yathā syādevaṃ nayananimīlanameva vighnamavalokayantyevaṃ bhaṇiṣyāmi dviguṇitāveṣṭanaparirambheṇa saṃbhāvayiṣya iti/ madagrataḥ prāgevamaṅgīkṛtasya sa evāyamāśleṣāvasarasya pariṇāmo'yamadhunā saṃlāpite'pi vaidagdhyamityāśayaḥ// mālatī sāsūyamiva paśyatītyāśayaḥ/ na tu sāsūyameva/ asūyā ca kopo vijñeyaḥ/ kopāviṣkaraṇaṃ madrahasyamīdṛśamanayā vyaktīkṛtamiti hetoḥ/ vastutastu pūrvānurāgadarśitamanoratho'nayāvasare prakaṭīkṛta iti sānandameva paśyatītyāśayaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ prathamāntevāsinyā ityuktyā kāmandakyā tādṛgdūtyācāryakamapi kimasyai samyagupadiṣṭaṃ yataḥ prastāve madvidheyānuguṇamasyā rahasyodbhedanamiti kṛtavatītyāśayaḥ/ akṣayyo'śoṣyo navanavasphūrtiśobhanabhāṣitānyeva ratnāni teṣāṃ saṃcāro'nyatra mukhyavṛtteranyatrāpi kāryānukūlatvena niveśanam/ saṃskaraṇamagrāmyatāpādanam/ prakāśam/ pṛcchatīti śeṣaḥ/ yadavalokitayoktaṃ tatsatyamutotpādyaṃ kathayatīti praśnasyāyamabhiprāyaḥ/ yadi tāvatpṛṣṭā nottaraṃ dāsyati tadānumatirasyā ityākūtopalabdhirbhaviṣyati/ uttarapradāne cāsyā vacanaśravaṇalābho bhaviteti vātsyāyanokterālāpayojane'ntarbhāvaḥ praśnasyetyāśayaḥ/ tadāha - ``sakhīṃ cānukūlāmubhayorapi visrabdhāmantarā kṛtvā kathāyojanam/ tasminnadhomukhī vihaset/ tāṃ cātivādinīmadhikṣipedvivadecca/ parihāsārthamidamanayoktamiti cānuktamapi brūyāt/ tatra tāmapanudya prativacanārthamasaṃpūrṇavākyārthaṃ tūṣṇīmāsīt/ nirbadhyamānā tu nāhamevaṃ bravīmītyavyaktākṣaramanavasitārthaṃ vacanaṃ brūyāt/ nāyakaṃ ca vihasantī kaṭākṣaiḥ prekṣeta'' ityādayo'pītyatrānavasitārthamasaṃpūrṇavākyārtham/ mālatī na mayaivamuktamiti pratipādanārthaṃ prātikūlyena śiraścālayati/ ``nirbadhyamānā tu śiraḥkampena prativacanaṃ dadāti'' iti vātsyāyanaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ lavaṅgikāvalokitayorjīvitena śāpitāsītyuktyā lavaṅgikāvalokite tavātyantahitapravṛtte'natikramaṇīyavacane cetyāśayaḥ// mālatī/ nāhaṃ kimapi jānāmītyuktyā cātmanaḥ kumārībhāvapratipādanārthaṃ lajjāmiva lajjāṃ darśayati/ yathā gurujanānanapekṣya sāhasānuṣṭhānena sahajalajjāyā asaṃbhavāt// mādhavaḥ/ anavasitārthatvena ramyaṃ yadvacastasya cārutā/ nirūpya/ vadatīti śeṣaḥ/ etenānavasitārthatvaṃ gamyate/ ``tena ca nirbadhyamānā nāhamevaṃ bravīmītyanavasitārthaṃ vacanaṃ brūyāt'' iti vātsyāyanoktaṃ smarayati/ atha mālatyekakarārpitakapolamaśru pātayatīti gamyate/ tenāśrupātanimittaṃ mādhavo'valokitāṃ pṛcchati/ avalokite, kimetadanaupayikamaśrupātanamiti/ tvamasyā rahasyavedinīti bhāvaḥ/ bāṣpeti/ asyā mṛgākṣyāḥ svabhāvata eva malaśūnyaṃ kimiti prakṣālyate/ sapadi/ āvayorvacanāntaramityarthaḥ/ yatra kapole svabhāvanirmalatvenāśrujalapūtatvena cenduḥ pratiphalitamayūkhatvena rājate/ asyā mukhāmṛtasarasyamṛtaṃ pātumicchanvikṣiptaraśmibisakāṇḍa ivaikakarasaṃkrāntatvenaikakapolasyāśruṇā prakṣālanaṃ yujyate//5//

 avalokitā/ sakhi, kimidānīmuccalitabāṣpotpīḍaṃ yathā syādevaṃ rudyate// mālatī/ sakhi, kiyacciraṃ lavaṅgikāyā asaṃnidhānaduḥkhamanubhaviṣyāmi/ pravṛttilābho'pi tasyā durlabhaḥ/ iti rudyata iti śeṣaḥ// avalokitā/ tavaiva vacanopanyāsenaiṣā lavaṅgikāṃ smṛtvā tasyāḥ pravṛttilābhanimittamuttāmyati// mādhavaḥ/ api nāmeti saṃbhāvanāyām/ madayantikāṃ prati dūtīvyāpāra udarkastenaivottaraphale labdhe punaḥ phalapadopādānaṃ prastutaphalapratipādanārtham// avalokitā/ mahābhāga, prathamameva śārdūlanakharālaṃkṛtasya makarandasya mohavicchedaṃ nivedayantyā bhagavatyā niyuktena bhavatā mālatyā samaṃ jīvitena hṛdayaṃ prasādīkṛtam/ ko'pi sāṃprataṃ madayantikālābho vardhayiṣyati/ tasya kimidānīṃ pāritoṣakaṃ bhaviṣyati/ mādhavaḥ/ praṣṭavyameva pṛṣṭo'smi/ tvayā vimuktaṃ karma tāvannopādeyaṃ vastu/ veti/ pṛcchidhātordvikarmakatvāditi śeṣaḥ/ atha mādhavo hṛdayajīvitayoḥ prāgeva pāritoṣikatvena dattatvāttayorapyadhikā madvakṣasi kesarasragavaśiṣyate/ pāritoṣikārthaṃ hṛdayamālokyeyamastīti śeṣaḥ/ mālatyā yā matprathamadarśane'bhiṣaṅgasya sākṣiṇī tadadhobhāgāvasthitasya mama preyasīdarśanaphalaparyantaṃ gatamiti śakunatvena bahumanyamānā/ kāmakānanālaṃkārakāriṇo lakṣmīvata iti viśeṣaṇadvayam/ tasyaiva kesarataroḥ/ kusumagrathiteti yāvat/ svotkaṇṭhātiśayadyotanaṃ tathā premṇeti ślokena vyanakti - premṇeti/ yā prasavamālāsyāḥ priyasakhīhasta upanītā/ yā ca vistārī pariṇāhī yaḥ stanakumbhakuḍmalabharastasyotsaṅgastenotsaṅgāropaṇena saṃbhāvitā svābhipretārthasādhakatvenaiva gauravaṃ nītā/ atra kumbhaśabdena sthūlatvam/ kuḍmalaśabdena kalikāparyāyeṇa vardhiṣṇutā ca stanasya sūcitā/ athānantaraṃ nandanena saha pāṇigrahe saṃprāptakalpe māṃ prati nirāśatayā yā kusumasraglavaṅgikeyamityavagate mayi sarvasvadāyaḥ sarvasvadānaṃ kṛtā/ etena pūrvamanurāgastavedṛgāsīt, idānīṃ madabhīṣṭārthasaṃpādanena samayo'tivāhita ityāśayaḥ//6//

 avalokitā/ sakhi mālati, vallabhā khalu ta iyaṃ bakulamālā/ eṣedānīṃ parasya tvadanyasya priyanivedakasya hastaṃ gamiṣyati/ tadadya tvameva priyanivedikā bhavetyāśayaḥ// mālatī/ mālatyāstu priyatamakaṇṭhātsvakaṇṭhaṃ gatāṃ bhūyaśca svakaṇṭhātpriyatamakaṇṭhaṃ gatāṃ vilokyāpūrvānurāgeṣṭasaṃpādakasyeyaṃ tathā taddvāraiṣāvayorantaḥkaraṇasaṃbandhadārḍhyārthaṃ snehasūtrasthānīyeyamiti saṃcintyāveśātpriyaṃ priyasakhyupadiśatīti dviruktiḥ/ avalokite, ubhayamapi tvamevopadiśa/ yena parahastaṃ vrajati kiṃ bravīmītyāśayaḥ// avalokitā/ kathaṃ padaśabda iva/ śrūyata iti śeṣaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ ayeśabda āścarye// mālatī/ diṣṭyānandena vardhase madayantikālābhena// mādhavaḥ/ pariṣvajya/ prathamaṃ madayantikābhilāṣataḥ prasaṅgādevāliṅganaṃ vidhāya paścādevānurāgapāśamiva priyaṃ naḥ saṃvṛttaṃ yanmadayantikālābho jāta ityuktvā kesarasrajaṃ premasūtrapāśamiva mālatyāḥ kaṇṭhe dadāti// avalokitā/ nirvyūḍho nirvāhaṃ nīto bhagavatyāḥ saṃbhāvanābhāro buddharakṣitayā/ saṃbhāvanābhāro madayantikāmakarandayordūtyavyāpāraḥ// mālatī/ asmābhirapi priyasakhi lavaṅgikā dṛśyate/ api saṃbhāvanāyām/ idamapi saṃbhāvyata iti śeṣaḥ// sarvāḥ/ paritrāyatāṃ mahābhāgaḥ/ ardhamārge khalu nagararakṣipuruṣābhiyogo makarandasya jātaḥ/ abhiyogaḥ pratirodhaḥ/ tatastatkālamilitena makarandasaṃbādhādvinirgatena vṛttāvedanāya kalahaṃsakena samaṃ vayamanupreṣitāḥ// kalahaṃsaḥ/ yatheto mukhāgatairasmābhirāyodhanakalakalaḥ śrutaḥ, tathā tarkayāmyanyadapi pārakyaṃ balamupāgatamiti/ pārakyaṃ parakīyasainyamiti deśī/ ``pāra'' iti loke// mālatyavalokite/ samameva harṣodvegasaṃbheda upanata upāgataḥ// mādhavaḥ/ asmadgṛhamanugṛhītaṃ bhavatyetyuktyā makarande svātmanirviśeṣatvaṃ makarandasvīkāreṇāsmadbhavanameva tvayālaṃkṛtamityāśayaḥ/ ekākino'pi vīrasya bahubhirabhiyogaḥ/ tathāvidhaśārdūlavyatikarapratyakṣīkṛtapauruṣasya matsahacaratayā matsamānaparākramasya makarandasya na kiṃcidetadbhavati saṃtrāsanimittaṃ citte na bhavitavyam/ svasthā bhavantvityarthaḥ/ hareriti/ siṃhasyaikākinaḥ sāhase vidheye sa eva svakīyaḥ karaḥ sakhā bhavati/ kīdṛśaḥ/ atule'sādhāraṇe vikrame yaḥ praṇayaḥ prītistatra lālasā vāñchā yasya sa tathāvidhaḥ/ tathā kvaṇantaḥ śabdāyamānā gajāsthigranthyādiṣu ye kararuhāstaiḥ pracaṇḍaḥ/ tathā nakhotpīḍena sphuṭanvikasankaraṭo gaṇḍastasya koṭarādagrātskhalitaṃ yaddānaṃ tena siktamānanaṃ yasya dvipendrasya śirasi sthiraṃ dṛḍhaṃ yadasthi taddalana ekavīraḥ śreṣṭhavīro'sahāyavīro vā/ etena makarandasyārisainyadalane śastrāpekṣāpi nāstīti madayantikāvitrāsanirāsārthamuktikramaḥ//7//

 tadahamapi vikrāntyā vikrameṇa pavitraṃ yathā syādevaṃ vilasata iva vilasato'vajñayaiva taiḥ saha yudhyataḥ pratyanantarībhavāmi dvitīyo bhavāmi// avalokitādayaḥ/ vadantīti śeṣaḥ/ api nāmāpratihatau pratinivartiṣyete mahānubhāvau mādhavamakarandau// mālatī/ sakhyau buddharakṣitāvalokite, tvaritaṃ gatvā bhagavatyā imaṃ vṛttāntaṃ nivedayatam/ tvamapi sakhi lavaṅgike, tvaritaṃ vijñāpayāryaputram/ yadi tāvadyuṣmākaṃ vayamanukampanīyāstato'pramattaṃ yathā syādevaṃ parikrāmateti// mālatī/ hā dhik/ na jñāyate kathamiyatī velātikramyatām/ kena prakāreṇātivāhyatāmityarthaḥ/ bhavatu/ priyasakhyā lavaṅgikāyāḥ pratinivṛttimārgaṃ vyāvartanamārgamavalokayantī sthāsyāmi/ sakhīpratyāvṛttavartmāvalokanameva samayātivāhanopāya ityāśayaḥ/ sphuritaṃ me vāmamananukūlaṃ yathā syādevamavāmanayanena dakṣiṇacakṣuṣā/ strīṇāṃ dakṣiṇākṣisphuraṇasyānimittatvāt/ etaccānimittaṃ saṃnihitamālatīvipatsūcakamityāśayaḥ/ atha samastaparijanarahitāyā mālatyā haraṇārthe baddhavairāyāḥ kapālakuṇḍalāyāḥ praveśaḥ// āḥ pāpe iti kapālakuṇḍalāyā vaco niśamya satrāsaṃ mālatī roditi/ mālatī/ hā āryaputreti/ vākstambho gadgadākhyaḥ sātvikabhāvarūpaḥ/ sa cākasmikamahābhayanimittaḥ/ tallakṣaṇaṃ ca - ``madapramādavrīḍādervākstambho gadgadaṃ viduḥ'' iti/ ādiśabdādbhayādayo'pi gadgadanimittāni bhavanti// kapālakuṇḍalā/ tvadvallabha iti/ pātīti patiḥ/ kanyāviṭaḥ kanyādūṣakaḥ/ tapasvijanasyaiva hantā, na vīrajanasya/ śyenasya māṃsādapakṣiviśeṣasya patanena cakitānanā vyākṣiptānanā vartikā pakṣiviśeṣayuvatiriva tvaṃ mayā kavalīkṛtāsītyātmānaṃ nekṣase/ yadi śaktistadātarhi parirakṣatu//8//

 yāvadityavadhāraṇe/ śrīparvataṃ nītvaiva/ parva parva prati pratiparva/ pratisaṃdhītyarthaḥ/ tilaṃ tilaṃ tilaśaḥ/ tilamātrānmāṃsakhaṇḍānkṛtvā citravadhenaināṃ duḥkhamāriṇīṃ karomi/ iti bandhuvargairavijñātāṃ mālatīmādāya kapālakuṇḍalā niṣkrāntā// madayantikā/ ahamapi mālatīmevānuvartiṣye/ atha/ mālatībhrameṇa madayantikā lavaṅgikāmākārayati/ sakhi mālatīti// praviśya/ lavaṅgikā/ vadatīti śeṣaḥ/ sakhi madayantike, lavaṅgikā khalvaham/ na mālatīti bhāvaḥ// madayantikā/ ayīti sukumārasaṃbodhane/ saṃbhāvito dṛṣṭastvayā mahānubhāvaḥ/ mādhava ityarthaḥ// lavaṅgikā/ nahi nahi/ sa khalūdyānavāṭanirgamādeva kalakalaṃ śrutvā sākṣepāpaviddhavikaṭanijorudaṇḍaniṣṭhuraṃ pradhāvya parānīkaṃ praviṣṭaḥ/ sākṣepaṃ sakopavacanam/ apaviddhastāḍito nijorudaṇḍo yena sa tādṛk/ vāṭo vṛtiḥ/ prākāra iti yāvat/ mārgo vā/ tataḥ pratinivṛttāsmi mandabhāginī/ śṛṇomi ca gṛhe gṛhe guṇānurāganirbharasya pauralokasya hā mādhava mahābhāga hā makaranda sāhasiketi paridevanāni/ mahārājaḥ kila mantriduhitrorvipralambhavṛttāntaṃ śrutvā saṃjātamatsarāvegastatkṣaṇavisarjitānekaprauḍhapadātinivahaścandrātapaśobhitasaudhaśikharasthitaḥ prekṣata iti mantryate// madayantikā/ hā, hatāsmi mandabhāginī// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, mālatī punaḥ kva vartate// madayantikā/ sakhi, sā khalu prathamameva te mārgamavalokayituṃ prasṛtā/ paścādahaṃ tāṃ na paśyāmi/ sā nāmodyānagahanaṃ praviṣṭā bhavet/ nāmeti saṃbhāvanāyām// lavaṅgikā/ sakhi, tvaritamaŸŸyiāvaḥ/ atikātarā me priyasakhyupavanasthitāsminnavasare na dhārayatyātmānam/ lavaṅgikāmadayantike/ sakhi mālati, nanu bhaṇāmi sakhi mālatīti// kalahaṃsaḥ/ diṣṭyā kuśalenāsmi nirgataḥ saṃghaṭṭamārgāt/ saharṣaṃ himāṇahe/ ``himāṇahe bhayaharṣe'' iti deśī/ paśyāmīva nirmalanirantarodvṛttataravāridhārāpratiphalitacandrakiraṇojjvalatpiñjaritabhīṣaṇadarśanaṃ madalīlākalitakāmapālavikaṭabhujadaṇḍāpaviddhahalahelāvistāritordhvakṣubhitakalindatanayāsrotaḥsaṃnibhaṃ viśṛṅkhalotpatitanirdayānandamakarandakṣobhavikalapratirodhapratinivartanoddhatasamastagaganāṅgaṇāvakāśavikasatkolāhalaṃ pārakyasamūhamidānīmapi paśyāmīva/ pratinivartanaṃ palāyanam/ smarāmi ca bhīṣaṇabhujavajrakhacitapañjaratayā paryastāḥ prasṛtāḥ samaravimukhā ye subhaṭāsteṣāṃ hastebhyo'valuptānyāccheditāni vividhānyāyudhāni tairuparuddhaṃ pātitamaśeṣaṃ yadripusainyaṃ tena datto yo vikaṭāpasāraḥ palāyanaṃ tena vyatiriktaḥ śūnyo yo mārgastatra saṃcāreṇa nirvartitaviṣamasāhasaṃ smarāmi ca mādhavamiti saṃbandhaḥ/ aho guṇānurāgo narendrasya, yadidānīṃ saudhaśikharāvatīrṇapratihāravinayopanyāsapraśamitavirodhaḥ saumyaikarasopanītau yau saṃkaṭānniṣkrāntau mādhavamakarandau tayormukhacandrāvavalokya vāraṃvāraṃ prasāritasnigdhalocanaḥ kalahaṃsakādabhijanaṃ śrutvā nirvartitamahārghagurubahumānaḥ sphuranmatsarerṣyāvailakṣyamaṣīmalinitamukhau bhūrivasunandanau madhuropanyāsaiḥ kimidānīṃ yuvayorbhuvanābhogabhūṣaṇābhyāṃ mahānubhāvābhyāṃ navayauvanaguṇābhirāmābhyāṃ jāmātṛbhyāṃ paritoṣa iti pratibodhya tato gato'bhyantaraṃ rājā/ atra matsaraḥ krodhaḥ, udvega īrṣyā etau dvau maṣīsthānīyau/ ābhyāṃ malinitaṃ mukhaṃ nandanasya/ vailakṣyaṃ maṣīsthānīyaṃ bhūrivasoriti vivekaḥ/ etāvapi mādhavamakarandāvāgacchata ityahamapyetaṃ bhagavatyai vṛttāntaṃ nivedayāmi// mādhavaḥ/ preyaso makarandasya/ nirvyājaṃ svābhāvikamūrjitaṃ balavattejaḥ// dorniṣpeṣeti/ tathā hyasya preyaso mitrasya prākprathamaṃ vīrānunmathnataḥ/ kathamunmathnata ityapekṣāyāṃ dorbhyāmeva yo niṣpeṣaḥ saṃcūrṇanaṃ tena śīrṇaṃ saṃcchinnatvena dalatkaṅkālaṃ yathā syāditi kriyāviśeṣaṇam/ aparānyatra ca teṣāṃ praharaṇānyācchidya vikrāmato vikramaṃ kurvataḥ/ evaṃbhūtasya preyaso yaḥ saṃkhyodadhiḥ saṃgrāmasamudrastasya panthāḥ purodeśo dvedhā pārṣṇidvaye stambhitā niśceṣṭā yā padātipaṅktistayā vikaṭo vipulaḥ purastādabhūt/ udvellanta ūrdhvaṃ lalanto ye ruṇḍakhaṇḍanikarāstaiḥ śīrṇasya vyāptasyeti saṃkhyodadherviśeṣaṇam//9//

 vayasya, aho etajjagatsamarakarma vānuśayasthānaṃ paścāttāpasthānam/ adyeti/ yaiḥ subhaṭaiścandrikopabhogasamaye niśīthe līlāliṅganadāyipreyasīpānāvaśiṣṭaṃ tathā pratiphalitendukarakarambitaminduprabhākhaṇḍaruciraṃ madhu pītaṃ saṃpratyeva te gātrairbhavadīyavyāpārabhagnāsthibhiḥ saṃsāriṇaḥ puruṣānasārāṃstucchāṃstathā bhidurānvikalānkathayanti//10//

 tanmālatyagrato madayantikāyāḥ kṛte kalahaṃsakena vistarato varṇyamānamāvayorvyāpāramatarkitopasthitamanubhavāmaḥ/ puro'valokya/ kathaṃ śūnyā iva mālatyā virahitā ivāmī pradeśāḥ/ dṛśyanta iti śeṣaḥ// makarandaḥ/ nūnaṃ śaṅke āvayoḥ samarasaṃkaṭodvegenākulatvādudvignatvāditastato bhramantyastā mālatyādayo'traivodyānagambhīrasthāna ātmānaṃ vinodayanti// mādhavaḥ/ kathayatīti/ vayasya makaranda, tvayi kathayati satyāvayoḥ samarasaṃkaṭavṛttāntaṃ mālatyā sakhī madayantikā mannimittamanayoḥ puruṣaprakāṇḍayoḥ śarīrasaṃdehādayo jāyanta ityatyantaśrāntatayollasitavrīḍaṃ tathā stimitadṛṣṭi niścalanayanaṃ svakaṃ mukhaṃ kartavyābhāvānnamayiṣyati/ punaḥ kīdṛśam/ sasmitā yā mālatī tasyāścalanto ye kaṭākṣāstaiḥ parāhatamiva parāhatam/ parāhatirjugupsā yathā syādevaṃ kaṭākṣairavalokita īdṛśanimittamatyāhitanimittamiti mālatyāḥ sasmitatve kaṭākṣairāhanane ca hetuḥ//11//

 atha mādhavamakarandayoḥ padaśabdaṃ śrutvā mālatīmadhigatāṃ jñātvā tadvirahavyākule lavaṅgikāmadayantike/ vadata iti śeṣaḥ/ sakhi mālati/ anantaraṃ mādhavamakarandāvāgatau vilokya saharṣaṃ vadataḥ/ diṣṭyā punarapi ca tau mahānubhāvau dṛśyete/ atha lavaṅgikāmadayantikayoḥ sakhi mālatīti vacaḥ śrutvā saṃpratyutpannadurnimittaśaṅkitau mādhavamakarandau pṛcchataḥ kva sā mālatīti// ubhe/ kutaḥ kutra sā mālatīti/ kuta iti saptamyarthe tasiḥ/ etena tvatra mālatī nāstyevetyarthaḥ/ padaśabdena bhavatorāvāṃ mandabhāginyau vipralabdhe/ bhavatpadaśabdaṃ mālatīpadaśabdamākalayya sakhi mālatīyuktamityarthaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ bhavatyāviti saṃbuddhau/ kathaṃ kathamapi kenakenāpi prakāreṇa vaktumapyaśakyaṃ sahasradhaiva dhvaṃsate hṛdayam/ tata idamabhidhīyatām/ mama hīti/ mama tveṣo'ntarātmā mama ceto hi sphuṭaṃ kuvalayākṣīṃ mālatīṃ prati ``prema paśyati bhayānyapade'pi'' ityaniṣṭhaikacintanaśīlaṃ tairanubaddhotkampa eva/ yataḥ atra sa eva tāvadavaśyamanubhaviṣyasi kapālakuṇḍalākopasya vijṛmbhitamiti vacanasmaraṇādapi sa tathotkampa ityāśayaḥ/ tathā vāmākṣispandanaṃ kaṣṭahetutvātkaṣṭam, tathaivedṛksakhyorvacanaṃ kaṣṭam/ taddhatakadaivena hato'smi//12//

 madayantikā/ tathā khalvito vinirgate mahānubhāve tvayi sati buddharakṣitāmavalokitāṃ ca bhagavatīsakāśaṃ visṛjyāpramādanimittaṃ vijñāpayāryaputramiti lavaṅgikānupreṣitā/ tata uttāmyamānā caitasyā mārgamavalokayitumagrataḥ prasṛtā mālatī/ paścādahaṃ prasṛtetyarthaḥ/ na tataḥ paśyāmi tato'smābhiratra vicitā yāvadyuvāṃ dṛṣṭāviti// mādhavaḥ/ kimapīti/ he caṇḍi atikopane, ayaṃ parihāsaḥ svātmapracchādanarūpo viramatu/ parihāsaṃ parityaja/ tataḥ kimapi kimapīti yadvaktumapi na śakyate maṅgalebhyo yadanyadamaṅgalaṃ tadahaṃ tvayi śaṅke/ atastvaddarśanārthaṃ paryutsuko'smi/ athavā kalayasi jñātumicchasi māmanurāgavānayaṃ virāgo veti rūpeṇa, tarhi kalito'haṃ tvayaiva mahāmāṃsavikrayaprakramādiṣu/ tadidānīṃ vallabhe, vācaṃ prayaccha/ yato madīyaṃ hṛdayaṃ vihvalaṃ vikalamantarevāvasthitiṃ na labhate/ tvaṃ caivaṃ nirdayāsi//13//

 makarandaḥ/ avijñāyāniścitya/ tadvipadamityāśayaḥ// mādhavaḥ/ anyastāvadudāsīno na jānāti/ tvamapi kiṃ mālatīti na jānāsi/ mama prāptau matsnehena duḥkhitayā kātarayā tayā yacceṣṭitamiti caurikāvivāhaprastāve mālatyāḥ svaśarīraparityāgādhyavasāyaṃ smārayati// ubhe/ etadapi saṃbhāvyate/ yadbhagavatīpādasevāṃ kartuṃ gatā bhaviṣyatyevam// mādhavaḥ/ evaṃ nāmāstu/ nāmaśabdaḥ saṃbhāvanāyām// makarandaḥ/ yāteti/ sā no'smākaṃ sarveṣāṃ mādhavapremaviṣayatayā tasyā eva guṇaviśeṣaiśca niḥsīmasnehaviṣayatvātsakhīva sakhī mālatī yātā gatā/ tadbhagavatībhavanaṃ nagarādhyakṣapuruṣāvarodhavyatikarasaṃbhramādibhiḥ satyameva jīvantī prāṇāndhārayantī punarapyeṣyati na vetyāśaṅkito'smi/ aghoraghaṇṭavyāghravadharājaviruddhavyāpāraśca mālatījīvitasaṃdehāśaṅkābhijñānāni/ atha mālatīvipadamanayā niścitya vaiṣayikasukhasyāsthiratvaṃ ślokasyottarārdhena vyavasthāpayati - prāyeṇeti/ bāndhavādisaṃgamasaukhyaṃ saudāminīsphuraṇavaccañcalam/ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ/ bāndhavādisaṅga ādiryasya saukhyasya tattādṛk/ atha ca prathamāṅkamukhasaṃdhisūcitāyāḥ saudāminyāḥ sphuraṇaṃ sphūrtirvyāpāraviśeṣo vā tanmātranidānaṃ mālatīvipatpratikaraṇaṃ mādhavaduḥkhaprahāṇaṃ ca manasi kṛtvā prakaraṇakṛdagrimāṅke saudāminīpraveśasūcanāya ca saudāminīpadamaṅkāntaśloke niveśitavān//14//


śabarasvāmiprītyā prakaraṇaratnākare'tra bhavabhūteḥ/

sāroddhāre mandaramakṛta kṛtī vyākṛtiṃ nānyaḥ//

iti śrīnānyadevasya kṛtau bibudharatnāvalyāṃ mālatīmādhavaṭīkāyāmaṣṭamāṅkavivaraṇam//

 navamo'ṅkaḥṣṭaśrīparvate kapālakuṇḍalāpahṛtāṃ mālatīmācchidya niṣpratyūhe deśe nidhāya mālatīvināśaśaṅkino mādhavasyāvaśyaṃbhāvivināśamāśaṅkya jīvatyeva samāgamiṣyatīti mādhavapratītihetoḥ mālatīkaṇṭhādbakulāvalīmādāya saudāminī satvaramutpatitā/ asminnavasare navamāṅkārambhaḥ/ tata iti/ saudāminī svātmagatameva svavidheyaṃ vicārayati - eṣāsmīti/ eṣāhaṃ saiva saudāminī nānyā/ yā bhagavatyāḥ kāmandakyā yogābhyāsādyupadeśenānugṛhyaitāvatpadaṃ prāpitā/ tadavaśyaṃ mayā kāmandakyā prāṇenāpi rakṣaṇīyayormālatīmādhavayorvipatpratīkāraḥ prāṇavyayenāpi vidheya ityeṣāsmīti padasyāśayaḥ/ bhagavataḥ pūjyācchrīparvatādupetya gatvā padmāvatīm/ tatra padmāvatyāṃ mālatīviprayuktasya mādhavasya bṛhaddroṇyopalakṣito yaḥ śailastasya kāntāraṃ durgamārgastatra pradeśastatra praveśamupaśrutyādhunā tadantikamanuprayāmi/ bhavatu tasya kāntārapraveśaḥ kimetāvatā syādityāśayaḥ/ kiṃbhūtasya mādhavasya - mālatyā saha krīḍādyadhikaraṇatvena saṃstutaḥ paricito deśastasyedānīṃ mālatīṃ vinā tadavalokanāsahiṣṇoḥ kathaṃ kāntārapraveśaḥ kiṃvidho vetyapekṣādvaye saha suhṛdvargeṇa saṃstyāyaṃ gṛhaṃ parityajyeti padadvayaṃ yojyam/ atra bhagavacchabdoktapūjyatvaṃ ca śrīparvatasyānekamantrasādhanakṣetratvena/ tathāhamiti/ tathā tena vegātiśayena tathoccadeśagamanamāśritya votpatitenātmanaḥ śarīralaghutotkarṣasahitaṃ yogaparipālanaliṅgabhūtaṃ tathā śīghragāmitāṃ cāścaryahetutvenātmagatameva cintayati/ laghimā laghurbhavati yena sūryakarānavalambya tadbimbaṃ praviśatītyuccairgaganārohaṇaṃ ca mādhavasya giridroṇyā śrīparvatasyāpi spaṣṭadarśanārtham/ vyatikaro viśeṣaḥ samūho vā/ cakṣū raśmimaṇḍalena pariṣicyate'bhivyāpya gṛhyate/ sādhu sādhu/ padmāvatīṃ śīghraṃ prāptāsmi/ bhadramāsannamiti sādhupadābhyāsahetuḥ/ padmāvatīti/ padmāvatī nagarī antarikṣaṃ gaganamiva bibharti/ kasmādityapekṣāyāṃ vimalavāribhirviśālā vipulā yā sindhurnāma nadīviśeṣaḥ/ kīdṛśamantarikṣam/ uttuṅgā ye saudhāsteṣāṃ saṃghaṭṭanena pūrvaṃ pāṭitaṃ paścādvimuktamivetyarthaḥ/ antarikṣaṃ cātra yannīlākāreṇordhvadeśe pratibhāti tajjñeyam/ anyathā vibhutvenāntarikṣasya patanādivārtānupapatteḥ//1//

 seti/ seyaṃ lavaṇā nāma nadī/ janapadaśabdena janapadalokā gṛhyante/ teṣāṃ pramadāya gogarbhiṇīnāṃ priyāstathā navatṛṇarājidhāriṇyo dhāraṇaśīlā yāstā eva vanālayo vibhāntītyanvayaḥ/ atra mālāśabdasya ``iṣṭakeṣīkāmālānāṃ citatūlabhāriṣu'' iti hrasvarūpam/ gogarbhiṇīti rūpaṃ ca ``catuṣpādo garbhiṇyā'' iti sūtreṇa jātisamāse gogarbhiṇī, ajāgarbhiṇīti ca/ acatuṣpāde'pi tu mānuṣīgarbhiṇīti, ajātāvapi kālākṣigarbhiṇīti rūpaṃ syāt//2//

 sa eṣa pūrvaparicitaḥ/ bhagavatīpadena pūjyatvābhidhāyakena tīrthatvaṃ sindhvabhidhānāyāḥ saritaḥ sūcayati/ uccairdeśādadhaḥ śikhareṣu prapatanaṃ prapātaḥ/ uccaiḥpatanāddāritabhūtalaḥ/ yatratyeti/ yatra jāto'yaṃ saṃkulo vibhāvyānekabhaṅgidhvanirabhinavaghanadhvānavatpracaṇḍaḥ/ ayaṃ ca samīpagiripratiphaliteṣu satsu gaṇapatigalagarjitasāmyaṃ gataḥ/ atra ca nūtanaghanadhvanisāmyena manohare prakṛte dhvanau pracaṇḍatvābhidhānaṃ kadācidayaṃ mālatīvirahiṇo mādhavasya śrutipathamavatarannavāmbhodadhvanivatgra[tpra]tibhātīti śaṅkāmutpādayannaniṣṭakāri syāditi hetoḥ//3//

 etā iti/ etāścāraṇyabhūmayaścandanādipracuravṛkṣavanādyutprekṣaṇāddakṣiṇāraṇyagirindrānsmārayanti/ tatsādṛśyenetyarthaḥ/ kīdṛśyo'raṇyabhūmayaḥ/ pakvabilvaphalasugandhāḥ/ kiṃbhūtānbhūdharānityapekṣāyāṃ taruṇakadambādivanairavabaddho dṛḍhīkṛto yo'ndhakārastena guravo ye nikuñjā latādipihitāḥ kaṃdaraviśeṣāsteṣu guñjantī śabdāyamānā gadgadadhvanipradhānapravāhaniḥsaraṇā yā godāvarī tayā bhukharitavipulamekhalābhuva ityanvayaḥ/ ayamiti/ madhumatī nadīviśeṣaḥ saṃbhedaḥ saṃgamastasya pāvanaḥ pavitratotpādakaḥ/ apauruṣeyapratiṣṭhaḥ puruṣaprayatnājanyasthāpanaḥ śrutipratipādyamāhātmyo vā svarṇabinduriti nāma tasya bhagavataḥ/ jayeti/ bhuvanabhāvana caturdaśabhuvanotpādaka, akhilavarada samastānatakāmaprada, nigamanidhe samastavāṅmayanidhe/ ādigurutvaṃ ca sargādau brahmaṇo'pyupadeśakatvāt//4//

 ayamiti/ ayaṃ parvato netrayoḥ sukhaṃ dṛśyamāno janayatītyanvayaḥ/ tasya viśeṣaṇāni/ navajaladaiḥ śyāmikṛtoccasānuḥ/ ata eva hṛṣṭamayūrīmuktāvicchinnakekaḥ/ tathā śakunibhiḥ pakṣibhiḥ śabalāḥ karburā ye nīḍānokahāstaiḥ snigdhaṃ vapurasya sa tādṛk/ śakunayaśca balākāḥ kekinaśca grāhyāḥ/ teṣāmeva tadānīṃ nīḍārambhaḥ/ taireva śvetaśyāmaiḥ karburatā nīḍānāṃ syāt// dadhatīti/ atra ye taruṇāśca bhallūkāsteṣāmambūkṛtāni saniṣṭhīvanāni bhuktāni kuharabhāktvena pratiśabdaguṇotkṛṣṭāni kartṝṇi styānaṃ styānatvaṃ dadhati bibhrati/ yathā sallakīnāṃ vṛkṣaviśeṣāṇāmibhaiḥ pūrvaṃ dalitāḥ paścādvikīrṇā vikṣiptā ye granthayasteṣāṃ yo niṣyando dravaḥ kṣaraṇaṃ tadgandhaḥ styāyate sarvataḥ saṃhato bhavati/ prasaratītyarthaḥ//6//

 kāśmaryā iti/ kāśmaryāḥ madhuparṇītarostasminkāle niṣpatrāt/ udgatadalaṃ taruṇanavapatram/ kṛtamālacchāyam/ koyaṣṭikaḥ pakṣiviśeṣaḥ/ tasya vane saṃbhavāt/ ṭīkate dhāvati/ tathā sarittīre ye'śmantakākhyā atasīparyāyāstacchimbicumbitamukhāstadagraspṛṣṭamātramukhāḥ/ na tu pipāsayā te khāditavanta iti cumbitapadasyāśayaḥ/ pūrṇikā aśokāparanāmānaḥ pakṣiviśeṣāḥ/ jalaṃ gacchanti/ dātyūhaistiniśasya syandanāparanāmno mūle skandhe jātisvabhāvānnilīya sthitam/ tathā latāsthitakulāyavartipārāvatakūjitamanukṛtyādhodeśe kukkubhāḥ kūjanti/ kukkubho grāmacaṭakākṛtiḥ pakṣiviśeṣaḥ/ yadāha - ``śaraccandrikākārasitapuccho nīlagalaḥ syādgrāmacaṭakākṛtiḥ/ kukkubhaḥ kukkuṭārāvaḥ sthalajo raktavarṇakaḥ//'' iti//7//

 iti śuddhaviṣkambhaḥ/ makarandaḥ/ sakaruṇaṃ niḥśvasyātmagatameva/ na yatreti/ ime vayaṃ sarve buddhipauruṣotsāhayuktā api tasyāṃ vipadi vartāmahe tiṣṭhāmaḥ/ yatra vipadi manaḥ kartṛ/ tāmapi tatprāptihetuṃ kāmandakyāśvāsanarūpāṃ pratyāśāṃ nānupatati nāvalambate/ tadāśvāsanasyāviṣayatvenaivābhāvāt/ na vā pratikṣiptaṃ nirāśaṃ tatprāptipratyāśāṃ rahayati tyājayati/ bahuśo'ghoraghaṇṭādivyatikare'pi punaḥpunastallābhadarśanāt/ mohāndhakārameva kevalaṃ praviśati/ mohaścātrākartavyatāparicchedaḥ/ akiṃcitkurvāṇā vipatpratīkārānurūpaṃ kimapi kurmākurvanta[] āhāramātravyāpārāḥ paśava iva/ atra ca pratikūladaivavilasitameva heturnāsmākaṃ puruṣakāravaiguṇyamityāśayaḥ//8//

 mādhavaḥ/ avijñātatattvamavilakṣitavināśakāraṇaṃ yathā syādevaṃ paryavasitāsi vināśaṃ gatāsi/ ata evādbhutam/ priyeti/ priyo mādhavo yasyā iti priyamādhavā svabhāvato bhūtvā kimasya nimittavatsalā/ na cāhaṃ[ha]manyaḥ kiṃtu sa eva mādhavo yastvadarthe kṛtamahāmāṃsavikrayaprastāvocitatādṛśavyāpāra ityātmānurāgaṃ mālatyāstādṛgvipatpratīkārakartṛtvaṃ cātmano manasi kṛtvāha - yamanandayaditi/ māṃ tava karo mūrtimānmahotsava ivānandayat/ kīdṛkkara ityapekṣāyāmāgṛhītakamanīyakaṃkaṇa iti/ kathamanandayadityapekṣāyām/ svayamiti/ yatastvaddarśanajaḍībhūtasya mama kaṇṭhe lavaṅgikādhiyā svakareṇa bakulāvalīsamarpaṇatayā nijadehadānādapīti yojyam//9//

 vayasyeti/ durlabha iti yāvatsneho mayi tayā madanodyānaprastāvanādārabhya darśitastāvataḥ snehasya jagatyanyatra mālatīṃ vihāyāsaṃbhavaḥ/ anyatra nāyaka īdṛganurāgo na darśita ityarthaḥ/ tameva snehotkarṣamāha - saraseti/ madhvārdranavamallikādikusumavatkṣāmaiḥ sukumāraiściramanavaratamunmathanaśīlastāvatsoḍhaḥ/ kṣāmapadasya sukumārārthatāyāmabhibhavāsahiṣṇutāyāmubhayatrāpi mamaiveti bījaṃ pratikṣaṇadāruṇamṛtyorviśeṣeṇa nimittamiti jvaraviśeṣaṇam/ tada[ta]stadanantaraṃ ca madaprāptiśaṅkāyāṃ tṛṇaprāyānprāṇānmamatvāttāneva tyaktuṃ cittaṃ vidhṛtaṃ vyavasthāpitam/ āstāṃ sarvametadyattayā kulakumāryā gurujanamanādṛtya karārpaṇasāhasamanurāgeṇa pāṇigrahaṇasāhasaṃ nirvyūḍhaṃ nirvāhaṃ nītamataḥ paraṃ kiṃ brūma ityāśayaḥ//10//

 api ceti kṛtvā mālatīguṇāntarakīrtanena caturthasmarāvasthāliṅgairunmādākhyasaptamāvasthāyā bhūmikāṃ racayannāha - mayīti/ smarasi yathā tvaṃ nandanavivāhaprastāvātpūrvaṃ matprāptau vigalitapratyāśatvādvikalārūṇaistathā marmacchede yā vyathā tayā vidhurairivodvegadāyibhī ruditaistathāpi tasyāmapi daśāyāṃ mayi snehākūtaṃ snehapradhānamāśayamatanodvistāritavatī/ asāvahamapi yathābhūvaṃ yādṛgāsaṃ pīḍātaraṅgitacetāstathā tvameva sarvaṃ smarasītyanvayaḥ//11//
